/* =============================================================
   001. APPLICATION CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================= */

const PBL = Object.freeze({
  APP_NAME: "PONT BASCULE LIVE",
  VERSION: "2.0.0",
  TIME_ZONE: "Africa/Casablanca",
  DEFAULT_PIN: "1234",
  SESSION_DURATION_SECONDS: 1800,
  STORAGE_PREFIX: "PBL_",
  MAX_STORAGE_CHUNK: 8000,
  MAX_TICKETS_PER_PAGE: 100,
  DEFAULT_CURRENCY: "MAD",
  DEFAULT_WEIGHT_UNIT: "KG",
  REPORT_DATE_FORMAT: "dd/MM/yyyy",
  DISPLAY_DATE_FORMAT: "dd/MM/yyyy",
  DISPLAY_TIME_FORMAT: "HH:mm:ss",
  TIMESTAMP_FORMAT: "dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm:ss",
  SYSTEM_STATUS: "ONLINE"
});

/* =============================================================
   002. STORAGE KEYS
   ============================================================= */

const PBL_KEYS = Object.freeze({
  INITIALIZED: "INITIALIZED",
  PIN_HASH: "PIN_HASH",
  PIN_SALT: "PIN_SALT",
  SETTINGS: "SETTINGS",
  TICKETS: "TICKETS",
  PRODUCTS: "PRODUCTS",
  CLIENTS: "CLIENTS",
  TRUCKS: "TRUCKS",
  DRIVERS: "DRIVERS",
  OBJECTIVES: "OBJECTIVES",
  COUNTERS: "COUNTERS",
  AUDIT: "AUDIT",
  BACKUPS: "BACKUPS",
  SYSTEM: "SYSTEM",
  STORAGE_VERSION: "STORAGE_VERSION"
});

/* =============================================================
   003. WEB APPLICATION ENTRY POINT
   ============================================================= */

function doGet(e) {
  initializeApplication();

  const template = HtmlService.createTemplateFromFile("Index");

  template.applicationName = PBL.APP_NAME;
  template.applicationVersion = PBL.VERSION;
  template.timeZone = PBL.TIME_ZONE;

  return template.evaluate()
    .setTitle(PBL.APP_NAME)
    .addMetaTag("viewport", "width=device-width, initial-scale=1")
    .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.DEFAULT);
}

/* =============================================================
   004. HTML INCLUDE SUPPORT
   ============================================================= */

function include(filename) {
  return HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile(filename).getContent();
}

/* =============================================================
   005. INITIALIZATION
   ============================================================= */

function initializeApplication() {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();

  lock.waitLock(30000);

  try {
    const properties = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

    if (properties.getProperty(PBL_KEYS.INITIALIZED) === "TRUE") {
      return {
        success: true,
        message: "PONT BASCULE LIVE is already initialized."
      };
    }

    const salt = generateSalt();

    properties.setProperty(
      PBL_KEYS.PIN_SALT,
      salt
    );

    properties.setProperty(
      PBL_KEYS.PIN_HASH,
      hashPin(PBL.DEFAULT_PIN, salt)
    );

    initializeStorageCollection(PBL_KEYS.TICKETS, []);
    initializeStorageCollection(PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS, []);
    initializeStorageCollection(PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS, []);
    initializeStorageCollection(PBL_KEYS.TRUCKS, []);
    initializeStorageCollection(PBL_KEYS.DRIVERS, []);
    initializeStorageCollection(PBL_KEYS.OBJECTIVES, []);
    initializeStorageCollection(PBL_KEYS.AUDIT, []);
    initializeStorageCollection(PBL_KEYS.BACKUPS, []);

    initializeStorageCollection(
      PBL_KEYS.COUNTERS,
      {
        ticket: 0,
        audit: 0,
        backup: 0
      }
    );

    initializeStorageCollection(
      PBL_KEYS.SETTINGS,
      getDefaultSettings()
    );

    initializeStorageCollection(
      PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM,
      {
        application: PBL.APP_NAME,
        version: PBL.VERSION,
        initializedAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
        timeZone: PBL.TIME_ZONE,
        status: "ONLINE"
      }
    );

    properties.setProperty(
      PBL_KEYS.STORAGE_VERSION,
      "1"
    );

    properties.setProperty(
      PBL_KEYS.INITIALIZED,
      "TRUE"
    );

    writeAuditLog(
      "SYSTEM_INITIALIZED",
      "Application initialized"
    );

    return {
      success: true,
      message: "PONT BASCULE LIVE initialized."
    };

  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/* =============================================================
   006. DEFAULT SETTINGS
   ============================================================= */

function getDefaultSettings() {
  return {
    applicationName: PBL.APP_NAME,
    companyName: "",
    companyAddress: "",
    companyPhone: "",
    companyEmail: "",
    timeZone: PBL.TIME_ZONE,
    currency: PBL.DEFAULT_CURRENCY,
    weightUnit: PBL.DEFAULT_WEIGHT_UNIT,
    defaultTicketPrefix: "BL",
    ticketNumberLength: 6,
    allowNegativeRemaining: false,
    requireProduct: true,
    requireClient: false,
    requireTruck: false,
    requireDriver: false,
    showLiveClock: true,
    readOnlyByDefault: true,
    requireUnlockForChanges: true,
    printCompanyHeader: true,
    printTicketFooter: true,
    automaticTicketNumber: true,
    createdAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
    updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   007. MOROCCAN LIVE DATE AND TIME
   ============================================================= */

function getLiveDate() {
  return Utilities.formatDate(
    new Date(),
    PBL.TIME_ZONE,
    "dd/MM/yyyy"
  );
}

function getLiveTime() {
  return Utilities.formatDate(
    new Date(),
    PBL.TIME_ZONE,
    "HH:mm:ss"
  );
}

function getLiveTimestamp() {
  return Utilities.formatDate(
    new Date(),
    PBL.TIME_ZONE,
    "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss"
  );
}

function getLiveTimestampDisplay() {
  return Utilities.formatDate(
    new Date(),
    PBL.TIME_ZONE,
    PBL.TIMESTAMP_FORMAT
  );
}

function getLiveClockData() {
  const now = new Date();

  return {
    applicationName: PBL.APP_NAME,
    date: Utilities.formatDate(
      now,
      PBL.TIME_ZONE,
      "dd/MM/yyyy"
    ),
    time: Utilities.formatDate(
      now,
      PBL.TIME_ZONE,
      "HH:mm:ss"
    ),
    day: Utilities.formatDate(
      now,
      PBL.TIME_ZONE,
      "EEEE"
    ),
    month: Utilities.formatDate(
      now,
      PBL.TIME_ZONE,
      "MMMM"
    ),
    year: Utilities.formatDate(
      now,
      PBL.TIME_ZONE,
      "yyyy"
    ),
    timestamp: Utilities.formatDate(
      now,
      PBL.TIME_ZONE,
      "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss"
    ),
    timeZone: PBL.TIME_ZONE,
    status: "ONLINE"
  };
}

/* =============================================================

   ============================================================= */

function apiGetLiveClock() {
  return getLiveClockData();
}

/* =============================================================
   009. STORAGE INITIALIZATION
   ============================================================= */

function initializeStorageCollection(key, value) {
  const properties = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

  if (properties.getProperty(key) !== null) {
    return false;
  }

  saveCollection(key, value);

  return true;
}

/* =============================================================
   010. STORAGE SERIALIZATION
   ============================================================= */

function serializeData(data) {
  return JSON.stringify(data);
}

function deserializeData(serialized) {
  if (!serialized) {
    return null;
  }

  return JSON.parse(serialized);
}

/* =============================================================
   011. STORAGE READ
   ============================================================= */

function loadCollection(key) {
  const properties = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

  const directValue = properties.getProperty(key);

  if (directValue !== null) {
    return deserializeData(directValue);
  }

  const chunkCount = Number(
    properties.getProperty(key + "_CHUNKS") || 0
  );

  if (chunkCount === 0) {
    return [];
  }

  let serialized = "";

  for (let index = 0; index < chunkCount; index++) {
    const chunk = properties.getProperty(
      key + "_CHUNK_" + index
    );

    if (chunk === null) {
      throw new Error(
        "Storage integrity error: missing chunk " + index
      );
    }

    serialized += chunk;
  }

  return deserializeData(serialized);
}

/* =============================================================
   012. STORAGE WRITE
   ============================================================= */

function saveCollection(key, data) {
  const properties = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

  const serialized = serializeData(data);

  const existingCount = Number(
    properties.getProperty(key + "_CHUNKS") || 0
  );

  properties.deleteProperty(key);

  for (let index = 0; index < existingCount; index++) {
    properties.deleteProperty(
      key + "_CHUNK_" + index
    );
  }

  if (serialized.length <= PBL.MAX_STORAGE_CHUNK) {
    properties.setProperty(key, serialized);
    properties.deleteProperty(key + "_CHUNKS");
    return true;
  }

  const chunks = splitIntoChunks(
    serialized,
    PBL.MAX_STORAGE_CHUNK
  );

  chunks.forEach(function(chunk, index) {
    properties.setProperty(
      key + "_CHUNK_" + index,
      chunk
    );
  });

  properties.setProperty(
    key + "_CHUNKS",
    String(chunks.length)
  );

  return true;
}

/* =============================================================
   013. STORAGE CHUNK SPLITTER
   ============================================================= */

function splitIntoChunks(value, size) {
  const chunks = [];

  for (let position = 0; position < value.length; position += size) {
    chunks.push(
      value.substring(position, position + size)
    );
  }

  return chunks;
}

/* =============================================================
   014. STORAGE DELETE
   ============================================================= */

function deleteCollection(key) {
  const properties = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

  const count = Number(
    properties.getProperty(key + "_CHUNKS") || 0
  );

  properties.deleteProperty(key);

  properties.deleteProperty(key + "_CHUNKS");

  for (let index = 0; index < count; index++) {
    properties.deleteProperty(
      key + "_CHUNK_" + index
    );
  }

  return true;
}

/* =============================================================
   015. SECURITY — RANDOM SALT
   ============================================================= */

function generateSalt() {
  return Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, "") +
    Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, "");
}

/* =============================================================
   016. SECURITY — PIN HASH
   ============================================================= */

function hashPin(pin, salt) {
  const input = String(salt) + ":" + String(pin);

  const digest = Utilities.computeDigest(
    Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256,
    input,
    Utilities.Charset.UTF_8
  );

  return digest.map(function(byte) {
    const unsigned = (byte + 256) % 256;
    return ("0" + unsigned.toString(16)).slice(-2);
  }).join("");
}

/* =============================================================
   017. SECURITY — CONSTANT-TIME COMPARISON
   ============================================================= */

function secureCompare(first, second) {
  first = String(first || "");
  second = String(second || "");

  if (first.length !== second.length) {
    return false;
  }

  let difference = 0;

  for (let index = 0; index < first.length; index++) {
    difference |= first.charCodeAt(index) ^
      second.charCodeAt(index);
  }

  return difference === 0;
}

/* =============================================================
   018. SECURITY — SESSION CREATION
   ============================================================= */

function createUnlockSession(pin) {
  initializeApplication();

  const properties = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

  const salt = properties.getProperty(PBL_KEYS.PIN_SALT);
  const storedHash = properties.getProperty(PBL_KEYS.PIN_HASH);

  const suppliedHash = hashPin(pin, salt);

  if (!secureCompare(suppliedHash, storedHash)) {
    writeAuditLog(
      "UNLOCK_FAILED",
      "Incorrect PIN entered"
    );

    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Incorrect PIN."
    };
  }

  const token = Utilities.getUuid() +
    Utilities.getUuid();

  CacheService.getScriptCache().put(
    "PBL_SESSION_" + token,
    "UNLOCKED",
    PBL.SESSION_DURATION_SECONDS
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "UNLOCK_SUCCESS",
    "Editing session opened"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    token: token,
    expiresIn: PBL.SESSION_DURATION_SECONDS,
    message: "Editing unlocked."
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   019. SECURITY — SESSION VALIDATION
   ============================================================= */

function validateUnlockSession(token) {
  if (!token || typeof token !== "string") {
    return false;
  }

  const session = CacheService.getScriptCache().get(
    "PBL_SESSION_" + token
  );

  return session === "UNLOCKED";
}

/* =============================================================
   020. SECURITY — LOCK SESSION
   ============================================================= */

function lockUnlockSession(token) {
  if (token) {
    CacheService.getScriptCache().remove(
      "PBL_SESSION_" + token
    );
  }

  writeAuditLog(
    "SESSION_LOCKED",
    "Editing session closed"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Application returned to read-only mode."
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   021. SECURITY — REQUIRE EDIT ACCESS
   ============================================================= */

function requireEditAccess(token) {
  if (!validateUnlockSession(token)) {
    throw new Error(
      "EDIT ACCESS DENIED. Unlock PONT BASCULE LIVE first."
    );
  }

  return true;
}

/* =============================================================
   022. CHANGE PIN
   ============================================================= */

function changeUnlockPin(token, oldPin, newPin) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (!/^[0-9]{4,12}$/.test(String(newPin))) {
    throw new Error(
      "PIN must contain between 4 and 12 digits."
    );
  }

  const properties = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

  const salt = properties.getProperty(PBL_KEYS.PIN_SALT);
  const storedHash = properties.getProperty(PBL_KEYS.PIN_HASH);

  const suppliedHash = hashPin(oldPin, salt);

  if (!secureCompare(suppliedHash, storedHash)) {
    throw new Error("Current PIN is incorrect.");
  }

  const newSalt = generateSalt();

  properties.setProperty(
    PBL_KEYS.PIN_SALT,
    newSalt
  );

  properties.setProperty(
    PBL_KEYS.PIN_HASH,
    hashPin(newPin, newSalt)
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "PIN_CHANGED",
    "Unlock PIN changed"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "PIN successfully changed."
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   023. ACCESS STATUS
   ============================================================= */

function getAccessStatus(token) {
  return {
    application: PBL.APP_NAME,
    mode: validateUnlockSession(token)
      ? "EDITING"
      : "READ ONLY",
    unlocked: validateUnlockSession(token),
    time: getLiveTimestampDisplay()
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   024. AUDIT LOG
   ============================================================= */

function writeAuditLog(action, description) {
  try {
    const properties = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

    if (
      properties.getProperty(PBL_KEYS.INITIALIZED) !== "TRUE"
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    const records = loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.AUDIT) || [];

    const counters = loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.COUNTERS) || {
      ticket: 0,
      audit: 0,
      backup: 0
    };

    counters.audit++;

    records.push({
      id: counters.audit,
      action: String(action || ""),
      description: String(description || ""),
      timestamp: getLiveTimestamp(),
      application: PBL.APP_NAME
    });

    const maximumRecords = 5000;

    while (records.length > maximumRecords) {
      records.shift();
    }

    saveCollection(PBL_KEYS.AUDIT, records);
    saveCollection(PBL_KEYS.COUNTERS, counters);

    return true;

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Audit log error: " + error.message);
    return false;
  }
}

/* =============================================================
   025. AUDIT LOG RETRIEVAL
   ============================================================= */

function getAuditLogs(token, limit) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const records = loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.AUDIT) || [];

  const maximum = Math.min(
    Math.max(Number(limit) || 100, 1),
    1000
  );

  return records.slice(-maximum).reverse();
}

/* =============================================================
   026. APPLICATION INFORMATION
   ============================================================= */

function getApplicationInformation() {
  return {
    name: PBL.APP_NAME,
    version: PBL.VERSION,
    timeZone: PBL.TIME_ZONE,
    date: getLiveDate(),
    time: getLiveTime(),
    timestamp: getLiveTimestamp(),
    status: PBL.SYSTEM_STATUS,
    defaultMode: "READ ONLY",
    unlockRequiredForEditing: true
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   027. SYSTEM HEALTH
   ============================================================= */

function getSystemHealth() {
  const properties = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

  const requiredKeys = [
    PBL_KEYS.INITIALIZED,
    PBL_KEYS.PIN_HASH,
    PBL_KEYS.PIN_SALT,
    PBL_KEYS.SETTINGS,
    PBL_KEYS.TICKETS,
    PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS,
    PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS,
    PBL_KEYS.TRUCKS,
    PBL_KEYS.DRIVERS
  ];

  const checks = requiredKeys.map(function(key) {
    return {
      key: key,
      available: properties.getProperty(key) !== null
    };
  });

  return {
    application: PBL.APP_NAME,
    status: checks.every(function(check) {
      return check.available;
    }) ? "HEALTHY" : "WARNING",
    checkedAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
    checks: checks
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   028. API — INITIAL APPLICATION DATA
   ============================================================= */

function apiGetInitialData(token) {
  initializeApplication();

  const settings = loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.SETTINGS) ||
    getDefaultSettings();

  const tickets = loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.TICKETS) || [];

  const products = loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS) || [];

  const clients = loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS) || [];

  return {
    application: getApplicationInformation(),
    clock: getLiveClockData(),
    access: getAccessStatus(token),
    settings: settings,
    ticketCount: tickets.length,
    productCount: products.length,
    clientCount: clients.length,
    health: getSystemHealth()
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   036. GENERAL VALIDATION HELPERS
   ============================================================= */

function isBlank(value) {
  return value === null ||
    value === undefined ||
    String(value).trim() === "";
}

function cleanText(value, maximumLength) {
  const text = String(value === undefined ? "" : value);

  return text.trim().substring(
    0,
    maximumLength || 500
  );
}

function toNumber(value, fallback) {
  const number = Number(value);

  return Number.isFinite(number)
    ? number
    : (fallback || 0);
}

function roundNumber(value, decimals) {
  const factor = Math.pow(
    10,
    Number(decimals) || 2
  );

  return Math.round(
    (Number(value) + Number.EPSILON) * factor
  ) / factor;
}

/* =============================================================
   037. UNIQUE IDENTIFIERS
   ============================================================= */

function createUniqueId(prefix) {
  return String(prefix || "PBL") +
    "_" +
    Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, "").toUpperCase();
}

/* =============================================================
   038. SAFE COLLECTION ACCESS
   ============================================================= */

function getCollectionSafely(key) {
  const value = loadCollection(key);

  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
    return [];
  }

  return value;
}

   039. SYSTEM CONFIGURATION API
   ============================================================= */

function apiGetSettings() {
  return loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.SETTINGS) ||
    getDefaultSettings();
}

/* =============================================================
   041. WEIGHING CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================= */

const PBL_WEIGHING = Object.freeze({
  MIN_WEIGHT_KG: 0,
  MAX_WEIGHT_KG: 150000,
  DEFAULT_DECIMALS: 0,
  TONNE_DIVISOR: 1000,
  DEFAULT_TICKET_STATUS: "VALID",
  DELETED_STATUS: "DELETED",
  PENDING_STATUS: "PENDING",
  COMPLETED_STATUS: "COMPLETED",
  WEIGHING_TYPES: [
    "ENTRY_EXIT",
    "GROSS_TARE",
    "MANUAL"
  ]
});

/* =============================================================
   042. WEIGHT VALIDATION
   ============================================================= */

function validateWeight(value, fieldName) {
  const weight = Number(value);

  if (!Number.isFinite(weight)) {
    throw new Error(
      (fieldName || "Weight") + " must be a valid number."
    );
  }

  if (weight < PBL_WEIGHING.MIN_WEIGHT_KG) {
    throw new Error(
      (fieldName || "Weight") + " cannot be negative."
    );
  }

  if (weight > PBL_WEIGHING.MAX_WEIGHT_KG) {
    throw new Error(
      (fieldName || "Weight") + " exceeds the permitted limit."
    );
  }

  return roundNumber(
    weight,
    PBL_WEIGHING.DEFAULT_DECIMALS
  );
}

/* =============================================================
   043. WEIGHT CALCULATIONS
   ============================================================= */

function calculateNetWeight(grossWeight, tareWeight) {
  const gross = validateWeight(
    grossWeight,
    "Gross weight"
  );

  const tare = validateWeight(
    tareWeight,
    "Tare weight"
  );

  if (gross < tare) {
    throw new Error(
      "Gross weight cannot be lower than tare weight."
    );
  }

  return roundNumber(
    gross - tare,
    PBL_WEIGHING.DEFAULT_DECIMALS
  );
}

function calculateWeightInTonnes(weightKg) {
  const weight = validateWeight(
    weightKg,
    "Weight"
  );

  return roundNumber(
    weight / PBL_WEIGHING.TONNE_DIVISOR,
    3
  );
}

function calculateWeightInKilograms(weightTonnes) {
  const tonnes = Number(weightTonnes);

  if (!Number.isFinite(tonnes) || tonnes < 0) {
    throw new Error("Invalid tonne quantity.");
  }

  return roundNumber(
    tonnes * PBL_WEIGHING.TONNE_DIVISOR,
    0
  );
}

function calculateWeighing(grossWeight, tareWeight) {
  const gross = validateWeight(
    grossWeight,
    "Gross weight"
  );

  const tare = validateWeight(
    tareWeight,
    "Tare weight"
  );

  const net = calculateNetWeight(gross, tare);

  return {
    grossKg: gross,
    tareKg: tare,
    netKg: net,
    grossTonnes: calculateWeightInTonnes(gross),
    tareTonnes: calculateWeightInTonnes(tare),
    netTonnes: calculateWeightInTonnes(net)
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   044. TICKET NUMBERING
   ============================================================= */

function getTicketCounter() {
  const counters = loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.COUNTERS) || {};

  return Number(counters.ticket || 0);
}

function updateTicketCounter(value) {
  const counters = loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.COUNTERS) || {
    ticket: 0,
    audit: 0,
    backup: 0
  };

  counters.ticket = Number(value);

  saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.COUNTERS,
    counters
  );

  return counters.ticket;
}

function generateTicketNumber() {
  const settings = loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.SETTINGS) ||
    getDefaultSettings();

  const prefix = cleanText(
    settings.defaultTicketPrefix || "BL",
    12
  );

  const length = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(
      Number(settings.ticketNumberLength) || 6,
      12
    )
  );

  const nextNumber = getTicketCounter() + 1;

  updateTicketCounter(nextNumber);

  return prefix + String(nextNumber).padStart(length, "0");
}

function findTicketByNumber(ticketNumber) {
  const tickets = getCollectionSafely(
    PBL_KEYS.TICKETS
  );

  const target = String(ticketNumber || "").trim();

  return tickets.find(function(ticket) {
    return String(ticket.ticketNumber) === target &&
      ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
  }) || null;
}

function ticketNumberExists(ticketNumber, excludedId) {
  const tickets = getCollectionSafely(
    PBL_KEYS.TICKETS
  );

  return tickets.some(function(ticket) {
    return String(ticket.ticketNumber) ===
        String(ticketNumber) &&
      ticket.id !== excludedId &&
      ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   045. TICKET DATE AND TIME
   ============================================================= */

function createTicketTimestamp() {
  return {
    date: getLiveDate(),
    time: getLiveTime(),
    timestamp: getLiveTimestamp(),
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    timeZone: PBL.TIME_ZONE
  };
}

function getTicketDateKey(ticket) {
  if (ticket.dateKey) {
    return String(ticket.dateKey);
  }

  return String(ticket.timestamp || "").substring(0, 10);
}

/* =============================================================
   046. TICKET INPUT NORMALIZATION
   ============================================================= */

function normalizeTicketInput(input) {
  if (!input || typeof input !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Ticket data is required.");
  }

  const grossWeight = validateWeight(
    input.grossKg !== undefined
      ? input.grossKg
      : input.grossWeight,
    "Gross weight"
  );

  const tareWeight = validateWeight(
    input.tareKg !== undefined
      ? input.tareKg
      : input.tareWeight,
    "Tare weight"
  );

  const calculation = calculateWeighing(
    grossWeight,
    tareWeight
  );

  const settings = loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.SETTINGS) ||
    getDefaultSettings();

  const product = cleanText(input.product || "", 120);
  const client = cleanText(input.client || "", 160);
  const truck = cleanText(input.truck || "", 80);
  const driver = cleanText(input.driver || "", 120);

  if (settings.requireProduct && isBlank(product)) {
    throw new Error("Product is required.");
  }

  if (settings.requireClient && isBlank(client)) {
    throw new Error("Client is required.");
  }

  if (settings.requireTruck && isBlank(truck)) {
    throw new Error("Truck registration is required.");
  }

  if (settings.requireDriver && isBlank(driver)) {
    throw new Error("Driver is required.");
  }

  return {
    product: product,
    productCode: cleanText(input.productCode || "", 60),
    client: client,
    clientCode: cleanText(input.clientCode || "", 60),
    truck: truck,
    truckRegistration: cleanText(
      input.truckRegistration || truck,
      80
    ),
    driver: driver,
    driverPhone: cleanText(input.driverPhone || "", 40),
    grossKg: calculation.grossKg,
    tareKg: calculation.tareKg,
    netKg: calculation.netKg,
    grossTonnes: calculation.grossTonnes,
    tareTonnes: calculation.tareTonnes,
    netTonnes: calculation.netTonnes,
    weighingType: cleanText(
      input.weighingType || "GROSS_TARE",
      30
    ),
    reference: cleanText(input.reference || "", 100),
    orderNumber: cleanText(input.orderNumber || "", 100),
    deliveryLocation: cleanText(
      input.deliveryLocation || "",
      160
    ),
    remarks: cleanText(input.remarks || "", 1000),
    operator: cleanText(input.operator || "", 100),
    status: PBL_WEIGHING.DEFAULT_TICKET_STATUS
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   047. TICKET CREATION
   ============================================================= */

function createTicket(token, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();

  lock.waitLock(30000);

  try {
    const normalized = normalizeTicketInput(input);

    const tickets = getCollectionSafely(
      PBL_KEYS.TICKETS
    );

    const ticketNumber = generateTicketNumber();

    const timestamp = createTicketTimestamp();

    const ticket = Object.assign(
      {},
      normalized,
      timestamp,
      {
        id: createUniqueId("TICKET"),
        ticketNumber: ticketNumber,
        status: PBL_WEIGHING.DEFAULT_TICKET_STATUS,
        createdBy: cleanText(
          input.operator || "OPERATOR",
          100
        ),
        updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
        updatedBy: "",
        revision: 1,
        deletedAt: null,
        deletedBy: null
      }
    );

    tickets.push(ticket);

    saveCollection(
      PBL_KEYS.TICKETS,
      tickets
    );

    writeAuditLog(
      "TICKET_CREATED",
      "Ticket " + ticketNumber + " created"
    );

    return {
      success: true,
      ticket: ticket,
      message: "Ticket created successfully."
    };

  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/* =============================================================
   048. TICKET MODIFICATION
   ============================================================= */

function updateTicket(token, ticketId, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();

  lock.waitLock(30000);

  try {
    const tickets = getCollectionSafely(
      PBL_KEYS.TICKETS
    );

    const index = tickets.findIndex(function(ticket) {
      return ticket.id === String(ticketId) &&
        ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
    });

    if (index === -1) {
      throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
    }

    const oldTicket = tickets[index];

    const normalized = normalizeTicketInput(input);

    const updatedTicket = Object.assign(
      {},
      oldTicket,
      normalized,
      {
        id: oldTicket.id,
        ticketNumber: oldTicket.ticketNumber,
        date: oldTicket.date,
        time: oldTicket.time,
        timestamp: oldTicket.timestamp,
        createdAt: oldTicket.createdAt,
        createdBy: oldTicket.createdBy,
        updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
        updatedBy: cleanText(
          input.operator || "OPERATOR",
          100
        ),
        revision: Number(oldTicket.revision || 1) + 1
      }
    );

    tickets[index] = updatedTicket;

    saveCollection(
      PBL_KEYS.TICKETS,
      tickets
    );

    writeAuditLog(
      "TICKET_UPDATED",
      "Ticket " + oldTicket.ticketNumber + " updated"
    );

    return {
      success: true,
      ticket: updatedTicket,
      message: "Ticket updated successfully."
    };

  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/* =============================================================
   049. TICKET DELETION
   ============================================================= */

function deleteTicket(token, ticketId, reason) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();

  lock.waitLock(30000);

  try {
    const tickets = getCollectionSafely(
      PBL_KEYS.TICKETS
    );

    const index = tickets.findIndex(function(ticket) {
      return ticket.id === String(ticketId) &&
        ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
    });

    if (index === -1) {
      throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
    }

    const ticket = tickets[index];

    ticket.status = PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
    ticket.deletedAt = getLiveTimestamp();
    ticket.deletedBy = "UNLOCKED OPERATOR";
    ticket.deletionReason = cleanText(
      reason || "",
      500
    );

    ticket.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

    tickets[index] = ticket;

    saveCollection(
      PBL_KEYS.TICKETS,
      tickets
    );

    writeAuditLog(
      "TICKET_DELETED",
      "Ticket " + ticket.ticketNumber +
      " deleted. Reason: " + cleanText(reason || "", 200)
    );

    return {
      success: true,
      message: "Ticket deleted."
    };

  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/* =============================================================
   050. TICKET RESTORATION
   ============================================================= */

function restoreTicket(token, ticketId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();

  lock.waitLock(30000);

  try {
    const tickets = getCollectionSafely(
      PBL_KEYS.TICKETS
    );

    const index = tickets.findIndex(function(ticket) {
      return ticket.id === String(ticketId) &&
        ticket.status === PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
    });

    if (index === -1) {
      throw new Error("Deleted ticket not found.");
    }

    tickets[index].status = PBL_WEIGHING.DEFAULT_TICKET_STATUS;
    tickets[index].deletedAt = null;
    tickets[index].deletedBy = null;
    tickets[index].deletionReason = "";
    tickets[index].updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

    saveCollection(
      PBL_KEYS.TICKETS,
      tickets
    );

    writeAuditLog(
      "TICKET_RESTORED",
      "Ticket " + tickets[index].ticketNumber + " restored"
    );

    return {
      success: true,
      ticket: tickets[index]
    };

  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/* =============================================================
   051. GET TICKET BY ID
   ============================================================= */

function getTicketById(ticketId, includeDeleted) {
  const tickets = getCollectionSafely(
    PBL_KEYS.TICKETS
  );

  return tickets.find(function(ticket) {
    const matches = ticket.id === String(ticketId);

    if (!includeDeleted) {
      return matches &&
        ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
    }

    return matches;
  }) || null;
}

/* =============================================================
   052. GET TICKET BY NUMBER
   ============================================================= */

function getTicketByNumber(ticketNumber) {
  const ticket = findTicketByNumber(ticketNumber);

  if (!ticket) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Ticket not found."
    };
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    ticket: ticket
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   053. TICKET LIST
   ============================================================= */

function getAllTickets(options) {
  options = options || {};

  const tickets = getCollectionSafely(
    PBL_KEYS.TICKETS
  );

  let results = tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
    return options.includeDeleted ||
      ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
  });

  results.sort(function(a, b) {
    return String(b.timestamp || "").localeCompare(
      String(a.timestamp || "")
    );
  });

  return results;
}

/* =============================================================
   054. TICKET SEARCH
   ============================================================= */

function searchTickets(searchText, options) {
  options = options || {};

  const query = cleanText(
    searchText || "",
    200
  ).toLowerCase();

  const tickets = getAllTickets(options);

  if (!query) {
    return tickets;
  }

  return tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
    const searchableFields = [
      ticket.ticketNumber,
      ticket.product,
      ticket.productCode,
      ticket.client,
      ticket.clientCode,
      ticket.truck,
      ticket.truckRegistration,
      ticket.driver,
      ticket.reference,
      ticket.orderNumber,
      ticket.deliveryLocation,
      ticket.remarks,
      ticket.date,
      ticket.time
    ];

    return searchableFields.some(function(value) {
      return String(value || "")
        .toLowerCase()
        .includes(query);
    });
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   055. TICKET FILTERING
   ============================================================= */

function filterTickets(filters) {
  filters = filters || {};

  let results = getAllTickets({
    includeDeleted: filters.includeDeleted === true
  });

  if (filters.product) {
    results = results.filter(function(ticket) {
      return ticket.product === filters.product;
    });
  }

  if (filters.client) {
    results = results.filter(function(ticket) {
      return ticket.client === filters.client;
    });
  }

  if (filters.truck) {
    results = results.filter(function(ticket) {
      return ticket.truckRegistration === filters.truck ||
        ticket.truck === filters.truck;
    });
  }

  if (filters.driver) {
    results = results.filter(function(ticket) {
      return ticket.driver === filters.driver;
    });
  }

  if (filters.status) {
    results = results.filter(function(ticket) {
      return ticket.status === filters.status;
    });
  }

  if (filters.dateFrom) {
    results = results.filter(function(ticket) {
      return ticket.date >= filters.dateFrom;
    });
  }

  if (filters.dateTo) {
    results = results.filter(function(ticket) {
      return ticket.date <= filters.dateTo;
    });
  }

  if (filters.minimumNetKg !== undefined) {
    results = results.filter(function(ticket) {
      return Number(ticket.netKg) >=
        Number(filters.minimumNetKg);
    });
  }

  if (filters.maximumNetKg !== undefined) {
    results = results.filter(function(ticket) {
      return Number(ticket.netKg) <=
        Number(filters.maximumNetKg);
    });
  }

  return results;
}

/* =============================================================
   056. TICKET PAGINATION
   ============================================================= */

function paginateTickets(tickets, page, pageSize) {
  page = Math.max(1, Number(page) || 1);

  pageSize = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(
      Number(pageSize) || 25,
      PBL.MAX_TICKETS_PER_PAGE
    )
  );

  const total = tickets.length;

  const start = (page - 1) * pageSize;

  const records = tickets.slice(
    start,
    start + pageSize
  );

  return {
    records: records,
    page: page,
    pageSize: pageSize,
    totalRecords: total,
    totalPages: Math.ceil(total / pageSize),
    hasPrevious: page > 1,
    hasNext: start + pageSize < total
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   057. TICKET TOTALS
   ============================================================= */

function calculateTicketTotals(tickets) {
  const validTickets = (tickets || []).filter(
    function(ticket) {
      return ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
    }
  );

  const totals = validTickets.reduce(
    function(result, ticket) {
      result.grossKg += Number(ticket.grossKg || 0);
      result.tareKg += Number(ticket.tareKg || 0);
      result.netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
      result.netTonnes += Number(ticket.netTonnes || 0);

      return result;
    },
    {
      grossKg: 0,
      tareKg: 0,
      netKg: 0,
      netTonnes: 0,
      voyages: validTickets.length
    }
  );

  totals.grossKg = roundNumber(totals.grossKg, 0);
  totals.tareKg = roundNumber(totals.tareKg, 0);
  totals.netKg = roundNumber(totals.netKg, 0);
  totals.netTonnes = roundNumber(totals.netTonnes, 3);

  return totals;
}

/* =============================================================
   058. TICKET STATISTICS
   ============================================================= */

function getTicketStatistics() {
  const tickets = getAllTickets();

  const totals = calculateTicketTotals(tickets);

  const products = {};
  const clients = {};
  const trucks = {};
  const drivers = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const product = ticket.product || "UNSPECIFIED";
    const client = ticket.client || "UNSPECIFIED";
    const truck = ticket.truckRegistration || "UNSPECIFIED";
    const driver = ticket.driver || "UNSPECIFIED";

    products[product] = (products[product] || 0) + 1;
    clients[client] = (clients[client] || 0) + 1;
    trucks[truck] = (trucks[truck] || 0) + 1;
    drivers[driver] = (drivers[driver] || 0) + 1;
  });

  return {
    totals: totals,
    productCounts: products,
    clientCounts: clients,
    truckCounts: trucks,
    driverCounts: drivers,
    calculatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   059. TICKET PRINT DATA
   ============================================================= */

function getTicketPrintData(ticketId) {
  const ticket = getTicketById(ticketId, false);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  const settings = loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.SETTINGS) ||
    getDefaultSettings();

  return {
    applicationName: PBL.APP_NAME,
    companyName: settings.companyName,
    companyAddress: settings.companyAddress,
    companyPhone: settings.companyPhone,
    ticket: ticket,
    printedAt: getLiveTimestampDisplay(),
    timeZone: PBL.TIME_ZONE
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   060. PRODUCT VALIDATION
   ============================================================= */

function normalizeProductInput(input) {
  if (!input || typeof input !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Product information is required.");
  }

  const name = cleanText(input.name, 120);

  if (isBlank(name)) {
    throw new Error("Product name is required.");
  }

  return {
    name: name,
    code: cleanText(input.code || "", 60),
    category: cleanText(input.category || "", 80),
    description: cleanText(input.description || "", 500),
    active: input.active !== false,
    unit: cleanText(input.unit || "TONNE", 20)
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   061. GENERIC REFERENCE COLLECTION
   ============================================================= */

function getReferenceCollection(type) {
  const collections = {
    product: PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS,
    client: PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS,
    truck: PBL_KEYS.TRUCKS,
    driver: PBL_KEYS.DRIVERS
  };

  const key = collections[String(type || "").toLowerCase()];

  if (!key) {
    throw new Error("Unknown reference collection.");
  }

  return key;
}

/* =============================================================
   062. REFERENCE ITEM CREATION
   ============================================================= */

function createReferenceItem(token, type, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const key = getReferenceCollection(type);

  if (!input || typeof input !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Record information is required.");
  }

  const name = cleanText(
    input.name || input.registration || "",
    160
  );

  if (isBlank(name)) {
    throw new Error("Name or registration is required.");
  }

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();

  lock.waitLock(30000);

  try {
    const records = getCollectionSafely(key);

    const duplicate = records.some(function(record) {
      return record.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase() &&
        record.active !== false;
    });

    if (duplicate) {
      throw new Error("This record already exists.");
    }

    const record = {
      id: createUniqueId(type.toUpperCase()),
      type: String(type).toLowerCase(),
      name: name,
      code: cleanText(input.code || "", 60),
      registration: cleanText(
        input.registration || "",
        80
      ),
      phone: cleanText(input.phone || "", 40),
      address: cleanText(input.address || "", 250),
      description: cleanText(input.description || "", 500),
      active: input.active !== false,
      createdAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
      updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
    };

    records.push(record);

    saveCollection(key, records);

    writeAuditLog(
      String(type).toUpperCase() + "_CREATED",
      name + " created"
    );

    return {
      success: true,
      record: record
    };

  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/* =============================================================
   063. REFERENCE ITEM UPDATE
   ============================================================= */

function updateReferenceItem(token, type, recordId, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const key = getReferenceCollection(type);

  const records = getCollectionSafely(key);

  const index = records.findIndex(function(record) {
    return record.id === String(recordId);
  });

  if (index === -1) {
    throw new Error("Record not found.");
  }

  const oldRecord = records[index];

  const newName = cleanText(
    input.name || oldRecord.name,
    160
  );

  if (isBlank(newName)) {
    throw new Error("Name cannot be empty.");
  }

  const duplicate = records.some(function(record) {
    return record.id !== oldRecord.id &&
      record.name.toLowerCase() === newName.toLowerCase() &&
      record.active !== false;
  });

  if (duplicate) {
    throw new Error("Another record has the same name.");
  }

  records[index] = Object.assign(
    {},
    oldRecord,
    input,
    {
      id: oldRecord.id,
      type: oldRecord.type,
      name: newName,
      updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
    }
  );

  saveCollection(key, records);

  writeAuditLog(
    String(type).toUpperCase() + "_UPDATED",
    newName + " updated"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    record: records[index]
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   064. REFERENCE ITEM DEACTIVATION
   ============================================================= */

function deactivateReferenceItem(token, type, recordId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const key = getReferenceCollection(type);

  const records = getCollectionSafely(key);

  const index = records.findIndex(function(record) {
    return record.id === String(recordId);
  });

  if (index === -1) {
    throw new Error("Record not found.");
  }

  records[index].active = false;
  records[index].updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  saveCollection(key, records);

  writeAuditLog(
    String(type).toUpperCase() + "_DEACTIVATED",
    records[index].name + " deactivated"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Record deactivated."
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   065. REFERENCE ITEM LIST
   ============================================================= */

function listReferenceItems(type, includeInactive) {
  const key = getReferenceCollection(type);

  const records = getCollectionSafely(key);

  if (includeInactive) {
    return records;
  }

  return records.filter(function(record) {
    return record.active !== false;
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   066. PRODUCT CREATION
   ============================================================= */

function createProduct(token, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const product = normalizeProductInput(input);

  return createReferenceItem(
    token,
    "product",
    product
  );
}

/* =============================================================
   067. PRODUCT UPDATE
   ============================================================= */

function updateProduct(token, productId, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const product = normalizeProductInput(input);

  return updateReferenceItem(
    token,
    "product",
    productId,
    product
  );
}

   ============================================================= */

function getProducts(includeInactive) {
  return listReferenceItems(
    "product",
    includeInactive === true
  );
}

/* =============================================================

    data.registration || data.name || "",

  );

  data.registration = data.name;

  return createReferenceItem(
    token,
    "truck",
    data
  );
}

    160
  );

  data.registration = data.name;

/* =============================================================

  requireEditAccess(token);

  return createReferenceItem(
    token,
    "driver",
    input
  );
}

/* =============================================================
   076. DRIVER UPDATE
   ============================================================= */

function updateDriver(token, driverId, input) {

  );
}

/* =============================================================
   106. API — NEW TICKET NUMBER PREVIEW
   ============================================================= */

function apiPreviewNextTicketNumber() {
  const settings = loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.SETTINGS) ||
    getDefaultSettings();

  const prefix = cleanText(
    settings.defaultTicketPrefix || "BL",
    12
  );

  const length = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(
      Number(settings.ticketNumberLength) || 6,
      12
    )
  );

  const nextNumber = getTicketCounter() + 1;

  return prefix + String(nextNumber).padStart(length, "0");
}

  return getAllTickets().filter(function(ticket) {

  });
}

   ============================================================= */

  requireEditAccess(token);

  return getAllTickets({
    includeDeleted: true
  }).filter(function(ticket) {
    return ticket.status === PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   114. API — SEARCH DELETED TICKETS
   ============================================================= */

function apiSearchDeletedTickets(token, query) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const search = cleanText(query || "", 200).toLowerCase();

  return apiGetDeletedTickets(token).filter(function(ticket) {
    return [
      ticket.ticketNumber,
      ticket.product,
      ticket.client,
      ticket.truckRegistration,
      ticket.driver
    ].some(function(value) {
      return String(value || "").toLowerCase().includes(search);
    });
  });
}

   ============================================================= */

  const maximum = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(Number(limit) || 10, 100)
  );

  return getAllTickets().slice(0, maximum);
}

    exists: ticketNumberExists(ticketNumber),
    ticketNumber: String(ticketNumber || "")
  };
}

   117. API — CHECK PRODUCT
   ============================================================= */

function apiCheckProduct(productName) {
  const products = getProducts(false);

  return products.some(function(product) {
    return product.name.toLowerCase() ===
      String(productName || "").toLowerCase();
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   118. API — CHECK CLIENT
   ============================================================= */

function apiCheckClient(clientName) {
  const clients = getClients(false);

  return clients.some(function(client) {
    return client.name.toLowerCase() ===
      String(clientName || "").toLowerCase();
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   119. API — CHECK TRUCK
   ============================================================= */

function apiCheckTruck(registration) {
  const trucks = getTrucks(false);

  return trucks.some(function(truck) {
    return truck.registration.toLowerCase() ===
      String(registration || "").toLowerCase();
  });
}

 * 131. CLIENT REPORTS
 * 132. DASHBOARD API
 ***************************************************************/

/* =============================================================
   121. OBJECTIVE CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================= */

const PBL_OBJECTIVES = Object.freeze({
  STATUS_ACTIVE: "ACTIVE",
  STATUS_COMPLETED: "COMPLETED",
  STATUS_ARCHIVED: "ARCHIVED",
  DEFAULT_UNIT: "TONNE",
  DECIMALS: 3
});

/* =============================================================
   122. OBJECTIVE VALIDATION
   ============================================================= */

function normalizeObjectiveInput(input) {
  if (!input || typeof input !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Objective information is required.");
  }

  const quantity = Number(
    input.targetTonnes !== undefined
      ? input.targetTonnes
      : input.targetQuantity
  );

  if (!Number.isFinite(quantity) || quantity <= 0) {
    throw new Error(
      "Target quantity must be greater than zero."
    );
  }

  return {
    name: cleanText(
      input.name || "DELIVERY OBJECTIVE",
      160
    ),
    product: cleanText(input.product || "", 120),
    client: cleanText(input.client || "", 160),
    targetTonnes: roundNumber(
      quantity,
      PBL_OBJECTIVES.DECIMALS
    ),
    startDate: cleanText(input.startDate || "", 30),
    endDate: cleanText(input.endDate || "", 30),
    reference: cleanText(input.reference || "", 100),
    description: cleanText(input.description || "", 1000),
    status: PBL_OBJECTIVES.STATUS_ACTIVE
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   123. CREATE DELIVERY OBJECTIVE
   ============================================================= */

function createDeliveryObjective(token, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const normalized = normalizeObjectiveInput(input);

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();

  lock.waitLock(30000);

  try {
    const objectives = getCollectionSafely(
      PBL_KEYS.OBJECTIVES
    );

    const objective = Object.assign(
      {},
      normalized,
      {
        id: createUniqueId("OBJECTIVE"),
        createdAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
        updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
        createdBy: "UNLOCKED OPERATOR",
        completedAt: null,
        archivedAt: null
      }
    );

    objectives.push(objective);

    saveCollection(
      PBL_KEYS.OBJECTIVES,
      objectives
    );

    writeAuditLog(
      "OBJECTIVE_CREATED",
      objective.name + " — " +
      objective.targetTonnes + " tonnes"
    );

    return {
      success: true,
      objective: objective,
      message: "Delivery objective created."
    };

  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/* =============================================================
   124. GET DELIVERY OBJECTIVES
   ============================================================= */

function getDeliveryObjectives(includeArchived) {
  const objectives = getCollectionSafely(
    PBL_KEYS.OBJECTIVES
  );

  if (includeArchived) {
    return objectives;
  }

  return objectives.filter(function(objective) {
    return objective.status !==
      PBL_OBJECTIVES.STATUS_ARCHIVED;
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   125. GET OBJECTIVE BY ID
   ============================================================= */

function getObjectiveById(objectiveId) {
  const objectives = getDeliveryObjectives(true);

  return objectives.find(function(objective) {
    return objective.id === String(objectiveId);
  }) || null;
}

/* =============================================================
   126. UPDATE DELIVERY OBJECTIVE
   ============================================================= */

function updateDeliveryObjective(token, objectiveId, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const normalized = normalizeObjectiveInput(input);

  const objectives = getDeliveryObjectives(true);

  const index = objectives.findIndex(function(objective) {
    return objective.id === String(objectiveId);
  });

  if (index === -1) {
    throw new Error("Objective not found.");
  }

  const previous = objectives[index];

  objectives[index] = Object.assign(
    {},
    previous,
    normalized,
    {
      id: previous.id,
      createdAt: previous.createdAt,
      updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
    }
  );

  saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.OBJECTIVES,
    objectives
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "OBJECTIVE_UPDATED",
    objectives[index].name + " updated"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    objective: objectives[index]
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   127. ARCHIVE OBJECTIVE
   ============================================================= */

function archiveDeliveryObjective(token, objectiveId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const objectives = getDeliveryObjectives(true);

  const objective = objectives.find(function(item) {
    return item.id === String(objectiveId);
  });

  if (!objective) {
    throw new Error("Objective not found.");
  }

  objective.status = PBL_OBJECTIVES.STATUS_ARCHIVED;
  objective.archivedAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  objective.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.OBJECTIVES,
    objectives
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "OBJECTIVE_ARCHIVED",
    objective.name + " archived"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Objective archived."
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   128. RESTORE OBJECTIVE
   ============================================================= */

function restoreDeliveryObjective(token, objectiveId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const objectives = getDeliveryObjectives(true);

  const objective = objectives.find(function(item) {
    return item.id === String(objectiveId);
  });

  if (!objective) {
    throw new Error("Objective not found.");
  }

  objective.status = PBL_OBJECTIVES.STATUS_ACTIVE;
  objective.archivedAt = null;
  objective.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.OBJECTIVES,
    objectives
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    objective: objective
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   129. GET ACTIVE OBJECTIVE
   ============================================================= */

function getActiveDeliveryObjective() {
  const objectives = getDeliveryObjectives(false);

  const active = objectives.filter(function(objective) {
    return objective.status ===
      PBL_OBJECTIVES.STATUS_ACTIVE;
  });

  active.sort(function(a, b) {
    return String(b.createdAt).localeCompare(
      String(a.createdAt)
    );
  });

  return active[0] || null;
}

/* =============================================================
   130. OBJECTIVE TICKET MATCHING
   ============================================================= */

function ticketMatchesObjective(ticket, objective) {
  if (
    ticket.status === PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  if (
    objective.product &&
    ticket.product !== objective.product
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  if (
    objective.client &&
    ticket.client !== objective.client
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  if (
    objective.startDate &&
    ticket.date < objective.startDate
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  if (
    objective.endDate &&
    ticket.date > objective.endDate
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  return true;
}

/* =============================================================
   131. OBJECTIVE DELIVERY TICKETS
   ============================================================= */

function getObjectiveTickets(objectiveId) {
  const objective = getObjectiveById(objectiveId);

  if (!objective) {
    throw new Error("Objective not found.");
  }

  return getAllTickets().filter(function(ticket) {
    return ticketMatchesObjective(ticket, objective);
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   132. CUMUL TONNES
   ============================================================= */

function calculateCumulTonnes(tickets) {
  const validTickets = (tickets || []).filter(
    function(ticket) {
      return ticket.status !==
        PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
    }
  );

  const totalKg = validTickets.reduce(
    function(total, ticket) {
      return total + Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
    },
    0
  );

  return roundNumber(
    totalKg / 1000,
    3
  );
}

/* =============================================================
   133. NB VOYAGES
   ============================================================= */

function calculateNumberOfVoyages(tickets) {
  return (tickets || []).filter(function(ticket) {
    return ticket.status !==
      PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
  }).length;
}

/* =============================================================
   134. TARGET QUANTITY
   ============================================================= */

function calculateTargetQuantity(objective) {
  if (!objective) {
    return 0;
  }

  return roundNumber(
    Number(objective.targetTonnes || 0),
    3
  );
}

/* =============================================================
   135. REST A LIVRER
   ============================================================= */

function calculateRemainingQuantity(targetTonnes, cumulTonnes) {
  return roundNumber(
    Math.max(
      0,
      Number(targetTonnes || 0) -
      Number(cumulTonnes || 0)
    ),
    3
  );
}

/* =============================================================
   136. OVERDELIVERY QUANTITY
   ============================================================= */

function calculateOverdelivery(targetTonnes, cumulTonnes) {
  return roundNumber(
    Math.max(
      0,
      Number(cumulTonnes || 0) -
      Number(targetTonnes || 0)
    ),
    3
  );
}

/* =============================================================
   137. DELIVERY PERCENTAGE
   ============================================================= */

function calculateDeliveryPercentage(targetTonnes, cumulTonnes) {
  const target = Number(targetTonnes || 0);

  if (target <= 0) {
    return 0;
  }

  return roundNumber(
    (Number(cumulTonnes || 0) / target) * 100,
    2
  );
}

/* =============================================================
   138. DELIVERY PROGRESS
   ============================================================= */

function calculateDeliveryProgress(objective, tickets) {
  const target = calculateTargetQuantity(objective);

  const cumul = calculateCumulTonnes(tickets);

  const remaining = calculateRemainingQuantity(
    target,
    cumul
  );

  const overdelivery = calculateOverdelivery(
    target,
    cumul
  );

  const percentage = calculateDeliveryPercentage(
    target,
    cumul
  );

  return {
    targetQuantity: target,
    cumulTonnes: cumul,
    remainingTonnes: remaining,
    overdeliveryTonnes: overdelivery,
    percentage: percentage,
    progressBarPercentage: Math.min(
      100,
      percentage
    ),
    voyages: calculateNumberOfVoyages(tickets),
    completed: cumul >= target && target > 0,
    status: cumul >= target && target > 0
      ? "TARGET REACHED"
      : "IN PROGRESS"
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   139. OBJECTIVE SUMMARY
   ============================================================= */

function getObjectiveSummary(objectiveId) {
  const objective = getObjectiveById(objectiveId);

  if (!objective) {
    throw new Error("Objective not found.");
  }

  const tickets = getObjectiveTickets(objectiveId);

  return {
    objective: objective,
    progress: calculateDeliveryProgress(
      objective,
      tickets
    ),
    calculatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   140. MAIN DASHBOARD DATA
   ============================================================= */

function getMainDashboardData() {
  const objective = getActiveDeliveryObjective();

  const tickets = getAllTickets();

  const relevantTickets = objective
    ? tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
        return ticketMatchesObjective(ticket, objective);
      })
    : tickets;

  const target = calculateTargetQuantity(objective);

  const cumul = calculateCumulTonnes(
    relevantTickets
  );

  const voyages = calculateNumberOfVoyages(
    relevantTickets
  );

  const remaining = calculateRemainingQuantity(
    target,
    cumul
  );

  const percentage = calculateDeliveryPercentage(
    target,
    cumul
  );

  return {
    applicationName: PBL.APP_NAME,
    date: getLiveDate(),
    time: getLiveTime(),
    timestamp: getLiveTimestamp(),

    targetQuantity: target,
    cumulTonnes: cumul,
    numberOfVoyages: voyages,
    remainingTonnes: remaining,

    deliveryPercentage: percentage,
    progressBarPercentage: Math.min(
      percentage,
      100
    ),

    overdeliveryTonnes: calculateOverdelivery(
      target,
      cumul
    ),

    objectiveName: objective
      ? objective.name
      : "NO ACTIVE OBJECTIVE",

    objectiveId: objective
      ? objective.id
      : null,

    objectiveStatus: objective
      ? objective.status
      : "NOT CONFIGURED",

    targetReached: target > 0 && cumul >= target,

    message: target <= 0
      ? "SET A TARGET QUANTITY"
      : cumul >= target
        ? "TARGET QUANTITY REACHED"
        : "DELIVERY IN PROGRESS"
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   141. DAILY DELIVERY FILTER
   ============================================================= */

function getTicketsForDate(dateValue) {
  const targetDate = String(dateValue || getLiveDate());

  return getAllTickets().filter(function(ticket) {
    return ticket.date === targetDate;
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   142. DAILY DELIVERY REPORT
   ============================================================= */

function getDailyDeliveryReport(dateValue) {
  const tickets = getTicketsForDate(dateValue);

  const totals = calculateTicketTotals(tickets);

  return {
    reportType: "DAILY",
    date: dateValue || getLiveDate(),
    voyages: calculateNumberOfVoyages(tickets),
    cumulTonnes: calculateCumulTonnes(tickets),
    grossKg: totals.grossKg,
    tareKg: totals.tareKg,
    netKg: totals.netKg,
    tickets: tickets,
    generatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   143. DAILY PRODUCT TOTALS
   ============================================================= */

function getDailyProductTotals(dateValue) {
  const tickets = getTicketsForDate(dateValue);

  const groups = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const name = ticket.product || "UNSPECIFIED";

    if (!groups[name]) {
      groups[name] = {
        product: name,
        voyages: 0,
        netKg: 0,
        tonnes: 0
      };
    }

    groups[name].voyages++;
    groups[name].netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).map(function(name) {
    const item = groups[name];

    item.tonnes = roundNumber(
      item.netKg / 1000,
      3
    );

    return item;
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   144. DAILY CLIENT TOTALS
   ============================================================= */

function getDailyClientTotals(dateValue) {
  const tickets = getTicketsForDate(dateValue);

  const groups = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const name = ticket.client || "UNSPECIFIED";

    if (!groups[name]) {
      groups[name] = {
        client: name,
        voyages: 0,
        netKg: 0,
        tonnes: 0
      };
    }

    groups[name].voyages++;
    groups[name].netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).map(function(name) {
    const item = groups[name];

    item.tonnes = roundNumber(
      item.netKg / 1000,
      3
    );

    return item;
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   145. MONTH DATE FILTER
   ============================================================= */

function getMonthKey(dateString) {
  const parts = String(dateString || "").split("/");

  if (parts.length !== 3) {
    return "";
  }

  return parts[2] + "-" + parts[1];
}

/* =============================================================
   146. MONTHLY DELIVERY REPORT
   ============================================================= */

function getMonthlyDeliveryReport(month, year) {
  const selectedMonth = String(month).padStart(2, "0");
  const selectedYear = String(year);

  const tickets = getAllTickets().filter(
    function(ticket) {
      const key = getMonthKey(ticket.date);

      return key === selectedYear + "-" + selectedMonth;
    }
  );

  const totals = calculateTicketTotals(tickets);

  return {
    reportType: "MONTHLY",
    month: selectedMonth,
    year: selectedYear,
    voyages: calculateNumberOfVoyages(tickets),
    cumulTonnes: calculateCumulTonnes(tickets),
    grossKg: totals.grossKg,
    tareKg: totals.tareKg,
    netKg: totals.netKg,
    tickets: tickets,
    generatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   147. MONTHLY DAILY BREAKDOWN
   ============================================================= */

function getMonthlyDailyBreakdown(month, year) {
  const report = getMonthlyDeliveryReport(month, year);

  const groups = {};

  report.tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const date = ticket.date;

    if (!groups[date]) {
      groups[date] = {
        date: date,
        voyages: 0,
        netKg: 0
      };
    }

    groups[date].voyages++;
    groups[date].netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).sort().map(function(date) {
    return {
      date: date,
      voyages: groups[date].voyages,
      netKg: roundNumber(groups[date].netKg, 0),
      tonnes: roundNumber(
        groups[date].netKg / 1000,
        3
      )
    };
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   148. YEARLY DELIVERY REPORT
   ============================================================= */

function getYearlyDeliveryReport(year) {
  const selectedYear = String(year);

  const tickets = getAllTickets().filter(
    function(ticket) {
      const parts = String(ticket.date || "").split("/");

      return parts.length === 3 &&
        parts[2] === selectedYear;
    }
  );

  const totals = calculateTicketTotals(tickets);

  return {
    reportType: "YEARLY",
    year: selectedYear,
    voyages: calculateNumberOfVoyages(tickets),
    cumulTonnes: calculateCumulTonnes(tickets),
    grossKg: totals.grossKg,
    tareKg: totals.tareKg,
    netKg: totals.netKg,
    tickets: tickets,
    generatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   149. YEARLY MONTHLY BREAKDOWN
   ============================================================= */

function getYearlyMonthlyBreakdown(year) {
  const selectedYear = String(year);

  const tickets = getAllTickets().filter(
    function(ticket) {
      const parts = String(ticket.date || "").split("/");

      return parts.length === 3 &&
        parts[2] === selectedYear;
    }
  );

  const months = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const parts = ticket.date.split("/");
    const month = parts[1];

    if (!months[month]) {
      months[month] = {
        month: month,
        voyages: 0,
        netKg: 0
      };
    }

    months[month].voyages++;
    months[month].netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
  });

  return Object.keys(months).sort().map(function(month) {
    return {
      month: month,
      voyages: months[month].voyages,
      netKg: roundNumber(months[month].netKg, 0),
      tonnes: roundNumber(
        months[month].netKg / 1000,
        3
      )
    };
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   154. DASHBOARD RECENT DELIVERIES
   ============================================================= */

function getDashboardRecentDeliveries(limit) {
  const maximum = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(Number(limit) || 10, 50)
  );

  return getAllTickets().slice(0, maximum).map(
    function(ticket) {
      return {
        ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber,
        date: ticket.date,
        time: ticket.time,
        product: ticket.product,
        client: ticket.client,
        truck: ticket.truckRegistration,
        netKg: ticket.netKg,
        netTonnes: ticket.netTonnes
      };
    }
  );
}

/* =============================================================
   155. DASHBOARD PRODUCT SUMMARY
   ============================================================= */

function getDashboardProductSummary() {
  const tickets = getAllTickets();

  const groups = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const product = ticket.product || "UNSPECIFIED";

    if (!groups[product]) {
      groups[product] = {
        product: product,
        voyages: 0,
        netKg: 0
      };
    }

    groups[product].voyages++;
    groups[product].netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).map(function(product) {
    return {
      product: product,
      voyages: groups[product].voyages,
      netKg: roundNumber(groups[product].netKg, 0),
      tonnes: roundNumber(
        groups[product].netKg / 1000,
        3
      )
    };
  }).sort(function(a, b) {
    return b.tonnes - a.tonnes;
  });
}
