/* =============================================================
   181. ADVANCED SEARCH NORMALIZATION
   ============================================================= */

function normalizeSearchValue(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase()
    .normalize("NFD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "");
}

/* =============================================================
   182. TICKET SEARCH MATCH
   ============================================================= */

function ticketMatchesSearch(ticket, query) {
  const search = normalizeSearchValue(query);

  if (!search) {
    return true;
  }

  const fields = [
    ticket.ticketNumber,
    ticket.product,
    ticket.client,
    ticket.truckRegistration,
    ticket.driver,
    ticket.date,
    ticket.time,
    ticket.reference,
    ticket.notes
  ];

  return fields.some(function(value) {
    return normalizeSearchValue(value).includes(search);
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   183. DATE STRING TO COMPARABLE VALUE
   ============================================================= */

function parseDisplayDate(dateValue) {
  const parts = String(dateValue || "").split("/");

  if (parts.length !== 3) {
    return null;
  }

  const day = Number(parts[0]);
  const month = Number(parts[1]);
  const year = Number(parts[2]);

  if (
    !day || !month || !year ||
    month > 12 || month < 1 ||
    day > 31 || day < 1
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  return year * 10000 + month * 100 + day;
}

/* =============================================================
   184. DATE RANGE VALIDATION
   ============================================================= */

function validateDateRange(startDate, endDate) {
  const start = parseDisplayDate(startDate);
  const end = parseDisplayDate(endDate);

  if (startDate && start === null) {
    throw new Error("Invalid start date.");
  }

  if (endDate && end === null) {
    throw new Error("Invalid end date.");
  }

  if (start !== null && end !== null && start > end) {
    throw new Error("Start date cannot be after end date.");
  }

  return {
    start: start,
    end: end
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   185. TICKET DATE RANGE MATCH
   ============================================================= */

function ticketMatchesDateRange(ticket, range) {
  const ticketDate = parseDisplayDate(ticket.date);

  if (ticketDate === null) {
    return false;
  }

  if (range.start !== null && ticketDate < range.start) {
    return false;
  }

  if (range.end !== null && ticketDate > range.end) {
    return false;
  }

  return true;
}

/* =============================================================
   186. ADVANCED TICKET FILTER
   ============================================================= */

function filterTicketsAdvanced(filters) {
  filters = filters || {};

  const range = validateDateRange(
    filters.startDate || "",
    filters.endDate || ""
  );

  let tickets = getAllTickets();

  tickets = tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
    if (ticket.status === PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS) {
      return false;
    }

    if (!ticketMatchesDateRange(ticket, range)) {
      return false;
    }

    if (!ticketMatchesSearch(ticket, filters.search || "")) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      filters.product &&
      ticket.product !== filters.product
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      filters.client &&
      ticket.client !== filters.client
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      filters.truck &&
      ticket.truckRegistration !== filters.truck
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      filters.driver &&
      ticket.driver !== filters.driver
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      filters.ticketNumber &&
      !normalizeSearchValue(ticket.ticketNumber)
        .includes(normalizeSearchValue(filters.ticketNumber))
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    return true;
  });

  const sortField = filters.sortField || "date";
  const sortDirection = filters.sortDirection === "asc"
    ? 1
    : -1;

  tickets.sort(function(a, b) {
    const first = String(a[sortField] || "");
    const second = String(b[sortField] || "");

    return first.localeCompare(second) * sortDirection;
  });

  return tickets;
}

/* =============================================================
   187. PAGINATED TICKET SEARCH
   ============================================================= */

function searchTicketsPaginated(filters, page, pageSize) {
  const all = filterTicketsAdvanced(filters);

  const currentPage = Math.max(
    1,
    Number(page) || 1
  );

  const limit = Math.min(
    PBL.MAX_TICKETS_PER_PAGE,
    Math.max(1, Number(pageSize) || 25)
  );

  const start = (currentPage - 1) * limit;

  return {
    page: currentPage,
    pageSize: limit,
    totalRecords: all.length,
    totalPages: Math.ceil(all.length / limit),
    records: all.slice(start, start + limit)
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   188. SEARCH TICKETS API
   ============================================================= */

function apiSearchTickets(filters, page, pageSize) {
  return searchTicketsPaginated(
    filters,
    page,
    pageSize
  );
}

/* =============================================================
   189. DATE RANGE REPORT
   ============================================================= */

function getDateRangeDeliveryReport(startDate, endDate) {
  const range = validateDateRange(startDate, endDate);

  const tickets = getAllTickets().filter(function(ticket) {
    return ticketMatchesDateRange(ticket, range);
  });

  const totals = calculateTicketTotals(tickets);

  return {
    reportType: "DATE RANGE",
    startDate: startDate || "ALL",
    endDate: endDate || "ALL",
    voyages: calculateNumberOfVoyages(tickets),
    cumulTonnes: calculateCumulTonnes(tickets),
    grossKg: totals.grossKg,
    tareKg: totals.tareKg,
    netKg: totals.netKg,
    tickets: tickets,
    generatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}

function getDateRangeProductBreakdown(startDate, endDate) {

  report.tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const product = ticket.product || "UNSPECIFIED";

    if (!groups[product]) {
      groups[product] = {
        product: product,
        voyages: 0,
        netKg: 0
      };
    }

    groups[product].voyages++;
    groups[product].netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).map(function(product) {
    return {
      product: product,
      voyages: groups[product].voyages,
      netKg: roundNumber(groups[product].netKg, 0),
      tonnes: roundNumber(
        groups[product].netKg / 1000,
        3
      )
    };
  });
}

function getDateRangeClientBreakdown(startDate, endDate) {

/* =============================================================
   192. REPORT TOTALS
   ============================================================= */

function calculateReportTotals(tickets) {
  const list = tickets || [];

  const totals = {
    voyages: 0,
    grossKg: 0,
    tareKg: 0,
    netKg: 0,
    tonnes: 0
  };

  list.forEach(function(ticket) {
    if (ticket.status === PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS) {
      return;
    }

    totals.voyages++;
    totals.grossKg += Number(ticket.grossKg || 0);
    totals.tareKg += Number(ticket.tareKg || 0);
    totals.netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
  });

  totals.grossKg = roundNumber(totals.grossKg, 0);
  totals.tareKg = roundNumber(totals.tareKg, 0);
  totals.netKg = roundNumber(totals.netKg, 0);
  totals.tonnes = roundNumber(totals.netKg / 1000, 3);

  return totals;
}

/* =============================================================
   193. REPORT EXPORT ROW
   ============================================================= */

function ticketToExportRow(ticket) {
  return [
    ticket.ticketNumber || "",
    ticket.date || "",
    ticket.time || "",
    ticket.product || "",
    ticket.client || "",
    ticket.truckRegistration || "",
    ticket.driver || "",
    ticket.grossKg || 0,
    ticket.tareKg || 0,
    ticket.netKg || 0,
    ticket.netTonnes || 0,
    ticket.reference || "",
    ticket.notes || ""
  ];
}

/* =============================================================
   194. CSV ESCAPE
   ============================================================= */

function csvEscape(value) {
  const text = String(
    value === null || value === undefined
      ? ""
      : value
  );

  return '"' + text.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
}

/* =============================================================
   195. CSV GENERATION
   ============================================================= */

function generateTicketsCsv(tickets) {
  const headers = [
    "N° BON",
    "DATE",
    "HEURE",
    "PRODUIT",
    "CLIENT",
    "CAMION",
    "CHAUFFEUR",
    "POIDS BRUT KG",
    "TARE KG",
    "POIDS NET KG",
    "TONNES",
    "REFERENCE",
    "OBSERVATIONS"
  ];

  const rows = (tickets || []).map(
    ticketToExportRow
  );

  const lines = [headers].concat(rows).map(
    function(row) {
      return row.map(csvEscape).join(";");
    }
  );

  return "\uFEFF" + lines.join("\r\n");
}

/* =============================================================
   196. CSV EXPORT API
   ============================================================= */

function apiExportTicketsCsv(token, filters) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const tickets = filterTicketsAdvanced(filters || {});

  const csv = generateTicketsCsv(tickets);

  writeAuditLog(
    "CSV_EXPORTED",
    tickets.length + " tickets exported"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    filename: "PONT_BASCULE_LIVE_" +
      Utilities.formatDate(
        new Date(),
        PBL.TIME_ZONE,
        "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"
      ) +
      ".csv",
    content: csv,
    records: tickets.length
  };
}

    PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS,
    PBL_KEYS.TRUCKS,
    PBL_KEYS.DRIVERS,
    PBL_KEYS.OBJECTIVES,
    PBL_KEYS.COUNTERS,
    PBL_KEYS.AUDIT,
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM
  ];
}

/* =============================================================
   199. CREATE APPLICATION BACKUP
   ============================================================= */

function createApplicationBackup(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const backup = {
    application: PBL.APP_NAME,
    version: PBL.VERSION,
    createdAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
    timeZone: PBL.TIME_ZONE,
    collections: {}
  };

  getBackupCollectionKeys().forEach(function(key) {
    backup.collections[key] = loadCollection(key);
  });

  const json = JSON.stringify(backup);

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();

  lock.waitLock(30000);

  try {
    const backups = getCollectionSafely(
      PBL_KEYS.BACKUPS
    );

    const counters = loadCollection(
      PBL_KEYS.COUNTERS
    ) || {
      ticket: 0,
      audit: 0,
      backup: 0
    };

    counters.backup++;

    const record = {
      id: counters.backup,
      createdAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
      size: json.length,
      recordCount: (
        backup.collections[PBL_KEYS.TICKETS] || []
      ).length,
      data: backup
    };

    backups.push(record);

    while (backups.length > 20) {
      backups.shift();
    }

    saveCollection(PBL_KEYS.BACKUPS, backups);
    saveCollection(PBL_KEYS.COUNTERS, counters);

    writeAuditLog(
      "BACKUP_CREATED",
      "Backup " + record.id + " created"
    );

    return {
      success: true,
      backupId: record.id,
      createdAt: record.createdAt,
      size: record.size,
      recordCount: record.recordCount,
      json: json
    };

  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/* =============================================================
   200. LIST BACKUPS
   ============================================================= */

function listApplicationBackups(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return getCollectionSafely(
    PBL_KEYS.BACKUPS
  ).map(function(backup) {
    return {
      id: backup.id,
      createdAt: backup.createdAt,
      size: backup.size,
      recordCount: backup.recordCount
    };
  }).reverse();
}

/* =============================================================
   201. BACKUP VALIDATION
   ============================================================= */

function validateBackupData(backup) {
  if (!backup || typeof backup !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Invalid backup format.");
  }

  if (!backup.collections) {
    throw new Error("Backup collections are missing.");
  }

  const required = getBackupCollectionKeys();

  required.forEach(function(key) {
    if (
      !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
        backup.collections,
        key
      )
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        "Backup is missing collection: " + key
      );
    }
  });

  return true;
}

/* =============================================================
   202. RESTORE APPLICATION BACKUP
   ============================================================= */

function restoreApplicationBackup(token, backupJson) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  let backup;

  try {
    backup = typeof backupJson === "string"
      ? JSON.parse(backupJson)
      : backupJson;
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error("Backup JSON could not be parsed.");
  }

  validateBackupData(backup);

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();

  lock.waitLock(30000);

  try {
    const current = {};

    getBackupCollectionKeys().forEach(function(key) {
      current[key] = loadCollection(key);
    });

    try {
      getBackupCollectionKeys().forEach(function(key) {
        saveCollection(
          key,
          backup.collections[key]
        );
      });
    } catch (restoreError) {
      getBackupCollectionKeys().forEach(function(key) {
        saveCollection(key, current[key]);
      });

      throw restoreError;
    }

    writeAuditLog(
      "BACKUP_RESTORED",
      "Application data restored"
    );

    return {
      success: true,
      message: "Backup restored successfully.",
      restoredAt: getLiveTimestamp()
    };

  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/* =============================================================
   203. RESTORE BACKUP API
   ============================================================= */

function apiRestoreBackup(token, backupJson) {
  return restoreApplicationBackup(token, backupJson);
}

/* =============================================================
   204. CREATE BACKUP API
   ============================================================= */

function apiCreateBackup(token) {
  return createApplicationBackup(token);
}

/* =============================================================
   205. LIST BACKUPS API
   ============================================================= */

function apiListBackups(token) {
  return listApplicationBackups(token);
}

/* =============================================================
   206. SETTINGS VALIDATION
   ============================================================= */

function normalizeApplicationSettings(input) {
  const defaults = getDefaultSettings();

  const settings = Object.assign(
    {},
    defaults,
    input || {}
  );

  settings.applicationName = PBL.APP_NAME;

  settings.companyName = cleanText(
    settings.companyName,
    200
  );

  settings.companyAddress = cleanText(
    settings.companyAddress,
    500
  );

  settings.companyPhone = cleanText(
    settings.companyPhone,
    50
  );

  settings.companyEmail = cleanText(
    settings.companyEmail,
    200
  );

  settings.defaultTicketPrefix = cleanText(
    settings.defaultTicketPrefix || "BL",
    12
  ).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_-]/g, "");

  settings.ticketNumberLength = Math.min(
    12,
    Math.max(3, Number(settings.ticketNumberLength) || 6)
  );

  settings.timeZone = PBL.TIME_ZONE;

  settings.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  return settings;
}

/* =============================================================
   207. SAVE SETTINGS
   ============================================================= */

function saveApplicationSettings(token, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const settings = normalizeApplicationSettings(input);

  saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.SETTINGS,
    settings
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "SETTINGS_UPDATED",
    "Application settings changed"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    settings: settings
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   208. SETTINGS API
   ============================================================= */

function apiSaveSettings(token, input) {
  return saveApplicationSettings(token, input);
}

/* =============================================================
   209. STORAGE COLLECTION SIZE
   ============================================================= */

function getCollectionSize(key) {
  const value = loadCollection(key);

  return JSON.stringify(value).length;
}

/* =============================================================
   210. STORAGE DIAGNOSTICS
   ============================================================= */

function getStorageDiagnostics() {
  const keys = getBackupCollectionKeys();

  const collections = keys.map(function(key) {
    let size = 0;
    let records = 0;
    let healthy = true;

    try {
      const value = loadCollection(key);

      size = JSON.stringify(value).length;

      records = Array.isArray(value)
        ? value.length
        : 1;

    } catch (error) {
      healthy = false;
    }

    return {
      collection: key,
      sizeCharacters: size,
      records: records,
      healthy: healthy
    };
  });

  return {
    application: PBL.APP_NAME,
    checkedAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
    collections: collections,
    healthy: collections.every(function(item) {
      return item.healthy;
    })
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   211. STORAGE DIAGNOSTICS API
   ============================================================= */

function apiStorageDiagnostics(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return getStorageDiagnostics();
}

/* =============================================================
   212. COLLECTION INTEGRITY CHECK
   ============================================================= */

function checkCollectionIntegrity(key) {
  try {
    const value = loadCollection(key);

    return {
      key: key,
      valid: value !== null && value !== undefined,
      type: Array.isArray(value)
        ? "ARRAY"
        : typeof value
    };

  } catch (error) {
    return {
      key: key,
      valid: false,
      error: error.message
    };
  }
}

/* =============================================================
   213. FULL INTEGRITY REPORT
   ============================================================= */

function getFullIntegrityReport() {
  const checks = getBackupCollectionKeys().map(
    function(key) {
      return checkCollectionIntegrity(key);
    }
  );

  return {
    application: PBL.APP_NAME,
    checkedAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
    valid: checks.every(function(check) {
      return check.valid;
    }),
    checks: checks
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   214. INTEGRITY API
   ============================================================= */

function apiIntegrityReport(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return getFullIntegrityReport();
}

/* =============================================================
   215. CLEAN OLD AUDIT RECORDS
   ============================================================= */

function cleanOldAuditRecords(token, maximumRecords) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const maximum = Math.max(
    100,
    Math.min(Number(maximumRecords) || 5000, 20000)
  );

  const records = getCollectionSafely(
    PBL_KEYS.AUDIT
  );

  const cleaned = records.slice(-maximum);

  saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.AUDIT,
    cleaned
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "AUDIT_CLEANUP",
    "Audit records retained: " + cleaned.length
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    retained: cleaned.length
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   224. REPORT PERIOD LABEL
   ============================================================= */

function getReportPeriodLabel(startDate, endDate) {
  if (!startDate && !endDate) {
    return "ALL AVAILABLE DATES";
  }

  if (startDate && endDate) {
    return startDate + " — " + endDate;
  }

  if (startDate) {
    return "FROM " + startDate;
  }

  return "UNTIL " + endDate;
}

   225. REPORT HEADER
   ============================================================= */

function getReportHeader(reportTitle) {
  const settings = apiGetApplicationSettings();

  return {
    application: PBL.APP_NAME,
    title: cleanText(reportTitle || "DELIVERY REPORT", 150),
    company: settings.companyName,
    address: settings.companyAddress,
    phone: settings.companyPhone,
    date: getLiveDate(),
    time: getLiveTime(),
    timeZone: PBL.TIME_ZONE
  };
}

   226. PRINT REPORT DATA
   ============================================================= */

function getPrintableReportData(startDate, endDate) {
  const report = getDateRangeDeliveryReport(
    startDate,
    endDate
  );

  return {
    header: getReportHeader("DELIVERY REPORT"),
    period: getReportPeriodLabel(startDate, endDate),
    summary: generateReportSummary(report),
    totals: calculateReportTotals(report.tickets),
    tickets: report.tickets,
    products: getDateRangeProductBreakdown(
      startDate,
      endDate
    ),
    clients: getDateRangeClientBreakdown(
      startDate,
      endDate
    )
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   229. VERIFY BACKUP CONTENT
   ============================================================= */

function verifyBackupContent(backupJson) {
  try {
    const backup = typeof backupJson === "string"
      ? JSON.parse(backupJson)
      : backupJson;

    validateBackupData(backup);

    return {
      valid: true,
      application: backup.application,
      version: backup.version,
      createdAt: backup.createdAt
    };

  } catch (error) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      error: error.message
    };
  }
}

  return verifyBackupContent(backupJson);

/* =============================================================
   231. GENERAL COLLECTION MANAGEMENT
   ============================================================= */

function pbl5GetCollection(key) {
  const value = getCollectionSafely(key);
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
}

function pbl5SaveCollection(key, records) {
  if (!Array.isArray(records)) {
    throw new Error("Collection must be an array.");
  }

  saveCollection(key, records);
  return true;
}

function pbl5FindById(records, id) {
  return records.find(function(record) {
    return String(record.id) === String(id);
  }) || null;
}

function pbl5CreateId(prefix) {
  return String(prefix || "PBL") +
    "_" +
    Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, "").substring(0, 16);
}

function pbl5EnsureUnique(records, field, value, excludedId) {
  const normalized = normalizeSearchValue(value);

  return !records.some(function(record) {
    if (
      excludedId &&
      String(record.id) === String(excludedId)
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    return normalizeSearchValue(record[field]) === normalized;
  });
}

function pbl5SortByName(records) {
  return records.slice().sort(function(a, b) {
    return String(a.name || "").localeCompare(
      String(b.name || "")
    );
  });
}

function pbl5ValidateName(value, label) {
  const result = cleanText(value, 150);

  if (!result) {
    throw new Error(label + " is required.");
  }

  return result;
}

function pbl5RequireActiveRecord(record, label) {
  if (!record) {
    throw new Error(label + " not found.");
  }

  if (record.archived === true) {
    throw new Error(label + " is archived.");
  }

  return record;
}


/* =============================================================
   232. PRODUCTS MANAGEMENT
   ============================================================= */

function getAllProducts(includeArchived) {
  const products = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS);

  return products.filter(function(product) {
    return includeArchived || product.archived !== true;
  });
}

function getProductById(productId) {
  return pbl5FindById(
    getAllProducts(true),
    productId
  );
}

function createProduct(token, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  input = input || {};

  const products = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS);
  const name = pbl5ValidateName(input.name, "Product name");

  if (!pbl5EnsureUnique(products, "name", name)) {
    throw new Error("A product with this name already exists.");
  }

  const product = {
    id: pbl5CreateId("PROD"),
    name: name,
    code: cleanText(input.code, 50),
    description: cleanText(input.description, 500),
    unit: cleanText(input.unit || "TONNE", 30),
    color: cleanText(input.color || "", 30),
    active: true,
    archived: false,
    createdAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
    updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };

  products.push(product);
  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS, products);

  writeAuditLog("PRODUCT_CREATED", product.name);

  return {
    success: true,
    product: product
  };
}

function updateProduct(token, productId, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  input = input || {};

  const products = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS);
  const product = pbl5FindById(products, productId);

  pbl5RequireActiveRecord(product, "Product");

  const name = pbl5ValidateName(
    input.name || product.name,
    "Product name"
  );

  if (!pbl5EnsureUnique(products, "name", name, productId)) {
    throw new Error("Another product already uses this name.");
  }

  product.name = name;
  product.code = cleanText(
    input.code !== undefined ? input.code : product.code,
    50
  );
  product.description = cleanText(
    input.description !== undefined
      ? input.description
      : product.description,
    500
  );
  product.unit = cleanText(
    input.unit || product.unit || "TONNE",
    30
  );
  product.color = cleanText(
    input.color !== undefined ? input.color : product.color,
    30
  );
  product.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS, products);

  writeAuditLog("PRODUCT_UPDATED", product.name);

  return {
    success: true,
    product: product
  };
}

function archiveProduct(token, productId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const products = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS);
  const product = pbl5FindById(products, productId);

  if (!product) {
    throw new Error("Product not found.");
  }

  product.archived = true;
  product.active = false;
  product.archivedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS, products);

  writeAuditLog("PRODUCT_ARCHIVED", product.name);

  return { success: true };
}

function restoreProduct(token, productId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const products = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS);
  const product = pbl5FindById(products, productId);

  if (!product) {
    throw new Error("Product not found.");
  }

  product.archived = false;
  product.active = true;
  product.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS, products);

  writeAuditLog("PRODUCT_RESTORED", product.name);

  return { success: true };
}

function apiGetProducts(includeArchived) {
  return pbl5SortByName(getAllProducts(includeArchived));
}

function apiCreateProduct(token, input) {
  return createProduct(token, input);
}

function apiUpdateProduct(token, id, input) {
  return updateProduct(token, id, input);
}

function apiArchiveProduct(token, id) {
  return archiveProduct(token, id);
}

function apiRestoreProduct(token, id) {
  return restoreProduct(token, id);
}


/* =============================================================
   233. CLIENTS MANAGEMENT
   ============================================================= */

function getAllClients(includeArchived) {
  return pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS).filter(
    function(client) {
      return includeArchived || client.archived !== true;
    }
  );
}

function getClientById(clientId) {
  return pbl5FindById(
    getAllClients(true),
    clientId
  );
}

function createClient(token, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  input = input || {};

  const clients = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS);
  const name = pbl5ValidateName(input.name, "Client name");

  if (!pbl5EnsureUnique(clients, "name", name)) {
    throw new Error("This client already exists.");
  }

  const client = {
    id: pbl5CreateId("CLIENT"),
    name: name,
    code: cleanText(input.code, 50),
    contactPerson: cleanText(input.contactPerson, 150),
    phone: cleanText(input.phone, 50),
    email: cleanText(input.email, 150),
    address: cleanText(input.address, 500),
    taxNumber: cleanText(input.taxNumber, 100),
    notes: cleanText(input.notes, 1000),
    active: true,
    archived: false,
    createdAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
    updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };

  clients.push(client);
  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS, clients);

  writeAuditLog("CLIENT_CREATED", client.name);

  return {
    success: true,
    client: client
  };
}

function updateClient(token, clientId, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  input = input || {};

  const clients = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS);
  const client = pbl5FindById(clients, clientId);

  pbl5RequireActiveRecord(client, "Client");

  const name = pbl5ValidateName(
    input.name || client.name,
    "Client name"
  );

  if (!pbl5EnsureUnique(clients, "name", name, clientId)) {
    throw new Error("Another client already uses this name.");
  }

  [
    "code",
    "contactPerson",
    "phone",
    "email",
    "address",
    "taxNumber",
    "notes"
  ].forEach(function(field) {
    if (input[field] !== undefined) {
      client[field] = cleanText(input[field], 1000);
    }
  });

  client.name = name;
  client.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS, clients);

  writeAuditLog("CLIENT_UPDATED", client.name);

  return {
    success: true,
    client: client
  };
}

function archiveClient(token, clientId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const clients = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS);
  const client = pbl5FindById(clients, clientId);

  if (!client) {
    throw new Error("Client not found.");
  }

  client.archived = true;
  client.active = false;
  client.archivedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS, clients);

  writeAuditLog("CLIENT_ARCHIVED", client.name);

  return { success: true };
}

function restoreClient(token, clientId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const clients = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS);
  const client = pbl5FindById(clients, clientId);

  if (!client) {
    throw new Error("Client not found.");
  }

  client.archived = false;
  client.active = true;
  client.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS, clients);

  writeAuditLog("CLIENT_RESTORED", client.name);

  return { success: true };
}

function apiGetClients(includeArchived) {
  return pbl5SortByName(getAllClients(includeArchived));
}

function apiCreateClient(token, input) {
  return createClient(token, input);
}

function apiUpdateClient(token, id, input) {
  return updateClient(token, id, input);
}

function apiArchiveClient(token, id) {
  return archiveClient(token, id);
}

function apiRestoreClient(token, id) {
  return restoreClient(token, id);
}


/* =============================================================
   234. TRUCKS MANAGEMENT
   ============================================================= */

function getAllTrucks(includeArchived) {
  return pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.TRUCKS).filter(
    function(truck) {
      return includeArchived || truck.archived !== true;
    }
  );
}

function getTruckById(truckId) {
  return pbl5FindById(
    getAllTrucks(true),
    truckId
  );
}

function normalizeRegistration(value) {
  return cleanText(value, 50)
    .toUpperCase()
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ");
}

function createTruck(token, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  input = input || {};

  const trucks = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.TRUCKS);

  const registration = normalizeRegistration(
    input.registration || input.plate
  );

  if (!registration) {
    throw new Error("Truck registration is required.");
  }

  if (
    !pbl5EnsureUnique(
      trucks,
      "registration",
      registration
    )
  ) {
    throw new Error("This truck registration already exists.");
  }

  const truck = {
    id: pbl5CreateId("TRUCK"),
    registration: registration,
    brand: cleanText(input.brand, 100),
    model: cleanText(input.model, 100),
    capacityKg: Math.max(0, Number(input.capacityKg) || 0),
    owner: cleanText(input.owner, 150),
    notes: cleanText(input.notes, 500),
    active: true,
    archived: false,
    createdAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
    updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };

  trucks.push(truck);
  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.TRUCKS, trucks);

  writeAuditLog("TRUCK_CREATED", registration);

  return {
    success: true,
    truck: truck
  };
}

function updateTruck(token, truckId, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  input = input || {};

  const trucks = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.TRUCKS);
  const truck = pbl5FindById(trucks, truckId);

  pbl5RequireActiveRecord(truck, "Truck");

  const registration = normalizeRegistration(
    input.registration || truck.registration
  );

  if (
    !pbl5EnsureUnique(
      trucks,
      "registration",
      registration,
      truckId
    )
  ) {
    throw new Error("Another truck uses this registration.");
  }

  truck.registration = registration;
  truck.brand = cleanText(
    input.brand !== undefined ? input.brand : truck.brand,
    100
  );
  truck.model = cleanText(
    input.model !== undefined ? input.model : truck.model,
    100
  );
  truck.capacityKg = Math.max(
    0,
    Number(
      input.capacityKg !== undefined
        ? input.capacityKg
        : truck.capacityKg
    ) || 0
  );
  truck.owner = cleanText(
    input.owner !== undefined ? input.owner : truck.owner,
    150
  );
  truck.notes = cleanText(
    input.notes !== undefined ? input.notes : truck.notes,
    500
  );
  truck.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.TRUCKS, trucks);

  writeAuditLog("TRUCK_UPDATED", registration);

  return {
    success: true,
    truck: truck
  };
}

function archiveTruck(token, truckId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const trucks = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.TRUCKS);
  const truck = pbl5FindById(trucks, truckId);

  if (!truck) {
    throw new Error("Truck not found.");
  }

  truck.archived = true;
  truck.active = false;
  truck.archivedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.TRUCKS, trucks);

  writeAuditLog("TRUCK_ARCHIVED", truck.registration);

  return { success: true };
}

function restoreTruck(token, truckId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const trucks = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.TRUCKS);
  const truck = pbl5FindById(trucks, truckId);

  if (!truck) {
    throw new Error("Truck not found.");
  }

  truck.archived = false;
  truck.active = true;
  truck.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.TRUCKS, trucks);

  writeAuditLog("TRUCK_RESTORED", truck.registration);

  return { success: true };
}

function apiGetTrucks(includeArchived) {
  return getAllTrucks(includeArchived).sort(
    function(a, b) {
      return String(a.registration || "").localeCompare(
        String(b.registration || "")
      );
    }
  );
}

function apiCreateTruck(token, input) {
  return createTruck(token, input);
}

function apiUpdateTruck(token, id, input) {
  return updateTruck(token, id, input);
}

function apiArchiveTruck(token, id) {
  return archiveTruck(token, id);
}

function apiRestoreTruck(token, id) {
  return restoreTruck(token, id);
}


/* =============================================================
   235. DRIVERS MANAGEMENT
   ============================================================= */

function getAllDrivers(includeArchived) {
  return pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.DRIVERS).filter(
    function(driver) {
      return includeArchived || driver.archived !== true;
    }
  );
}

function getDriverById(driverId) {
  return pbl5FindById(
    getAllDrivers(true),
    driverId
  );
}

function createDriver(token, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  input = input || {};

  const drivers = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.DRIVERS);
  const name = pbl5ValidateName(input.name, "Driver name");

  if (!pbl5EnsureUnique(drivers, "name", name)) {
    throw new Error("This driver already exists.");
  }

  const driver = {
    id: pbl5CreateId("DRIVER"),
    name: name,
    phone: cleanText(input.phone, 50),
    licenseNumber: cleanText(input.licenseNumber, 100),
    licenseExpiry: cleanText(input.licenseExpiry, 30),
    notes: cleanText(input.notes, 500),
    active: true,
    archived: false,
    createdAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
    updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };

  drivers.push(driver);
  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.DRIVERS, drivers);

  writeAuditLog("DRIVER_CREATED", driver.name);

  return {
    success: true,
    driver: driver
  };
}

function updateDriver(token, driverId, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  input = input || {};

  const drivers = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.DRIVERS);
  const driver = pbl5FindById(drivers, driverId);

  pbl5RequireActiveRecord(driver, "Driver");

  const name = pbl5ValidateName(
    input.name || driver.name,
    "Driver name"
  );

  if (!pbl5EnsureUnique(drivers, "name", name, driverId)) {
    throw new Error("Another driver already uses this name.");
  }

  driver.name = name;

  [
    "phone",
    "licenseNumber",
    "licenseExpiry",
    "notes"
  ].forEach(function(field) {
    if (input[field] !== undefined) {
      driver[field] = cleanText(input[field], 500);
    }
  });

  driver.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.DRIVERS, drivers);

  writeAuditLog("DRIVER_UPDATED", driver.name);

  return {
    success: true,
    driver: driver
  };
}

function archiveDriver(token, driverId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const drivers = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.DRIVERS);
  const driver = pbl5FindById(drivers, driverId);

  if (!driver) {
    throw new Error("Driver not found.");
  }

  driver.archived = true;
  driver.active = false;
  driver.archivedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.DRIVERS, drivers);

  writeAuditLog("DRIVER_ARCHIVED", driver.name);

  return { success: true };
}

function restoreDriver(token, driverId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const drivers = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.DRIVERS);
  const driver = pbl5FindById(drivers, driverId);

  if (!driver) {
    throw new Error("Driver not found.");
  }

  driver.archived = false;
  driver.active = true;
  driver.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.DRIVERS, drivers);

  writeAuditLog("DRIVER_RESTORED", driver.name);

  return { success: true };
}

function apiGetDrivers(includeArchived) {
  return pbl5SortByName(getAllDrivers(includeArchived));
}

function apiCreateDriver(token, input) {

/* =============================================================
   236. TICKET LIFECYCLE MANAGEMENT
   ============================================================= */

function pbl5GetTicketCollection() {
  return pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.TICKETS);
}

function pbl5SaveTicketCollection(tickets) {
  pbl5SaveCollection(PBL_KEYS.TICKETS, tickets);
}

function getTicketById(ticketId) {
  return pbl5FindById(
    pbl5GetTicketCollection(),
    ticketId
  );
}

function getTicketByNumber(ticketNumber) {
  const tickets = pbl5GetTicketCollection();

  return tickets.find(function(ticket) {
    return String(ticket.ticketNumber) ===
      String(ticketNumber);
  }) || null;
}

function validateTicketForUpdate(input) {
  input = input || {};

  const grossKg = Number(input.grossKg);
  const tareKg = Number(input.tareKg);

  if (!Number.isFinite(grossKg) || grossKg < 0) {
    throw new Error("Gross weight must be a valid positive number.");
  }

  if (!Number.isFinite(tareKg) || tareKg < 0) {
    throw new Error("Tare weight must be a valid positive number.");
  }

  if (tareKg > grossKg) {
    throw new Error("Tare weight cannot exceed gross weight.");
  }

  const netKg = grossKg - tareKg;

  return {
    grossKg: roundNumber(grossKg, 0),
    tareKg: roundNumber(tareKg, 0),
    netKg: roundNumber(netKg, 0),
    netTonnes: roundNumber(netKg / 1000, 3)
  };
}

function updateTicketRecord(token, ticketId, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  input = input || {};

  const tickets = pbl5GetTicketCollection();
  const ticket = pbl5FindById(tickets, ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  if (ticket.status === PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS) {
    throw new Error("Deleted tickets cannot be edited.");
  }

  const weights = validateTicketForUpdate(input);

  const editableFields = [
    "product",
    "client",
    "truckRegistration",
    "driver",
    "reference",
    "notes"
  ];

  editableFields.forEach(function(field) {
    if (input[field] !== undefined) {
      ticket[field] = cleanText(input[field], 500);
    }
  });

  ticket.grossKg = weights.grossKg;
  ticket.tareKg = weights.tareKg;
  ticket.netKg = weights.netKg;
  ticket.netTonnes = weights.netTonnes;
  ticket.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  ticket.edited = true;

  pbl5SaveTicketCollection(tickets);

  writeAuditLog(
    "TICKET_UPDATED",
    ticket.ticketNumber || ticket.id
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    ticket: ticket
  };
}

function cancelTicket(token, ticketId, reason) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const tickets = pbl5GetTicketCollection();
  const ticket = pbl5FindById(tickets, ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  if (ticket.status === PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS) {
    throw new Error("Ticket is already deleted.");
  }

  ticket.previousStatus = ticket.status;
  ticket.status = PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
  ticket.cancelReason = cleanText(reason, 500);
  ticket.cancelledAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl5SaveTicketCollection(tickets);

  writeAuditLog(
    "TICKET_CANCELLED",
    String(ticket.ticketNumber || ticket.id) +
      " — " +
      ticket.cancelReason
  );

  return { success: true };
}

function restoreCancelledTicket(token, ticketId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const tickets = pbl5GetTicketCollection();
  const ticket = pbl5FindById(tickets, ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  if (ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS) {
    throw new Error("Ticket is not cancelled.");
  }

  ticket.status = ticket.previousStatus || "VALID";
  ticket.restoredAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  delete ticket.cancelReason;
  delete ticket.cancelledAt;

  pbl5SaveTicketCollection(tickets);

  writeAuditLog(
    "TICKET_RESTORED",
    ticket.ticketNumber || ticket.id
  );

  return { success: true };
}

function apiGetTicketById(ticketId) {
  return getTicketById(ticketId);
}

function apiUpdateTicket(token, ticketId, input) {
  return updateTicketRecord(token, ticketId, input);
}

function apiCancelTicket(token, ticketId, reason) {
  return cancelTicket(token, ticketId, reason);
}

function apiRestoreTicket(token, ticketId) {
  return restoreCancelledTicket(token, ticketId);
}


/* =============================================================
   237. OPERATIONAL STATISTICS
   ============================================================= */

function getOperationalStatistics() {
  const tickets = getAllTickets().filter(function(ticket) {
    return ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
  });

  const products = getAllProducts(false);
  const clients = getAllClients(false);
  const trucks = getAllTrucks(false);
  const drivers = getAllDrivers(false);

  const totals = calculateReportTotals(tickets);

  return {
    application: PBL.APP_NAME,
    generatedAt: getLiveTimestamp(),

    totalTickets: tickets.length,
    totalVoyages: totals.voyages,

    totalGrossKg: totals.grossKg,
    totalTareKg: totals.tareKg,
    totalNetKg: totals.netKg,
    totalTonnes: totals.tonnes,

    activeProducts: products.length,
    activeClients: clients.length,
    activeTrucks: trucks.length,
    activeDrivers: drivers.length,

    today: getLiveDate(),
    todayTickets: tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
      return ticket.date === getLiveDate();
    }).length,

    todayTonnes: roundNumber(
      tickets
        .filter(function(ticket) {
          return ticket.date === getLiveDate();
        })
        .reduce(function(total, ticket) {
          return total + Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
        }, 0) / 1000,
      3
    )
  };
}

function apiGetOperationalStatistics() {
  return getOperationalStatistics();
}


/* =============================================================
   238. TOP PRODUCTS
   ============================================================= */

function getTopProducts(limit) {
  const tickets = getAllTickets().filter(function(ticket) {
    return ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
  });

  const groups = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const name = ticket.product || "UNSPECIFIED";

    if (!groups[name]) {
      groups[name] = {
        name: name,
        voyages: 0,
        netKg: 0
      };
    }

    groups[name].voyages++;
    groups[name].netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups)
    .map(function(key) {
      const item = groups[key];

      return {
        name: item.name,
        voyages: item.voyages,
        netKg: roundNumber(item.netKg, 0),
        tonnes: roundNumber(item.netKg / 1000, 3)
      };
    })
    .sort(function(a, b) {
      return b.netKg - a.netKg;
    })
    .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit) || 10));
}

function apiGetTopProducts(limit) {
  return getTopProducts(limit);
}


/* =============================================================
   239. TOP CLIENTS
   ============================================================= */

function getTopClients(limit) {
  const tickets = getAllTickets().filter(function(ticket) {
    return ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;

  const groups = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const name = ticket.client || "UNSPECIFIED";

    if (!groups[name]) {
      groups[name] = {
        name: name,
        voyages: 0,
        netKg: 0
      };
    }

    groups[name].voyages++;
    groups[name].netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups)
    .map(function(key) {
      const item = groups[key];

      return {
        name: item.name,
        voyages: item.voyages,
        netKg: roundNumber(item.netKg, 0),
        tonnes: roundNumber(item.netKg / 1000, 3)
      };
    })
    .sort(function(a, b) {
      return b.netKg - a.netKg;
    })
    .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit) || 10));
}

function apiGetTopClients(limit) {

/* =============================================================
   240. TOP TRUCKS
   ============================================================= */

function getTopTrucks(limit) {
  const tickets = getAllTickets().filter(function(ticket) {
    return ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS;
  });

  const groups = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const name = ticket.truckRegistration || "UNSPECIFIED";

    if (!groups[name]) {
      groups[name] = {
        registration: name,
        voyages: 0,
        netKg: 0
      };
    }

    groups[name].voyages++;
    groups[name].netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups)
    .map(function(key) {
      const item = groups[key];

      return {
        registration: item.registration,
        voyages: item.voyages,
        netKg: roundNumber(item.netKg, 0),
        tonnes: roundNumber(item.netKg / 1000, 3)
      };
    })
    .sort(function(a, b) {
      return b.voyages - a.voyages;
    })
    .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit) || 10));
}

function apiGetTopTrucks(limit) {
  return getTopTrucks(limit);
}


/* =============================================================
   241. HOURLY DELIVERY DISTRIBUTION
   ============================================================= */

function getHourlyDeliveryDistribution(dateValue) {
  const targetDate = dateValue || getLiveDate();

  const tickets = getAllTickets().filter(function(ticket) {
    return (
      ticket.date === targetDate &&
      ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS
    );
  });

  const hours = {};

  for (let i = 0; i < 24; i++) {
    const key = String(i).padStart(2, "0");

    hours[key] = {
      hour: key,
      voyages: 0,
      netKg: 0
    };
  }

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const hour = String(ticket.time || "").substring(0, 2);

    if (!hours[hour]) {
      return;
    }

    hours[hour].voyages++;
    hours[hour].netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
  });

  return Object.keys(hours).map(function(key) {
    const item = hours[key];

    return {
      hour: item.hour,
      voyages: item.voyages,
      netKg: roundNumber(item.netKg, 0),
      tonnes: roundNumber(item.netKg / 1000, 3)
    };
  });
}

function apiGetHourlyDistribution(dateValue) {
  return getHourlyDeliveryDistribution(dateValue);
}


/* =============================================================
   242. AVERAGE WEIGHT PER VOYAGE
   ============================================================= */

function getAverageNetWeightPerVoyage(filters) {
  const tickets = filterTicketsAdvanced(filters || {});

  const totals = calculateReportTotals(tickets);

  return {
    voyages: totals.voyages,
    totalNetKg: totals.netKg,
    averageNetKg: totals.voyages
      ? roundNumber(totals.netKg / totals.voyages, 0)
      : 0,
    averageTonnes: totals.voyages
      ? roundNumber(
          totals.netKg / totals.voyages / 1000,
          3
        )
      : 0
  };
}

function apiGetAverageWeight(filters) {
  return getAverageNetWeightPerVoyage(filters);
}

  };

/* =============================================================
   245. WEIGHING CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================= */

var PBL6_CONFIG = {
  DEFAULT_UNIT: "KG",
  MAX_WEIGHT_KG: 1000000,
  MIN_WEIGHT_KG: 0,
  DEFAULT_TICKET_PREFIX: "BL",
  MAX_DECIMAL_PLACES: 0,
  REQUIRE_PRODUCT: true,
  REQUIRE_CLIENT: true,
  REQUIRE_TRUCK: true,
  REQUIRE_FIRST_WEIGHT: true,
  REQUIRE_SECOND_WEIGHT: true,
  MANUAL_ENTRY_DEFAULT: false,
  SCALE_STABLE_REQUIRED: true
};


/* =============================================================
   246. WEIGHT VALIDATION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl6ValidateWeight(value, label) {
  const weight = Number(value);

  if (!Number.isFinite(weight)) {
    throw new Error(label + " must be a valid number.");
  }

  if (weight < PBL6_CONFIG.MIN_WEIGHT_KG) {
    throw new Error(label + " cannot be negative.");
  }

  if (weight > PBL6_CONFIG.MAX_WEIGHT_KG) {
    throw new Error(label + " exceeds the maximum permitted weight.");
  }

  return Math.round(weight);
}


/* =============================================================
   247. WEIGHT CALCULATION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl6CalculateWeights(grossKg, tareKg) {
  const gross = pbl6ValidateWeight(
    grossKg,
    "Gross weight"
  );

  const tare = pbl6ValidateWeight(
    tareKg,
    "Tare weight"
  );

  if (tare > gross) {
    throw new Error(
      "Tare weight cannot be greater than gross weight."
    );
  }

  const net = gross - tare;

  return {
    grossKg: gross,
    tareKg: tare,
    netKg: net,
    netTonnes: roundNumber(net / 1000, 3)
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   248. WEIGHING DATE HELPERS
   ============================================================= */

function pbl6CurrentDateParts() {
  const now = new Date();

  return {
    date: Utilities.formatDate(
      now,
      PBL6_CONFIG_TIMEZONE(),
      "dd/MM/yyyy"
    ),
    time: Utilities.formatDate(
      now,
      PBL6_CONFIG_TIMEZONE(),
      "HH:mm:ss"
    ),
    timestamp: now.getTime()
  };
}

function PBL6_CONFIG_TIMEZONE() {
  return (
    PBL.TIME_ZONE ||
    PBL.TIMEZONE ||
    "Africa/Casablanca"
  );
}


/* =============================================================
   249. WEIGHING RECORD COLLECTION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl6GetWeighingRecords() {
  const records = getCollectionSafely(
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM
  );

  if (!Array.isArray(records)) {
    return [];
  }

  return records.filter(function(record) {
    return record && record.recordType === "WEIGHING_SESSION";
  });
}

function pbl6SaveWeighingRecords(records) {
  const systemRecords = getCollectionSafely(
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM
  );

  const otherRecords = Array.isArray(systemRecords)
    ? systemRecords.filter(function(record) {
        return !record ||
          record.recordType !== "WEIGHING_SESSION";
      })
    : [];

  saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM,
    otherRecords.concat(records)
  );
}


/* =============================================================
   250. FIND ACTIVE WEIGHING SESSION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl6FindSession(sessionId) {
  const records = pbl6GetWeighingRecords();

  return records.find(function(record) {
    return String(record.id) === String(sessionId);
  }) || null;
}


/* =============================================================
   251. CREATE FIRST WEIGHING SESSION
   ============================================================= */

function startFirstWeighing(token, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  input = input || {};

  const product = cleanText(input.product, 150);
  const client = cleanText(input.client, 150);
  const truck = cleanText(
    input.truckRegistration,
    80
  ).toUpperCase();

  const driver = cleanText(input.driver, 150);

  if (PBL6_CONFIG.REQUIRE_PRODUCT && !product) {
    throw new Error("Select a product before weighing.");
  }

  if (PBL6_CONFIG.REQUIRE_CLIENT && !client) {
    throw new Error("Select a client before weighing.");
  }

  if (PBL6_CONFIG.REQUIRE_TRUCK && !truck) {
    throw new Error("Truck registration is required.");
  }

  const firstWeight = pbl6ValidateWeight(
    input.weightKg,
    "First weight"
  );

  const dateParts = pbl6CurrentDateParts();

  const session = {
    id: pbl5CreateId("WEIGH"),
    recordType: "WEIGHING_SESSION",
    status: "WAITING_SECOND_WEIGHT",

    product: product,
    client: client,
    truckRegistration: truck,
    driver: driver,

    firstWeightKg: firstWeight,
    firstWeightDate: dateParts.date,
    firstWeightTime: dateParts.time,
    firstWeightTimestamp: dateParts.timestamp,

    secondWeightKg: null,
    secondWeightDate: null,
    secondWeightTime: null,
    secondWeightTimestamp: null,

    grossKg: null,
    tareKg: null,
    netKg: null,
    netTonnes: null,

    reference: cleanText(input.reference, 150),
    notes: cleanText(input.notes, 1000),

    createdAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
    updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };

  const records = pbl6GetWeighingRecords();

  records.push(session);

  pbl6SaveWeighingRecords(records);

  writeAuditLog(
    "FIRST_WEIGHING_RECORDED",
    session.id + " / " + firstWeight + " KG"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    session: session,
    message: "First weighing recorded. Awaiting second weighing."
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   252. SECOND WEIGHING
   ============================================================= */

function completeSecondWeighing(token, sessionId, weightKg) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const records = pbl6GetWeighingRecords();

  const session = records.find(function(record) {
    return String(record.id) === String(sessionId);
  });

  if (!session) {
    throw new Error("Weighing session not found.");
  }

  if (session.status !== "WAITING_SECOND_WEIGHT") {
    throw new Error(
      "This session is not waiting for a second weighing."
    );
  }

  const secondWeight = pbl6ValidateWeight(
    weightKg,
    "Second weight"
  );

  if (secondWeight === session.firstWeightKg) {
    throw new Error(
      "The two weights are identical. Verify the weighing."
    );
  }

  const weights = pbl6CalculateWeights(
    Math.max(session.firstWeightKg, secondWeight),
    Math.min(session.firstWeightKg, secondWeight)
  );

  const dateParts = pbl6CurrentDateParts();

  session.secondWeightKg = secondWeight;
  session.secondWeightDate = dateParts.date;
  session.secondWeightTime = dateParts.time;
  session.secondWeightTimestamp = dateParts.timestamp;

  session.grossKg = weights.grossKg;
  session.tareKg = weights.tareKg;
  session.netKg = weights.netKg;
  session.netTonnes = weights.netTonnes;

  session.status = "READY_TO_VALIDATE";
  session.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl6SaveWeighingRecords(records);

  writeAuditLog(
    "SECOND_WEIGHING_RECORDED",
    session.id + " / NET " + weights.netKg + " KG"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    session: session,
    weights: weights
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   253. AUTOMATIC GROSS AND TARE ASSIGNMENT
   ============================================================= */

function pbl6DetermineWeightRoles(firstWeight, secondWeight) {
  const first = Number(firstWeight);
  const second = Number(secondWeight);

  if (first >= second) {
    return {
      grossKg: first,
      tareKg: second,
      firstWeightRole: "GROSS",
      secondWeightRole: "TARE"
    };
  }

  return {
    grossKg: second,
    tareKg: first,
    firstWeightRole: "TARE",
    secondWeightRole: "GROSS"
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   254. AUTOMATIC TICKET NUMBER
   ============================================================= */

function pbl6GetNextTicketNumber() {
  const counters = loadCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.COUNTERS
  ) || {};

  const settings = loadCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.SETTINGS
  ) || getDefaultSettings();

  const prefix = cleanText(
    settings.defaultTicketPrefix ||
    PBL6_CONFIG.DEFAULT_TICKET_PREFIX,
    12
  );

  const length = Math.max(
    3,
    Math.min(12, Number(settings.ticketNumberLength) || 6)
  );

  let next = Number(counters.ticket || 0) + 1;

  const existing = getAllTickets();

  while (existing.some(function(ticket) {
    return String(ticket.ticketNumber) ===
      prefix + String(next).padStart(length, "0");
  })) {
    next++;
  }

  return {
    number: next,
    ticketNumber: prefix + String(next).padStart(length, "0"),
    counters: counters
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   255. COMMIT TICKET COUNTER
   ============================================================= */

function pbl6CommitTicketCounter(number, counters) {
  counters.ticket = Number(number);

  saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.COUNTERS,
    counters
  );
}


/* =============================================================
   256. FINALIZE WEIGHING SESSION
   ============================================================= */

function finalizeWeighingSession(token, sessionId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();

  lock.waitLock(30000);

  try {
    const records = pbl6GetWeighingRecords();

    const session = records.find(function(record) {
      return String(record.id) === String(sessionId);
    });

    if (!session) {
      throw new Error("Weighing session not found.");
    }

    if (session.status !== "READY_TO_VALIDATE") {
      throw new Error(
        "The session must have two recorded weights."
      );
    }

    if (
      session.grossKg === null ||
      session.tareKg === null ||
      session.netKg === null
    ) {
      throw new Error("Weight calculation is incomplete.");
    }

    const existingTickets = getAllTickets();

    const alreadyFinalized = existingTickets.some(
      function(ticket) {
        return ticket.weighingSessionId === session.id;
      }
    );

    if (alreadyFinalized) {
      throw new Error(
        "A ticket already exists for this weighing session."
      );
    }

    const numberData = pbl6GetNextTicketNumber();

    const dateParts = pbl6CurrentDateParts();

    const ticket = {
      id: pbl5CreateId("TICKET"),
      ticketNumber: numberData.ticketNumber,

      weighingSessionId: session.id,

      date: dateParts.date,
      time: dateParts.time,
      timestamp: dateParts.timestamp,

      product: session.product,
      client: session.client,
      truckRegistration: session.truckRegistration,
      driver: session.driver,

      firstWeightKg: session.firstWeightKg,
      secondWeightKg: session.secondWeightKg,

      grossKg: session.grossKg,
      tareKg: session.tareKg,
      netKg: session.netKg,
      netTonnes: session.netTonnes,

      reference: session.reference || "",
      notes: session.notes || "",

      status: "VALID",
      createdAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
      updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
    };

    const tickets = pbl5GetTicketCollection();

    tickets.push(ticket);

    pbl5SaveTicketCollection(tickets);

    pbl6CommitTicketCounter(
      numberData.number,
      numberData.counters
    );

    session.status = "COMPLETED";
    session.ticketId = ticket.id;
    session.ticketNumber = ticket.ticketNumber;
    session.completedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

    pbl6SaveWeighingRecords(records);

    writeAuditLog(
      "TICKET_FINALIZED",
      ticket.ticketNumber
    );

    return {
      success: true,
      ticket: ticket,
      message: "Ticket created successfully."
    };

  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}


/* =============================================================
   257. CANCEL WEIGHING SESSION
   ============================================================= */

function cancelWeighingSession(token, sessionId, reason) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const records = pbl6GetWeighingRecords();

  const session = records.find(function(record) {
    return String(record.id) === String(sessionId);
  });

  if (!session) {
    throw new Error("Weighing session not found.");
  }

  if (session.status === "COMPLETED") {
    throw new Error(
      "A completed weighing session cannot be cancelled here."
    );
  }

  session.status = "CANCELLED";
  session.cancelReason = cleanText(reason, 500);
  session.cancelledAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl6SaveWeighingRecords(records);

  writeAuditLog(
    "WEIGHING_SESSION_CANCELLED",
    session.id + " / " + session.cancelReason
  );

  return { success: true };
}


/* =============================================================
   258. MANUAL ENTRY PERMISSION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl6IsManualEntryEnabled() {
  const settings = loadCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.SETTINGS
  ) || {};

  return settings.allowManualWeightEntry === true;
}


/* =============================================================
   259. MANUAL ENTRY SETTING
   ============================================================= */

function setManualEntryPermission(token, enabled) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const settings = loadCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.SETTINGS
  ) || getDefaultSettings();

  settings.allowManualWeightEntry = enabled === true;
  settings.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.SETTINGS,
    settings
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "MANUAL_ENTRY_PERMISSION_CHANGED",
    enabled === true ? "ENABLED" : "DISABLED"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    enabled: settings.allowManualWeightEntry
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   260. MANUAL WEIGHT ENTRY
   ============================================================= */

function recordManualFirstWeight(token, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (!pbl6IsManualEntryEnabled()) {
    throw new Error(
      "Manual weight entry is disabled in settings."
    );
  }

  input = input || {};

  const reason = cleanText(input.reason, 500);

  if (reason.length < 5) {
    throw new Error(
      "A reason is required for manual weight entry."
    );
  }

  const result = startFirstWeighing(token, {
    product: input.product,
    client: input.client,
    truckRegistration: input.truckRegistration,
    driver: input.driver,
    weightKg: input.weightKg,
    reference: input.reference,
    notes: (
      cleanText(input.notes, 800) +
      " | MANUAL ENTRY: " +
      reason
    )
  });

  const records = pbl6GetWeighingRecords();

  const session = records.find(function(record) {
    return record.id === result.session.id;
  });

  if (session) {
    session.weightSource = "MANUAL";
    session.manualEntryReason = reason;
    session.manualEntryAt = getLiveTimestamp();

    pbl6SaveWeighingRecords(records);
  }

  writeAuditLog(
    "MANUAL_FIRST_WEIGHT",
    result.session.id + " / " + reason
  );

  return result;
}


/* =============================================================
   261. MANUAL SECOND WEIGHT
   ============================================================= */

function recordManualSecondWeight(token, sessionId, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (!pbl6IsManualEntryEnabled()) {
    throw new Error(
      "Manual weight entry is disabled in settings."
    );
  }

  input = input || {};

  const reason = cleanText(input.reason, 500);

  if (reason.length < 5) {
    throw new Error(
      "A reason is required for manual weight entry."
    );
  }

  const result = completeSecondWeighing(
    token,
    sessionId,
    input.weightKg
  );

  const records = pbl6GetWeighingRecords();

  const session = records.find(function(record) {
    return record.id === String(sessionId);
  });

  if (session) {
    session.secondWeightSource = "MANUAL";
    session.secondManualEntryReason = reason;
    session.secondManualEntryAt = getLiveTimestamp();

    pbl6SaveWeighingRecords(records);
  }

  writeAuditLog(
    "MANUAL_SECOND_WEIGHT",
    sessionId + " / " + reason
  );

  return result;
}


/* =============================================================
   262. SCALE READING VALIDATION
   ============================================================= */

function validateScaleReading(reading) {
  if (!reading || typeof reading !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Invalid scale reading.");
  }

  const weight = pbl6ValidateWeight(
    reading.weightKg,
    "Scale weight"
  );

  return {
    weightKg: weight,
    stable: reading.stable === true,
    connected: reading.connected === true,
    source: cleanText(reading.source || "SCALE", 50),
    receivedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   263. SCALE STATUS
   ============================================================= */

function getScaleConnectionStatus() {
  return {
    application: PBL.APP_NAME,
    connectionStatus: "NOT_CONNECTED",
    message: "No live scale connection has been configured.",
    readingAvailable: false,
    checkedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   264. SCALE STATUS API
   ============================================================= */

function apiGetScaleStatus() {
  return getScaleConnectionStatus();
}


/* =============================================================
   265. RECEIVE SCALE READING
   ============================================================= */

function apiReceiveScaleReading(token, reading) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const result = validateScaleReading(reading);

  if (
    PBL6_CONFIG.SCALE_STABLE_REQUIRED &&
    result.stable !== true
  ) {
    return {
      success: false,
      stable: false,
      message: "Waiting for stable scale reading."
    };
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    reading: result
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   266. GET ACTIVE WEIGHING SESSIONS
   ============================================================= */

function getActiveWeighingSessions() {
  return pbl6GetWeighingRecords()
    .filter(function(session) {
      return (
        session.status === "WAITING_SECOND_WEIGHT" ||
        session.status === "READY_TO_VALIDATE"
      );
    })
    .sort(function(a, b) {
      return Number(b.createdAtTimestamp || 0) -
        Number(a.createdAtTimestamp || 0);
    });
}


/* =============================================================
   267. GET WEIGHING SESSION HISTORY
   ============================================================= */

function getWeighingSessionHistory(limit) {
  const maximum = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(Number(limit) || 100, 1000)
  );

  return pbl6GetWeighingRecords()
    .sort(function(a, b) {
      return String(b.createdAt || "").localeCompare(
        String(a.createdAt || "")
      );
    })
    .slice(0, maximum);
}


/* =============================================================
   268. GET SESSION DETAILS
   ============================================================= */

function getWeighingSessionDetails(sessionId) {
  const session = pbl6FindSession(sessionId);

  if (!session) {
    throw new Error("Weighing session not found.");
  }

  const ticket = session.ticketId
    ? getTicketById(session.ticketId)
    : null;

  return {
    session: session,
    ticket: ticket,
    hasTicket: Boolean(ticket)
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   269. WEIGHING WORKFLOW SUMMARY
   ============================================================= */

function getWeighingWorkflowSummary() {
  const records = pbl6GetWeighingRecords();

  return {
    waitingSecondWeight: records.filter(function(item) {
      return item.status === "WAITING_SECOND_WEIGHT";
    }).length,

    readyToValidate: records.filter(function(item) {
      return item.status === "READY_TO_VALIDATE";
    }).length,

    completed: records.filter(function(item) {
      return item.status === "COMPLETED";
    }).length,

    cancelled: records.filter(function(item) {
      return item.status === "CANCELLED";
    }).length,

    manualEntryEnabled: pbl6IsManualEntryEnabled(),
    generatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}
