/* =============================================================
   282. TICKET PRINT CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================= */

var PBL7_CONFIG = {
  PAPER_WIDTH_MM: 100,
  PAPER_HEIGHT_MM: 150,
  DEFAULT_COPIES: 1,
  MAX_COPIES: 5,
  INCLUDE_LOGO: false,
  INCLUDE_SIGNATURES: true,
  INCLUDE_QR_DATA: true,
  COMPANY_FOOTER: "PONT BASCULE LIVE"
};


/* =============================================================
   283. TICKET LOOKUP BY NUMBER
   ============================================================= */

function findTicketByNumber(ticketNumber) {
  const number = cleanText(ticketNumber, 100);

  if (!number) {
    return null;
  }

  return getAllTickets().find(function(ticket) {
    return normalizeSearchValue(ticket.ticketNumber) ===
      normalizeSearchValue(number);
  }) || null;
}


/* =============================================================
   284. TICKET SEARCH
   ============================================================= */

function searchTicketByNumber(query) {
  const search = normalizeSearchValue(query);

  if (!search) {
    return [];
  }

  return getAllTickets()
    .filter(function(ticket) {
      return (
        ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS &&
        normalizeSearchValue(ticket.ticketNumber).includes(search)
      );
    })
    .sort(function(a, b) {
      return String(b.timestamp || "").localeCompare(
        String(a.timestamp || "")
      );
    })
    .slice(0, 100);
}


/* =============================================================
   285. TICKET REQUIRED FIELD CHECK
   ============================================================= */

function validateStoredTicket(ticket) {
  const errors = [];

  if (!ticket) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      errors: ["Ticket does not exist."]
    };
  }

  if (!ticket.ticketNumber) {
    errors.push("Missing ticket number.");
  }

  if (!ticket.date) {
    errors.push("Missing ticket date.");
  }

  if (!ticket.product) {
    errors.push("Missing product.");
  }

  if (!ticket.client) {
    errors.push("Missing client.");
  }

  if (!ticket.truckRegistration) {
    errors.push("Missing truck registration.");
  }

  const gross = Number(ticket.grossKg);
  const tare = Number(ticket.tareKg);
  const net = Number(ticket.netKg);

  if (!Number.isFinite(gross) || gross < 0) {
    errors.push("Invalid gross weight.");
  }

  if (!Number.isFinite(tare) || tare < 0) {
    errors.push("Invalid tare weight.");
  }

  if (!Number.isFinite(net) || net < 0) {
    errors.push("Invalid net weight.");
  }

  if (
    Number.isFinite(gross) &&
    Number.isFinite(tare) &&
    Number.isFinite(net) &&
    gross - tare !== net
  ) {
    errors.push("Weight calculation does not match.");
  }

  return {
    valid: errors.length === 0,
    errors: errors
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   286. VERIFY TICKET
   ============================================================= */

function verifyTicket(ticketNumber) {
  const ticket = findTicketByNumber(ticketNumber);

  if (!ticket) {
    return {
      found: false,
      valid: false,
      message: "Ticket not found."
    };
  }

  const validation = validateStoredTicket(ticket);

  return {
    found: true,
    valid: validation.valid,
    ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber,
    date: ticket.date,
    time: ticket.time,
    client: ticket.client,
    product: ticket.product,
    truckRegistration: ticket.truckRegistration,
    netKg: ticket.netKg,
    netTonnes: ticket.netTonnes,
    status: ticket.status,
    errors: validation.errors
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   287. TICKET VERIFICATION API
   ============================================================= */

function apiVerifyTicket(ticketNumber) {
  return verifyTicket(ticketNumber);
}


/* =============================================================
   288. DUPLICATE TICKET DETECTION
   ============================================================= */

function findDuplicateTicket(ticket) {
  const tickets = getAllTickets();

  return tickets.filter(function(existing) {
    if (existing.id === ticket.id) {
      return false;
    }

    if (existing.status === PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS) {
      return false;
    }

    return (
      String(existing.ticketNumber) ===
      String(ticket.ticketNumber)
    );
  });
}


/* =============================================================
   289. DUPLICATE TICKET REPORT
   ============================================================= */

function getDuplicateTicketReport() {
  const tickets = getAllTickets();
  const seen = {};
  const duplicates = [];

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const number = String(ticket.ticketNumber || "");

    if (!number) {
      return;
    }

    if (seen[number]) {
      duplicates.push({
        ticketNumber: number,
        firstTicketId: seen[number],
        duplicateTicketId: ticket.id
      });
    } else {
      seen[number] = ticket.id;
    }
  });

  return {
    count: duplicates.length,
    duplicates: duplicates,
    checkedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   290. DUPLICATE REPORT API
   ============================================================= */

function apiGetDuplicateTicketReport(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);
  return getDuplicateTicketReport();
}


/* =============================================================
   291. PRINT HISTORY COLLECTION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl7GetPrintHistory() {
  const data = getCollectionSafely(PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM);

  if (!Array.isArray(data)) {
    return [];
  }

  return data.filter(function(item) {
    return item && item.recordType === "PRINT_HISTORY";
  });
}


/* =============================================================
   292. SAVE PRINT HISTORY
   ============================================================= */

function pbl7SavePrintHistory(history) {
  const data = getCollectionSafely(PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM);

  const other = Array.isArray(data)
    ? data.filter(function(item) {
        return !item || item.recordType !== "PRINT_HISTORY";
      })
    : [];

  saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM,
    other.concat(history)
  );
}


/* =============================================================
   293. RECORD TICKET PRINT
   ============================================================= */

function recordTicketPrint(token, ticketId, copies, reason) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const ticket = getTicketById(ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  if (ticket.status === PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS) {
    throw new Error("Cancelled ticket cannot be printed.");
  }

  const copyCount = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(Number(copies) || 1, PBL7_CONFIG.MAX_COPIES)
  );

  const history = pbl7GetPrintHistory();

  const record = {
    id: pbl5CreateId("PRINT"),
    recordType: "PRINT_HISTORY",
    ticketId: ticket.id,
    ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber,
    copies: copyCount,
    reason: cleanText(reason || "ORIGINAL PRINT", 300),
    printedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };

  history.push(record);

  pbl7SavePrintHistory(history);

  writeAuditLog(
    "TICKET_PRINT_REQUESTED",
    ticket.ticketNumber + " / " + copyCount + " copies"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    printRecord: record
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   294. TICKET PRINT HISTORY API
   ============================================================= */

function apiGetTicketPrintHistory(token, ticketId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return pbl7GetPrintHistory().filter(function(record) {
    return String(record.ticketId) === String(ticketId);
  });
}

   295. PRINT REQUEST API
   ============================================================= */

function apiRecordTicketPrint(token, ticketId, copies, reason) {
  return recordTicketPrint(
    token,
    ticketId,
    copies,
    reason
  );
}


/* =============================================================
   296. COMPANY PRINT INFORMATION
   ============================================================= */

function getCompanyPrintInformation() {
  const settings = apiGetApplicationSettings();

  return {
    applicationName: PBL.APP_NAME,
    companyName: settings.companyName || "",
    companyAddress: settings.companyAddress || "",
    companyPhone: settings.companyPhone || "",
    companyEmail: settings.companyEmail || "",
    footer: PBL7_CONFIG.COMPANY_FOOTER,
    logoAvailable: false
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   297. PRINTABLE TICKET DATA
   ============================================================= */

function getPrintableTicket(ticketId) {
  const ticket = getTicketById(ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  const validation = validateStoredTicket(ticket);

  if (!validation.valid) {
    throw new Error(
      "Ticket cannot be printed: " +
      validation.errors.join(" ")
    );
  }

  return {
    company: getCompanyPrintInformation(),

    ticket: {
      id: ticket.id,
      number: ticket.ticketNumber,
      date: ticket.date,
      time: ticket.time,

      product: ticket.product,
      client: ticket.client,
      truck: ticket.truckRegistration,
      driver: ticket.driver || "",

      grossKg: Number(ticket.grossKg || 0),
      tareKg: Number(ticket.tareKg || 0),
      netKg: Number(ticket.netKg || 0),
      netTonnes: Number(ticket.netKg || 0) / 1000,

      reference: ticket.reference || "",
      notes: ticket.notes || "",
      status: ticket.status
    },

    signatures: {
      operator: "",
      driver: ""
    },

    paper: {
      widthMm: PBL7_CONFIG.PAPER_WIDTH_MM,
      heightMm: PBL7_CONFIG.PAPER_HEIGHT_MM,
      orientation: "PORTRAIT"
    },

    generatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   298. PRINTABLE TICKET API
   ============================================================= */

function apiGetPrintableTicket(ticketId) {
  return getPrintableTicket(ticketId);
}


/* =============================================================
   299. TICKET REPRINT DATA
   ============================================================= */

function getTicketReprintData(ticketId) {
  const data = getPrintableTicket(ticketId);

  data.reprint = true;
  data.reprintLabel = "DUPLICATA";
  data.reprintWarning =
    "This document is a reprint of the original ticket.";

  return data;
}


/* =============================================================
   300. REPRINT API
   ============================================================= */

function apiGetTicketReprintData(token, ticketId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const result = recordTicketPrint(
    token,
    ticketId,
    1,
    "DUPLICATE / REPRINT"
  );

  const data = getTicketReprintData(ticketId);

  data.printRecord = result.printRecord;

  return data;
}

/* =============================================================
   303. TICKET SUMMARY
   ============================================================= */

function getTicketSummary(ticketId) {
  const ticket = getTicketById(ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  return {
    ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber,
    date: ticket.date,
    time: ticket.time,
    product: ticket.product,
    client: ticket.client,
    truck: ticket.truckRegistration,
    grossKg: ticket.grossKg,
    tareKg: ticket.tareKg,
    netKg: ticket.netKg,
    netTonnes: roundNumber(
      Number(ticket.netKg || 0) / 1000,
      3
    ),
    status: ticket.status
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   305. DELIVERY DOCUMENT DATA
   ============================================================= */

function getDeliveryDocumentData(filters) {
  const tickets = filterTicketsAdvanced(filters || {});

  const totals = calculateReportTotals(tickets);

  return {
    company: getCompanyPrintInformation(),

    documentTitle: "DELIVERY STATEMENT",

    period: getReportPeriodLabel(
      filters && filters.startDate,
      filters && filters.endDate
    ),

    generatedAt: getLiveTimestamp(),

    summary: {
      voyages: totals.voyages,
      grossKg: totals.grossKg,
      tareKg: totals.tareKg,
      netKg: totals.netKg,
      tonnes: totals.tonnes
    },

    tickets: tickets.map(function(ticket) {
      return {
        ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber,
        date: ticket.date,
        time: ticket.time,
        product: ticket.product,
        client: ticket.client,
        truck: ticket.truckRegistration,
        grossKg: ticket.grossKg,
        tareKg: ticket.tareKg,
        netKg: ticket.netKg,
        tonnes: roundNumber(
          Number(ticket.netKg || 0) / 1000,
          3
        )
      };
    })
  };
}

  return getDeliveryDocumentData(filters);

/* =============================================================
   307. TICKET STATISTICS
   ============================================================= */

function getTicketPrintStatistics() {
  const history = pbl7GetPrintHistory();

  return {
    totalPrintRequests: history.length,

    totalCopies: history.reduce(function(total, record) {
      return total + Number(record.copies || 0);
    }, 0),

    originalPrints: history.filter(function(record) {
      return normalizeSearchValue(record.reason)
        .includes("original");
    }).length,

    reprints: history.filter(function(record) {
      return normalizeSearchValue(record.reason)
        .includes("duplicate") ||
        normalizeSearchValue(record.reason)
          .includes("reprint");
    }).length
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   308. PRINT STATISTICS API
   ============================================================= */

function apiGetTicketPrintStatistics(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);
  return getTicketPrintStatistics();
}

  if (!ticket) {

      found: false,
      ticket: null
    };
  }

  return {
    found: true,
    ticket: ticket
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   313. TICKET ARCHIVE SEARCH
   ============================================================= */

function searchAllTicketRecords(query) {
  const search = normalizeSearchValue(query);

  return getAllTickets().filter(function(ticket) {
    return (
      normalizeSearchValue(ticket.ticketNumber).includes(search) ||
      normalizeSearchValue(ticket.client).includes(search) ||
      normalizeSearchValue(ticket.product).includes(search) ||
      normalizeSearchValue(ticket.truckRegistration).includes(search)
    );
  });
}


/* =============================================================
   314. TICKET ARCHIVE API
   ============================================================= */

function apiSearchAllTicketRecords(query) {
  return searchAllTicketRecords(query);
}

/* =============================================================
   317. TICKET IMPORT VALIDATION
   ============================================================= */

function validateTicketImportObject(input) {
  if (!input || input.exportType !== "PBL_TICKET") {
    throw new Error("Unsupported ticket export format.");
  }

  const ticket = input.ticket;

  const validation = validateStoredTicket(ticket);

  if (!validation.valid) {
    throw new Error(
      "Imported ticket is invalid: " +
      validation.errors.join(" ")
    );
  }

  return true;
}


/* =============================================================
   318. TICKET IMPORT API
   ============================================================= */

function apiValidateTicketImport(token, input) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  try {
    validateTicketImportObject(input);

    return {
      valid: true,
      message: "Ticket import data is valid."
    };

  } catch (error) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      message: error.message
    };
  }
}

  const ticketData = getPrintableTicket(ticketId);

  return {
    title: "PONT BASCULE LIVE — " +
      ticketData.ticket.number,

    paper: {
      widthMm: 100,
      heightMm: 150,
      orientation: "PORTRAIT"
    },

    content: ticketData,
    format: "PDF"
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   322. LIVE MONITORING CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================= */

var PBL8_CONFIG = {
  APP_NAME: "PONT BASCULE LIVE",
  REFRESH_INTERVAL_SECONDS: 10,
  MAX_ACTIVITY_RECORDS: 100,
  MAX_NOTIFICATIONS: 200,
  MAX_ALERTS: 100,
  TIMEZONE: "Africa/Casablanca",
  LOW_PROGRESS_THRESHOLD: 25,
  HIGH_PROGRESS_THRESHOLD: 100
};


/* =============================================================
   323. LIVE CLOCK DATA
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8GetClockData() {
  const now = new Date();

  return {
    date: Utilities.formatDate(
      now,
      PBL8_CONFIG.TIMEZONE,
      "dd/MM/yyyy"
    ),

    time: Utilities.formatDate(
      now,
      PBL8_CONFIG.TIMEZONE,
      "HH:mm:ss"
    ),

    dateTime: Utilities.formatDate(
      now,
      PBL8_CONFIG.TIMEZONE,
      "dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm:ss"
    ),

    iso: now.toISOString(),

    timezone: PBL8_CONFIG.TIMEZONE,

    timestamp: now.getTime()
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   324. LIVE CLOCK API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl8GetLiveClock() {
  return pbl8GetClockData();
}


/* =============================================================
   325. DASHBOARD SAFE DATA
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8SafeNumber(value) {
  const number = Number(value);

  return Number.isFinite(number) ? number : 0;
}


/* =============================================================
   326. MAIN LIVE DASHBOARD
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8GetLiveDashboard() {
  const dashboard = getMainDashboardData();

  const clock = pbl8GetClockData();

  return {
    application: PBL8_CONFIG.APP_NAME,

    live: true,

    date: clock.date,
    time: clock.time,
    dateTime: clock.dateTime,
    timezone: clock.timezone,

    targetQuantity: pbl8SafeNumber(
      dashboard.targetQuantity
    ),

    cumulTonnes: pbl8SafeNumber(
      dashboard.cumulTonnes
    ),

    numberOfVoyages: pbl8SafeNumber(
      dashboard.numberOfVoyages
    ),

    remainingTonnes: pbl8SafeNumber(
      dashboard.remainingTonnes
    ),

    deliveryPercentage: pbl8SafeNumber(
      dashboard.deliveryPercentage
    ),

    progressBarPercentage: pbl8SafeNumber(
      dashboard.progressBarPercentage
    ),

    overdeliveryTonnes: pbl8SafeNumber(
      dashboard.overdeliveryTonnes
    ),

    objectiveName: dashboard.objectiveName || "",

    objectiveStatus: dashboard.objectiveStatus || "",

    targetReached: dashboard.targetReached === true,

    message: dashboard.message || "",

    refreshIntervalSeconds:
      PBL8_CONFIG.REFRESH_INTERVAL_SECONDS
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   330. ACTIVITY COLLECTION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8GetActivityCollection() {
  const records = getCollectionSafely(
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM
  );

  if (!Array.isArray(records)) {
    return [];
  }

  return records.filter(function(record) {
    return record &&
      record.recordType === "PBL_ACTIVITY";
  });
}


/* =============================================================
   331. SAVE ACTIVITY COLLECTION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8SaveActivityCollection(activity) {
  const records = getCollectionSafely(
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM
  );

  const other = Array.isArray(records)
    ? records.filter(function(record) {
        return !record ||
          record.recordType !== "PBL_ACTIVITY";
      })
    : [];

  saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM,
    other.concat(activity)
  );
}


/* =============================================================
   332. RECORD ACTIVITY
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8RecordActivity(type, title, description, severity) {
  const activity = pbl8GetActivityCollection();

  const record = {
    id: pbl5CreateId("ACTIVITY"),

    recordType: "PBL_ACTIVITY",

    type: cleanText(type, 80),

    title: cleanText(title, 200),

    description: cleanText(description, 1000),

    severity: cleanText(severity || "INFO", 20),

    date: getLiveDate(),

    time: getLiveTime(),

    timestamp: Date.now(),

    createdAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };

  activity.push(record);

  while (
    activity.length > PBL8_CONFIG.MAX_ACTIVITY_RECORDS
  ) {
    activity.shift();
  }

  pbl8SaveActivityCollection(activity);

  return record;
}


/* =============================================================
   333. ACTIVITY TIMELINE
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8GetActivityTimeline(limit) {
  const maximum = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(
      Number(limit) || 30,
      PBL8_CONFIG.MAX_ACTIVITY_RECORDS
    )
  );

  return pbl8GetActivityCollection()
    .sort(function(a, b) {
      return Number(b.timestamp || 0) -
        Number(a.timestamp || 0);
    })
    .slice(0, maximum);
}


/* =============================================================
   334. ACTIVITY TIMELINE API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl8GetActivityTimeline(limit) {
  return pbl8GetActivityTimeline(limit);
}


/* =============================================================
   335. NOTIFICATION COLLECTION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8GetNotificationCollection() {
  const records = getCollectionSafely(
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM
  );

  if (!Array.isArray(records)) {
    return [];
  }

  return records.filter(function(record) {
    return record &&
      record.recordType === "PBL_NOTIFICATION";
  });
}


/* =============================================================
   336. SAVE NOTIFICATIONS
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8SaveNotificationCollection(notifications) {
  const records = getCollectionSafely(
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM
  );

  const other = Array.isArray(records)
    ? records.filter(function(record) {
        return !record ||
          record.recordType !== "PBL_NOTIFICATION";
      })
    : [];

  saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM,
    other.concat(notifications)
  );
}


/* =============================================================
   337. CREATE NOTIFICATION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8CreateNotification(
  title,
  message,
  type,
  reference
) {
  const notifications = pbl8GetNotificationCollection();

  const notification = {
    id: pbl5CreateId("NOTIFY"),

    recordType: "PBL_NOTIFICATION",

    title: cleanText(title, 200),

    message: cleanText(message, 1000),

    type: cleanText(type || "INFO", 30),

    reference: cleanText(reference, 150),

    read: false,

    createdAt: getLiveTimestamp(),

    timestamp: Date.now()
  };

  notifications.push(notification);

  while (
    notifications.length > PBL8_CONFIG.MAX_NOTIFICATIONS
  ) {
    notifications.shift();
  }

  pbl8SaveNotificationCollection(notifications);

  return notification;
}


/* =============================================================
   338. GET NOTIFICATIONS
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8GetNotifications(includeRead, limit) {
  const maximum = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(Number(limit) || 50, 200)
  );

  return pbl8GetNotificationCollection()
    .filter(function(notification) {
      return includeRead === true ||
        notification.read !== true;
    })
    .sort(function(a, b) {
      return Number(b.timestamp || 0) -
        Number(a.timestamp || 0);
    })
    .slice(0, maximum);
}


/* =============================================================
   339. NOTIFICATIONS API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl8GetNotifications(includeRead, limit) {
  return pbl8GetNotifications(includeRead, limit);
}


/* =============================================================
   340. MARK NOTIFICATION AS READ
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8MarkNotificationRead(token, notificationId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const notifications = pbl8GetNotificationCollection();

  const notification = notifications.find(function(item) {
    return String(item.id) === String(notificationId);
  });

  if (!notification) {
    throw new Error("Notification not found.");
  }

  notification.read = true;
  notification.readAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl8SaveNotificationCollection(notifications);

  return {
    success: true,
    notification: notification
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   341. MARK ALL NOTIFICATIONS READ
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8MarkAllNotificationsRead(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const notifications = pbl8GetNotificationCollection();

  notifications.forEach(function(item) {
    item.read = true;
    item.readAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  });

  pbl8SaveNotificationCollection(notifications);

  return {
    success: true,
    count: notifications.length
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   342. NOTIFICATION API — SINGLE
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl8MarkNotificationRead(token, id) {
  return pbl8MarkNotificationRead(token, id);
}


/* =============================================================
   343. NOTIFICATION API — ALL
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl8MarkAllNotificationsRead(token) {
  return pbl8MarkAllNotificationsRead(token);
}


/* =============================================================
   344. UNREAD NOTIFICATION COUNT
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8GetUnreadNotificationCount() {
  return pbl8GetNotificationCollection()
    .filter(function(item) {
      return item.read !== true;
    }).length;
}

/* =============================================================

  const records = getCollectionSafely(
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM
  );

  const other = Array.isArray(records)
    ? records.filter(function(record) {
        return !record ||
          record.recordType !== "PBL_ALERT";

/* =============================================================
   347. CREATE ALERT
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8CreateAlert(code, title, message, severity) {
  const alerts = pbl8GetAlertCollection();

  const alert = {
    id: pbl5CreateId("ALERT"),

    recordType: "PBL_ALERT",

    code: cleanText(code, 80),

    title: cleanText(title, 200),

    message: cleanText(message, 1000),

    severity: cleanText(severity || "WARNING", 20),

    active: true,

    createdAt: getLiveTimestamp(),

    timestamp: Date.now()
  };

  alerts.push(alert);

  while (alerts.length > PBL8_CONFIG.MAX_ALERTS) {
    alerts.shift();
  }

  pbl8SaveAlertCollection(alerts);

  return alert;
}


/* =============================================================
   348. GET ACTIVE ALERTS
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8GetActiveAlerts() {
  return pbl8GetAlertCollection()
    .filter(function(alert) {
      return alert.active === true;
    })
    .sort(function(a, b) {
      return Number(b.timestamp || 0) -
        Number(a.timestamp || 0);
    });
}


/* =============================================================
   349. CLOSE ALERT
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8CloseAlert(token, alertId) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const alerts = pbl8GetAlertCollection();

  const alert = alerts.find(function(item) {
    return String(item.id) === String(alertId);
  });

  if (!alert) {
    throw new Error("Alert not found.");
  }

  alert.active = false;
  alert.closedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

  pbl8SaveAlertCollection(alerts);

  writeAuditLog("ALERT_CLOSED", alert.title);

  return { success: true };
}


/* =============================================================
   350. ALERT API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl8GetActiveAlerts() {
  return pbl8GetActiveAlerts();
}

function apiPbl8CloseAlert(token, alertId) {
  return pbl8CloseAlert(token, alertId);
}


/* =============================================================
   351. DELIVERY TARGET ALERT CHECK
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8CheckDeliveryTarget() {
  const dashboard = getMainDashboardData();

  const percentage = pbl8SafeNumber(
    dashboard.deliveryPercentage
  );

  if (dashboard.targetReached === true) {
    return {
      alert: true,
      code: "TARGET_REACHED",
      severity: "SUCCESS",
      message: "Delivery target has been reached."
    };
  }

  if (
    percentage > 0 &&
    percentage < PBL8_CONFIG.LOW_PROGRESS_THRESHOLD
  ) {
    return {
      alert: true,
      code: "LOW_DELIVERY_PROGRESS",
      severity: "WARNING",
      message: "Delivery progress is below 25%."
    };
  }

  return {
    alert: false,
    code: "NORMAL",
    severity: "INFO",
    message: "Delivery progress is normal."
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   352. OPERATIONAL ALERT CHECK
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8CheckOperationalAlerts() {
  const result = [];

  const workflow = getWeighingWorkflowSummary();

  const target = pbl8CheckDeliveryTarget();

  if (target.alert) {
    result.push(target);
  }

  if (workflow.waitingSecondWeight > 10) {
    result.push({
      alert: true,
      code: "MANY_OPEN_WEIGHINGS",
      severity: "WARNING",
      message: "More than 10 sessions are waiting for a second weight."
    });
  }

  if (workflow.readyToValidate > 10) {
    result.push({
      alert: true,
      code: "MANY_PENDING_TICKETS",
      severity: "WARNING",
      message: "More than 10 tickets are waiting for validation."
    });
  }

  return result;
}

  return {

    status: "ONLINE",

    version: PBL.VERSION || "1.0",

    date: clock.date,

    time: clock.time,

    timezone: clock.timezone,

    refreshIntervalSeconds:
      PBL8_CONFIG.REFRESH_INTERVAL_SECONDS,

    notificationsUnread:
      pbl8GetUnreadNotificationCount(),

    activeAlerts:
      pbl8GetActiveAlerts().length,

    checkedAt: clock.dateTime
  };
}

    todayActivities: todaysActivity.length,

    latestActivity: activity.length
      ? activity[activity.length - 1]
      : null,

  };
}

/* =============================================================
   365. REGISTER TICKET ACTIVITY
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8RegisterTicketActivity(ticket) {
  if (!ticket) {
    return false;
  }

  pbl8RecordActivity(
    "TICKET_CREATED",
    "New weighing ticket",
    "Ticket " + ticket.ticketNumber +
      " created for " + ticket.client,
    "SUCCESS"
  );

  pbl8CreateNotification(
    "New ticket created",
    "Ticket " + ticket.ticketNumber +
      " — " + ticket.netTonnes + " tonnes",
    "SUCCESS",
    ticket.id
  );

  return true;
}


/* =============================================================
   366. REGISTER CANCELLED TICKET ACTIVITY
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8RegisterCancelledTicket(ticketNumber, reason) {
  pbl8RecordActivity(
    "TICKET_CANCELLED",
    "Ticket cancelled",
    String(ticketNumber) + " — " + String(reason || ""),
    "WARNING"
  );

  pbl8CreateNotification(
    "Ticket cancelled",
    "Ticket " + ticketNumber + " has been cancelled.",
    "WARNING",
    ticketNumber
  );

  return true;
}


/* =============================================================
   367. REGISTER WEIGHING ACTIVITY
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8RegisterWeighingActivity(session, eventName) {
  if (!session) {
    return false;
  }

  pbl8RecordActivity(
    eventName || "WEIGHING_ACTIVITY",
    "Weighing operation",
    "Session " + session.id +
      " / " + session.status,
    "INFO"
  );

  return true;
}


/* =============================================================
   368. DAILY ACTIVITY COUNTS
   ============================================================= */

function pbl8GetDailyActivityCounts() {
  const activities = pbl8GetActivityCollection();

  const result = {};

  activities.forEach(function(item) {
    const date = item.date || "UNKNOWN";

    if (!result[date]) {
      result[date] = {
        date: date,
        count: 0
      };
    }

    result[date].count++;
  });

  return Object.keys(result).sort().reverse().map(
    function(key) {
      return result[key];
    }
  );
}


/* =============================================================
   369. DAILY ACTIVITY API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl8GetDailyActivityCounts() {
  return pbl8GetDailyActivityCounts();
}

/* =============================================================
   373. DATABASE COLLECTION INVENTORY
   ============================================================= */

function pbl9GetCollectionInventory() {
  const keys = [
    PBL_KEYS.TICKETS,
    PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS,
    PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS,
    PBL_KEYS.TRUCKS,
    PBL_KEYS.DRIVERS,
    PBL_KEYS.OBJECTIVES,
    PBL_KEYS.AUDIT,
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM,
    "PBL_WEIGHING_SESSIONS",
    "PBL_PRINT_HISTORY"
  ];

  return keys.map(function(key) {
    const data = getCollectionSafely(key);

    return {
      key: key,
      exists: Array.isArray(data),
      recordCount: Array.isArray(data)
        ? data.length
        : 0
    };
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   375. VALIDATE TICKET DATASET
   ============================================================= */

function pbl9ValidateTicketDataset() {
  const tickets = getAllTickets();

  const errors = [];
  const warnings = [];

  let validCount = 0;

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket, index) {
    if (!ticket || typeof ticket !== "object") {
      errors.push({
        index: index,
        issue: "INVALID_RECORD"
      });
      return;
    }

    if (!ticket.ticketNumber) {
      errors.push({
        index: index,
        ticketId: ticket.id || "",
        issue: "MISSING_TICKET_NUMBER"
      });
    }

    if (!ticket.date) {
      warnings.push({
        index: index,
        ticketId: ticket.id || "",
        issue: "MISSING_DATE"
      });
    }

    const gross = Number(ticket.grossKg);
    const tare = Number(ticket.tareKg);
    const net = Number(ticket.netKg);

    if (
      !Number.isFinite(gross) ||
      !Number.isFinite(tare) ||
      !Number.isFinite(net)
    ) {
      errors.push({
        index: index,
        ticketId: ticket.id || "",
        issue: "INVALID_WEIGHT"
      });

      return;
    }

    if (gross - tare !== net) {
      errors.push({
        index: index,
        ticketId: ticket.id || "",
        issue: "WEIGHT_CALCULATION_MISMATCH"
      });
    } else {
      validCount++;
    }

    if (gross < tare) {
      errors.push({
        index: index,
        ticketId: ticket.id || "",
        issue: "GROSS_LOWER_THAN_TARE"
      });
    }
  });

  return {
    total: tickets.length,
    valid: validCount,
    errors: errors,
    warnings: warnings,
    validDataset: errors.length === 0,
    checkedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   376. TICKET DATASET VALIDATION API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl9ValidateTicketDataset(token) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl9ValidateTicketDataset();
}


/* =============================================================
   377. DUPLICATE TICKET NUMBER DETECTION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl9FindDuplicateTicketNumbers() {
  const tickets = getAllTickets();

  const map = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    if (!ticket || !ticket.ticketNumber) {
      return;
    }

    const number = String(ticket.ticketNumber).trim();

    if (!map[number]) {
      map[number] = [];
    }

    map[number].push({
      id: ticket.id,
      date: ticket.date,
      client: ticket.client,
      product: ticket.product
    });
  });

  return Object.keys(map)
    .filter(function(number) {
      return map[number].length > 1;
    })
    .map(function(number) {
      return {
        ticketNumber: number,
        occurrences: map[number].length,
        records: map[number]
      };
    });
}


/* =============================================================
   378. DUPLICATE DETECTION API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl9FindDuplicateTicketNumbers(token) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl9FindDuplicateTicketNumbers();
}


/* =============================================================
   379. PRODUCT DATA VALIDATION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl9ValidateProducts() {
  const products = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS);

  const errors = [];

  products.forEach(function(product, index) {
    if (!product || typeof product !== "object") {
      errors.push({
        index: index,
        issue: "INVALID_PRODUCT"
      });

      return;
    }

    if (!product.name) {
      errors.push({
        index: index,
        id: product.id || "",
        issue: "MISSING_PRODUCT_NAME"
      });
    }
  });

  return {
    total: products.length,
    errors: errors,
    valid: errors.length === 0
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   380. CLIENT DATA VALIDATION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl9ValidateClients() {
  const clients = pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.CLIENTS);

  const errors = [];

  clients.forEach(function(client, index) {
    if (!client || typeof client !== "object") {
      errors.push({
        index: index,
        issue: "INVALID_CLIENT"
      });

      return;
    }

    if (!client.name) {
      errors.push({
        index: index,
        id: client.id || "",
        issue: "MISSING_CLIENT_NAME"
      });
    }
  });

  return {
    total: clients.length,
    errors: errors,
    valid: errors.length === 0
  };
}

/* =============================================================
   383. NORMALIZE TICKET WEIGHTS
   ============================================================= */

function pbl9NormalizeTicketWeights(ticket) {
  const gross = Math.round(Number(ticket.grossKg));
  const tare = Math.round(Number(ticket.tareKg));
  const net = Math.round(gross - tare);

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(gross) ||
    !Number.isFinite(tare) ||
    gross < 0 ||
    tare < 0 ||
    gross < tare
  ) {
    throw new Error("Invalid gross or tare weight.");
  }

  return {
    grossKg: gross,
    tareKg: tare,
    netKg: net,
    netTonnes: roundNumber(net / 1000, 3)
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   384. PREVIEW TICKET WEIGHT REPAIR
   ============================================================= */

function pbl9PreviewWeightRepair() {
  const tickets = getAllTickets();

  const changes = [];

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    if (!ticket || !ticket.id) {
      return;
    }

    const gross = Number(ticket.grossKg);
    const tare = Number(ticket.tareKg);
    const net = Number(ticket.netKg);

    if (
      Number.isFinite(gross) &&
      Number.isFinite(tare) &&
      Number.isFinite(net) &&
      gross >= tare &&
      Math.round(gross - tare) !== Math.round(net)
    ) {
      changes.push({
        ticketId: ticket.id,
        ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber,
        oldNetKg: net,
        proposedNetKg: Math.round(gross - tare)
      });
    }
  });

  return {
    changesFound: changes.length,
    changes: changes,
    previewOnly: true
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   385. REPAIR TICKET WEIGHTS
   ============================================================= */

function pbl9RepairTicketWeights(token, confirmation) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  if (confirmation !== "REPAIR_WEIGHTS") {
    throw new Error("Explicit repair confirmation required.");
  }

  const tickets = getAllTickets();

  const repaired = [];

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    if (!ticket || !ticket.id) {
      return;
    }

    const gross = Number(ticket.grossKg);
    const tare = Number(ticket.tareKg);

    if (
      !Number.isFinite(gross) ||
      !Number.isFinite(tare) ||
      gross < tare
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const net = Math.round(gross - tare);

    if (Number(ticket.netKg) !== net) {
      ticket.netKg = net;
      ticket.netTonnes = roundNumber(net / 1000, 3);

      ticket.repairedAt = getLiveTimestamp();

      repaired.push(ticket.id);
    }
  });

  if (repaired.length) {
    saveCollection(PBL_KEYS.TICKETS, tickets);

    writeAuditLog(
      "TICKET_WEIGHT_REPAIR",
      "Repaired " + repaired.length + " ticket records"
    );
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    repairedCount: repaired.length,
    repairedIds: repaired
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   386. REPAIR API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl9RepairTicketWeights(token, confirmation) {
  return pbl9RepairTicketWeights(token, confirmation);
}

/* =============================================================
   391. MAINTENANCE LOG COLLECTION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl9GetMaintenanceLogs() {
  const records = getCollectionSafely(PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM);

  if (!Array.isArray(records)) {
    return [];
  }

  return records.filter(function(record) {
    return record &&
      record.recordType === "PBL_MAINTENANCE";
  });
}


/* =============================================================
   392. SAVE MAINTENANCE LOGS
   ============================================================= */

function pbl9SaveMaintenanceLogs(logs) {
  const records = getCollectionSafely(PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM);

  const other = Array.isArray(records)
    ? records.filter(function(record) {
        return !record ||
          record.recordType !== "PBL_MAINTENANCE";
      })
    : [];

  saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM,
    other.concat(logs)
  );
}


/* =============================================================
   393. WRITE MAINTENANCE LOG
   ============================================================= */

function pbl9WriteMaintenanceLog(action, result, details) {
  const logs = pbl9GetMaintenanceLogs();

  logs.push({
    id: pbl5CreateId("MAINTENANCE"),

    recordType: "PBL_MAINTENANCE",

    action: cleanText(action, 150),

    result: cleanText(result, 50),

    details: cleanText(details, 1000),

    timestamp: Date.now(),

    dateTime: getLiveTimestamp()
  });

  while (
    logs.length > PBL9_CONFIG.MAINTENANCE_LOG_LIMIT
  ) {
    logs.shift();
  }

  pbl9SaveMaintenanceLogs(logs);
}


/* =============================================================
   394. GET MAINTENANCE HISTORY
   ============================================================= */

function pbl9GetMaintenanceHistory(limit) {
  return pbl9GetMaintenanceLogs()
    .sort(function(a, b) {
      return Number(b.timestamp || 0) -
        Number(a.timestamp || 0);
    })
    .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit) || 30));
}


/* =============================================================
   395. MAINTENANCE HISTORY API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl9GetMaintenanceHistory(token, limit) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl9GetMaintenanceHistory(limit);
}

  );

}

/* =============================================================
   406. CHECK MISSING TICKET FIELDS
   ============================================================= */

function pbl9FindIncompleteTickets() {
  const tickets = getAllTickets();

  const required = [
    "ticketNumber",
    "date",
    "product",
    "client",
    "truckRegistration",
    "grossKg",
    "tareKg",
    "netKg"
  ];

  return tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
    return required.some(function(field) {
      return ticket[field] === undefined ||
        ticket[field] === null ||
        ticket[field] === "";
    });
  }).map(function(ticket) {
    return {
      id: ticket.id,
      ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber || "",
      missingFields: required.filter(function(field) {
        return ticket[field] === undefined ||
          ticket[field] === null ||
          ticket[field] === "";
      })
    };
  });
}

   407. INCOMPLETE TICKETS API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl9FindIncompleteTickets(token) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl9FindIncompleteTickets();
}

/* =============================================================
   411. ANALYTICS CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================= */

var PBL10_CONFIG = {
  MAX_REPORT_ROWS: 5000,
  DEFAULT_TOP_LIMIT: 10,
  MAX_TOP_LIMIT: 100,
  WEIGHT_PRECISION: 3
};


/* =============================================================
   412. ACTIVE TICKET HELPER
   ============================================================= */

function pbl10GetActiveTickets() {
  return getAllTickets().filter(function(ticket) {
    return ticket &&
      ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS &&
      ticket.status !== "CANCELLED" &&
      ticket.status !== "DELETED";
  });
}


/* =============================================================
   413. NORMALIZE TEXT FOR GROUPING
   ============================================================= */

function pbl10NormalizeGroup(value) {
  const textValue = String(value || "").trim();

  return textValue || "NON RENSEIGNÉ";
}


/* =============================================================
   414. NORMALIZE WEIGHT TO TONNES
   ============================================================= */

function pbl10GetTonnes(ticket) {
  const kg = Number(ticket.netKg);

  return Number.isFinite(kg)
    ? kg / 1000
    : 0;
}


/* =============================================================
   415. GENERIC GROUP AGGREGATION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl10AggregateTickets(tickets, field) {
  const groups = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const name = pbl10NormalizeGroup(ticket[field]);

    if (!groups[name]) {
      groups[name] = {
        name: name,
        voyages: 0,
        totalKg: 0,
        totalTonnes: 0,
        averageKg: 0
      };
    }

    groups[name].voyages++;

    groups[name].totalKg += Number(ticket.netKg) || 0;

    groups[name].totalTonnes += pbl10GetTonnes(ticket);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).map(function(key) {
    const item = groups[key];

    item.totalKg = roundNumber(item.totalKg, 0);

    item.totalTonnes = roundNumber(item.totalTonnes, 3);

    item.averageKg = item.voyages
      ? roundNumber(item.totalKg / item.voyages, 0)
      : 0;

    return item;
  });
}


/* =============================================================
   416. SORT AGGREGATION BY TONNES
   ============================================================= */

function pbl10SortByTonnes(items) {
  return items.sort(function(a, b) {
    return b.totalTonnes - a.totalTonnes;
  });
}


/* =============================================================
   417. TOP LIMIT VALIDATION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl10GetLimit(value) {
  return Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(
      Number(value) || PBL10_CONFIG.DEFAULT_TOP_LIMIT,
      PBL10_CONFIG.MAX_TOP_LIMIT
    )
  );
}


/* =============================================================
   418. TRUCK PERFORMANCE
   ============================================================= */

function pbl10GetTruckPerformance() {
  const tickets = pbl10GetActiveTickets();

  const grouped = pbl10AggregateTickets(
    tickets,
    "truckRegistration"
  );

  return pbl10SortByTonnes(grouped);
}

   419. TRUCK PERFORMANCE API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl10GetTruckPerformance(token, limit) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl10GetTruckPerformance()
    .slice(0, pbl10GetLimit(limit));
}

  );

/* =============================================================
   426. DAILY PRODUCTIVITY
   ============================================================= */

function pbl10GetDailyProductivity() {
  const tickets = pbl10GetActiveTickets();

  const groups = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const date = pbl10NormalizeGroup(ticket.date);

    if (!groups[date]) {
      groups[date] = {
        date: date,
        voyages: 0,
        totalKg: 0,
        totalTonnes: 0
      };
    }

    groups[date].voyages++;

    groups[date].totalKg += Number(ticket.netKg) || 0;

    groups[date].totalTonnes += pbl10GetTonnes(ticket);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).map(function(key) {
    const item = groups[key];

    item.totalKg = roundNumber(item.totalKg, 0);

    item.totalTonnes = roundNumber(item.totalTonnes, 3);

    return item;
  }).sort(function(a, b) {
    return parseDisplayDate(a.date) -
      parseDisplayDate(b.date);
  });
}


/* =============================================================
   427. DAILY PRODUCTIVITY API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl10GetDailyProductivity(token) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl10GetDailyProductivity();
}


/* =============================================================
   428. HOURLY PRODUCTIVITY
   ============================================================= */

function pbl10GetHourlyProductivity() {
  const tickets = pbl10GetActiveTickets();

  const groups = {};

  for (let hour = 0; hour < 24; hour++) {
    const key = String(hour).padStart(2, "0");

    groups[key] = {
      hour: key,
      voyages: 0,
      totalKg: 0,
      totalTonnes: 0
    };
  }

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const time = String(ticket.time || "");

    const hour = time.substring(0, 2);

    if (!groups[hour]) {
      return;
    }

    groups[hour].voyages++;

    groups[hour].totalKg += Number(ticket.netKg) || 0;

    groups[hour].totalTonnes += pbl10GetTonnes(ticket);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).map(function(key) {
    const item = groups[key];

    item.totalKg = roundNumber(item.totalKg, 0);

    item.totalTonnes = roundNumber(item.totalTonnes, 3);

    return item;
  });
}


/* =============================================================
   429. HOURLY PRODUCTIVITY API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl10GetHourlyProductivity(token) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl10GetHourlyProductivity();
}


/* =============================================================
   430. AVERAGE VOYAGE WEIGHT
   ============================================================= */

function pbl10GetAverageVoyageWeight() {
  const tickets = pbl10GetActiveTickets();

  if (!tickets.length) {
    return {
      averageKg: 0,
      averageTonnes: 0,
      voyages: 0
    };
  }

  const totalKg = tickets.reduce(function(total, ticket) {
    return total + (Number(ticket.netKg) || 0);
  }, 0);

  return {
    averageKg: roundNumber(totalKg / tickets.length, 0),

    averageTonnes: roundNumber(
      totalKg / tickets.length / 1000,
      3
    ),

    voyages: tickets.length
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   431. AVERAGE WEIGHT API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl10GetAverageVoyageWeight(token) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl10GetAverageVoyageWeight();
}


/* =============================================================
   432. DATE RANGE ANALYTICS
   ============================================================= */

function pbl10GetDateRangeAnalytics(startDate, endDate) {
  const range = validateDateRange(startDate, endDate);

  if (!range.valid) {
    throw new Error(range.message || "Invalid date range.");
  }

  const filtered = filterTicketsAdvanced({
    startDate: startDate,
    endDate: endDate
  });

  const tickets = filtered.filter(function(ticket) {
    return ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS &&
      ticket.status !== "CANCELLED";
  });

  const totalKg = tickets.reduce(function(total, ticket) {
    return total + (Number(ticket.netKg) || 0);
  }, 0);

  return {
    startDate: startDate,
    endDate: endDate,

    voyages: tickets.length,

    totalKg: roundNumber(totalKg, 0),

    totalTonnes: roundNumber(totalKg / 1000, 3),

    averageKg: tickets.length
      ? roundNumber(totalKg / tickets.length, 0)
      : 0,

    products: pbl10SortByTonnes(
      pbl10AggregateTickets(tickets, "product")
    ),

    clients: pbl10SortByTonnes(
      pbl10AggregateTickets(tickets, "client")
    ),

    trucks: pbl10SortByTonnes(
      pbl10AggregateTickets(tickets, "truckRegistration")
    )
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   433. DATE RANGE ANALYTICS API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl10GetDateRangeAnalytics(
  token,
  startDate,
  endDate
) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl10GetDateRangeAnalytics(
    startDate,
    endDate
  );
}


/* =============================================================
   434. PRODUCT CLIENT MATRIX
   ============================================================= */

function pbl10GetProductClientMatrix() {
  const tickets = pbl10GetActiveTickets();

  const matrix = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const product = pbl10NormalizeGroup(ticket.product);

    const client = pbl10NormalizeGroup(ticket.client);

    if (!matrix[product]) {
      matrix[product] = {};
    }

    if (!matrix[product][client]) {
      matrix[product][client] = {
        product: product,
        client: client,
        voyages: 0,
        totalKg: 0,
        totalTonnes: 0
      };
    }

    matrix[product][client].voyages++;

    matrix[product][client].totalKg +=
      Number(ticket.netKg) || 0;

    matrix[product][client].totalTonnes +=
      pbl10GetTonnes(ticket);
  });

  const result = [];

  Object.keys(matrix).forEach(function(product) {
    Object.keys(matrix[product]).forEach(function(client) {
      const item = matrix[product][client];

      item.totalKg = roundNumber(item.totalKg, 0);

      item.totalTonnes = roundNumber(
        item.totalTonnes,
        3
      );

      result.push(item);
    });
  });

  return result;
}


/* =============================================================
   435. PRODUCT CLIENT MATRIX API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl10GetProductClientMatrix(token) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl10GetProductClientMatrix();
}

    function(ticket) {
      return String(ticket.truckRegistration || "")
        .toLowerCase() === String(registration || "")
        .toLowerCase();
    }
  );

  const groups = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const date = pbl10NormalizeGroup(ticket.date);

    if (!groups[date]) {
      groups[date] = {
        date: date,
        voyages: 0,
        totalKg: 0,
        totalTonnes: 0
      };
    }

    groups[date].voyages++;

    groups[date].totalKg += Number(ticket.netKg) || 0;

    groups[date].totalTonnes += pbl10GetTonnes(ticket);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).map(function(key) {
    const item = groups[key];

    item.totalKg = roundNumber(item.totalKg, 0);

    item.totalTonnes = roundNumber(item.totalTonnes, 3);

    return item;
  });
}

/* =============================================================
   440. DELIVERY PERFORMANCE SCORE
   ============================================================= */

function pbl10GetDeliveryPerformanceScore() {
  const dashboard = getMainDashboardData();

  const percentage = Math.max(
    0,
    Number(dashboard.deliveryPercentage) || 0
  );

  let classification = "NOT STARTED";

  if (percentage >= 100) {
    classification = "TARGET REACHED";
  } else if (percentage >= 75) {
    classification = "HIGH PROGRESS";
  } else if (percentage >= 50) {
    classification = "GOOD PROGRESS";
  } else if (percentage > 0) {
    classification = "IN PROGRESS";
  }

  return {
    targetQuantity: dashboard.targetQuantity,

    cumulTonnes: dashboard.cumulTonnes,

    numberOfVoyages: dashboard.numberOfVoyages,

    remainingTonnes: dashboard.remainingTonnes,

    deliveryPercentage: percentage,

    classification: classification
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   441. DELIVERY PERFORMANCE API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl10GetDeliveryPerformanceScore() {
  return pbl10GetDeliveryPerformanceScore();
}


/* =============================================================
   442. VOYAGE DISTRIBUTION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl10GetVoyageWeightDistribution() {
  const tickets = pbl10GetActiveTickets();

  const ranges = [
    { label: "0–5 T", min: 0, max: 5000 },
    { label: "5–10 T", min: 5000, max: 10000 },
    { label: "10–15 T", min: 10000, max: 15000 },
    { label: "15–20 T", min: 15000, max: 20000 },
    { label: "20–25 T", min: 20000, max: 25000 },
    { label: "25 T+", min: 25000, max: Infinity }
  ];

  return ranges.map(function(range) {
    const matching = tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
      const weight = Number(ticket.netKg) || 0;

      return weight >= range.min &&
        weight < range.max;
    });

    return {
      range: range.label,
      voyages: matching.length,

      totalTonnes: roundNumber(
        matching.reduce(function(total, ticket) {
          return total + pbl10GetTonnes(ticket);
        }, 0),
        3
      )
    };
  });
}


/* =============================================================
   443. VOYAGE DISTRIBUTION API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl10GetVoyageWeightDistribution(token) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl10GetVoyageWeightDistribution();
}


/* =============================================================
   444. MONTHLY PERFORMANCE COMPARISON
   ============================================================= */

function pbl10GetMonthlyPerformanceComparison(year) {
  const selectedYear = Number(year) || new Date().getFullYear();

  const tickets = pbl10GetActiveTickets();

  const months = [];

  for (let month = 1; month <= 12; month++) {
    const key = String(month).padStart(2, "0");

    const matching = tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
      const parsed = parseDisplayDate(ticket.date);

      return parsed &&
        parsed.getFullYear() === selectedYear &&
        parsed.getMonth() + 1 === month;
    });

    const totalKg = matching.reduce(function(total, ticket) {
      return total + (Number(ticket.netKg) || 0);
    }, 0);

    months.push({
      month: key,

      year: selectedYear,

      voyages: matching.length,

      totalKg: roundNumber(totalKg, 0),

      totalTonnes: roundNumber(totalKg / 1000, 3)
    });
  }

  return months;
}


/* =============================================================
   445. MONTHLY COMPARISON API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl10GetMonthlyPerformanceComparison(
  token,
  year
) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl10GetMonthlyPerformanceComparison(year);
}

/* =============================================================
   455. EXPORT CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================= */

var PBL11_CONFIG = {
  APP_NAME: "PONT BASCULE LIVE",
  DEFAULT_ENCODING: "UTF-8",
  MAX_EXPORT_ROWS: 10000,
  MAX_REPORT_HISTORY: 100,
  CSV_SEPARATOR: ";"
};


/* =============================================================
   456. SAFE EXPORT VALUE
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11SafeValue(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) {
    return "";
  }

  if (value instanceof Date) {
    return Utilities.formatDate(
      value,
      "Africa/Casablanca",
      "dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm:ss"
    );
  }

  return String(value);
}


/* =============================================================
   457. CSV FIELD ESCAPE
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11EscapeCsv(value) {
  let textValue = pbl11SafeValue(value);

  if (/^[=+\-@]/.test(textValue)) {
    textValue = "'" + textValue;
  }

  return '"' + textValue.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
}


/* =============================================================
   458. CSV GENERATOR
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11GenerateCsv(headers, rows) {
  const separator = PBL11_CONFIG.CSV_SEPARATOR;

  const output = [];

  output.push(
    headers.map(pbl11EscapeCsv).join(separator)
  );

  rows.forEach(function(row) {
    output.push(
      headers.map(function(header) {
        return pbl11EscapeCsv(row[header]);
      }).join(separator)
    );
  });

  return "\uFEFF" + output.join("\r\n");
}


/* =============================================================
   459. STANDARD TICKET EXPORT FIELDS
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11GetTicketExportFields() {
  return [
    "ticketNumber",
    "date",
    "time",
    "product",
    "client",
    "truckRegistration",
    "driver",
    "grossKg",
    "tareKg",
    "netKg",
    "netTonnes",
    "status"
  ];
}


/* =============================================================
   460. TICKET EXPORT ROW NORMALIZER
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11NormalizeTicketExport(ticket) {
  return {
    ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber || "",

    date: ticket.date || "",

    time: ticket.time || "",

    product: ticket.product || "",

    client: ticket.client || "",

    truckRegistration: ticket.truckRegistration || "",

    driver: ticket.driver || "",

    grossKg: Number(ticket.grossKg) || 0,

    tareKg: Number(ticket.tareKg) || 0,

    netKg: Number(ticket.netKg) || 0,

    netTonnes: roundNumber(
      (Number(ticket.netKg) || 0) / 1000,
      3
    ),

    status: ticket.status || ""
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   461. GET EXPORTABLE TICKETS
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11GetExportableTickets(filters) {
  filters = filters || {};

  const tickets = pbl10GetActiveTickets();

  let result = tickets;

  if (filters.startDate || filters.endDate) {
    result = filterTicketsAdvanced({
      startDate: filters.startDate || "",
      endDate: filters.endDate || ""
    }).filter(function(ticket) {
      return ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS &&
        ticket.status !== "CANCELLED";
    });
  }

  if (filters.product) {
    result = result.filter(function(ticket) {
      return String(ticket.product || "").toLowerCase()
        .includes(String(filters.product).toLowerCase());
    });
  }

  if (filters.client) {
    result = result.filter(function(ticket) {
      return String(ticket.client || "").toLowerCase()
        .includes(String(filters.client).toLowerCase());
    });
  }

  if (filters.truckRegistration) {
    result = result.filter(function(ticket) {
      return String(ticket.truckRegistration || "")
        .toLowerCase()
        .includes(String(filters.truckRegistration).toLowerCase());
    });
  }

  return result.slice(0, PBL11_CONFIG.MAX_EXPORT_ROWS);
}


/* =============================================================
   462. PREPARE TICKET EXPORT
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11PrepareTicketExport(filters) {
  const tickets = pbl11GetExportableTickets(filters);

  return {
    application: PBL11_CONFIG.APP_NAME,

    generatedAt: getLiveTimestamp(),

    count: tickets.length,

    headers: pbl11GetTicketExportFields(),

    rows: tickets.map(pbl11NormalizeTicketExport)
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   463. TICKET EXPORT API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl11PrepareTicketExport(token, filters) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl11PrepareTicketExport(filters);
}


/* =============================================================
   464. TICKET CSV EXPORT
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11ExportTicketsCsv(filters) {
  const data = pbl11PrepareTicketExport(filters);

  return {
    filename: "PONT_BASCULE_TICKETS_" +
      Utilities.formatDate(
        new Date(),
        "Africa/Casablanca",
        "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"
      ) + ".csv",

    mimeType: "text/csv;charset=utf-8",

    content: pbl11GenerateCsv(
      data.headers,
      data.rows
    ),

    count: data.count
  };
}

   465. TICKET CSV API
   ============================================================= */

  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

/* =============================================================
   470. GENERIC REPORT CSV
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11ExportGenericCsv(title, rows) {
  if (!Array.isArray(rows)) {
    throw new Error("Invalid report rows.");
  }

  if (!rows.length) {
    return {
      filename: "PBL_REPORT.csv",
      mimeType: "text/csv;charset=utf-8",
      content: "\uFEFF",
      count: 0
    };
  }

  const headers = Object.keys(rows[0]);

  return {
    filename: String(title || "PBL_REPORT")
      .replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "_") + ".csv",

    mimeType: "text/csv;charset=utf-8",

    content: pbl11GenerateCsv(headers, rows),

    count: rows.length
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   471. PRODUCT CSV API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl11ExportProductsCsv(token) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl11ExportGenericCsv(
    "PBL_PRODUCTS",
    pbl11PrepareProductReportExport()
  );
}

/* =============================================================
   482. REPORT HISTORY COLLECTION
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11GetReportHistoryCollection() {
  const records = getCollectionSafely(PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM);

  if (!Array.isArray(records)) {
    return [];
  }

  return records.filter(function(record) {
    return record &&
      record.recordType === "PBL_REPORT_HISTORY";
  });
}


/* =============================================================
   483. SAVE REPORT HISTORY
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11SaveReportHistoryCollection(history) {
  const records = getCollectionSafely(PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM);

  const other = Array.isArray(records)
    ? records.filter(function(record) {
        return !record ||
          record.recordType !== "PBL_REPORT_HISTORY";
      })
    : [];

  saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM,
    other.concat(history)
  );
}


/* =============================================================
   484. REGISTER REPORT
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11RegisterReport(type, description, count) {
  const history = pbl11GetReportHistoryCollection();

  const record = {
    id: pbl5CreateId("REPORT"),

    recordType: "PBL_REPORT_HISTORY",

    type: cleanText(type, 100),

    description: cleanText(description, 500),

    recordCount: Number(count) || 0,

    generatedAt: getLiveTimestamp(),

    timestamp: Date.now()
  };

  history.push(record);

  while (history.length > PBL11_CONFIG.MAX_REPORT_HISTORY) {
    history.shift();
  }

  pbl11SaveReportHistoryCollection(history);

  return record;
}


/* =============================================================
   485. GET REPORT HISTORY
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11GetReportHistory(limit) {
  return pbl11GetReportHistoryCollection()
    .sort(function(a, b) {
      return Number(b.timestamp || 0) -
        Number(a.timestamp || 0);
    })
    .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit) || 30));
}


/* =============================================================
   486. REPORT HISTORY API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl11GetReportHistory(token, limit) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl11GetReportHistory(limit);
}

/* =============================================================
   489. EXPORT SUMMARY
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11GetExportSummary() {
  const tickets = pbl10GetActiveTickets();

  const totalKg = tickets.reduce(function(total, ticket) {
    return total + (Number(ticket.netKg) || 0);
  }, 0);

  return {
    ticketCount: tickets.length,

    totalKg: roundNumber(totalKg, 0),

    totalTonnes: roundNumber(totalKg / 1000, 3),

    generatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   490. EXPORT SUMMARY API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl11GetExportSummary(token) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl11GetExportSummary();
}


/* =============================================================
   491. REPORT GENERATION LOGGING
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11LogReportGeneration(type, description, count) {
  const record = pbl11RegisterReport(
    type,
    description,
    count
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "REPORT_GENERATED",
    type + " — " + Number(count || 0) + " records"
  );

  return record;
}

/* =============================================================
   455. EXPORT CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================= */

var PBL11_CONFIG = {
  APP_NAME: "PONT BASCULE LIVE",
  DEFAULT_ENCODING: "UTF-8",
  MAX_EXPORT_ROWS: 10000,
  MAX_REPORT_HISTORY: 100,
  CSV_SEPARATOR: ";"
};


/* =============================================================
   456. SAFE EXPORT VALUE
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11SafeValue(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) {
    return "";
  }

  if (value instanceof Date) {
    return Utilities.formatDate(
      value,
      "Africa/Casablanca",
      "dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm:ss"
    );
  }

  return String(value);
}


/* =============================================================
   457. CSV FIELD ESCAPE
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11EscapeCsv(value) {
  let textValue = pbl11SafeValue(value);

  if (/^[=+\-@]/.test(textValue)) {
    textValue = "'" + textValue;
  }

  return '"' + textValue.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
}


/* =============================================================
   458. CSV GENERATOR
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11GenerateCsv(headers, rows) {
  const separator = PBL11_CONFIG.CSV_SEPARATOR;

  const output = [];

  output.push(
    headers.map(pbl11EscapeCsv).join(separator)
  );

  rows.forEach(function(row) {
    output.push(
      headers.map(function(header) {
        return pbl11EscapeCsv(row[header]);
      }).join(separator)
    );
  });

  return "\uFEFF" + output.join("\r\n");
}


/* =============================================================
   459. STANDARD TICKET EXPORT FIELDS
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11GetTicketExportFields() {
  return [
    "ticketNumber",
    "date",
    "time",
    "product",
    "client",
    "truckRegistration",
    "driver",
    "grossKg",
    "tareKg",
    "netKg",
    "netTonnes",
    "status"
  ];
}


/* =============================================================
   460. TICKET EXPORT ROW NORMALIZER
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11NormalizeTicketExport(ticket) {
  return {
    ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber || "",

    date: ticket.date || "",

    time: ticket.time || "",

    product: ticket.product || "",

    client: ticket.client || "",

    truckRegistration: ticket.truckRegistration || "",

    driver: ticket.driver || "",

    grossKg: Number(ticket.grossKg) || 0,

    tareKg: Number(ticket.tareKg) || 0,

    netKg: Number(ticket.netKg) || 0,

    netTonnes: roundNumber(
      (Number(ticket.netKg) || 0) / 1000,
      3
    ),

    status: ticket.status || ""
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   461. GET EXPORTABLE TICKETS
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11GetExportableTickets(filters) {
  filters = filters || {};

  const tickets = pbl10GetActiveTickets();

  let result = tickets;

  if (filters.startDate || filters.endDate) {
    result = filterTicketsAdvanced({
      startDate: filters.startDate || "",
      endDate: filters.endDate || ""
    }).filter(function(ticket) {
      return ticket.status !== PBL_WEIGHING.DELETED_STATUS &&
        ticket.status !== "CANCELLED";
    });
  }

  if (filters.product) {
    result = result.filter(function(ticket) {
      return String(ticket.product || "").toLowerCase()
        .includes(String(filters.product).toLowerCase());
    });
  }

  if (filters.client) {
    result = result.filter(function(ticket) {
      return String(ticket.client || "").toLowerCase()
        .includes(String(filters.client).toLowerCase());
    });
  }

  if (filters.truckRegistration) {
    result = result.filter(function(ticket) {
      return String(ticket.truckRegistration || "")
        .toLowerCase()
        .includes(String(filters.truckRegistration).toLowerCase());
    });
  }

  return result.slice(0, PBL11_CONFIG.MAX_EXPORT_ROWS);
}


/* =============================================================
   462. PREPARE TICKET EXPORT
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11PrepareTicketExport(filters) {
  const tickets = pbl11GetExportableTickets(filters);

  return {
    application: PBL11_CONFIG.APP_NAME,

    generatedAt: getLiveTimestamp(),

    count: tickets.length,

    headers: pbl11GetTicketExportFields(),

    rows: tickets.map(pbl11NormalizeTicketExport)
  };
}

function pbl11GetReportHistoryCollection() {
