  if (!Array.isArray(records)) {
    return [];
  }

  return records.filter(function(record) {
    return record &&
      record.recordType === "PBL_REPORT_HISTORY";
  });
}


/* =============================================================
   483. SAVE REPORT HISTORY
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11SaveReportHistoryCollection(history) {
  const records = getCollectionSafely(PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM);

  const other = Array.isArray(records)
    ? records.filter(function(record) {
        return !record ||
          record.recordType !== "PBL_REPORT_HISTORY";
      })
    : [];

  saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM,
    other.concat(history)
  );
}


/* =============================================================
   484. REGISTER REPORT
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11RegisterReport(type, description, count) {
  const history = pbl11GetReportHistoryCollection();

  const record = {
    id: pbl5CreateId("REPORT"),

    recordType: "PBL_REPORT_HISTORY",

    type: cleanText(type, 100),

    description: cleanText(description, 500),

    recordCount: Number(count) || 0,

    generatedAt: getLiveTimestamp(),

    timestamp: Date.now()
  };

  history.push(record);

  while (history.length > PBL11_CONFIG.MAX_REPORT_HISTORY) {
    history.shift();
  }

  pbl11SaveReportHistoryCollection(history);

  return record;
}


/* =============================================================
   485. GET REPORT HISTORY
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11GetReportHistory(limit) {
  return pbl11GetReportHistoryCollection()
    .sort(function(a, b) {
      return Number(b.timestamp || 0) -
        Number(a.timestamp || 0);
    })
    .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit) || 30));
}


/* =============================================================
   486. REPORT HISTORY API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl11GetReportHistory(token, limit) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl11GetReportHistory(limit);
}

/* =============================================================
   489. EXPORT SUMMARY
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11GetExportSummary() {
  const tickets = pbl10GetActiveTickets();

  const totalKg = tickets.reduce(function(total, ticket) {
    return total + (Number(ticket.netKg) || 0);
  }, 0);

  return {
    ticketCount: tickets.length,

    totalKg: roundNumber(totalKg, 0),

    totalTonnes: roundNumber(totalKg / 1000, 3),

    generatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


/* =============================================================
   490. EXPORT SUMMARY API
   ============================================================= */

function apiPbl11GetExportSummary(token) {
  pbl9RequireAdministration(token);

  return pbl11GetExportSummary();
}


/* =============================================================
   491. REPORT GENERATION LOGGING
   ============================================================= */

function pbl11LogReportGeneration(type, description, count) {
  const record = pbl11RegisterReport(
    type,
    description,
    count
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "REPORT_GENERATED",
    type + " — " + Number(count || 0) + " records"
  );

  return record;
}

/* ============================================================
   494. SAFE CONFIGURATION STORAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12ReadConfiguration(key, defaults) {
  try {
    const result = loadCollection(key);

    if (
      result &&
      !Array.isArray(result) &&
      typeof result === "object"
    ) {
      return Object.assign({}, defaults, result);
    }

    return Object.assign({}, defaults);

  } catch (error) {
    return Object.assign({}, defaults);
  }
}


function pbl12WriteConfiguration(key, configuration) {
  if (!configuration || typeof configuration !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Configuration invalide.");
  }

  saveCollection(key, configuration);

  return {
    success: true,
    key: key,
    savedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   495. COMPANY CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12GetCompanyConfiguration() {
  return pbl12ReadConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.COMPANY,
    PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_COMPANY
  );
}


function pbl12ValidateCompanyConfiguration(data) {
  if (!data || typeof data !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Informations entreprise invalides.");
  }

  const name = cleanText(data.name || "");

  if (!name) {
    throw new Error("Le nom de l'entreprise est obligatoire.");
  }

  return {
    name: name.substring(0, 120),
    legalName: cleanText(data.legalName || "").substring(0, 150),
    address: cleanText(data.address || "").substring(0, 250),
    city: cleanText(data.city || "").substring(0, 100),
    country: cleanText(data.country || "Maroc").substring(0, 80),
    phone: cleanText(data.phone || "").substring(0, 40),
    email: cleanText(data.email || "").substring(0, 120),
    taxNumber: cleanText(data.taxNumber || "").substring(0, 80),
    registrationNumber:
      cleanText(data.registrationNumber || "").substring(0, 80),
    logoEnabled: data.logoEnabled === true,
    logoUrl: cleanText(data.logoUrl || "").substring(0, 500),
    footer: cleanText(data.footer || "").substring(0, 250)
  };
}


function pbl12SaveCompanyConfiguration(data, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const validated = pbl12ValidateCompanyConfiguration(data);

  pbl12WriteConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.COMPANY,
    validated
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "COMPANY_CONFIGURATION_UPDATED",
    "Configuration entreprise modifiée"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    company: validated
  };
}


function apiPbl12GetCompanyConfiguration() {
  return pbl12GetCompanyConfiguration();
}


function apiPbl12SaveCompanyConfiguration(data, token) {
  return pbl12SaveCompanyConfiguration(data, token);
}


/* ============================================================
   496. WEIGHING STATION CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12GetStationConfiguration() {
  return pbl12ReadConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.STATION,
    PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_STATION
  );
}


function pbl12ValidateStationConfiguration(data) {
  const capacity = toNumber(data.scaleCapacityKg);
  const minimum = toNumber(data.minimumWeightKg);
  const maximum = toNumber(data.maximumWeightKg);
  const division = toNumber(data.divisionKg);

  if (capacity <= 0) {
    throw new Error("Capacité de pont invalide.");
  }

  if (minimum < 0 || maximum <= minimum) {
    throw new Error("Limites de pesage invalides.");
  }

  if (maximum > capacity) {
    throw new Error(
      "Le poids maximum dépasse la capacité du pont."
    );
  }

  if (division <= 0) {
    throw new Error("Division de pesage invalide.");
  }

  return {
    stationName:
      cleanText(data.stationName || "PONT BASCULE PRINCIPAL"),
    stationCode:
      cleanText(data.stationCode || "PB-01"),
    location:
      cleanText(data.location || ""),
    scaleCapacityKg: capacity,
    divisionKg: division,
    minimumWeightKg: minimum,
    maximumWeightKg: maximum,
    stableReadingRequired:
      data.stableReadingRequired !== false,
    automaticReadingEnabled:
      data.automaticReadingEnabled === true,
    hardwareConnected: false,
    manualEntryEnabled: false
  };
}


function pbl12SaveStationConfiguration(data, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const validated =
    pbl12ValidateStationConfiguration(data);

  pbl12WriteConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.STATION,
    validated
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "STATION_CONFIGURATION_UPDATED",
    "Configuration du pont bascule modifiée"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    station: validated
  };
}


function apiPbl12GetStationConfiguration() {
  return pbl12GetStationConfiguration();
}


function apiPbl12SaveStationConfiguration(data, token) {
  return pbl12SaveStationConfiguration(data, token);
}


/* ============================================================
   497. PRINTING CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12GetPrintingConfiguration() {
  return pbl12ReadConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.PRINTING,
    PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_PRINTING
  );
}


function pbl12ValidatePrintingConfiguration(data) {
  const width = toNumber(data.paperWidthMm);
  const height = toNumber(data.paperHeightMm);
  const copies = toNumber(data.copies);

  if (width < 50 || width > 300) {
    throw new Error("Largeur papier invalide.");
  }

  if (height < 50 || height > 500) {
    throw new Error("Hauteur papier invalide.");
  }

  if (copies < 1 || copies > 5) {
    throw new Error("Nombre de copies invalide.");
  }

  const fields = [
    "showCompanyName",
    "showCompanyAddress",
    "showStationName",
    "showTicketNumber",
    "showDate",
    "showTime",
    "showProduct",
    "showClient",
    "showTruck",
    "showDriver",
    "showGross",
    "showTare",
    "showNet",
    "showTonnes",
    "showSignatures",
    "showFooter",
    "autoPrint"
  ];

  const result = {
    paperWidthMm: width,
    paperHeightMm: height,
    copies: copies
  };

  fields.forEach(function(field) {
    result[field] = data[field] === true;
  });

  return result;
}


function pbl12SavePrintingConfiguration(data, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const validated =
    pbl12ValidatePrintingConfiguration(data);

  pbl12WriteConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.PRINTING,
    validated
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "PRINTING_CONFIGURATION_UPDATED",
    "Configuration impression modifiée"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    printing: validated
  };
}


function apiPbl12GetPrintingConfiguration() {
  return pbl12GetPrintingConfiguration();
}


function apiPbl12SavePrintingConfiguration(data, token) {
  return pbl12SavePrintingConfiguration(data, token);
}


/* ============================================================
   498. INTERFACE CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12GetInterfaceConfiguration() {
  return pbl12ReadConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.INTERFACE,
    PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_INTERFACE
  );
}


function pbl12ValidateInterfaceConfiguration(data) {
  const allowedLanguages = ["fr", "en", "ar"];
  const allowedThemes = ["dark", "light"];

  const language = allowedLanguages.indexOf(data.language) >= 0
    ? data.language
    : "fr";

  const theme = allowedThemes.indexOf(data.theme) >= 0
    ? data.theme
    : "dark";

  const refresh = toNumber(data.refreshSeconds);

  return {
    language: language,
    theme: theme,
    compactTables: data.compactTables === true,
    showAnimations: data.showAnimations !== false,
    showNotifications: data.showNotifications !== false,
    refreshSeconds:
      refresh >= 5 && refresh <= 120 ? refresh : 10,
    showLiveClock: data.showLiveClock !== false,
    showLiveWeight: data.showLiveWeight !== false,
    defaultScreen:
      cleanText(data.defaultScreen || "dashboard")
  };
}


function pbl12SaveInterfaceConfiguration(data, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const validated =
    pbl12ValidateInterfaceConfiguration(data);

  pbl12WriteConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.INTERFACE,
    validated
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "INTERFACE_CONFIGURATION_UPDATED",
    "Préférences interface modifiées"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    interface: validated
  };
}


function apiPbl12GetInterfaceConfiguration() {
  return pbl12GetInterfaceConfiguration();
}


function apiPbl12SaveInterfaceConfiguration(data, token) {
  return pbl12SaveInterfaceConfiguration(data, token);
}


/* ============================================================
   499. ADVANCED SYSTEM CONFIGURATION

  return pbl12ReadConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.ADVANCED,
    PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_ADVANCED
  );

function pbl12ValidateAdvancedConfiguration(data) {
  const decimalPlaces = toNumber(data.decimalPlaces);

  return {
    timezone: "Africa/Casablanca",
    weightUnit: "KG",
    reportingUnit: "TONNES",
    decimalPlaces:
      decimalPlaces >= 0 && decimalPlaces <= 4
        ? decimalPlaces
        : 2,
    automaticBackup: data.automaticBackup === true,
    backupFrequency:
      ["DAILY", "WEEKLY"].indexOf(data.backupFrequency) >= 0
        ? data.backupFrequency
        : "DAILY",
    auditEnabled: data.auditEnabled !== false,
    requireConfirmationForCancellation:
      data.requireConfirmationForCancellation !== false,
    requireConfirmationForRestore:
      data.requireConfirmationForRestore !== false,
    preventDuplicateTicketNumbers:
      data.preventDuplicateTicketNumbers !== false,
    preventNegativeWeights:
      data.preventNegativeWeights !== false,
    readOnlyOnStartup: true
  };
}


function pbl12SaveAdvancedConfiguration(data, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const validated =
    pbl12ValidateAdvancedConfiguration(data);

  pbl12WriteConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.ADVANCED,
    validated
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "ADVANCED_CONFIGURATION_UPDATED",
    "Paramètres avancés modifiés"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    advanced: validated
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   500. MANUAL ENTRY PERMISSION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12GetManualConfiguration() {
  return pbl12ReadConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.MANUAL,
    PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_MANUAL
  );
}


function pbl12SaveManualConfiguration(data, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const enabled = data.enabled === true;

  const validated = {
    enabled: enabled,
    requireReason: true,
    requireSupervisorPin: true,
    showManualControls: enabled,
    requireAuditLog: true,
    allowFirstWeight: enabled && data.allowFirstWeight !== false,
    allowSecondWeight: enabled && data.allowSecondWeight !== false
  };

  pbl12WriteConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.MANUAL,
    validated
  );

  if (typeof setManualEntryPermission === "function") {
    setManualEntryPermission(enabled, token);
  }

  writeAuditLog(
    "MANUAL_ENTRY_PERMISSION_CHANGED",
    enabled
      ? "Saisie manuelle activée"
      : "Saisie manuelle désactivée"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    manualEntry: validated
  };
}


function apiPbl12GetManualConfiguration() {
  return pbl12GetManualConfiguration();
}


function apiPbl12SaveManualConfiguration(data, token) {
  return pbl12SaveManualConfiguration(data, token);
}

/* ============================================================
   502. SAVE CONFIGURATION PACKAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12SaveConfigurationPackage(data, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (!data || typeof data !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Package de configuration invalide.");
  }

  const results = {};

  if (data.company) {
    results.company =
      pbl12SaveCompanyConfiguration(data.company, token);
  }

  if (data.station) {
    results.station =
      pbl12SaveStationConfiguration(data.station, token);
  }

  if (data.printing) {
    results.printing =
      pbl12SavePrintingConfiguration(data.printing, token);
  }

  if (data.interface) {
    results.interface =
      pbl12SaveInterfaceConfiguration(data.interface, token);
  }

  if (data.advanced) {
    results.advanced =
      pbl12SaveAdvancedConfiguration(data.advanced, token);
  }

  if (data.manualEntry) {
    results.manualEntry =
      pbl12SaveManualConfiguration(data.manualEntry, token);
  }

  writeAuditLog(
    "CONFIGURATION_PACKAGE_SAVED",
    "Package complet de configuration enregistré"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    results: results,
    savedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


function apiPbl12SaveConfigurationPackage(data, token) {
  return pbl12SaveConfigurationPackage(data, token);
}


/* ============================================================
   503. COMPANY HEADER FOR TICKETS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12GetTicketCompanyHeader() {
  const company = pbl12GetCompanyConfiguration();
  const station = pbl12GetStationConfiguration();

  return {
    companyName: company.name,
    legalName: company.legalName,
    address: company.address,
    city: company.city,
    country: company.country,
    phone: company.phone,
    taxNumber: company.taxNumber,
    stationName: station.stationName,
    stationCode: station.stationCode,
    footer: company.footer
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   504. PRINTING VALIDATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12ValidateTicketPrinting(ticket) {
  if (!ticket || typeof ticket !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Ticket introuvable.");
  }

  const required = [
    "ticketNumber",
    "date",
    "time",
    "product",
    "netKg"
  ];

  const missing = required.filter(function(field) {
    return ticket[field] === undefined ||
      ticket[field] === null ||
      ticket[field] === "";
  });

  if (missing.length) {
    throw new Error(
      "Ticket incomplet : " + missing.join(", ")
    );
  }

  return {
    valid: true,
    missingFields: []
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   505. PAPER DIMENSIONS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12GetPaperDimensions() {
  const config = pbl12GetPrintingConfiguration();

  return {
    widthMm: config.paperWidthMm,
    heightMm: config.paperHeightMm,
    orientation:
      config.paperWidthMm > config.paperHeightMm
        ? "LANDSCAPE"
        : "PORTRAIT"
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   506. WEIGHT DISPLAY FORMAT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12FormatWeightKg(value) {
  const number = toNumber(value);

  return number.toLocaleString("fr-FR", {
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2
  }) + " kg";
}


function pbl12FormatWeightTonnes(valueKg) {
  const tonnes = toNumber(valueKg) / 1000;
  const config = pbl12GetAdvancedConfiguration();

  return tonnes.toLocaleString("fr-FR", {
    minimumFractionDigits: config.decimalPlaces,
    maximumFractionDigits: config.decimalPlaces
  }) + " t";
}

  const company = pbl12GetCompanyConfiguration();

  return {
    applicationName: "PONT BASCULE LIVE",
    companyName: company.name,
    logoEnabled: company.logoEnabled,
    logoUrl: company.logoEnabled ? company.logoUrl : "",
    footer: company.footer,
    version: PBL12_CONFIG.VERSION
  };
}


function apiPbl12GetBranding() {
  return pbl12GetBranding();
}


/* ============================================================
   508. SETTINGS HEALTH CHECK
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12CheckConfigurationHealth() {
  const checks = [];

  const company = pbl12GetCompanyConfiguration();
  const station = pbl12GetStationConfiguration();
  const printing = pbl12GetPrintingConfiguration();
  const manual = pbl12GetManualConfiguration();

  checks.push({
    name: "Company identity",
    valid: Boolean(company.name)
  });

  checks.push({
    name: "Station capacity",
    valid: station.scaleCapacityKg > 0
  });

  checks.push({
    name: "Station weight limits",
    valid:
      station.maximumWeightKg <= station.scaleCapacityKg &&
      station.minimumWeightKg < station.maximumWeightKg
  });

  checks.push({
    name: "Printing dimensions",
    valid:
      printing.paperWidthMm > 0 &&
      printing.paperHeightMm > 0
  });

  checks.push({
    name: "Manual entry security",
    valid:
      manual.requireReason === true &&
      manual.requireSupervisorPin === true &&
      manual.requireAuditLog === true
  });

  return {
    valid: checks.every(function(item) {
      return item.valid;
    }),
    checks: checks,
    checkedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


function apiPbl12CheckConfigurationHealth() {
  return pbl12CheckConfigurationHealth();
}

/* ============================================================
   510. SETTINGS RESET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12ResetSettings(token, confirmation) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (confirmation !== "RESET_SETTINGS") {
    throw new Error("Confirmation de réinitialisation requise.");
  }

  pbl12WriteConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.COMPANY,
    PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_COMPANY
  );

  pbl12WriteConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.STATION,
    PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_STATION
  );

  pbl12WriteConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.PRINTING,
    PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_PRINTING
  );

  pbl12WriteConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.INTERFACE,
    PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_INTERFACE
  );

  pbl12WriteConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.ADVANCED,
    PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_ADVANCED
  );

  pbl12WriteConfiguration(
    PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.MANUAL,
    PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_MANUAL
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "SETTINGS_RESET",
    "Paramètres réinitialisés"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Paramètres réinitialisés."
  };
}


function apiPbl12ResetSettings(token, confirmation) {
  return pbl12ResetSettings(token, confirmation);
}


/* ============================================================
   511. STATION STATUS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12GetStationStatus() {
  const station = pbl12GetStationConfiguration();

  let hardware = {
    connected: false,
    status: "NOT_CONNECTED",
    message: "Aucune connexion matérielle confirmée."
  };

  if (typeof getScaleConnectionStatus === "function") {
    try {
      hardware = getScaleConnectionStatus();
    } catch (error) {
      hardware.status = "ERROR";
    }
  }

  return {
    stationName: station.stationName,
    stationCode: station.stationCode,
    capacityKg: station.scaleCapacityKg,
    minimumWeightKg: station.minimumWeightKg,
    maximumWeightKg: station.maximumWeightKg,
    hardware: hardware,
    manualEntryEnabled:
      pbl12GetManualConfiguration().enabled,
    checkedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


function apiPbl12GetStationStatus() {
  return pbl12GetStationStatus();
}

/* ============================================================
   513. CONFIGURATION IMPORT VALIDATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12ValidateConfigurationImport(data) {
  if (!data || data.application !== "PONT BASCULE LIVE") {
    throw new Error("Fichier de configuration non reconnu.");
  }

  const checks = [];

  if (data.company) {
    pbl12ValidateCompanyConfiguration(data.company);
    checks.push("company");
  }

  if (data.station) {
    pbl12ValidateStationConfiguration(data.station);
    checks.push("station");
  }

  if (data.printing) {
    pbl12ValidatePrintingConfiguration(data.printing);
    checks.push("printing");
  }

  if (data.interface) {
    pbl12ValidateInterfaceConfiguration(data.interface);
    checks.push("interface");
  }

  if (data.advanced) {
    pbl12ValidateAdvancedConfiguration(data.advanced);
    checks.push("advanced");
  }

  return {
    valid: true,
    sections: checks
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   514. CONFIGURATION IMPORT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12ImportConfiguration(data, token, confirmation) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (confirmation !== "IMPORT_CONFIGURATION") {
    throw new Error("Confirmation d'importation requise.");
  }

  const validation =
    pbl12ValidateConfigurationImport(data);

  const result = {};

  if (data.company) {
    result.company =
      pbl12SaveCompanyConfiguration(data.company, token);
  }

  if (data.station) {
    result.station =
      pbl12SaveStationConfiguration(data.station, token);
  }

  if (data.printing) {
    result.printing =
      pbl12SavePrintingConfiguration(data.printing, token);
  }

  if (data.interface) {
    result.interface =
      pbl12SaveInterfaceConfiguration(data.interface, token);
  }

  if (data.advanced) {
    result.advanced =
      pbl12SaveAdvancedConfiguration(data.advanced, token);
  }

  writeAuditLog(
    "CONFIGURATION_IMPORTED",
    "Configuration importée"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    validation: validation,
    result: result
  };
}

    paperSize:
      printing.paperWidthMm + " x " +
      printing.paperHeightMm + " mm",
    manualEntry: manual.enabled ? "ENABLED" : "DISABLED",
    startupMode: "READ ONLY",
    pinProtected: true,
    currentDate: getLiveDate(),
    currentTime: getLiveTime()

/* ============================================================
   518. MANUAL ENTRY REASON VALIDATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12ValidateManualReason(reason) {
  const value = cleanText(reason || "");

  if (value.length < 5) {
    throw new Error(
      "Veuillez saisir un motif détaillé."
    );
  }

  if (value.length > 500) {
    throw new Error(
      "Le motif ne doit pas dépasser 500 caractères."
    );
  }

  return value;
}


/* ============================================================
   519. MANUAL ENTRY AUDIT RECORD
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12RecordManualAudit(details, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (!pbl12GetManualConfiguration().enabled) {
    throw new Error(
      "La saisie manuelle est désactivée."
    );
  }

  const reason =
    pbl12ValidateManualReason(details.reason);

  writeAuditLog(
    "MANUAL_WEIGHT_ENTRY",
    JSON.stringify({
      sessionId: details.sessionId || "",
      weightKg: toNumber(details.weightKg),
      stage: details.stage || "",
      reason: reason
    })
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    recordedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   520. STATION CAPACITY VALIDATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12ValidateWeightAgainstStation(weightKg) {
  const station = pbl12GetStationConfiguration();
  const weight = toNumber(weightKg);

  if (weight < station.minimumWeightKg) {
    throw new Error("Poids inférieur au minimum autorisé.");
  }

  if (weight > station.maximumWeightKg) {
    throw new Error("Capacité maximale du pont dépassée.");
  }

  return {
    valid: true,
    weightKg: weight,
    weightTonnes: weight / 1000,
    stationCode: station.stationCode
  };
}

   ============================================================ */

    canView: true,
    canEdit: Boolean(status && status.unlocked === true),
    startupReadOnly: true
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   528. SAFE CONFIGURATION VALUE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12GetSafeConfigurationValue(section, field) {
  const sections = {
    company: pbl12GetCompanyConfiguration(),
    station: pbl12GetStationConfiguration(),
    printing: pbl12GetPrintingConfiguration(),
    interface: pbl12GetInterfaceConfiguration(),
    advanced: pbl12GetAdvancedConfiguration(),
    manual: pbl12GetManualConfiguration()
  };

  if (!sections[section]) {
    throw new Error("Section inconnue.");
  }

  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
    sections[section],
    field
  )) {
    throw new Error("Champ de configuration inconnu.");
  }

  return sections[section][field];
}

/* ============================================================
   531. RESTORE CONFIGURATION DATA
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12RestoreConfigurationBackup(data, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (!data || typeof data !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Sauvegarde invalide.");
  }

  const result = {};

  if (data.company) {
    result.company =
      pbl12SaveCompanyConfiguration(data.company, token);
  }

  if (data.station) {
    result.station =
      pbl12SaveStationConfiguration(data.station, token);
  }

  if (data.printing) {
    result.printing =
      pbl12SavePrintingConfiguration(data.printing, token);
  }

  if (data.interface) {
    result.interface =
      pbl12SaveInterfaceConfiguration(data.interface, token);
  }

  if (data.advanced) {
    result.advanced =
      pbl12SaveAdvancedConfiguration(data.advanced, token);
  }

  if (data.manual) {
    result.manual =
      pbl12SaveManualConfiguration(data.manual, token);
  }

  writeAuditLog(
    "CONFIGURATION_BACKUP_RESTORED",
    "Sauvegarde de configuration restaurée"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    restored: result
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   534. CONFIGURATION INITIALIZATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12InitializeConfigurations() {
  const sections = [
    {
      key: PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.COMPANY,
      defaults: PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_COMPANY
    },
    {
      key: PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.STATION,
      defaults: PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_STATION
    },
    {
      key: PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.PRINTING,
      defaults: PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_PRINTING
    },
    {
      key: PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.INTERFACE,
      defaults: PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_INTERFACE
    },
    {
      key: PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.ADVANCED,
      defaults: PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_ADVANCED
    },
    {
      key: PBL12_CONFIG.KEYS.MANUAL,
      defaults: PBL12_CONFIG.DEFAULT_MANUAL
    }
  ];

  const initialized = [];

  sections.forEach(function(section) {
    try {
      const existing = loadCollection(section.key);

      if (!existing || typeof existing !== "object") {
        saveCollection(section.key, section.defaults);
        initialized.push(section.key);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      saveCollection(section.key, section.defaults);
      initialized.push(section.key);
    }
  });

  return {
    success: true,
    initialized: initialized
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   535. CONFIGURATION INITIALIZATION API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl12InitializeConfigurations(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);
  return pbl12InitializeConfigurations();
}

/* ============================================================
   538. SETTINGS VALIDATION BEFORE SAVE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12ValidateAllSettings() {
  const checks = [];

  try {
    pbl12ValidateCompanyConfiguration(
      pbl12GetCompanyConfiguration()
    );
    checks.push({ section: "company", valid: true });
  } catch (error) {
    checks.push({
      section: "company",
      valid: false,
      error: error.message
    });
  }

  try {
    pbl12ValidateStationConfiguration(
      pbl12GetStationConfiguration()
    );
    checks.push({ section: "station", valid: true });
  } catch (error) {
    checks.push({
      section: "station",
      valid: false,
      error: error.message
    });
  }

  try {
    pbl12ValidatePrintingConfiguration(
      pbl12GetPrintingConfiguration()
    );
    checks.push({ section: "printing", valid: true });
  } catch (error) {
    checks.push({
      section: "printing",
      valid: false,
      error: error.message
    });
  }

  return {
    valid: checks.every(function(item) {
      return item.valid;
    }),
    checks: checks
  };
}

    health: pbl12CheckConfigurationHealth()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   546. MANUAL ENTRY DISPLAY GUARD
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12CanDisplayManualEntry() {
  const manual = pbl12GetManualConfiguration();

  return manual.enabled === true &&
    manual.showManualControls === true;
}


/* ============================================================
   547. MANUAL ENTRY SERVER GUARD
   ============================================================ */

function pbl12RequireManualEntryPermission(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const manual = pbl12GetManualConfiguration();

  if (!manual.enabled) {
    throw new Error(
      "Saisie manuelle interdite : permission désactivée."
    );
  }

  if (!manual.requireReason ||
      !manual.requireSupervisorPin ||
      !manual.requireAuditLog) {
    throw new Error(
      "Configuration de sécurité manuelle invalide."
    );
  }

  return true;
}

    status: pbl12GetManualEntryStatus()
  };
}

/***************************************************************
 * PONT BASCULE LIVE
 * WEIGHBRIDGE MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
 * PART 13 — DATABASE ARCHITECTURE & STORAGE REPAIR
 *
 * Functions 551–610
 *
 * Dedicated storage collections:
 * - PBL_WEIGHING_SESSIONS
 * - PBL_PRINT_HISTORY
 * - PBL_ACTIVITY
 * - PBL_NOTIFICATIONS
 * - PBL_ALERTS
 * - PBL_MAINTENANCE_LOGS
 * - PBL_REPORT_HISTORY
 *
 * The original SYSTEM collection is preserved.
 ***************************************************************/


/* ============================================================
   551. STORAGE ARCHITECTURE
   ============================================================ */

const PBL13_STORAGE = {
  VERSION: "13.0.0",

  KEYS: {
    WEIGHING: "PBL_WEIGHING_SESSIONS",
    PRINT: "PBL_PRINT_HISTORY",
    ACTIVITY: "PBL_ACTIVITY",
    NOTIFICATIONS: "PBL_NOTIFICATIONS",
    ALERTS: "PBL_ALERTS",
    MAINTENANCE: "PBL_MAINTENANCE_LOGS",
    REPORTS: "PBL_REPORT_HISTORY",
    MIGRATION: "PBL_STORAGE_MIGRATION"
  },

  MAX_RECORDS: {
    WEIGHING: 10000,
    PRINT: 10000,
    ACTIVITY: 5000,
    NOTIFICATIONS: 2000,
    ALERTS: 1000,
    MAINTENANCE: 2000,
    REPORTS: 2000
  }
};


/* ============================================================
   552. SAFE ARRAY READER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13ReadArray(key) {
  try {
    const result = loadCollection(key);
    return Array.isArray(result) ? result : [];
  } catch (error) {
    return [];
  }
}


/* ============================================================
   553. SAFE ARRAY WRITER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13WriteArray(key, records) {
  if (!Array.isArray(records)) {
    throw new Error("Collection invalide : " + key);
  }

  saveCollection(key, records);

  return {
    success: true,
    key: key,
    count: records.length
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   554. STORAGE COLLECTION DEFINITIONS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13GetCollectionDefinitions() {
  return [
    {
      name: "WEIGHING_SESSIONS",
      key: PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.WEIGHING,
      limit: PBL13_STORAGE.MAX_RECORDS.WEIGHING
    },
    {
      name: "PRINT_HISTORY",
      key: PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.PRINT,
      limit: PBL13_STORAGE.MAX_RECORDS.PRINT
    },
    {
      name: "ACTIVITY",
      key: PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.ACTIVITY,
      limit: PBL13_STORAGE.MAX_RECORDS.ACTIVITY
    },
    {
      name: "NOTIFICATIONS",
      key: PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.NOTIFICATIONS,
      limit: PBL13_STORAGE.MAX_RECORDS.NOTIFICATIONS
    },
    {
      name: "ALERTS",
      key: PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.ALERTS,
      limit: PBL13_STORAGE.MAX_RECORDS.ALERTS
    },
    {
      name: "MAINTENANCE",
      key: PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.MAINTENANCE,
      limit: PBL13_STORAGE.MAX_RECORDS.MAINTENANCE
    },
    {
      name: "REPORT_HISTORY",
      key: PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.REPORTS,
      limit: PBL13_STORAGE.MAX_RECORDS.REPORTS
    }
  ];
}


/* ============================================================
   555. INITIALIZE DEDICATED COLLECTIONS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13InitializeCollections() {
  const definitions = pbl13GetCollectionDefinitions();
  const initialized = [];

  definitions.forEach(function(definition) {
    try {
      const existing = loadCollection(definition.key);

      if (!Array.isArray(existing)) {
        saveCollection(definition.key, []);
        initialized.push(definition.key);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      saveCollection(definition.key, []);
      initialized.push(definition.key);
    }
  });

  return {
    success: true,
    initialized: initialized,
    totalCollections: definitions.length
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   556. COLLECTION COUNT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13GetCollectionCount(key) {
  return pbl13ReadArray(key).length;
}


/* ============================================================
   557. COLLECTION INVENTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13GetStorageInventory() {
  return pbl13GetCollectionDefinitions().map(function(definition) {
    const records = pbl13ReadArray(definition.key);

    return {
      name: definition.name,
      key: definition.key,
      recordCount: records.length,
      maximumRecords: definition.limit,
      percentageUsed: definition.limit
        ? Math.round(records.length / definition.limit * 10000) / 100
        : 0
    };
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   558. COLLECTION INTEGRITY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13CheckCollectionIntegrity(key) {
  const records = pbl13ReadArray(key);

  const invalid = [];
  const identifiers = {};

  records.forEach(function(record, index) {
    if (!record || typeof record !== "object") {
      invalid.push({
        index: index,
        reason: "INVALID_RECORD"
      });
      return;
    }

    if (record.id) {
      if (identifiers[record.id]) {
        invalid.push({
          index: index,
          reason: "DUPLICATE_ID",
          id: record.id
        });
      }

      identifiers[record.id] = true;
    }
  });

  return {
    key: key,
    valid: invalid.length === 0,
    total: records.length,
    invalid: invalid
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   559. CHECK ALL COLLECTIONS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13CheckAllCollections() {
  const definitions = pbl13GetCollectionDefinitions();

  const checks = definitions.map(function(definition) {
    return pbl13CheckCollectionIntegrity(definition.key);
  });

  return {
    valid: checks.every(function(check) {
      return check.valid;
    }),
    collections: checks,
    checkedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   560. GENERIC RECORD FINDER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13FindRecord(key, id) {
  if (!id) return null;

  const records = pbl13ReadArray(key);

  return records.find(function(record) {
    return String(record.id) === String(id);
  }) || null;
}


/* ============================================================
   561. GENERIC RECORD INSERTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13InsertRecord(key, record, limit) {
  if (!record || typeof record !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Enregistrement invalide.");
  }

  const records = pbl13ReadArray(key);

  const newRecord = Object.assign({}, record);

  if (!newRecord.id) {
    newRecord.id = createUniqueId();
  }

  if (records.some(function(item) {
    return String(item.id) === String(newRecord.id);
  })) {
    throw new Error("Identifiant déjà existant.");
  }

  newRecord.createdAt =
    newRecord.createdAt || getLiveTimestamp();

  records.push(newRecord);

  const maximum = limit || 10000;

  const retained = records.length > maximum
    ? records.slice(records.length - maximum)
    : records;

  pbl13WriteArray(key, retained);

  return newRecord;
}


/* ============================================================
   562. GENERIC RECORD UPDATE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13UpdateRecord(key, id, changes) {
  if (!id || !changes || typeof changes !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Modification invalide.");
  }

  const records = pbl13ReadArray(key);

  const index = records.findIndex(function(record) {
    return String(record.id) === String(id);
  });

  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error("Enregistrement introuvable.");
  }

  const protectedFields = [
    "id",
    "createdAt"
  ];

  protectedFields.forEach(function(field) {
    delete changes[field];
  });

  records[index] = Object.assign(
    {},
    records[index],
    changes,
    { updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp() }
  );

  pbl13WriteArray(key, records);

  return records[index];
}


/* ============================================================
   563. GENERIC RECORD DELETE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13DeleteRecord(key, id) {
  const records = pbl13ReadArray(key);

  const remaining = records.filter(function(record) {
    return String(record.id) !== String(id);
  });

  pbl13WriteArray(key, remaining);

  return {
    success: true,
    removed: records.length - remaining.length
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   569. ACTIVITY HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13GetActivityHistory(limit) {
  const records = pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.ACTIVITY
  );

  const maximum = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(toNumber(limit) || 50, 500)
  );

  return records.slice(-maximum).reverse();
}

/* ============================================================
   582. COLLECTION SEARCH
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13SearchCollection(key, query, fields) {
  const records = pbl13ReadArray(key);
  const search = cleanText(query || "").toLowerCase();

  if (!search) return records;

  const searchableFields = Array.isArray(fields)
    ? fields
    : ["id", "name", "title", "message"];

  return records.filter(function(record) {
    return searchableFields.some(function(field) {
      const value = record[field];

      return value !== undefined &&
        String(value).toLowerCase().indexOf(search) >= 0;
    });
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   583. STORAGE SIZE ESTIMATE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13EstimateCollectionSize(key) {
  const records = pbl13ReadArray(key);

  let bytes = 0;

  try {
    bytes = Utilities
      .newBlob(JSON.stringify(records))
      .getBytes()
      .length;
  } catch (error) {
    bytes = JSON.stringify(records).length * 2;
  }

  return {
    key: key,
    recordCount: records.length,
    estimatedBytes: bytes,
    estimatedKilobytes:
      Math.round(bytes / 1024 * 100) / 100
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   584. STORAGE REPORT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13GetStorageReport() {
  return pbl13GetCollectionDefinitions().map(function(definition) {
    return pbl13EstimateCollectionSize(definition.key);
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   585. BACKUP COLLECTION KEYS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13GetDedicatedBackupKeys() {
  return pbl13GetCollectionDefinitions().map(function(item) {
    return item.key;
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   586. BACKUP KEY INTEGRATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13GetAllBackupKeys() {
  let original = [];

  if (typeof getBackupCollectionKeys === "function") {
    try {
      original = getBackupCollectionKeys();
    } catch (error) {
      original = [];
    }
  }

  if (!Array.isArray(original)) original = [];

  return Array.from(new Set(
    original.concat(pbl13GetDedicatedBackupKeys())
  ));
}


/* ============================================================
   587. STORAGE MIGRATION STATUS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13GetMigrationStatus() {
  const status = pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.MIGRATION
  );

  return {
    migrationRecords: status,
    completed: status.some(function(item) {
      return item.status === "COMPLETED";
    })
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   588. LEGACY SYSTEM COLLECTION INSPECTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13InspectLegacySystemCollection() {
  let legacy;

  try {
    legacy = loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM);
  } catch (error) {
    legacy = null;
  }

  if (Array.isArray(legacy)) {
    return {
      type: "ARRAY",
      total: legacy.length,
      recordTypes: Array.from(new Set(
        legacy.map(function(record) {
          return record && record.recordType
            ? record.recordType
            : "UNCLASSIFIED";
        })
      ))
    };
  }

  return {
    type: typeof legacy,
    total: 0,
    recordTypes: []
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   589. LEGACY RECORD TYPE MAPPING
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13GetLegacyMapping() {
  return {
    WEIGHING_SESSION:
      PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.WEIGHING,

    PRINT_HISTORY:
      PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.PRINT,

    PBL_ACTIVITY:
      PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.ACTIVITY,

    PBL_NOTIFICATION:
      PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.NOTIFICATIONS,

    PBL_ALERT:
      PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.ALERTS,

    MAINTENANCE:
      PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.MAINTENANCE,

    PBL_REPORT_HISTORY:
      PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.REPORTS
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   590. LEGACY RECORD MIGRATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13MigrateLegacyRecords(token, confirmation) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (confirmation !== "MIGRATE_LEGACY_RECORDS") {
    throw new Error("Confirmation de migration requise.");
  }

  const inspection =
    pbl13InspectLegacySystemCollection();

  if (inspection.type !== "ARRAY") {
    return {
      success: true,
      migrated: 0,
      message: "Aucune ancienne collection de type tableau."
    };
  }

  const legacy = loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.SYSTEM);
  const mapping = pbl13GetLegacyMapping();

  const grouped = {};
  const counts = {};

  Object.keys(mapping).forEach(function(type) {
    grouped[type] = [];
    counts[type] = 0;
  });

  legacy.forEach(function(record) {
    if (!record || !record.recordType) return;

    const type = record.recordType;

    if (grouped[type]) {
      grouped[type].push(record);
      counts[type]++;
    }
  });

  Object.keys(mapping).forEach(function(type) {
    const key = mapping[type];
    const existing = pbl13ReadArray(key);
    const incoming = grouped[type];

    const known = {};

    existing.forEach(function(record) {
      known[String(record.id)] = true;
    });

    incoming.forEach(function(record) {
      if (!known[String(record.id)]) {
        existing.push(record);
        known[String(record.id)] = true;
      }
    });

    pbl13WriteArray(key, existing);
  });

  const migrationRecord = {
    id: createUniqueId(),
    status: "COMPLETED",
    completedAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
    counts: counts,
    legacyRecordCount: legacy.length
  };

  pbl13InsertRecord(
    PBL13_STORAGE.KEYS.MIGRATION,
    migrationRecord,
    100
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "LEGACY_STORAGE_MIGRATED",
    "Migration des anciennes collections effectuée"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    migratedCounts: counts,
    legacyRecordsInspected: legacy.length,
    originalSystemPreserved: true
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   591. STORAGE BACKUP SNAPSHOT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13CreateStorageSnapshot() {
  const keys = pbl13GetDedicatedBackupKeys();
  const snapshot = {};

  keys.forEach(function(key) {
    snapshot[key] = pbl13ReadArray(key);
  });

  return {
    application: "PONT BASCULE LIVE",
    version: PBL13_STORAGE.VERSION,
    createdAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
    collections: snapshot
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   592. STORAGE SNAPSHOT VALIDATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13ValidateStorageSnapshot(snapshot) {
  if (!snapshot || !snapshot.collections) {
    throw new Error("Snapshot invalide.");
  }

  const keys = pbl13GetDedicatedBackupKeys();
  const checks = [];

  keys.forEach(function(key) {
    const value = snapshot.collections[key];

    checks.push({
      key: key,
      valid: value === undefined || Array.isArray(value),
      count: Array.isArray(value) ? value.length : 0
    });
  });

  return {
    valid: checks.every(function(item) {
      return item.valid;
    }),
    checks: checks
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   593. STORAGE RESTORE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13RestoreStorageSnapshot(
  snapshot,
  token,
  confirmation
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (confirmation !== "RESTORE_STORAGE_SNAPSHOT") {
    throw new Error("Confirmation de restauration requise.");
  }

  const validation =
    pbl13ValidateStorageSnapshot(snapshot);

  if (!validation.valid) {
    throw new Error("Snapshot contient des collections invalides.");
  }

  const keys = pbl13GetDedicatedBackupKeys();

  keys.forEach(function(key) {
    if (Array.isArray(snapshot.collections[key])) {
      pbl13WriteArray(
        key,
        snapshot.collections[key]
      );
    }
  });

  writeAuditLog(
    "STORAGE_SNAPSHOT_RESTORED",
    "Snapshot des collections restauré"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    restoredCollections: keys.length
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   594. STORAGE CLEANUP PREVIEW
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13PreviewStorageCleanup() {
  return pbl13GetStorageInventory().map(function(item) {
    return {
      collection: item.name,
      currentRecords: item.recordCount,
      maximumRecords: item.maximumRecords,
      cleanupRequired:
        item.recordCount > item.maximumRecords
    };
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   595. STORAGE CLEANUP
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13CleanupStorage(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const definitions = pbl13GetCollectionDefinitions();
  const results = [];

  definitions.forEach(function(definition) {
    const records = pbl13ReadArray(definition.key);

    if (records.length > definition.limit) {
      const retained =
        records.slice(records.length - definition.limit);

      pbl13WriteArray(definition.key, retained);

      results.push({
        key: definition.key,
        removed: records.length - retained.length
      });
    }
  });

  writeAuditLog(
    "STORAGE_CLEANUP",
    "Nettoyage des collections effectué"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    results: results
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   596. STORAGE HEALTH
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13GetStorageHealth() {
  const integrity = pbl13CheckAllCollections();
  const inventory = pbl13GetStorageInventory();

  return {
    healthy: integrity.valid,
    integrity: integrity,
    inventory: inventory,
    checkedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   597. STORAGE HEALTH API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl13GetStorageHealth() {
  return pbl13GetStorageHealth();
}


/* ============================================================
   598. DATABASE COLLECTION SUMMARY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13GetDatabaseSummary() {
  const inventory = pbl13GetStorageInventory();

  return {
    dedicatedCollections: inventory.length,
    totalRecords: inventory.reduce(function(sum, item) {
      return sum + item.recordCount;
    }, 0),
    collections: inventory,
    legacySystem: pbl13InspectLegacySystemCollection()
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   599. DATABASE SUMMARY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl13GetDatabaseSummary() {
  return pbl13GetDatabaseSummary();
}

   600. DATABASE INITIALIZATION API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl13InitializeDatabase(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    initialization: pbl13InitializeCollections(),
    inventory: pbl13GetStorageInventory()
  };
}

  return {
    legacy: pbl13InspectLegacySystemCollection(),
    destinations: pbl13GetDedicatedBackupKeys(),

    originalCollectionWillBePreserved: true
  };
}

  };

}

   604. SAFE STORAGE KEY VALIDATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl13IsAllowedStorageKey(key) {
  return pbl13GetDedicatedBackupKeys().indexOf(key) >= 0;
}

   605. SAFE COLLECTION READ API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl13ReadCollection(key, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (!pbl13IsAllowedStorageKey(key)) {
    throw new Error("Collection non autorisée.");
  }

  return pbl13ReadArray(key);
}

  const inventory = pbl13GetStorageInventory();

  const result = {};

  inventory.forEach(function(item) {
    result[item.name] = item.recordCount;
  });

  return result;
}

  };
