/***************************************************************
 * PONT BASCULE LIVE
 * WEIGHBRIDGE MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
 * PART 14 — TRANSACTION SAFETY & WEIGHING WORKFLOW
 *
 * Functions 611–670
 *
 * Weight input: KG
 * Dashboard reporting: TONNES
 * Startup: READ ONLY
 * Editing: PIN protected
 ***************************************************************/


/* ============================================================
   611. PART 14 CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================ */

const PBL14_CONFIG = {
  VERSION: "14.0.0",

  STATUS: {
    CREATED: "CREATED",
    FIRST_WEIGHT: "FIRST_WEIGHT",
    WAITING_SECOND: "WAITING_SECOND",
    COMPLETED: "COMPLETED",
    CANCELLED: "CANCELLED"
  },

  MAX_WEIGHT_KG: 1000000,
  MIN_WEIGHT_KG: 0,
  WEIGHT_DECIMALS: 2,
  SESSION_EXPIRY_HOURS: 24,

  REQUIRED_FIELDS: [
    "product",
    "client",
    "truckRegistration"
  ]
};


/* ============================================================
   612. NORMALIZE VEHICLE REGISTRATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14NormalizeRegistration(value) {
  return cleanText(value || "")
    .toUpperCase()
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();
}


/* ============================================================
   613. VALIDATE VEHICLE REGISTRATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14ValidateRegistration(value) {
  const registration =
    pbl14NormalizeRegistration(value);

  if (registration.length < 2) {
    throw new Error("Immatriculation du camion obligatoire.");
  }

  if (registration.length > 30) {
    throw new Error("Immatriculation trop longue.");
  }

  return registration;
}


/* ============================================================
   614. NORMALIZE WEIGHT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14NormalizeWeight(value) {
  const weight = Number(value);

  if (!Number.isFinite(weight)) {
    throw new Error("Poids non numérique.");
  }

  const rounded = Math.round(
    weight * Math.pow(10, PBL14_CONFIG.WEIGHT_DECIMALS)
  ) / Math.pow(10, PBL14_CONFIG.WEIGHT_DECIMALS);

  if (rounded < PBL14_CONFIG.MIN_WEIGHT_KG) {
    throw new Error("Le poids ne peut pas être négatif.");
  }

  if (rounded > PBL14_CONFIG.MAX_WEIGHT_KG) {
    throw new Error("Poids supérieur à la limite autorisée.");
  }

  if (typeof pbl12ValidateWeightAgainstStation === "function") {
    pbl12ValidateWeightAgainstStation(rounded);
  }

  return rounded;
}


/* ============================================================
   615. CALCULATE NET WEIGHT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14CalculateNetWeight(grossKg, tareKg) {
  const gross = pbl14NormalizeWeight(grossKg);
  const tare = pbl14NormalizeWeight(tareKg);

  if (gross < tare) {
    throw new Error(
      "Le poids brut doit être supérieur ou égal à la tare."
    );
  }

  const net = Math.round(
    (gross - tare) * 100
  ) / 100;

  return {
    grossKg: gross,
    tareKg: tare,
    netKg: net,
    netTonnes: Math.round(net / 10) / 100,
    differenceKg: net
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   616. CREATE TRANSACTION ID
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14CreateTransactionId() {
  return "TX-" +
    Utilities.formatDate(
      new Date(),
      "Africa/Casablanca",
      "yyyyMMdd-HHmmss"
    ) +
    "-" +
    Math.floor(Math.random() * 90000 + 10000);
}


/* ============================================================
   617. GET ACTIVE TRANSACTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14GetActiveTransactions() {
  return pbl6GetWeighingRecords().filter(function(record) {
    return record &&
      record.status !== PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.COMPLETED &&
      record.status !== PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.CANCELLED;
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   618. FIND TRANSACTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14FindTransaction(id) {
  const records = pbl6GetWeighingRecords();

  return records.find(function(record) {
    return String(record.id) === String(id) ||
      String(record.transactionId) === String(id);
  }) || null;
}


/* ============================================================
   619. CHECK ACTIVE VEHICLE SESSION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14FindActiveVehicleSession(registration) {
  const normalized =
    pbl14NormalizeRegistration(registration);

  return pbl14GetActiveTransactions().find(function(record) {
    return pbl14NormalizeRegistration(
      record.truckRegistration || ""
    ) === normalized;
  }) || null;
}


/* ============================================================
   620. VALIDATE TICKET INFORMATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14ValidateTicketInformation(data) {
  if (!data || typeof data !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Données de pesage invalides.");
  }

  const result = {
    product: cleanText(data.product || ""),
    client: cleanText(data.client || ""),
    truckRegistration:
      pbl14ValidateRegistration(data.truckRegistration),
    driver: cleanText(data.driver || ""),
    reference: cleanText(data.reference || ""),
    notes: cleanText(data.notes || "")
  };

  if (!result.product) {
    throw new Error("Veuillez sélectionner un produit.");
  }

  if (!result.client) {
    throw new Error("Veuillez sélectionner un client.");
  }

  if (result.notes.length > 1000) {
    throw new Error("Les observations sont trop longues.");
  }

  return result;
}


/* ============================================================
   621. CHECK PRODUCT EXISTS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14CheckProductExists(productName) {
  const name = cleanText(productName).toLowerCase();

  if (!name) return false;

  if (typeof getAllProducts === "function") {
    const products = getAllProducts();

    return products.some(function(product) {
      return cleanText(product.name || product.product || "")
        .toLowerCase() === name &&
        product.archived !== true;
    });
  }

  return true;
}


/* ============================================================
   622. CHECK CLIENT EXISTS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14CheckClientExists(clientName) {
  const name = cleanText(clientName).toLowerCase();

  if (!name) return false;

  if (typeof getAllClients === "function") {
    const clients = getAllClients();

    return clients.some(function(client) {
      return cleanText(client.name || client.client || "")
        .toLowerCase() === name &&
        client.archived !== true;
    });
  }

  return true;
}


/* ============================================================
   623. VALIDATE PRODUCT AND CLIENT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14ValidateReferences(data) {
  if (!pbl14CheckProductExists(data.product)) {
    throw new Error("Produit introuvable ou archivé.");
  }

  if (!pbl14CheckClientExists(data.client)) {
    throw new Error("Client introuvable ou archivé.");
  }

  return true;
}


/* ============================================================
   624. GENERATE TRANSACTION RECORD
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14BuildTransaction(data, token) {
  const information =
    pbl14ValidateTicketInformation(data);

  pbl14ValidateReferences(information);

  const now = getLiveTimestamp();

  return {
    id: pbl14CreateTransactionId(),
    transactionId: pbl14CreateTransactionId(),
    status: PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.CREATED,

    product: information.product,
    client: information.client,
    truckRegistration: information.truckRegistration,
    driver: information.driver,
    reference: information.reference,
    notes: information.notes,

    firstWeightKg: null,
    secondWeightKg: null,
    grossKg: null,
    tareKg: null,
    netKg: null,
    netTonnes: null,

    firstWeighingAt: null,
    secondWeighingAt: null,

    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
    createdBy: "AUTHORIZED_OPERATOR"
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   625. SAVE TRANSACTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14SaveTransaction(record) {
  if (!record || !record.id) {
    throw new Error("Transaction invalide.");
  }

  const records = pbl6GetWeighingRecords();

  const index = records.findIndex(function(item) {
    return String(item.id) === String(record.id);
  });

  if (index >= 0) {
    records[index] = Object.assign(
      {},
      records[index],
      record,
      { updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp() }
    );
  } else {
    records.push(record);
  }

  pbl6SaveWeighingRecords(records);

  return record;
}


/* ============================================================
   626. START NEW TRANSACTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14StartTransaction(data, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const transaction =
    pbl14BuildTransaction(data, token);

  const existing =
    pbl14FindActiveVehicleSession(
      transaction.truckRegistration
    );

  if (existing) {
    throw new Error(
      "Ce camion possède déjà une pesée en cours."
    );
  }

  transaction.status = PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.CREATED;

  pbl14SaveTransaction(transaction);

  writeAuditLog(
    "WEIGHING_TRANSACTION_STARTED",
    "Nouvelle transaction : " + transaction.id
  );

  if (typeof pbl13RegisterActivity === "function") {
    pbl13RegisterActivity(
      "WEIGHING_STARTED",
      "Nouvelle pesée démarrée",
      { transactionId: transaction.id }
    );
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    transaction: transaction
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   627. RECORD FIRST WEIGHING
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14RecordFirstWeight(id, weightKg, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const transaction = pbl14FindTransaction(id);

  if (!transaction) {
    throw new Error("Transaction introuvable.");
  }

  if (
    transaction.status !== PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.CREATED
  ) {
    throw new Error("Première pesée déjà enregistrée.");
  }

  const weight = pbl14NormalizeWeight(weightKg);

  transaction.firstWeightKg = weight;
  transaction.firstWeighingAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  transaction.status = PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.WAITING_SECOND;

  pbl14SaveTransaction(transaction);

  writeAuditLog(
    "FIRST_WEIGHT_RECORDED",
    transaction.id + " : " + weight + " kg"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    transaction: transaction,
    firstWeightKg: weight
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   628. RECORD SECOND WEIGHING
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14RecordSecondWeight(id, weightKg, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const transaction = pbl14FindTransaction(id);

  if (!transaction) {
    throw new Error("Transaction introuvable.");
  }

  if (
    transaction.status !== PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.WAITING_SECOND
  ) {
    throw new Error("La transaction n'attend pas la seconde pesée.");
  }

  const weight = pbl14NormalizeWeight(weightKg);

  const first = pbl14NormalizeWeight(
    transaction.firstWeightKg
  );

  const result = pbl14CalculateNetWeight(
    Math.max(first, weight),
    Math.min(first, weight)
  );

  transaction.secondWeightKg = weight;

  transaction.grossKg = result.grossKg;
  transaction.tareKg = result.tareKg;
  transaction.netKg = result.netKg;
  transaction.netTonnes = result.netTonnes;

  transaction.secondWeighingAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  transaction.status = PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.COMPLETED;

  pbl14SaveTransaction(transaction);

  writeAuditLog(
    "SECOND_WEIGHT_RECORDED",
    transaction.id + " : net " + result.netKg + " kg"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    transaction: transaction,
    calculation: result
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   629. TRANSACTION COMPLETION CHECK
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14IsTransactionComplete(transaction) {
  return Boolean(
    transaction &&
    transaction.firstWeightKg !== null &&
    transaction.secondWeightKg !== null &&
    transaction.grossKg !== null &&
    transaction.tareKg !== null &&
    transaction.netKg !== null &&
    transaction.status === PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.COMPLETED
  );
}


/* ============================================================
   630. VALIDATE COMPLETED TRANSACTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14ValidateCompletedTransaction(transaction) {
  if (!pbl14IsTransactionComplete(transaction)) {
    throw new Error("Transaction de pesage incomplète.");
  }

  const calculated = pbl14CalculateNetWeight(
    transaction.grossKg,
    transaction.tareKg
  );

  if (
    Math.abs(calculated.netKg - transaction.netKg) > 0.01
  ) {
    throw new Error("Incohérence dans le poids net.");
  }

  return {
    valid: true,
    calculation: calculated
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   631. CHECK DUPLICATE TICKET NUMBER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14TicketNumberExists(ticketNumber) {
  if (!ticketNumber) return false;

  const tickets = getAllTickets();

  return tickets.some(function(ticket) {
    return String(ticket.ticketNumber) ===
      String(ticketNumber) &&
      ticket.status !== "DELETED";
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   632. GENERATE UNIQUE TICKET NUMBER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14GenerateUniqueTicketNumber() {
  const prefix = Utilities.formatDate(
    new Date(),
    "Africa/Casablanca",
    "yyyyMMdd"
  );

  let counter = 1;
  let candidate = "";

  do {
    candidate = prefix + "-" +
      String(counter).padStart(4, "0");

    counter++;
  } while (
    pbl14TicketNumberExists(candidate) &&
    counter < 100000
  );

  if (counter >= 100000) {
    throw new Error("Impossible de générer un numéro unique.");
  }

  return candidate;
}


/* ============================================================
   633. BUILD FINAL TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14BuildFinalTicket(transaction, ticketNumber) {
  pbl14ValidateCompletedTransaction(transaction);

  return {
    id: createUniqueId(),
    ticketNumber: ticketNumber,

    date: getLiveDate(),
    time: getLiveTime(),

    product: transaction.product,
    client: transaction.client,
    truckRegistration: transaction.truckRegistration,
    driver: transaction.driver,

    grossKg: transaction.grossKg,
    tareKg: transaction.tareKg,
    netKg: transaction.netKg,
    netTonnes: transaction.netKg / 1000,

    reference: transaction.reference,
    notes: transaction.notes,

    status: "COMPLETED",

    weighingTransactionId: transaction.id,

    createdAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
    updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   634. SAVE FINAL TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14SaveFinalTicket(ticket) {
  if (!ticket || !ticket.ticketNumber) {
    throw new Error("Ticket invalide.");
  }

  if (pbl14TicketNumberExists(ticket.ticketNumber)) {
    throw new Error("Numéro de ticket déjà utilisé.");
  }

  if (typeof saveCollection !== "function") {
    throw new Error("Stockage indisponible.");
  }

  const tickets = getAllTickets();

  tickets.push(ticket);

  saveCollection(PBL_KEYS.TICKETS, tickets);

  return ticket;
}


/* ============================================================
   635. FINALIZE TRANSACTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14FinalizeTransaction(id, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const transaction = pbl14FindTransaction(id);

  if (!transaction) {
    throw new Error("Transaction introuvable.");
  }

  pbl14ValidateCompletedTransaction(transaction);

  if (transaction.ticketNumber) {
    throw new Error("Cette transaction possède déjà un ticket.");
  }

  const ticketNumber =
    pbl14GenerateUniqueTicketNumber();

  const ticket =
    pbl14BuildFinalTicket(transaction, ticketNumber);

  pbl14SaveFinalTicket(ticket);

  transaction.ticketNumber = ticketNumber;
  transaction.ticketCreatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  transaction.finalized = true;

  pbl14SaveTransaction(transaction);

  writeAuditLog(
    "WEIGHING_TRANSACTION_FINALIZED",
    "Ticket " + ticketNumber + " créé"
  );

  if (typeof pbl13RegisterActivity === "function") {
    pbl13RegisterActivity(
      "TICKET_CREATED",
      "Ticket " + ticketNumber + " créé",
      { ticketNumber: ticketNumber }
    );
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    ticket: ticket,
    transaction: transaction
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   636. CANCEL TRANSACTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14CancelTransaction(id, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const transaction = pbl14FindTransaction(id);

  if (!transaction) {
    throw new Error("Transaction introuvable.");
  }

  if (transaction.finalized === true) {
    throw new Error(
      "Un ticket finalisé ne peut pas être annulé ici."
    );
  }

  const cancellationReason =
    cleanText(reason || "");

  if (cancellationReason.length < 5) {
    throw new Error("Motif d'annulation obligatoire.");
  }

  transaction.status = PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.CANCELLED;
  transaction.cancelledAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  transaction.cancellationReason = cancellationReason;

  pbl14SaveTransaction(transaction);

  writeAuditLog(
    "WEIGHING_TRANSACTION_CANCELLED",
    transaction.id + " : " + cancellationReason
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    transaction: transaction
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   637. GET TRANSACTION DETAILS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14GetTransactionDetails(id) {
  const transaction = pbl14FindTransaction(id);

  if (!transaction) {
    throw new Error("Transaction introuvable.");
  }

  return {
    transaction: transaction,
    complete: pbl14IsTransactionComplete(transaction),
    canFinalize:
      pbl14IsTransactionComplete(transaction) &&
      transaction.finalized !== true,
    canCancel:
      transaction.finalized !== true &&
      transaction.status !== PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.CANCELLED
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   638. GET TRANSACTION HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14GetTransactionHistory(filters) {
  const records = pbl6GetWeighingRecords();

  filters = filters || {};

  let result = records.slice();

  if (filters.status) {
    result = result.filter(function(record) {
      return record.status === filters.status;
    });
  }

  if (filters.product) {
    result = result.filter(function(record) {
      return record.product === filters.product;
    });
  }

  if (filters.client) {
    result = result.filter(function(record) {
      return record.client === filters.client;
    });
  }

  if (filters.truckRegistration) {
    const registration =
      pbl14NormalizeRegistration(filters.truckRegistration);

    result = result.filter(function(record) {
      return pbl14NormalizeRegistration(
        record.truckRegistration
      ) === registration;
    });
  }

  return result.reverse();
}


/* ============================================================
   639. GET PENDING TRANSACTIONS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14GetPendingTransactions() {
  return pbl14GetActiveTransactions();
}


/* ============================================================
   640. TRANSACTION COUNTS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14GetTransactionCounts() {
  const records = pbl6GetWeighingRecords();

  return {
    total: records.length,

    created: records.filter(function(item) {
      return item.status === PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.CREATED;
    }).length,

    waitingSecond: records.filter(function(item) {
      return item.status === PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.WAITING_SECOND;
    }).length,

    completed: records.filter(function(item) {
      return item.status === PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.COMPLETED;
    }).length,

    cancelled: records.filter(function(item) {
      return item.status === PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.CANCELLED;
    }).length
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   641. CHECK SESSION EXPIRATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14IsSessionExpired(record) {
  if (!record || !record.createdAt) return false;

  const created = new Date(record.createdAt);

  if (isNaN(created.getTime())) return false;

  const age = Date.now() - created.getTime();

  return age >
    PBL14_CONFIG.SESSION_EXPIRY_HOURS * 60 * 60 * 1000;
}


/* ============================================================
   642. EXPIRED SESSION REPORT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14GetExpiredSessions() {
  return pbl14GetActiveTransactions().filter(function(record) {
    return pbl14IsSessionExpired(record);
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   643. SESSION EXPIRATION ALERT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14CreateExpiredSessionAlerts() {
  const expired = pbl14GetExpiredSessions();

  expired.forEach(function(record) {
    pbl13CreateAlert(
      "Pesée en attente depuis longtemps",
      "Transaction " + record.id +
        " : camion " + record.truckRegistration,
      "WARNING"
    );
  });

  return {
    success: true,
    expiredSessions: expired.length
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   644. TRANSACTION WEIGHT SUMMARY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14GetWeightSummary(id) {
  const transaction = pbl14FindTransaction(id);

  if (!transaction) {
    throw new Error("Transaction introuvable.");
  }

  return {
    firstWeightKg: transaction.firstWeightKg,
    secondWeightKg: transaction.secondWeightKg,
    grossKg: transaction.grossKg,
    tareKg: transaction.tareKg,
    netKg: transaction.netKg,
    netTonnes: transaction.netKg === null
      ? null
      : transaction.netKg / 1000
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   645. LIVE WEIGHING SCREEN DATA
   ============================================================ */

    ? pbl14GetTransactionDetails(id)
    : null;

  return {
    application: "PONT BASCULE LIVE",
    date: getLiveDate(),

    station: pbl12GetStationStatus(),
    manualEntry:
      pbl12GetManualEntryStatus(),
    activeTransactions:
      pbl14GetActiveTransactions().length,
    selectedTransaction: transaction
  };
}

   ============================================================ */

    pbl14FindActiveVehicleSession(registration);

  return {
    exists: Boolean(existing),
    transaction: existing
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   659. TRANSACTION VALIDATION REPORT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14GetValidationReport() {
  const records = pbl6GetWeighingRecords();

  const invalid = [];

  records.forEach(function(record) {
    try {
      if (
        record.status === PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.COMPLETED
      ) {
        pbl14ValidateCompletedTransaction(record);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      invalid.push({
        id: record.id,
        error: error.message
      });
    }
  });

  return {
    valid: invalid.length === 0,
    totalTransactions: records.length,
    invalidTransactions: invalid
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   660. TRANSACTION VALIDATION API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl14GetValidationReport() {
  return pbl14GetValidationReport();
}


/* ============================================================
   661. TRANSACTION OPERATIONAL SUMMARY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14GetOperationalSummary() {
  const counts = pbl14GetTransactionCounts();

  return {
    total: counts.total,
    pending:
      counts.created + counts.waitingSecond,
    completed: counts.completed,
    cancelled: counts.cancelled,
    expired: pbl14GetExpiredSessions().length,
    date: getLiveDate(),
    time: getLiveTime()
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   662. TRANSACTION OPERATIONAL API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl14GetOperationalSummary() {
  return pbl14GetOperationalSummary();
}


/* ============================================================
   663. TICKET DATA CONSISTENCY CHECK
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14CheckTicketConsistency(ticket) {
  if (!ticket) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      reason: "MISSING_TICKET"
    };
  }

  try {
    const result = pbl14CalculateNetWeight(
      ticket.grossKg,
      ticket.tareKg
    );

    return {
      valid:
        Math.abs(result.netKg - toNumber(ticket.netKg)) <= 0.01,
      expectedNetKg: result.netKg,
      storedNetKg: ticket.netKg
    };

  } catch (error) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      reason: error.message
    };
  }
}


/* ============================================================
   664. FULL TICKET CONSISTENCY REPORT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14GetTicketConsistencyReport() {
  const tickets = getAllTickets();

  const invalid = [];

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const result = pbl14CheckTicketConsistency(ticket);

    if (!result.valid) {
      invalid.push({
        ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber,
        result: result
      });
    }
  });

  return {
    total: tickets.length,
    valid: tickets.length - invalid.length,
    invalid: invalid.length,
    invalidTickets: invalid
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   665. API TICKET CONSISTENCY REPORT
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl14GetTicketConsistencyReport() {
  return pbl14GetTicketConsistencyReport();
}


/* ============================================================
   666. TRANSACTION LOCK CHECK
   ============================================================ */

function pbl14CheckTransactionLock(id) {
  const transaction = pbl14FindTransaction(id);

  if (!transaction) {
    throw new Error("Transaction introuvable.");
  }

  return {
    locked: transaction.finalized === true ||
      transaction.status === PBL14_CONFIG.STATUS.CANCELLED,
    finalized: transaction.finalized === true,
    status: transaction.status
  };
}

/***************************************************************
 * PONT BASCULE LIVE
 * WEIGHBRIDGE MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
 * PART 15 — ADVANCED TICKET MANAGEMENT
 *
 * Functions 671–730
 *
 * Ticket management
 * Search and filtering
 * Controlled modification
 * Cancellation and restoration
 * Reprinting and verification
 ***************************************************************/


/* ============================================================
   671. PART 15 CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================ */

const PBL15_CONFIG = {
  VERSION: "15.0.0",
  ACTIVE_STATUS: "COMPLETED",
  CANCELLED_STATUS: "CANCELLED",
  DELETED_STATUS: "DELETED",
  MAX_SEARCH_RESULTS: 500,
  MAX_HISTORY_RESULTS: 1000,
  REQUIRE_REASON_FOR_EDIT: true,
  REQUIRE_REASON_FOR_CANCEL: true,
  REQUIRE_PIN_FOR_REPRINT: false
};


/* ============================================================
   672. GET TICKET COLLECTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15GetTickets() {
  const tickets = getAllTickets();

  if (!Array.isArray(tickets)) {
    throw new Error("La collection des tickets est invalide.");
  }

  return tickets;
}


/* ============================================================
   673. SAVE TICKET COLLECTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15SaveTickets(tickets) {
  if (!Array.isArray(tickets)) {
    throw new Error("Liste de tickets invalide.");
  }

  saveCollection(PBL_KEYS.TICKETS, tickets);

  return tickets.length;
}


/* ============================================================
   674. FIND TICKET BY ID
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15FindTicketById(id) {
  if (!id) return null;

  return pbl15GetTickets().find(function(ticket) {
    return String(ticket.id) === String(id);
  }) || null;
}


/* ============================================================
   675. FIND TICKET BY NUMBER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15FindTicketByNumber(number) {
  if (!number) return null;

  const normalized = String(number).trim().toUpperCase();

  return pbl15GetTickets().find(function(ticket) {
    return String(ticket.ticketNumber || "")
      .trim()
      .toUpperCase() === normalized;
  }) || null;
}


/* ============================================================
   676. CHECK TICKET STATUS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15IsTicketActive(ticket) {
  if (!ticket) return false;

  return ticket.status !== PBL15_CONFIG.CANCELLED_STATUS &&
    ticket.status !== PBL15_CONFIG.DELETED_STATUS &&
    ticket.deleted !== true;
}


/* ============================================================
   677. GET ACTIVE TICKETS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15GetActiveTickets() {
  return pbl15GetTickets().filter(function(ticket) {
    return pbl15IsTicketActive(ticket);
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   678. GET CANCELLED TICKETS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15GetCancelledTickets() {
  return pbl15GetTickets().filter(function(ticket) {
    return ticket.status === PBL15_CONFIG.CANCELLED_STATUS ||
      ticket.status === PBL15_CONFIG.DELETED_STATUS ||
      ticket.deleted === true;
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   679. NORMALIZE SEARCH TEXT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15NormalizeSearch(value) {
  return cleanText(value || "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .normalize("NFD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
    .trim();
}


/* ============================================================
   680. SEARCH TICKET TEXT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15TicketMatchesText(ticket, query) {
  const search = pbl15NormalizeSearch(query);

  if (!search) return true;

  const fields = [
    ticket.ticketNumber,
    ticket.product,
    ticket.client,
    ticket.truckRegistration,
    ticket.driver,
    ticket.reference,
    ticket.notes,
    ticket.date,
    ticket.time
  ];

  return fields.some(function(value) {
    return pbl15NormalizeSearch(value).includes(search);
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   681. SEARCH TICKETS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15SearchTickets(query, options) {
  options = options || {};

  const includeCancelled =
    options.includeCancelled === true;

  const source = includeCancelled
    ? pbl15GetTickets()
    : pbl15GetActiveTickets();

  const results = source.filter(function(ticket) {
    return pbl15TicketMatchesText(ticket, query);
  });

  const limit = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(
      Number(options.limit) || 100,
      PBL15_CONFIG.MAX_SEARCH_RESULTS
    )
  );

  return results.slice(0, limit);
}


/* ============================================================
   682. FILTER BY TICKET DATE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15FilterByDate(tickets, startDate, endDate) {
  if (!startDate && !endDate) return tickets;

  return tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
    const ticketDate = pbl15ParseDate(ticket.date);

    if (!ticketDate) return false;

    if (startDate) {
      const start = pbl15ParseDate(startDate);

      if (start && ticketDate < start) return false;
    }

    if (endDate) {
      const end = pbl15ParseDate(endDate);
      if (end) {
        end.setHours(23, 59, 59, 999);

        if (ticketDate > end) return false;
      }
    }

    return true;
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   683. PARSE DATE SAFELY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15ParseDate(value) {
  if (!value) return null;

  if (value instanceof Date) {
    return isNaN(value.getTime())
      ? null
      : new Date(value.getTime());
  }

  const textValue = String(value).trim();

  let match = textValue.match(
    /^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/
  );

  if (match) {
    return new Date(
      Number(match[3]),
      Number(match[2]) - 1,
      Number(match[1])
    );
  }

  match = textValue.match(
    /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/
  );

  if (match) {
    return new Date(
      Number(match[1]),
      Number(match[2]) - 1,
      Number(match[3])
    );
  }

  const parsed = new Date(textValue);

  return isNaN(parsed.getTime()) ? null : parsed;
}


/* ============================================================
   684. FILTER BY PRODUCT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15FilterByProduct(tickets, product) {
  if (!product) return tickets;

  const search = pbl15NormalizeSearch(product);

  return tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
    return pbl15NormalizeSearch(ticket.product) === search;
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   685. FILTER BY CLIENT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15FilterByClient(tickets, client) {
  if (!client) return tickets;

  const search = pbl15NormalizeSearch(client);

  return tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
    return pbl15NormalizeSearch(ticket.client) === search;
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   686. FILTER BY TRUCK
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15FilterByTruck(tickets, registration) {
  if (!registration) return tickets;

  const search =
    pbl14NormalizeRegistration(registration);

  return tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
    return pbl14NormalizeRegistration(
      ticket.truckRegistration
    ) === search;
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   687. ADVANCED TICKET FILTER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15AdvancedFilter(filters) {
  filters = filters || {};

  let results = filters.includeCancelled === true
    ? pbl15GetTickets()
    : pbl15GetActiveTickets();

  results = pbl15FilterByDate(
    results,
    filters.startDate,
    filters.endDate
  );

  results = pbl15FilterByProduct(
    results,
    filters.product
  );

  results = pbl15FilterByClient(
    results,
    filters.client
  );

  results = pbl15FilterByTruck(
    results,
    filters.truckRegistration
  );

  if (filters.query) {
    results = results.filter(function(ticket) {
      return pbl15TicketMatchesText(
        ticket,
        filters.query
      );
    });
  }

  if (filters.minNetKg !== undefined &&
      filters.minNetKg !== "") {
    results = results.filter(function(ticket) {
      return toNumber(ticket.netKg) >=
        Number(filters.minNetKg);
    });
  }

  if (filters.maxNetKg !== undefined &&
      filters.maxNetKg !== "") {
    results = results.filter(function(ticket) {
      return toNumber(ticket.netKg) <=
        Number(filters.maxNetKg);
    });
  }

  return results.sort(function(a, b) {
    const dateA = pbl15ParseDate(a.date);
    const dateB = pbl15ParseDate(b.date);

    return (dateB ? dateB.getTime() : 0) -
      (dateA ? dateA.getTime() : 0);
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   688. API SEARCH TICKETS
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl15SearchTickets(query, options) {
  return pbl15SearchTickets(query, options);
}


/* ============================================================
   689. API ADVANCED FILTER
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl15AdvancedFilter(filters) {
  return pbl15AdvancedFilter(filters);
}


/* ============================================================
   690. GET TICKET DETAILS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15GetTicketDetails(id) {
  const ticket = pbl15FindTicketById(id);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket introuvable.");
  }

  return {
    ticket: ticket,
    active: pbl15IsTicketActive(ticket),
    consistency: pbl14CheckTicketConsistency(ticket)
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   691. API TICKET DETAILS
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl15GetTicketDetails(id) {
  return pbl15GetTicketDetails(id);
}


/* ============================================================
   692. VALIDATE TICKET EDIT DATA
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15ValidateTicketEdit(data) {
  if (!data || typeof data !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Modification invalide.");
  }

  const result = {
    product: cleanText(data.product || ""),
    client: cleanText(data.client || ""),
    truckRegistration:
      pbl14ValidateRegistration(data.truckRegistration),
    driver: cleanText(data.driver || ""),
    reference: cleanText(data.reference || ""),
    notes: cleanText(data.notes || "")
  };

  if (!result.product || !result.client) {
    throw new Error("Produit et client obligatoires.");
  }

  if (result.notes.length > 1000) {
    throw new Error("Observations trop longues.");
  }

  pbl14ValidateReferences(result);

  return result;
}


/* ============================================================
   693. UPDATE TICKET INFORMATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15UpdateTicket(id, data, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const tickets = pbl15GetTickets();

  const index = tickets.findIndex(function(ticket) {
    return String(ticket.id) === String(id);
  });

  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error("Ticket introuvable.");
  }

  const ticket = tickets[index];

  if (!pbl15IsTicketActive(ticket)) {
    throw new Error("Impossible de modifier un ticket annulé.");
  }

  const editReason = cleanText(reason || "");

  if (
    PBL15_CONFIG.REQUIRE_REASON_FOR_EDIT &&
    editReason.length < 5
  ) {
    throw new Error("Motif de modification obligatoire.");
  }

  const validated = pbl15ValidateTicketEdit(data);

  const previous = {
    product: ticket.product,
    client: ticket.client,
    truckRegistration: ticket.truckRegistration,
    driver: ticket.driver,
    reference: ticket.reference,
    notes: ticket.notes
  };

  Object.assign(ticket, validated);

  ticket.updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  ticket.lastEditReason = editReason;

  ticket.editHistory = Array.isArray(ticket.editHistory)
    ? ticket.editHistory
    : [];

  ticket.editHistory.push({
    date: getLiveTimestamp(),
    reason: editReason,
    previous: previous,
    updated: validated
  });

  tickets[index] = ticket;

  pbl15SaveTickets(tickets);

  writeAuditLog(
    "TICKET_INFORMATION_UPDATED",
    "Ticket " + ticket.ticketNumber + " : " + editReason
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    ticket: ticket
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   694. API UPDATE TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl15UpdateTicket(id, data, reason, token) {
  return pbl15UpdateTicket(id, data, reason, token);
}


/* ============================================================
   695. CANCEL TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15CancelTicket(id, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const tickets = pbl15GetTickets();

  const index = tickets.findIndex(function(ticket) {
    return String(ticket.id) === String(id);
  });

  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error("Ticket introuvable.");
  }

  const ticket = tickets[index];

  if (!pbl15IsTicketActive(ticket)) {
    throw new Error("Ticket déjà annulé.");
  }

  const cancellationReason = cleanText(reason || "");

  if (
    PBL15_CONFIG.REQUIRE_REASON_FOR_CANCEL &&
    cancellationReason.length < 5
  ) {
    throw new Error("Motif d'annulation obligatoire.");
  }

  ticket.status = PBL15_CONFIG.CANCELLED_STATUS;
  ticket.cancelled = true;
  ticket.cancelledAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  ticket.cancellationReason = cancellationReason;

  tickets[index] = ticket;

  pbl15SaveTickets(tickets);

  writeAuditLog(
    "TICKET_CANCELLED",
    ticket.ticketNumber + " : " + cancellationReason
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    ticket: ticket
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   696. API CANCEL TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl15CancelTicket(id, reason, token) {
  return pbl15CancelTicket(id, reason, token);
}


/* ============================================================
   697. RESTORE CANCELLED TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15RestoreTicket(id, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const tickets = pbl15GetTickets();

  const index = tickets.findIndex(function(ticket) {
    return String(ticket.id) === String(id);
  });

  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error("Ticket introuvable.");
  }

  const ticket = tickets[index];

  if (ticket.status !== PBL15_CONFIG.CANCELLED_STATUS) {
    throw new Error("Ce ticket n'est pas annulé.");
  }

  const restorationReason = cleanText(reason || "");

  if (restorationReason.length < 5) {
    throw new Error("Motif de restauration obligatoire.");
  }

  ticket.status = PBL15_CONFIG.ACTIVE_STATUS;
  ticket.cancelled = false;
  ticket.restoredAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  ticket.restorationReason = restorationReason;

  tickets[index] = ticket;

  pbl15SaveTickets(tickets);

  writeAuditLog(
    "TICKET_RESTORED",
    ticket.ticketNumber + " : " + restorationReason
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    ticket: ticket
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   698. API RESTORE TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl15RestoreTicket(id, reason, token) {
  return pbl15RestoreTicket(id, reason, token);
}


/* ============================================================
   699. DELETE TICKET RECORD
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15SoftDeleteTicket(id, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const tickets = pbl15GetTickets();

  const index = tickets.findIndex(function(ticket) {
    return String(ticket.id) === String(id);
  });

  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error("Ticket introuvable.");
  }

  const deletionReason = cleanText(reason || "");

  if (deletionReason.length < 5) {
    throw new Error("Motif de suppression obligatoire.");
  }

  tickets[index].status = PBL15_CONFIG.DELETED_STATUS;
  tickets[index].deleted = true;
  tickets[index].deletedAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  tickets[index].deletionReason = deletionReason;

  pbl15SaveTickets(tickets);

  writeAuditLog(
    "TICKET_SOFT_DELETED",
    tickets[index].ticketNumber + " : " + deletionReason
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    ticket: tickets[index]
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   700. API SOFT DELETE
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl15SoftDeleteTicket(id, reason, token) {
  return pbl15SoftDeleteTicket(id, reason, token);
}


/* ============================================================
   701. GET TICKET EDIT HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15GetTicketEditHistory(id) {
  const ticket = pbl15FindTicketById(id);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket introuvable.");
  }

  return Array.isArray(ticket.editHistory)
    ? ticket.editHistory
    : [];
}


/* ============================================================
   702. API TICKET EDIT HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl15GetTicketEditHistory(id) {
  return pbl15GetTicketEditHistory(id);
}


/* ============================================================
   703. CHECK DUPLICATE TICKET NUMBER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15FindDuplicateTicketNumbers() {
  const tickets = pbl15GetTickets();
  const grouped = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const number = String(
      ticket.ticketNumber || ""
    ).trim();

    if (!number) return;

    grouped[number] = grouped[number] || [];
    grouped[number].push(ticket.id);
  });

  return Object.keys(grouped)
    .filter(function(number) {
      return grouped[number].length > 1;
    })
    .map(function(number) {
      return {
        ticketNumber: number,
        ticketIds: grouped[number],
        count: grouped[number].length
      };
    });
}


/* ============================================================
   704. TICKET NUMBER VERIFICATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15VerifyTicketNumber(number) {
  const ticket = pbl15FindTicketByNumber(number);

  if (!ticket) {
    return {
      found: false,
      valid: false,
      message: "Ticket introuvable."
    };
  }

  const consistency =
    pbl14CheckTicketConsistency(ticket);

  return {
    found: true,
    valid:
      consistency.valid &&
      pbl15IsTicketActive(ticket),
    ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber,
    status: ticket.status,
    consistency: consistency,
    ticket: ticket
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   705. API VERIFY TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl15VerifyTicketNumber(number) {
  return pbl15VerifyTicketNumber(number);
}


/* ============================================================
   706. CREATE REPRINT RECORD
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15CreateReprintRecord(ticket, reason, operator) {
  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket introuvable.");
  }

  const record = {
    id: createUniqueId(),
    recordType: "PBL_REPRINT_HISTORY",
    ticketId: ticket.id,
    ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber,
    reason: cleanText(reason || "Réimpression"),
    operator: cleanText(operator || "OPERATOR"),
    printedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };

  pbl13InsertRecord(
    PBL13_STORAGE.PRINT_HISTORY,
    record
  );

  return record;
}


/* ============================================================
   707. PREPARE TICKET REPRINT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15PrepareReprint(id, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const ticket = pbl15FindTicketById(id);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket introuvable.");
  }

  const printReason = cleanText(reason || "");

  if (printReason.length < 3) {
    throw new Error("Motif de réimpression obligatoire.");
  }

  const printRecord = pbl15CreateReprintRecord(
    ticket,
    printReason,
    "AUTHORIZED_OPERATOR"
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "TICKET_REPRINT_PREPARED",
    ticket.ticketNumber + " : " + printReason
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    ticket: ticket,
    printRecord: printRecord,
    printableData: getPrintableTicket(ticket.id)
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   708. API PREPARE REPRINT
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl15PrepareReprint(id, reason, token) {
  return pbl15PrepareReprint(id, reason, token);
}


/* ============================================================
   709. GET REPRINT HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15GetReprintHistory(ticketId) {
  const records = pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.PRINT_HISTORY
  );

  return records.filter(function(record) {
    return record.ticketId === ticketId ||
      record.recordType === "PBL_REPRINT_HISTORY" &&
      record.ticketId === ticketId;
  }).reverse();
}


/* ============================================================
   710. API REPRINT HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl15GetReprintHistory(ticketId) {
  return pbl15GetReprintHistory(ticketId);
}


/* ============================================================
   711. GET TICKET DAILY COUNTS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15GetDailyTicketCounts(date) {
  const targetDate = date || getLiveDate();

  const tickets = pbl15GetTickets().filter(function(ticket) {
    return ticket.date === targetDate;
  });

  return {
    date: targetDate,
    total: tickets.length,
    active: tickets.filter(pbl15IsTicketActive).length,
    cancelled: tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
      return !pbl15IsTicketActive(ticket);
    }).length
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   712. GET PRODUCT TICKET COUNTS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15GetProductTicketCounts() {
  const grouped = {};

  pbl15GetActiveTickets().forEach(function(ticket) {
    const product = ticket.product || "Non défini";

    grouped[product] = grouped[product] || {
      product: product,
      voyages: 0,
      netKg: 0,
      netTonnes: 0
    };

    grouped[product].voyages++;
    grouped[product].netKg += toNumber(ticket.netKg);
    grouped[product].netTonnes =
      grouped[product].netKg / 1000;
  });

  return Object.keys(grouped).map(function(key) {
    return grouped[key];
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   713. GET CLIENT TICKET COUNTS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15GetClientTicketCounts() {
  const grouped = {};

  pbl15GetActiveTickets().forEach(function(ticket) {
    const client = ticket.client || "Non défini";

    grouped[client] = grouped[client] || {
      client: client,
      voyages: 0,
      netKg: 0,
      netTonnes: 0
    };

    grouped[client].voyages++;
    grouped[client].netKg += toNumber(ticket.netKg);
    grouped[client].netTonnes =
      grouped[client].netKg / 1000;
  });

  return Object.keys(grouped).map(function(key) {
    return grouped[key];
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   714. GET TRUCK TICKET COUNTS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15GetTruckTicketCounts() {
  const grouped = {};

  pbl15GetActiveTickets().forEach(function(ticket) {
    const truck =
      ticket.truckRegistration || "Non défini";

    grouped[truck] = grouped[truck] || {
      truckRegistration: truck,
      voyages: 0,
      netKg: 0,
      netTonnes: 0
    };

    grouped[truck].voyages++;
    grouped[truck].netKg += toNumber(ticket.netKg);
    grouped[truck].netTonnes =
      grouped[truck].netKg / 1000;
  });

  return Object.keys(grouped).map(function(key) {
    return grouped[key];
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   715. GET TICKET MANAGEMENT SUMMARY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15GetManagementSummary() {
  const tickets = pbl15GetTickets();
  const active = pbl15GetActiveTickets();
  const cancelled = pbl15GetCancelledTickets();

  const totalKg = active.reduce(function(sum, ticket) {
    return sum + toNumber(ticket.netKg);
  }, 0);

  return {
    totalTickets: tickets.length,
    activeTickets: active.length,
    cancelledTickets: cancelled.length,
    totalNetKg: totalKg,
    totalNetTonnes: totalKg / 1000,
    duplicateNumbers:
      pbl15FindDuplicateTicketNumbers().length,
    date: getLiveDate(),
    time: getLiveTime()
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   716. API MANAGEMENT SUMMARY
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl15GetManagementSummary() {
  return pbl15GetManagementSummary();
}

/* ============================================================
   721. TICKET DATE FORMAT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15FormatTicketDate(value) {
  const date = pbl15ParseDate(value);

  if (!date) return "";

  return Utilities.formatDate(
    date,
    "Africa/Casablanca",
    "dd/MM/yyyy"
  );
}


/* ============================================================
   722. TICKET TIME FORMAT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15FormatTicketTime(value) {
  if (!value) return "";

  const date = new Date(value);

  if (isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    return String(value);
  }

  return Utilities.formatDate(
    date,
    "Africa/Casablanca",
    "HH:mm:ss"
  );
}


/* ============================================================
   723. TICKET DISPLAY OBJECT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl15BuildTicketDisplay(ticket) {
  return {
    id: ticket.id,
    ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber,
    date: pbl15FormatTicketDate(ticket.date),
    time: ticket.time,
    product: ticket.product,
    client: ticket.client,
    truckRegistration: ticket.truckRegistration,
    driver: ticket.driver,
    grossKg: toNumber(ticket.grossKg),
    tareKg: toNumber(ticket.tareKg),
    netKg: toNumber(ticket.netKg),
    netTonnes: toNumber(ticket.netKg) / 1000,
    status: ticket.status,
    active: pbl15IsTicketActive(ticket)
  };
}

/***************************************************************
 * PONT BASCULE LIVE
 * WEIGHBRIDGE MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
 * PART 16 — SCALE CONNECTION & LIVE WEIGHT ENGINE
 *
 * Functions 731–790
 *
 * Live weight monitoring
 * Stable weight detection
 * Connection diagnostics
 * Weight capture
 * Protected manual weighing
 ***************************************************************/


/* ============================================================
   731. PART 16 CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================ */

const PBL16_CONFIG = {
  VERSION: "16.0.0",

  CONNECTION: {
    DISCONNECTED: "DISCONNECTED",
    CONNECTING: "CONNECTING",
    CONNECTED: "CONNECTED",
    ERROR: "ERROR",
    SIMULATION: "SIMULATION"
  },

  WEIGHT: {
    UNIT: "KG",
    MAXIMUM_KG: 1000000,
    MINIMUM_KG: 0,
    STABILITY_TOLERANCE_KG: 2,
    STABILITY_REQUIRED_READINGS: 5,
    STABILITY_INTERVAL_MS: 500,
    MAX_READING_AGE_SECONDS: 5
  },

  MANUAL: {
    DEFAULT_ENABLED: false,
    REQUIRE_REASON: true,
    REQUIRE_AUTHORIZATION: true
  }
};


/* ============================================================
   732. CREATE SCALE STATE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16CreateScaleState() {
  return {
    status: PBL16_CONFIG.CONNECTION.DISCONNECTED,
    connected: false,
    simulation: false,
    currentWeightKg: 0,
    stable: false,
    unit: "KG",
    lastReadingAt: null,
    lastError: null,
    readingsCount: 0,
    updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   733. GET SCALE STATE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16GetScaleState() {
  const state = pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  );

  const record = state.find(function(item) {
    return item.recordType === "PBL16_SCALE_STATE";
  });

  return record || pbl16CreateScaleState();
}


/* ============================================================
   734. SAVE SCALE STATE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16SaveScaleState(scaleState) {
  const records = pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  );

  const index = records.findIndex(function(item) {
    return item.recordType === "PBL16_SCALE_STATE";
  });

  const saved = Object.assign({}, scaleState, {
    recordType: "PBL16_SCALE_STATE",
    updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  });

  if (index >= 0) {
    records[index] = saved;
  } else {
    records.push(saved);
  }

  pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    records
  );

  return saved;
}


/* ============================================================
   735. VALIDATE SCALE READING
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16ValidateReading(value) {
  const weight = Number(value);

  if (!Number.isFinite(weight)) {
    throw new Error("Lecture de poids invalide.");
  }

  if (weight < PBL16_CONFIG.WEIGHT.MINIMUM_KG) {
    throw new Error("Poids négatif interdit.");
  }

  if (weight > PBL16_CONFIG.WEIGHT.MAXIMUM_KG) {
    throw new Error("Poids supérieur à la capacité configurée.");
  }

  return Math.round(weight * 100) / 100;
}


/* ============================================================
   736. REGISTER SCALE READING
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16RegisterReading(weightKg, source) {
  const weight = pbl16ValidateReading(weightKg);

  const state = pbl16GetScaleState();

  state.currentWeightKg = weight;
  state.lastReadingAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  state.readingsCount =
    Number(state.readingsCount || 0) + 1;

  state.source = source || "SCALE";

  pbl16SaveScaleState(state);

  return {
    weightKg: weight,
    weightTonnes: weight / 1000,
    unit: "KG",
    timestamp: state.lastReadingAt
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   737. REGISTER SCALE CONNECTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16SetConnectionStatus(status, details) {
  const allowed = Object.keys(
    PBL16_CONFIG.CONNECTION
  ).map(function(key) {
    return PBL16_CONFIG.CONNECTION[key];
  });

  if (allowed.indexOf(status) === -1) {
    throw new Error("État de connexion inconnu.");
  }

  const state = pbl16GetScaleState();

  state.status = status;
  state.connected =
    status === PBL16_CONFIG.CONNECTION.CONNECTED;

  state.simulation =
    status === PBL16_CONFIG.CONNECTION.SIMULATION;

  state.lastError = details
    ? cleanText(details)
    : null;

  pbl16SaveScaleState(state);

  return state;
}


/* ============================================================
   738. GET CONNECTION STATUS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16GetConnectionStatus() {
  const state = pbl16GetScaleState();

  return {
    status: state.status,
    connected: state.connected,
    simulation: state.simulation,
    lastReadingAt: state.lastReadingAt,
    lastError: state.lastError,
    updatedAt: state.updatedAt
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   739. DISCONNECT SCALE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16DisconnectScale(reason) {
  const state = pbl16GetScaleState();

  state.status = PBL16_CONFIG.CONNECTION.DISCONNECTED;
  state.connected = false;
  state.stable = false;
  state.lastError = cleanText(reason || "Déconnexion");

  pbl16SaveScaleState(state);

  writeAuditLog(
    "SCALE_DISCONNECTED",
    state.lastError
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    status: state.status
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   740. SCALE CONNECTION ERROR
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16RegisterConnectionError(error) {
  const message = error && error.message
    ? error.message
    : String(error || "Erreur inconnue");

  pbl16SetConnectionStatus(
    PBL16_CONFIG.CONNECTION.ERROR,
    message
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "SCALE_CONNECTION_ERROR",
    message
  );

  return {
    success: false,
    error: message
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   741. CREATE STABILITY BUFFER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16CreateStabilityBuffer() {
  return {
    readings: [],
    stable: false,
    averageKg: null,
    minimumKg: null,
    maximumKg: null,
    differenceKg: null,
    updatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   742. CALCULATE READING AVERAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16CalculateAverage(readings) {
  if (!Array.isArray(readings) || readings.length === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  const total = readings.reduce(function(sum, value) {
    return sum + Number(value);
  }, 0);

  return total / readings.length;
}


/* ============================================================
   743. DETECT STABLE WEIGHT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16DetectStableWeight(readings) {
  if (!Array.isArray(readings)) {
    throw new Error("Liste de lectures invalide.");
  }

  const required =
    PBL16_CONFIG.WEIGHT.STABILITY_REQUIRED_READINGS;

  if (readings.length < required) {
    return {
      stable: false,
      readingsCount: readings.length,
      requiredReadings: required,
      averageKg: null
    };
  }

  const recent = readings.slice(-required).map(
    pbl16ValidateReading
  );

  const minimum = Math.min.apply(null, recent);
  const maximum = Math.max.apply(null, recent);
  const difference = maximum - minimum;

  const average = pbl16CalculateAverage(recent);

  const stable =
    difference <=
    PBL16_CONFIG.WEIGHT.STABILITY_TOLERANCE_KG;

  return {
    stable: stable,
    readingsCount: recent.length,
    minimumKg: minimum,
    maximumKg: maximum,
    differenceKg: difference,
    averageKg: Math.round(average * 100) / 100,
    toleranceKg:
      PBL16_CONFIG.WEIGHT.STABILITY_TOLERANCE_KG
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   744. RECORD STABILITY RESULT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16UpdateStability(readings) {
  const result = pbl16DetectStableWeight(readings);

  const state = pbl16GetScaleState();

  state.stable = result.stable;
  state.stabilityResult = result;

  if (result.stable) {
    state.currentWeightKg = result.averageKg;
  }

  pbl16SaveScaleState(state);

  return result;
}


/* ============================================================
   745. CHECK READING FRESHNESS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16IsReadingFresh(timestamp) {
  if (!timestamp) return false;

  const date = new Date(timestamp);

  if (isNaN(date.getTime())) return false;

  const age = Date.now() - date.getTime();

  return age >= 0 &&
    age <=
    PBL16_CONFIG.WEIGHT.MAX_READING_AGE_SECONDS * 1000;
}


/* ============================================================
   746. GET LIVE WEIGHT DISPLAY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16GetLiveWeightDisplay() {
  const state = pbl16GetScaleState();

  const fresh =
    pbl16IsReadingFresh(state.lastReadingAt);

  return {
    application: "PONT BASCULE LIVE",
    currentWeightKg: state.currentWeightKg,
    currentWeightTonnes:
      Number(state.currentWeightKg || 0) / 1000,
    unit: "KG",
    stable: state.stable === true,
    fresh: fresh,
    connectionStatus: state.status,
    connected: state.connected === true,
    lastReadingAt: state.lastReadingAt,
    date: getLiveDate(),
    time: getLiveTime()
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   747. API LIVE WEIGHT DISPLAY
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl16GetLiveWeightDisplay() {
  return pbl16GetLiveWeightDisplay();
}


/* ============================================================
   748. API CONNECTION STATUS
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl16GetConnectionStatus() {
  return pbl16GetConnectionStatus();
}


/* ============================================================
   749. API REGISTER SCALE READING
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl16RegisterReading(weightKg, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const state = pbl16GetScaleState();

  if (
    state.status !== PBL16_CONFIG.CONNECTION.CONNECTED &&
    state.status !== PBL16_CONFIG.CONNECTION.SIMULATION
  ) {
    throw new Error("Balance non connectée.");
  }

  return pbl16RegisterReading(weightKg, "SCALE");
}


/* ============================================================
   750. CAPTURE STABLE WEIGHT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16CaptureStableWeight(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const state = pbl16GetScaleState();

  if (!state.connected && !state.simulation) {
    throw new Error("Aucune balance connectée.");
  }

  if (!state.stable) {
    throw new Error("Le poids n'est pas stable.");
  }

  if (!pbl16IsReadingFresh(state.lastReadingAt)) {
    throw new Error("Lecture de poids trop ancienne.");
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    weightKg: state.currentWeightKg,
    weightTonnes:
      state.currentWeightKg / 1000,
    capturedAt: getLiveTimestamp(),
    source: "SCALE"
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   751. API CAPTURE STABLE WEIGHT
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl16CaptureStableWeight(token) {
  return pbl16CaptureStableWeight(token);
}


/* ============================================================
   752. MANUAL ENTRY CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16GetManualEntryConfiguration() {
  if (typeof pbl12GetManualEntryStatus === "function") {
    return pbl12GetManualEntryStatus();
  }

  return {
    enabled: false,
    reasonRequired: true
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   753. CHECK MANUAL ENTRY AUTHORIZATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16RequireManualAuthorization(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const configuration =
    pbl16GetManualEntryConfiguration();

  if (
    configuration.enabled !== true &&
    configuration.manualEntryEnabled !== true
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "La saisie manuelle est désactivée."
    );
  }

  return true;
}


/* ============================================================
   754. VALIDATE MANUAL WEIGHT REASON
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16ValidateManualReason(reason) {
  const value = cleanText(reason || "");

  if (value.length < 5) {
    throw new Error(
      "Un motif détaillé est obligatoire pour la saisie manuelle."
    );
  }

  if (value.length > 500) {
    throw new Error("Motif trop long.");
  }

  return value;
}


/* ============================================================
   755. RECORD MANUAL WEIGHT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16RecordManualWeight(
  weightKg,
  reason,
  token
) {
  pbl16RequireManualAuthorization(token);

  const weight = pbl14NormalizeWeight(weightKg);
  const validatedReason =
    pbl16ValidateManualReason(reason);

  const record = {
    id: createUniqueId(),
    recordType: "PBL16_MANUAL_WEIGHT",
    weightKg: weight,
    reason: validatedReason,
    source: "MANUAL",
    createdAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };

  pbl13InsertRecord(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    record
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "MANUAL_WEIGHT_RECORDED",
    weight + " KG : " + validatedReason
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    weightKg: weight,
    weightTonnes: weight / 1000,
    source: "MANUAL",
    recordId: record.id
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   756. API MANUAL WEIGHT
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl16RecordManualWeight(
  weightKg,
  reason,
  token
) {
  return pbl16RecordManualWeight(
    weightKg,
    reason,
    token
  );
}


/* ============================================================
   757. GET MANUAL WEIGHT HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16GetManualWeightHistory() {
  return pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  ).filter(function(record) {
    return record.recordType === "PBL16_MANUAL_WEIGHT";
  }).reverse();
}


/* ============================================================
   758. API MANUAL WEIGHT HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl16GetManualWeightHistory() {
  return pbl16GetManualWeightHistory();
}


/* ============================================================
   759. SIMULATION MODE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16EnableSimulation(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  pbl16SetConnectionStatus(
    PBL16_CONFIG.CONNECTION.SIMULATION,
    "Mode de simulation activé"
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "SCALE_SIMULATION_ENABLED",
    "Simulation de balance activée"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    mode: "SIMULATION"
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   760. DISABLE SIMULATION MODE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16DisableSimulation(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  pbl16SetConnectionStatus(
    PBL16_CONFIG.CONNECTION.DISCONNECTED,
    "Mode simulation désactivé"
  );

  writeAuditLog(
    "SCALE_SIMULATION_DISABLED",
    "Simulation désactivée"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    mode: "DISCONNECTED"
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   761. SIMULATE WEIGHT READING
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16SimulateReading(weightKg, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const state = pbl16GetScaleState();

  if (!state.simulation) {
    throw new Error("Mode simulation non activé.");
  }

  const result = pbl16RegisterReading(
    weightKg,
    "SIMULATION"
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    simulated: true,
    reading: result
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   762. API SIMULATE READING
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl16SimulateReading(weightKg, token) {
  return pbl16SimulateReading(weightKg, token);
}


/* ============================================================
   763. SCALE CONNECTION DIAGNOSTICS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16GetConnectionDiagnostics() {
  const state = pbl16GetScaleState();

  return {
    status: state.status,
    connected: state.connected,
    simulation: state.simulation,
    lastReadingAt: state.lastReadingAt,
    fresh: pbl16IsReadingFresh(state.lastReadingAt),
    stable: state.stable,
    currentWeightKg: state.currentWeightKg,
    readingsCount: state.readingsCount,
    lastError: state.lastError,
    timestamp: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   764. API CONNECTION DIAGNOSTICS
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl16GetConnectionDiagnostics() {
  return pbl16GetConnectionDiagnostics();
}


/* ============================================================
   765. SCALE CAPACITY VALIDATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16ValidateScaleCapacity(
  weightKg,
  capacityKg
) {
  const weight = pbl16ValidateReading(weightKg);
  const capacity = pbl16ValidateReading(capacityKg);

  if (capacity <= 0) {
    throw new Error("Capacité de balance invalide.");
  }

  if (weight > capacity) {
    throw new Error(
      "Le poids dépasse la capacité de la balance."
    );
  }

  return {
    valid: true,
    weightKg: weight,
    capacityKg: capacity,
    remainingCapacityKg: capacity - weight
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   766. GET WEIGHT DISPLAY FORMATTED
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16FormatWeight(weightKg) {
  const weight = pbl16ValidateReading(weightKg);

  return weight.toLocaleString("fr-FR", {
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2
  }) + " KG";
}


/* ============================================================
   767. GET TONNES DISPLAY FORMATTED
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16FormatTonnes(weightKg) {
  const tonnes = pbl16ValidateReading(weightKg) / 1000;

  return tonnes.toLocaleString("fr-FR", {
    minimumFractionDigits: 3,
    maximumFractionDigits: 3
  }) + " T";
}


/* ============================================================
   768. WEIGHT SOURCE DESCRIPTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16GetWeightSourceDescription(source) {
  const descriptions = {
    SCALE: "Balance connectée",
    MANUAL: "Saisie manuelle autorisée",
    SIMULATION: "Simulation — aucune mesure réelle"
  };

  return descriptions[source] || "Source inconnue";
}

  return {
    unit: PBL16_CONFIG.WEIGHT.UNIT,

      PBL16_CONFIG.WEIGHT.MAXIMUM_KG,
    stabilityToleranceKg:
      PBL16_CONFIG.WEIGHT.STABILITY_TOLERANCE_KG,
    requiredReadings:
      PBL16_CONFIG.WEIGHT.STABILITY_REQUIRED_READINGS,
    stabilityIntervalMs:
      PBL16_CONFIG.WEIGHT.STABILITY_INTERVAL_MS,
    manualEntryDefault:
      PBL16_CONFIG.MANUAL.DEFAULT_ENABLED
  };
}
