}


/* ============================================================
   771. RESET STABILITY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16ResetStability(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const state = pbl16GetScaleState();

  state.stable = false;
  state.stabilityResult = null;

  pbl16SaveScaleState(state);

  return {
    success: true,
    stable: false
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   772. API RESET STABILITY
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl16ResetStability(token) {
  return pbl16ResetStability(token);
}


/* ============================================================
   773. CHECK SCALE BEFORE WEIGHING
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16ValidateScaleBeforeWeighing() {
  const state = pbl16GetScaleState();

  if (!state.connected && !state.simulation) {
    throw new Error("Balance non connectée.");
  }

  if (!pbl16IsReadingFresh(state.lastReadingAt)) {
    throw new Error("Aucune lecture récente.");
  }

  if (!state.stable) {
    throw new Error("Poids instable.");
  }

  return true;
}


/* ============================================================
   774. CAPTURE FIRST WEIGHT FROM SCALE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16CaptureFirstWeight(transactionId, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  pbl16ValidateScaleBeforeWeighing();

  const capture = pbl16CaptureStableWeight(token);

  return pbl14RecordFirstWeight(
    transactionId,
    capture.weightKg,
    token
  );
}


/* ============================================================
   775. CAPTURE SECOND WEIGHT FROM SCALE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl16CaptureSecondWeight(transactionId, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  pbl16ValidateScaleBeforeWeighing();

  const capture = pbl16CaptureStableWeight(token);

  return pbl14RecordSecondWeight(
    transactionId,
    capture.weightKg,
    token
  );
}

   ============================================================ */

   ============================================================ */

  const access = getAccessStatus(token);

  const authorized =
    access && access.unlocked === true;

  const configuration =
    pbl16GetManualEntryConfiguration();

  return {
    visible:
      authorized &&
      (
        configuration.enabled === true ||
        configuration.manualEntryEnabled === true
      ),
    enabled:
      configuration.enabled === true ||
      configuration.manualEntryEnabled === true,
    defaultHidden: true
  };
}

/***************************************************************
 * PONT BASCULE LIVE
 * WEIGHBRIDGE MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
 * PART 17 — DELIVERY OBJECTIVES & TARGET TRACKING
 *
 * Functions 791–850
 *
 * Target quantity
 * Cumulative tonnes
 * Number of voyages
 * Remaining quantity
 * Delivery progress
 * Objective history
 ***************************************************************/


/* ============================================================
   791. PART 17 CONFIGURATION
   ============================================================ */

const PBL17_CONFIG = {
  VERSION: "17.0.0",
  DEFAULT_UNIT: "TONNES",
  PRECISION: 3,
  OBJECTIVE_STATUSES: {
    ACTIVE: "ACTIVE",
    COMPLETED: "COMPLETED",
    PAUSED: "PAUSED",
    ARCHIVED: "ARCHIVED"
  },
  MAX_OBJECTIVES: 500
};


/* ============================================================
   792. NORMALIZE OBJECTIVE QUANTITY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17NormalizeQuantity(value) {
  const quantity = Number(value);

  if (!Number.isFinite(quantity) || quantity < 0) {
    throw new Error("Quantité objectif invalide.");
  }

  return Math.round(
    quantity * Math.pow(10, PBL17_CONFIG.PRECISION)
  ) / Math.pow(10, PBL17_CONFIG.PRECISION);
}


/* ============================================================
   793. CONVERT KG TO TONNES
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17KgToTonnes(weightKg) {
  const kg = Number(weightKg);

  if (!Number.isFinite(kg) || kg < 0) {
    throw new Error("Poids en KG invalide.");
  }

  return Math.round(
    (kg / 1000) * 1000
  ) / 1000;
}


/* ============================================================
   794. CONVERT TONNES TO KG
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17TonnesToKg(tonnes) {
  return pbl17NormalizeQuantity(tonnes) * 1000;
}


/* ============================================================
   795. GET ACTIVE OBJECTIVE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetActiveObjective() {
  if (typeof getActiveObjective === "function") {
    return getActiveObjective();
  }

  if (typeof getAllObjectives === "function") {
    const objectives = getAllObjectives();

    return objectives.find(function(objective) {
      return objective.status === "ACTIVE" &&
        objective.archived !== true;
    }) || null;
  }

  return null;
}


/* ============================================================
   796. GET OBJECTIVE TICKETS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetObjectiveTickets(objective) {
  if (!objective) return [];

  const tickets = pbl15GetActiveTickets();

  const objectiveId = objective.id;

  return tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
    if (ticket.objectiveId) {
      return String(ticket.objectiveId) ===
        String(objectiveId);
    }

    if (objective.product &&
        ticket.product !== objective.product) {
      return false;
    }

    if (objective.client &&
        ticket.client !== objective.client) {
      return false;
    }

    return true;
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   797. CALCULATE OBJECTIVE CUMULATIVE KG
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17CalculateCumulativeKg(tickets) {
  return tickets.reduce(function(total, ticket) {
    const weight = Number(ticket.netKg || 0);

    if (!Number.isFinite(weight) || weight < 0) {
      return total;
    }

    return total + weight;
  }, 0);
}


/* ============================================================
   798. CALCULATE OBJECTIVE CUMULATIVE TONNES
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17CalculateCumulativeTonnes(tickets) {
  return pbl17KgToTonnes(
    pbl17CalculateCumulativeKg(tickets)
  );
}


/* ============================================================
   799. COUNT OBJECTIVE VOYAGES
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17CountObjectiveVoyages(tickets) {
  return tickets.length;
}


/* ============================================================
   800. CALCULATE REMAINING QUANTITY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17CalculateRemaining(targetTonnes, deliveredTonnes) {
  const target = pbl17NormalizeQuantity(targetTonnes);
  const delivered = pbl17NormalizeQuantity(deliveredTonnes);

  return Math.max(
    0,
    Math.round((target - delivered) * 1000) / 1000
  );
}


/* ============================================================
   801. CALCULATE OVERDELIVERY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17CalculateOverdelivery(targetTonnes, deliveredTonnes) {
  const target = pbl17NormalizeQuantity(targetTonnes);
  const delivered = pbl17NormalizeQuantity(deliveredTonnes);

  return Math.max(
    0,
    Math.round((delivered - target) * 1000) / 1000
  );
}


/* ============================================================
   802. CALCULATE DELIVERY PERCENTAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17CalculatePercentage(targetTonnes, deliveredTonnes) {
  const target = Number(targetTonnes);
  const delivered = Number(deliveredTonnes);

  if (!Number.isFinite(target) || target <= 0) {
    return 0;
  }

  if (!Number.isFinite(delivered) || delivered < 0) {
    return 0;
  }

  return Math.round(
    (delivered / target) * 10000
  ) / 100;
}


/* ============================================================
   803. GET OBJECTIVE DELIVERY STATUS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetDeliveryStatus(target, delivered) {
  if (target <= 0) return "NO_TARGET";

  if (delivered >= target) return "TARGET_REACHED";

  if (delivered > 0) return "IN_PROGRESS";

  return "NOT_STARTED";
}


/* ============================================================
   804. BUILD OBJECTIVE PROGRESS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17BuildObjectiveProgress(objective) {
  if (!objective) {
    return {
      hasObjective: false,
      targetQuantity: 0,
      cumulTonnes: 0,
      numberOfVoyages: 0,
      remainingTonnes: 0,
      deliveryPercentage: 0,
      status: "NO_OBJECTIVE"
    };
  }

  const target = pbl17NormalizeQuantity(
    objective.targetQuantity ||
    objective.targetTonnes ||
    objective.quantity ||
    0
  );

  const tickets = pbl17GetObjectiveTickets(objective);

  const delivered = pbl17CalculateCumulativeTonnes(tickets);
  const voyages = pbl17CountObjectiveVoyages(tickets);

  return {
    hasObjective: true,
    objectiveId: objective.id,
    objectiveName: objective.name || objective.title || "Objectif",

    targetQuantity: target,
    cumulTonnes: delivered,
    numberOfVoyages: voyages,

    remainingTonnes:
      pbl17CalculateRemaining(target, delivered),

    overdeliveryTonnes:
      pbl17CalculateOverdelivery(target, delivered),

    deliveryPercentage:
      pbl17CalculatePercentage(target, delivered),

    status:
      pbl17GetDeliveryStatus(target, delivered),

    targetReached: delivered >= target && target > 0
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   805. GET MAIN DELIVERY INDICATORS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetMainDeliveryIndicators() {
  const objective = pbl17GetActiveObjective();

  if (!objective) {
    return {
      targetQuantity: 0,
      cumulTonnes: 0,
      numberOfVoyages: 0,
      remainingTonnes: 0,
      deliveryPercentage: 0,
      objectiveName: "Aucun objectif actif",
      objectiveStatus: "NO_OBJECTIVE",
      targetReached: false
    };
  }

  const progress =
    pbl17BuildObjectiveProgress(objective);

  return {
    targetQuantity: progress.targetQuantity,
    cumulTonnes: progress.cumulTonnes,
    numberOfVoyages: progress.numberOfVoyages,
    remainingTonnes: progress.remainingTonnes,
    deliveryPercentage: progress.deliveryPercentage,
    overdeliveryTonnes: progress.overdeliveryTonnes,
    objectiveName: progress.objectiveName,
    objectiveStatus: progress.status,
    targetReached: progress.targetReached
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   806. GET MAIN DELIVERY INDICATORS API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl17GetMainDeliveryIndicators() {
  return pbl17GetMainDeliveryIndicators();
}


/* ============================================================
   807. GET OBJECTIVE DAILY TICKETS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetDailyObjectiveTickets(objective, date) {
  const tickets = pbl17GetObjectiveTickets(objective);

  const targetDate = date || getLiveDate();

  return tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
    return String(ticket.date) === String(targetDate);
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   808. GET DAILY DELIVERY SUMMARY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetDailyDeliverySummary(date) {
  const objective = pbl17GetActiveObjective();

  if (!objective) {
    return {
      date: date || getLiveDate(),
      targetQuantity: 0,
      cumulTonnes: 0,
      numberOfVoyages: 0,
      remainingTonnes: 0
    };
  }

  const tickets =
    pbl17GetDailyObjectiveTickets(objective, date);

  const delivered =
    pbl17CalculateCumulativeTonnes(tickets);

  const dailyTarget =
    pbl17NormalizeQuantity(objective.dailyTargetTonnes || 0);

  return {
    date: date || getLiveDate(),
    targetQuantity: dailyTarget,
    cumulTonnes: delivered,
    numberOfVoyages: tickets.length,
    remainingTonnes:
      pbl17CalculateRemaining(dailyTarget, delivered),
    deliveryPercentage:
      pbl17CalculatePercentage(dailyTarget, delivered)
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   809. API DAILY DELIVERY SUMMARY
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl17GetDailyDeliverySummary(date) {
  return pbl17GetDailyDeliverySummary(date);
}


/* ============================================================
   810. GET MONTHLY DELIVERY SUMMARY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetMonthlyDeliverySummary(year, month) {
  const objective = pbl17GetActiveObjective();

  if (!objective) {
    return {
      year: year,
      month: month,
      cumulTonnes: 0,
      numberOfVoyages: 0
    };
  }

  const tickets = pbl17GetObjectiveTickets(objective);

  const filtered = tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
    const parsed = pbl15ParseDate(ticket.date);

    if (!parsed) return false;

    return parsed.getFullYear() === Number(year) &&
      parsed.getMonth() + 1 === Number(month);
  });

  const delivered =
    pbl17CalculateCumulativeTonnes(filtered);

  return {
    year: Number(year),
    month: Number(month),
    cumulTonnes: delivered,
    numberOfVoyages: filtered.length,
    totalKg: pbl17TonnesToKg(delivered)
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   811. GET OBJECTIVE PRODUCT BREAKDOWN
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetObjectiveProductBreakdown(objective) {
  const tickets = pbl17GetObjectiveTickets(objective);

  const grouped = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const name = ticket.product || "Non défini";

    if (!grouped[name]) {
      grouped[name] = {
        product: name,
        voyages: 0,
        totalKg: 0,
        totalTonnes: 0
      };
    }

    grouped[name].voyages++;
    grouped[name].totalKg += toNumber(ticket.netKg);
    grouped[name].totalTonnes =
      grouped[name].totalKg / 1000;
  });

  return Object.keys(grouped).map(function(key) {
    return grouped[key];
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   812. GET OBJECTIVE CLIENT BREAKDOWN
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetObjectiveClientBreakdown(objective) {
  const tickets = pbl17GetObjectiveTickets(objective);

  const grouped = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const name = ticket.client || "Non défini";

    if (!grouped[name]) {
      grouped[name] = {
        client: name,
        voyages: 0,
        totalKg: 0,
        totalTonnes: 0
      };
    }

    grouped[name].voyages++;
    grouped[name].totalKg += toNumber(ticket.netKg);
    grouped[name].totalTonnes =
      grouped[name].totalKg / 1000;
  });

  return Object.keys(grouped).map(function(key) {
    return grouped[key];
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   813. GET OBJECTIVE TRUCK BREAKDOWN
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetObjectiveTruckBreakdown(objective) {
  const tickets = pbl17GetObjectiveTickets(objective);

  const grouped = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const name =
      ticket.truckRegistration || "Non défini";

    if (!grouped[name]) {
      grouped[name] = {
        truckRegistration: name,
        voyages: 0,
        totalKg: 0,
        totalTonnes: 0
      };
    }

    grouped[name].voyages++;
    grouped[name].totalKg += toNumber(ticket.netKg);
    grouped[name].totalTonnes =
      grouped[name].totalKg / 1000;
  });

  return Object.keys(grouped).map(function(key) {
    return grouped[key];
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   814. GET OBJECTIVE HISTORY STORAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetObjectiveHistory() {
  return pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  ).filter(function(record) {
    return record.recordType === "PBL17_OBJECTIVE_HISTORY";
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   815. SAVE OBJECTIVE HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17SaveObjectiveHistory(record) {
  if (!record || !record.objectiveId) {
    throw new Error("Historique objectif invalide.");
  }

  const history = pbl17GetObjectiveHistory();

  history.push(Object.assign({}, record, {
    id: record.id || createUniqueId(),
    recordType: "PBL17_OBJECTIVE_HISTORY",
    recordedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  }));

  pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    pbl13ReadArray(PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY).filter(function(item) {
      return item.recordType !== "PBL17_OBJECTIVE_HISTORY";
    }).concat(history)
  );

  return record;
}


/* ============================================================
   816. RECORD OBJECTIVE CREATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17RecordObjectiveCreated(objective, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return pbl17SaveObjectiveHistory({
    objectiveId: objective.id,
    action: "CREATED",
    name: objective.name,
    targetQuantity:
      objective.targetQuantity || objective.targetTonnes
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   817. RECORD OBJECTIVE UPDATE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17RecordObjectiveUpdated(
  objective,
  previous,
  token
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return pbl17SaveObjectiveHistory({
    objectiveId: objective.id,
    action: "UPDATED",
    name: objective.name,
    previous: previous || {},
    current: objective
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   818. RECORD OBJECTIVE COMPLETION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17RecordObjectiveCompleted(objective, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return pbl17SaveObjectiveHistory({
    objectiveId: objective.id,
    action: "COMPLETED",
    name: objective.name,
    completedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  });
}


/* ============================================================
   819. GET OBJECTIVE HISTORY BY ID
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetObjectiveHistoryById(objectiveId) {
  return pbl17GetObjectiveHistory().filter(function(record) {
    return String(record.objectiveId) ===
      String(objectiveId);
  }).reverse();
}


/* ============================================================
   820. API OBJECTIVE HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl17GetObjectiveHistory(objectiveId) {
  return pbl17GetObjectiveHistoryById(objectiveId);
}


/* ============================================================
   821. VALIDATE OBJECTIVE CREATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17ValidateObjectiveData(data) {
  if (!data || typeof data !== "object") {
    throw new Error("Données objectif invalides.");
  }

  const name = cleanText(data.name || "");

  if (name.length < 2) {
    throw new Error("Nom de l'objectif obligatoire.");
  }

  const target = pbl17NormalizeQuantity(
    data.targetQuantity || data.targetTonnes || 0
  );

  if (target <= 0) {
    throw new Error("La quantité cible doit être supérieure à zéro.");
  }

  return {
    name: name,
    targetQuantity: target,
    targetTonnes: target,
    product: cleanText(data.product || ""),
    client: cleanText(data.client || ""),
    dailyTargetTonnes:
      pbl17NormalizeQuantity(data.dailyTargetTonnes || 0),
    startDate: data.startDate || getLiveDate(),
    endDate: data.endDate || "",
    notes: cleanText(data.notes || "")
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   822. CREATE DELIVERY OBJECTIVE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17CreateObjective(data, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const validated = pbl17ValidateObjectiveData(data);

  if (typeof createObjective !== "function") {
    throw new Error(
      "La fonction de création d'objectif de la partie 3 est indisponible."
    );
  }

  const objective = createObjective(
    validated,
    token
  );

  pbl17RecordObjectiveCreated(objective, token);

  return {
    success: true,
    objective: objective
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   823. API CREATE OBJECTIVE
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl17CreateObjective(data, token) {
  return pbl17CreateObjective(data, token);
}


/* ============================================================
   824. UPDATE OBJECTIVE TARGET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17UpdateObjectiveTarget(
  objectiveId,
  targetTonnes,
  reason,
  token
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const target = pbl17NormalizeQuantity(targetTonnes);
  const changeReason = cleanText(reason || "");

  if (target <= 0) {
    throw new Error("Objectif supérieur à zéro obligatoire.");
  }

  if (changeReason.length < 5) {
    throw new Error("Motif de modification obligatoire.");
  }

  if (typeof getAllObjectives !== "function") {
    throw new Error("Gestion des objectifs indisponible.");
  }

  const objectives = getAllObjectives();

  const index = objectives.findIndex(function(item) {
    return String(item.id) === String(objectiveId);
  });

  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error("Objectif introuvable.");
  }

  const previous = Object.assign({}, objectives[index]);

  objectives[index].targetQuantity = target;
  objectives[index].targetTonnes = target;
  objectives[index].updatedAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  objectives[index].targetChangeReason = changeReason;

  if (typeof saveCollection === "function") {
    saveCollection(PBL_KEYS.OBJECTIVES, objectives);
  } else {
    throw new Error("Stockage des objectifs indisponible.");
  }

  pbl17RecordObjectiveUpdated(
    objectives[index],
    previous,
    token
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    objective: objectives[index]
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   825. API UPDATE OBJECTIVE TARGET
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl17UpdateObjectiveTarget(
  id,
  target,
  reason,
  token
) {
  return pbl17UpdateObjectiveTarget(
    id,
    target,
    reason,
    token
  );
}


/* ============================================================
   826. PAUSE OBJECTIVE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17PauseObjective(id, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const objectives = getAllObjectives();

  const index = objectives.findIndex(function(item) {
    return String(item.id) === String(id);
  });

  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error("Objectif introuvable.");
  }

  const pauseReason = cleanText(reason || "");

  if (pauseReason.length < 5) {
    throw new Error("Motif de suspension obligatoire.");
  }

  objectives[index].status = "PAUSED";
  objectives[index].pausedAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  objectives[index].pauseReason = pauseReason;

  saveCollection(PBL_KEYS.OBJECTIVES, objectives);

  pbl17SaveObjectiveHistory({
    objectiveId: id,
    action: "PAUSED",
    reason: pauseReason
  });

  return {
    success: true,
    objective: objectives[index]
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   827. API PAUSE OBJECTIVE
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl17PauseObjective(id, reason, token) {
  return pbl17PauseObjective(id, reason, token);
}


/* ============================================================
   828. RESUME OBJECTIVE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17ResumeObjective(id, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const objectives = getAllObjectives();

  const index = objectives.findIndex(function(item) {
    return String(item.id) === String(id);
  });

  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error("Objectif introuvable.");
  }

  if (objectives[index].status !== "PAUSED") {
    throw new Error("Cet objectif n'est pas suspendu.");
  }

  const resumeReason = cleanText(reason || "");

  if (resumeReason.length < 5) {
    throw new Error("Motif de reprise obligatoire.");
  }

  objectives[index].status = "ACTIVE";
  objectives[index].resumedAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  objectives[index].resumeReason = resumeReason;

  saveCollection(PBL_KEYS.OBJECTIVES, objectives);

  pbl17SaveObjectiveHistory({
    objectiveId: id,
    action: "RESUMED",
    reason: resumeReason
  });

  return {
    success: true,
    objective: objectives[index]
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   829. API RESUME OBJECTIVE
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl17ResumeObjective(id, reason, token) {
  return pbl17ResumeObjective(id, reason, token);
}


/* ============================================================
   830. COMPLETE OBJECTIVE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17CompleteObjective(id, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const objectives = getAllObjectives();

  const index = objectives.findIndex(function(item) {
    return String(item.id) === String(id);
  });

  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error("Objectif introuvable.");
  }

  const completionReason = cleanText(reason || "");

  if (completionReason.length < 5) {
    throw new Error("Motif de clôture obligatoire.");
  }

  objectives[index].status = "COMPLETED";
  objectives[index].completedAt = getLiveTimestamp();
  objectives[index].completionReason = completionReason;

  saveCollection(PBL_KEYS.OBJECTIVES, objectives);

  pbl17RecordObjectiveCompleted(
    objectives[index],
    token
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    objective: objectives[index]
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   834. GET OBJECTIVE REPORT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetObjectiveReport(id) {
  const objectives = getAllObjectives();

  const objective = objectives.find(function(item) {
    return String(item.id) === String(id);
  });

  if (!objective) {
    throw new Error("Objectif introuvable.");
  }

  const progress =
    pbl17BuildObjectiveProgress(objective);

  return {
    objective: objective,
    progress: progress,
    products:
      pbl17GetObjectiveProductBreakdown(objective),
    clients:
      pbl17GetObjectiveClientBreakdown(objective),
    trucks:
      pbl17GetObjectiveTruckBreakdown(objective),
    generatedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}

  return pbl17GetObjectiveReport(id);
}


/* ============================================================
   836. GET OBJECTIVE DAILY HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetObjectiveDailyHistory(id) {
  const objectives = getAllObjectives();

  const objective = objectives.find(function(item) {
    return String(item.id) === String(id);
  });

  if (!objective) {
    throw new Error("Objectif introuvable.");
  }

  const tickets = pbl17GetObjectiveTickets(objective);

  const grouped = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const date = ticket.date || "Date inconnue";

    grouped[date] = grouped[date] || {
      date: date,
      voyages: 0,
      netKg: 0,
      netTonnes: 0
    };

    grouped[date].voyages++;
    grouped[date].netKg += toNumber(ticket.netKg);
    grouped[date].netTonnes =
      grouped[date].netKg / 1000;
  });

  return Object.keys(grouped)
    .sort()
    .map(function(key) {
      return grouped[key];
    });
}


/* ============================================================
   837. API OBJECTIVE DAILY HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl17GetObjectiveDailyHistory(id) {
  return pbl17GetObjectiveDailyHistory(id);
}


/* ============================================================
   838. GET OBJECTIVE FORECAST
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetObjectiveForecast(id) {
  const objectives = getAllObjectives();

  const objective = objectives.find(function(item) {
    return String(item.id) === String(id);
  });

  if (!objective) {
    throw new Error("Objectif introuvable.");
  }

  const tickets = pbl17GetObjectiveTickets(objective);

  const delivered =
    pbl17CalculateCumulativeTonnes(tickets);

  const remaining =
    pbl17CalculateRemaining(
      objective.targetQuantity ||
      objective.targetTonnes ||
      0,
      delivered
    );

  const days = {};

  tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
    const date = ticket.date || "";
    if (date) days[date] = true;
  });

  const activeDays = Object.keys(days).length;

  const averageDaily = activeDays > 0
    ? delivered / activeDays
    : 0;

  const estimatedDays = averageDaily > 0
    ? Math.ceil(remaining / averageDaily)
    : null;

  return {
    deliveredTonnes: delivered,
    remainingTonnes: remaining,
    activeDeliveryDays: activeDays,
    averageDailyTonnes: averageDaily,
    estimatedRemainingDays: estimatedDays
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   839. API OBJECTIVE FORECAST
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl17GetObjectiveForecast(id) {
  return pbl17GetObjectiveForecast(id);
}


/* ============================================================
   840. OBJECTIVE ALERT CHECK
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17CheckObjectiveAlerts() {
  const objective = pbl17GetActiveObjective();

  if (!objective) {
    return {
      hasObjective: false,
      alerts: []
    };
  }

  const progress =
    pbl17BuildObjectiveProgress(objective);

  const alerts = [];

  if (progress.targetReached) {
    alerts.push({
      type: "SUCCESS",
      code: "TARGET_REACHED",
      message: "Objectif de livraison atteint."
    });
  }

  if (
    progress.deliveryPercentage >= 90 &&
    !progress.targetReached
  ) {
    alerts.push({
      type: "WARNING",
      code: "TARGET_NEAR",
      message: "Objectif presque atteint."
    });
  }

  if (progress.remainingTonnes > 0) {
    alerts.push({
      type: "INFO",
      code: "REMAINING_QUANTITY",
      message:
        progress.remainingTonnes +
        " tonnes restantes à livrer."
    });
  }

  return {
    hasObjective: true,
    objectiveId: objective.id,
    alerts: alerts
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   841. API OBJECTIVE ALERTS
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl17CheckObjectiveAlerts() {
  return pbl17CheckObjectiveAlerts();
}


/* ============================================================
   842. GET OBJECTIVE DASHBOARD DATA
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17GetObjectiveDashboardData() {
  const objective = pbl17GetActiveObjective();

  const indicators =
    pbl17GetMainDeliveryIndicators();

  return {
    application: "PONT BASCULE LIVE",

    title: objective
      ? objective.name
      : "Suivi des livraisons",

    indicators: {
      targetQuantity: indicators.targetQuantity,
      cumulTonnes: indicators.cumulTonnes,
      numberOfVoyages: indicators.numberOfVoyages,
      remainingTonnes: indicators.remainingTonnes
    },

    deliveryPercentage:
      indicators.deliveryPercentage,

    objectiveStatus:
      indicators.objectiveStatus,

    alerts:
      pbl17CheckObjectiveAlerts().alerts,

    date: getLiveDate(),
    time: getLiveTime()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   846. GET OBJECTIVE DATA INTEGRITY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl17ValidateObjectiveIntegrity() {
  const objectives = getAllObjectives();

  const errors = [];

  objectives.forEach(function(objective) {
    const target = Number(
      objective.targetQuantity ||
      objective.targetTonnes ||
      0
    );

    if (!Number.isFinite(target) || target < 0) {
      errors.push({
        objectiveId: objective.id,
        error: "INVALID_TARGET"
      });
    }

    if (!objective.id) {
      errors.push({
        objectiveId: null,
        error: "MISSING_OBJECTIVE_ID"
      });
    }
  });

  return {
    valid: errors.length === 0,
    totalObjectives: objectives.length,
    errors: errors
  };
}


/* ============================================================
   847. API OBJECTIVE INTEGRITY
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl17ValidateObjectiveIntegrity() {
  return pbl17ValidateObjectiveIntegrity();
}

    ticketsAvailable:
      typeof getAllTickets === "function",
    weightUnit: "KG",
    reportingUnit: "TONNES",
    indicators: [
      "TARGET QUANTITY",
      "CUMUL TONNES",
      "NB VOYAGES",
      "REST À LIVRER"
    ],
    integrity:
      pbl17ValidateObjectiveIntegrity(),
    checkedAt: getLiveTimestamp()
  };
}

/***************************************************************
 * PONT BASCULE LIVE
 * WEIGHBRIDGE MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
 * Backend: Google Apps Script
 *
 * PART 18 — ADVANCED LIVE DASHBOARD ENGINE
 * Functions 851–910
 *
 * MAIN DASHBOARD INDICATORS:
 * 1. TARGET QUANTITY
 * 2. CUMUL TONNES
 * 3. NB VOYAGES
 * 4. REST À LIVRER
 *
 * Weight storage: KG
 * Dashboard display: TONNES
 * Time zone: Africa/Casablanca
 ***************************************************************/

const PBL18_CONFIG = {
  VERSION: "18.0.0",
  TIME_ZONE: "Africa/Casablanca",
  DEFAULT_REFRESH_SECONDS: 10,
  MAX_RECENT_TICKETS: 20,
  MAX_HISTORY_RECORDS: 500,
  DEFAULT_DASHBOARD_SETTINGS: {
    refreshSeconds: 10,
    showProgress: true,
    showDailyActivity: true,
    showTopProducts: true,
    showTopClients: true,
    showTopTrucks: true,
    recentTicketsLimit: 10
  }
};

/* 851 — SAFE EXECUTION */

function pbl18SafeCall(callback, fallbackValue) {
  try {
    if (typeof callback !== "function") {
      return fallbackValue;
    }

    const result = callback();

    return result === undefined || result === null
      ? fallbackValue
      : result;

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("PBL18 SAFE CALL:", error);
    return fallbackValue;
  }
}

/* 852 — CURRENT DATE AND TIME */

function pbl18Now() {
  return new Date();
}

/* 853 — DATE KEY */

function pbl18DateKey(dateValue) {
  const date = dateValue instanceof Date
    ? dateValue
    : new Date(dateValue);

  if (isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    return "";
  }

  return Utilities.formatDate(
    date,
    PBL18_CONFIG.TIME_ZONE,
    "yyyy-MM-dd"
  );
}

/* 854 — FLEXIBLE DATE PARSER */

function pbl18ParseDate(value) {
  if (!value) return null;

  if (value instanceof Date) {
    return isNaN(value.getTime()) ? null : value;
  }

  const textValue = String(value).trim();

  let match = textValue.match(
    /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/
  );

  if (match) {
    const date = new Date(
      Number(match[1]),
      Number(match[2]) - 1,
      Number(match[3])
    );

    return isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
  }

  match = textValue.match(
    /^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/
  );

  if (match) {
    const date = new Date(
      Number(match[3]),
      Number(match[2]) - 1,
      Number(match[1])
    );

    return isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
  }

  const parsed = new Date(textValue);

  return isNaN(parsed.getTime()) ? null : parsed;
}

/* 855 — FORMAT DATE */

function pbl18FormatDate(value) {
  const date = pbl18ParseDate(value);

  if (!date) return "";

  return Utilities.formatDate(
    date,
    PBL18_CONFIG.TIME_ZONE,
    "dd/MM/yyyy"
  );
}

/* 856 — NORMALIZE TICKET STATUS */

function pbl18NormalizeStatus(ticket) {
  return String(
    ticket && ticket.status
      ? ticket.status
      : "VALID"
  ).trim().toUpperCase();
}

/* 857 — GET ALL TICKETS SAFELY */

function pbl18GetTickets() {
  if (typeof getAllTickets === "function") {
    const tickets = pbl18SafeCall(
      function () {
        return getAllTickets();
      },
      []
    );

    return Array.isArray(tickets) ? tickets : [];
  }

  if (
    typeof PBL_KEYS !== "undefined" &&
    PBL_KEYS.TICKETS
  ) {
    const tickets = pbl18SafeCall(
      function () {
        return loadCollection(PBL_KEYS.TICKETS);
      },
      []
    );

    return Array.isArray(tickets) ? tickets : [];
  }

  return [];
}

/* 858 — CHECK ACTIVE TICKET */

function pbl18IsActiveTicket(ticket) {
  if (!ticket || typeof ticket !== "object") {
    return false;
  }

  const status = pbl18NormalizeStatus(ticket);

  const excludedStatuses = [
    "DELETED",
    "CANCELLED",
    "CANCELED",
    "SUPPRESSED",
    "VOID",
    "ANNULLED"
  ];

  if (excludedStatuses.indexOf(status) !== -1) {
    return false;
  }

  if (ticket.deleted === true) return false;
  if (ticket.archived === true) return false;

  return true;
}

/* 859 — GET ACTIVE TICKETS */

function pbl18GetActiveTickets() {
  return pbl18GetTickets().filter(
    pbl18IsActiveTicket
  );
}

/* 860 — GET ACTIVE OBJECTIVE SAFELY */

function pbl18GetObjective() {
  if (typeof getActiveObjective === "function") {
    const objective = pbl18SafeCall(
      function () {
        return getActiveObjective();
      },
      null
    );

    if (objective) return objective;
  }

  if (typeof pbl17GetActiveObjective === "function") {
    const objective = pbl18SafeCall(
      function () {
        return pbl17GetActiveObjective();
      },
      null
    );

    if (objective) return objective;
  }

  if (typeof getAllObjectives === "function") {
    const objectives = pbl18SafeCall(
      function () {
        return getAllObjectives();
      },
      []
    );

    if (Array.isArray(objectives)) {
      return objectives.find(function (objective) {
        return objective &&
          objective.active !== false &&
          objective.archived !== true &&
          String(objective.status || "").toUpperCase() !== "COMPLETED" &&
          String(objective.status || "").toUpperCase() !== "PAUSED";
      }) || null;
    }
  }

  return null;
}

/* 861 — GET BASE DASHBOARD */

function pbl18GetBaseDashboard() {
  if (typeof getMainDashboardData === "function") {
    const result = pbl18SafeCall(
      function () {
        return getMainDashboardData();
      },
      null
    );

    if (result) return result;
  }

  if (typeof pbl17GetMainIndicators === "function") {
    const result = pbl18SafeCall(
      function () {
        return pbl17GetMainIndicators();
      },
      null
    );

    if (result) return result;
  }

  if (typeof pbl17BuildObjectiveProgress === "function") {
    const result = pbl18SafeCall(
      function () {
        return pbl17BuildObjectiveProgress();
      },
      null
    );

    if (result) return result;
  }

  return {};
}

/* 862 — SAFE NUMBER */

function pbl18Number(value, fallback) {
  const number = Number(value);

  if (!isFinite(number)) {
    return fallback === undefined ? 0 : fallback;
  }

  return number;
}

/* 863 — ROUND NUMBER */

function pbl18Round(value, decimals) {
  const precision = decimals === undefined
    ? 2
    : decimals;

  const multiplier = Math.pow(10, precision);

  return Math.round(
    pbl18Number(value, 0) * multiplier
  ) / multiplier;
}

/* 864 — KG TO TONNES */

function pbl18KgToTonnes(kg) {
  return pbl18Round(
    pbl18Number(kg, 0) / 1000,
    3
  );
}

/* 865 — TONNES TO KG */

function pbl18TonnesToKg(tonnes) {
  return pbl18Round(
    pbl18Number(tonnes, 0) * 1000,
    2
  );
}

/* 866 — GET TICKET NET WEIGHT IN KG */

function pbl18TicketKg(ticket) {
  if (!ticket) return 0;

  if (ticket.netKg !== undefined && ticket.netKg !== null) {
    return Math.max(0, pbl18Number(ticket.netKg, 0));
  }

  if (ticket.netWeightKg !== undefined) {
    return Math.max(0, pbl18Number(ticket.netWeightKg, 0));
  }

  if (ticket.netTonnes !== undefined) {
    return Math.max(
      0,
      pbl18TonnesToKg(ticket.netTonnes)
    );
  }

  const gross = pbl18Number(
    ticket.grossKg || ticket.grossWeightKg,
    0
  );

  const tare = pbl18Number(
    ticket.tareKg || ticket.tareWeightKg,
    0
  );

  return Math.max(0, gross - tare);
}

/* 867 — GET TICKET DATE KEY */

function pbl18TicketDateKey(ticket) {
  if (!ticket) return "";

  const date = pbl18ParseDate(
    ticket.date ||
    ticket.createdAt ||
    ticket.timestamp
  );

  return date ? pbl18DateKey(date) : "";
}

/* 868 — FILTER TICKETS BY DATE */

function pbl18FilterByDate(tickets, startDate, endDate) {
  const start = pbl18ParseDate(startDate);
  const end = pbl18ParseDate(endDate);

  if (!start || !end) return [];

  const startKey = pbl18DateKey(start);
  const endKey = pbl18DateKey(end);

  return (tickets || []).filter(function (ticket) {
    const key = pbl18TicketDateKey(ticket);

    return key && key >= startKey && key <= endKey;
  });
}

/* 869 — SUM TICKET WEIGHTS */

function pbl18Sum(tickets) {
  return (tickets || []).reduce(
    function (total, ticket) {
      return total + pbl18TicketKg(ticket);
    },
    0
  );
}

/* 870 — COUNT TICKETS */

function pbl18Count(tickets) {
  return Array.isArray(tickets) ? tickets.length : 0;
}

/* 871 — DAILY KPIS */

function pbl18DailyKpis(dateValue) {
  const selectedDate = pbl18ParseDate(
    dateValue || pbl18Now()
  );

  if (!selectedDate) {
    throw new Error("Invalid dashboard date.");
  }

  const dateKey = pbl18DateKey(selectedDate);

  const tickets = pbl18GetActiveTickets().filter(
    function (ticket) {
      return pbl18TicketDateKey(ticket) === dateKey;
    }
  );

  const totalKg = pbl18Sum(tickets);

  return {
    date: dateKey,
    dateDisplay: pbl18FormatDate(selectedDate),
    voyages: pbl18Count(tickets),
    totalKg: pbl18Round(totalKg, 2),
    totalTonnes: pbl18KgToTonnes(totalKg),
    averageKg: tickets.length
      ? pbl18Round(totalKg / tickets.length, 2)
      : 0
  };
}

/* 872 — MONTHLY KPIS */

function pbl18MonthlyKpis(year, month) {
  const selectedYear = Number(year);
  const selectedMonth = Number(month);

  if (
    !selectedYear ||
    selectedMonth < 1 ||
    selectedMonth > 12
  ) {
    throw new Error("Invalid year or month.");
  }

  const tickets = pbl18GetActiveTickets().filter(
    function (ticket) {
      const date = pbl18ParseDate(ticket.date);

      return date &&
        date.getFullYear() === selectedYear &&
        date.getMonth() + 1 === selectedMonth;
    }
  );

  const totalKg = pbl18Sum(tickets);

  return {
    year: selectedYear,
    month: selectedMonth,
    voyages: tickets.length,
    totalKg: pbl18Round(totalKg, 2),
    totalTonnes: pbl18KgToTonnes(totalKg),
    averageKg: tickets.length
      ? pbl18Round(totalKg / tickets.length, 2)
      : 0
  };
}

/* 873 — HOURLY ACTIVITY */

function pbl18HourlyActivity(dateValue) {
  const selectedDate = pbl18ParseDate(
    dateValue || pbl18Now()
  );

  if (!selectedDate) {
    throw new Error("Invalid date.");
  }

  const dateKey = pbl18DateKey(selectedDate);

  const tickets = pbl18GetActiveTickets().filter(
    function (ticket) {
      return pbl18TicketDateKey(ticket) === dateKey;
    }
  );

  const hours = {};

  for (let hour = 0; hour < 24; hour++) {
    const key = String(hour).padStart(2, "0");

    hours[key] = {
      hour: key,
      voyages: 0,
      totalKg: 0,
      totalTonnes: 0
    };
  }

  tickets.forEach(function (ticket) {
    const time = String(ticket.time || "").trim();
    const match = time.match(/^(\d{1,2})/);

    if (!match) return;

    const hour = String(
      Math.min(23, Math.max(0, Number(match[1])))
    ).padStart(2, "0");

    hours[hour].voyages += 1;
    hours[hour].totalKg += pbl18TicketKg(ticket);
  });

  return Object.keys(hours).map(function (hour) {
    const item = hours[hour];

    item.totalKg = pbl18Round(item.totalKg, 2);
    item.totalTonnes = pbl18KgToTonnes(item.totalKg);

    return item;
  });
}

/* 874 — TOP PRODUCTS */

function pbl18TopProducts(limit) {
  const max = Math.max(1, Number(limit) || 10);
  const groups = {};

  pbl18GetActiveTickets().forEach(function (ticket) {
    const name = String(
      ticket.product || "NON RENSEIGNÉ"
    ).trim();

    if (!groups[name]) {
      groups[name] = {
        name: name,
        voyages: 0,
        totalKg: 0
      };
    }

    groups[name].voyages += 1;
    groups[name].totalKg += pbl18TicketKg(ticket);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).map(function (key) {
    const item = groups[key];

    item.totalKg = pbl18Round(item.totalKg, 2);
    item.totalTonnes = pbl18KgToTonnes(item.totalKg);

    return item;
  }).sort(function (a, b) {
    return b.totalKg - a.totalKg;
  }).slice(0, max);
}

/* 875 — TOP CLIENTS */

function pbl18TopClients(limit) {
  const max = Math.max(1, Number(limit) || 10);
  const groups = {};

  pbl18GetActiveTickets().forEach(function (ticket) {
    const name = String(
      ticket.client || "NON RENSEIGNÉ"
    ).trim();

    if (!groups[name]) {
      groups[name] = {
        name: name,
        voyages: 0,
        totalKg: 0
      };
    }

    groups[name].voyages += 1;
    groups[name].totalKg += pbl18TicketKg(ticket);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).map(function (key) {
    const item = groups[key];

    item.totalKg = pbl18Round(item.totalKg, 2);
    item.totalTonnes = pbl18KgToTonnes(item.totalKg);

    return item;
  }).sort(function (a, b) {
    return b.totalKg - a.totalKg;
  }).slice(0, max);
}

/* 876 — TOP TRUCKS */

function pbl18TopTrucks(limit) {
  const max = Math.max(1, Number(limit) || 10);
  const groups = {};

  pbl18GetActiveTickets().forEach(function (ticket) {
    const name = String(
      ticket.truckRegistration ||
      ticket.truck ||
      "NON RENSEIGNÉ"
    ).trim();

    if (!groups[name]) {
      groups[name] = {
        registration: name,
        voyages: 0,
        totalKg: 0
      };
    }

    groups[name].voyages += 1;
    groups[name].totalKg += pbl18TicketKg(ticket);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).map(function (key) {
    const item = groups[key];

    item.totalKg = pbl18Round(item.totalKg, 2);
    item.totalTonnes = pbl18KgToTonnes(item.totalKg);

    return item;
  }).sort(function (a, b) {
    return b.totalKg - a.totalKg;
  }).slice(0, max);
}

/* 877 — RECENT TICKETS */

function pbl18RecentTickets(limit) {
  const max = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(
      PBL18_CONFIG.MAX_RECENT_TICKETS,
      Number(limit) || 10
    )
  );

  return pbl18GetActiveTickets().slice().sort(
    function (a, b) {
      const dateA = pbl18ParseDate(a.date);
      const dateB = pbl18ParseDate(b.date);

      const timeA = dateA ? dateA.getTime() : 0;
      const timeB = dateB ? dateB.getTime() : 0;

      if (timeA !== timeB) {
        return timeB - timeA;
      }

      return String(b.time || "").localeCompare(
        String(a.time || "")
      );
    }
  ).slice(0, max).map(function (ticket) {
    return {
      id: ticket.id || "",
      ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber || "",
      date: ticket.date || "",
      time: ticket.time || "",
      product: ticket.product || "",
      client: ticket.client || "",
      truckRegistration: ticket.truckRegistration || "",
      netKg: pbl18TicketKg(ticket),
      netTonnes: pbl18KgToTonnes(
        pbl18TicketKg(ticket)
      ),
      status: ticket.status || "VALID"
    };
  });
}

/* 878 — DELIVERY PROGRESS */

function pbl18Progress(targetTonnes, deliveredTonnes) {
  const target = Math.max(
    0,
    pbl18Number(targetTonnes, 0)
  );

  const delivered = Math.max(
    0,
    pbl18Number(deliveredTonnes, 0)
  );

  const remaining = Math.max(
    0,
    target - delivered
  );

  const percentage = target > 0
    ? (delivered / target) * 100
    : 0;

  return {
    targetTonnes: pbl18Round(target, 3),
    deliveredTonnes: pbl18Round(delivered, 3),
    remainingTonnes: pbl18Round(remaining, 3),
    overdeliveryTonnes: pbl18Round(
      Math.max(0, delivered - target),
      3
    ),
    percentage: pbl18Round(percentage, 2),
    progressBarPercentage: pbl18Round(
      Math.min(100, percentage),
      2
    ),
    targetReached: target > 0 && delivered >= target
  };
}

/* 879 — BUILD FOUR MAIN INDICATORS */

function pbl18BuildMainKpis() {
  const base = pbl18GetBaseDashboard();
  const objective = pbl18GetObjective();

  const tickets = pbl18GetActiveTickets();
  const totalKg = pbl18Sum(tickets);
  const calculatedTonnes = pbl18KgToTonnes(totalKg);

  let target = pbl18Number(
    base.targetQuantity,
    NaN
  );

  if (!isFinite(target)) {
    target = pbl18Number(
      base.targetTonnes,
      NaN
    );
  }

  if (!isFinite(target)) {
    target = pbl18Number(
      objective && (
        objective.targetTonnes ||
        objective.targetQuantity
      ),
      0
    );
  }

  let delivered = pbl18Number(
    base.cumulTonnes,
    NaN
  );

  if (!isFinite(delivered)) {
    delivered = pbl18Number(
      base.totalTonnes,
      calculatedTonnes
    );
  }

  let voyages = pbl18Number(
    base.numberOfVoyages,
    NaN
  );

  if (!isFinite(voyages)) {
    voyages = pbl18Number(
      base.nbVoyages,
      tickets.length
    );
  }

  const progress = pbl18Progress(
    target,
    delivered
  );

  return {
    targetQuantity: progress.targetTonnes,
    cumulTonnes: pbl18Round(delivered, 3),
    numberOfVoyages: Math.max(0, voyages),
    remainingTonnes: pbl18Number(
      base.remainingTonnes,
      progress.remainingTonnes
    ),
    overdeliveryTonnes: progress.overdeliveryTonnes,
    deliveryPercentage: progress.percentage,
    progressBarPercentage: progress.progressBarPercentage,
    targetReached: progress.targetReached,
    objectiveName: objective
      ? objective.name || objective.title || "OBJECTIF ACTIF"
      : base.objectiveName || "AUCUN OBJECTIF ACTIF",
    objectiveStatus: objective
      ? objective.status || "ACTIVE"
      : base.objectiveStatus || "NONE",
    unit: "TONNES"
  };
}

/* 880 — BUILD COMPLETE DASHBOARD */

function pbl18BuildDashboard() {
  const now = pbl18Now();
  const today = pbl18DateKey(now);
  const kpis = pbl18BuildMainKpis();
  const daily = pbl18DailyKpis(now);
  const settings = pbl18GetDashboardSettings();

  return {
    application: "PONT BASCULE LIVE",
    version: PBL18_CONFIG.VERSION,

    generatedAt: Utilities.formatDate(
      now,
      PBL18_CONFIG.TIME_ZONE,
      "dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm:ss"
    ),

    date: today,
    timeZone: PBL18_CONFIG.TIME_ZONE,

    mainIndicators: {
      targetQuantity: kpis.targetQuantity,
      cumulTonnes: kpis.cumulTonnes,
      numberOfVoyages: kpis.numberOfVoyages,
      remainingTonnes: kpis.remainingTonnes
    },

    deliveryProgress: {
      percentage: kpis.deliveryPercentage,
      progressBarPercentage: kpis.progressBarPercentage,
      overdeliveryTonnes: kpis.overdeliveryTonnes,
      targetReached: kpis.targetReached
    },

    objective: {
      name: kpis.objectiveName,
      status: kpis.objectiveStatus
    },

    today: daily,

    dailyActivity: settings.showDailyActivity
      ? pbl18HourlyActivity(now)
      : [],

    topProducts: settings.showTopProducts
      ? pbl18TopProducts(5)
      : [],

    topClients: settings.showTopClients
      ? pbl18TopClients(5)
      : [],

    topTrucks: settings.showTopTrucks
      ? pbl18TopTrucks(5)
      : [],

    recentTickets: pbl18RecentTickets(
      settings.recentTicketsLimit
    ),

    settings: settings
  };
}

/* 881 — DASHBOARD API */

function apiPbl18Dashboard() {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl18BuildDashboard()
  };
}

/* 882 — FULL DASHBOARD REFRESH API */

function apiPbl18Refresh() {
  const dashboard = pbl18BuildDashboard();

  return {
    success: true,
    refreshedAt: dashboard.generatedAt,
    data: dashboard
  };
}

    data: pbl18DailyKpis(dateValue || pbl18Now())
  };
}

    success: true,
    data: pbl18MonthlyKpis(year, month)
  };

/* 885 — DATE RANGE REPORT API */

function apiPbl18Range(startDate, endDate) {
  const tickets = pbl18FilterByDate(
    pbl18GetActiveTickets(),
    startDate,
    endDate
  );

  const totalKg = pbl18Sum(tickets);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: {
      startDate: pbl18FormatDate(startDate),
      endDate: pbl18FormatDate(endDate),
      voyages: tickets.length,
      totalKg: pbl18Round(totalKg, 2),
      totalTonnes: pbl18KgToTonnes(totalKg),
      averageKg: tickets.length
        ? pbl18Round(totalKg / tickets.length, 2)
        : 0
    }
  };
}

}

  };
}

/* 891 — KPI HISTORY CONFIGURATION */

const PBL18_HISTORY_TYPE = "PBL18_KPI_SNAPSHOT";

/* 892 — GET KPI HISTORY */

function pbl18GetKpiHistory(limit) {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13ReadArray !== "function"
  ) {
    return [];
  }

  const records = pbl18SafeCall(
    function () {
      return pbl13ReadArray(
        PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
      );
    },
    []
  );

  return records.filter(function (record) {
    return record &&
      record.recordType === PBL18_HISTORY_TYPE;
  }).sort(function (a, b) {
    return String(b.createdAt || "").localeCompare(
      String(a.createdAt || "")
    );
  }).slice(
    0,
    Math.max(1, Number(limit) || 30)
  );
}

/* 893 — SAVE KPI HISTORY */

function pbl18SaveKpiHistory(snapshot) {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13ReadArray !== "function" ||
    typeof pbl13WriteArray !== "function"
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Dedicated activity storage is not available."
    );
  }

  const records = pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  );

  const filtered = records.filter(function (record) {
    return !record ||
      record.recordType !== PBL18_HISTORY_TYPE;
  });

  filtered.push(snapshot);

  const history = filtered.filter(function (record) {
    return record &&
      record.recordType === PBL18_HISTORY_TYPE;
  });

  const retainedIds = history
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(b.createdAt || "").localeCompare(
        String(a.createdAt || "")
      );
    })
    .slice(0, PBL18_CONFIG.MAX_HISTORY_RECORDS)
    .map(function (record) {
      return record.id;
    });

  const retained = filtered.filter(function (record) {
    return !record ||
      record.recordType !== PBL18_HISTORY_TYPE ||
      retainedIds.indexOf(record.id) !== -1;
  });

  return pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    retained
  );
}

/* 894 — CREATE KPI SNAPSHOT */

function pbl18RecordKpiSnapshot() {
  const dashboard = pbl18BuildDashboard();
  const kpis = dashboard.mainIndicators;

  const snapshot = {
    id: "PBL18-" + Utilities.getUuid(),
    recordType: PBL18_HISTORY_TYPE,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    date: dashboard.date,
    targetQuantity: kpis.targetQuantity,
    cumulTonnes: kpis.cumulTonnes,
    numberOfVoyages: kpis.numberOfVoyages,
    remainingTonnes: kpis.remainingTonnes,
    deliveryPercentage:
      dashboard.deliveryProgress.percentage,
    objectiveName: dashboard.objective.name
  };

  pbl18SaveKpiHistory(snapshot);

  return snapshot;
}

/* 895 — RECORD KPI SNAPSHOT API */

function apiPbl18RecordKpiSnapshot(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl18RecordKpiSnapshot()
  };
}

/* 896 — COMPARE TWO DAYS */

function pbl18CompareDays(firstDate, secondDate) {
  const first = pbl18DailyKpis(firstDate);
  const second = pbl18DailyKpis(secondDate);

  return {
    firstDay: first,
    secondDay: second,

    voyageDifference:
      second.voyages - first.voyages,

    tonneDifference: pbl18Round(
      second.totalTonnes - first.totalTonnes,
      3
    ),

    percentageChange: first.totalTonnes > 0
      ? pbl18Round(
          (
            (second.totalTonnes - first.totalTonnes) /
            first.totalTonnes
          ) * 100,
          2
        )
      : null
  };
}

/* 897 — COMPARE DAYS API */

function apiPbl18CompareDays(firstDate, secondDate) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl18CompareDays(firstDate, secondDate)
  };
}

/* 898 — GET DASHBOARD SETTINGS */

function pbl18GetDashboardSettings() {
  const defaults = Object.assign(
    {},
    PBL18_CONFIG.DEFAULT_DASHBOARD_SETTINGS
  );

  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13ReadArray !== "function"
  ) {
    return defaults;
  }

  const records = pbl18SafeCall(
    function () {
      return pbl13ReadArray(
        PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
      );
    },
    []
  );

  const saved = records.find(function (record) {
    return record &&
      record.recordType === "PBL18_DASHBOARD_SETTINGS";
  });

  if (!saved || !saved.settings) {
    return defaults;
  }

  return Object.assign(defaults, saved.settings);
}

/* 899 — SAVE DASHBOARD SETTINGS */

function pbl18SaveDashboardSettings(settings) {
  const current = pbl18GetDashboardSettings();

  const updated = Object.assign(
    {},
    current,
    settings || {}
  );

  updated.refreshSeconds = Math.max(
    5,
    Math.min(
      300,
      Number(updated.refreshSeconds) || 10
    )
  );

  updated.recentTicketsLimit = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(
      20,
      Number(updated.recentTicketsLimit) || 10
    )
  );

  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13ReadArray !== "function" ||
    typeof pbl13WriteArray !== "function"
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Dashboard settings storage is unavailable."
    );
  }

  const records = pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  );

  const filtered = records.filter(function (record) {
    return !record ||
      record.recordType !== "PBL18_DASHBOARD_SETTINGS";
  });

  filtered.push({
    id: "PBL18-DASHBOARD-SETTINGS",
    recordType: "PBL18_DASHBOARD_SETTINGS",
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    settings: updated
  });

  pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    filtered
  );

  return updated;
}

/* 900 — SAVE DASHBOARD SETTINGS API */

function apiPbl18SaveDashboardSettings(settings, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl18SaveDashboardSettings(settings)
  };
}

  return {
    title: "PONT BASCULE LIVE — DASHBOARD",
    generatedAt: dashboard.generatedAt,

    columns: [
      "INDICATEUR",
      "VALEUR",
      "UNITE"
    ],

    rows: [
      [

        dashboard.mainIndicators.targetQuantity,
        "TONNES"
      ],
      [
        "CUMUL TONNES",
        dashboard.mainIndicators.cumulTonnes,
        "TONNES"
      ],
      [
        "NB VOYAGES",
        dashboard.mainIndicators.numberOfVoyages,
        "VOYAGES"
      ],
      [
        "REST À LIVRER",
        dashboard.mainIndicators.remainingTonnes,
        "TONNES"
      ],
      [
        "PROGRESSION",
        dashboard.deliveryProgress.percentage,
        "%"
      ]
    ]
  };
}
