/* 903 — GET OPEN OPERATIONAL ALERTS */

function pbl18GetAlerts() {
  if (typeof pbl8GetAlertCollection !== "function") {
    return [];
  }

  const alerts = pbl18SafeCall(
    function () {
      return pbl8GetAlertCollection();
    },
    []
  );

  return Array.isArray(alerts)
    ? alerts.filter(function (alert) {
        return alert &&
          alert.closed !== true &&
          String(alert.status || "").toUpperCase() !== "CLOSED";
      })
    : [];
}

/* 904 — BUILD OPERATIONAL OVERVIEW */

function pbl18BuildOperationalOverview() {
  const tickets = pbl18GetActiveTickets();
  const today = pbl18DailyKpis(pbl18Now());
  const alerts = pbl18GetAlerts();
  const objective = pbl18GetObjective();

  const products = {};
  const clients = {};
  const trucks = {};

  tickets.forEach(function (ticket) {
    const product = String(
      ticket.product || "NON RENSEIGNÉ"
    );

    const client = String(
      ticket.client || "NON RENSEIGNÉ"
    );

    const truck = String(
      ticket.truckRegistration || "NON RENSEIGNÉ"
    );

    products[product] = (products[product] || 0) + 1;
    clients[client] = (clients[client] || 0) + 1;
    trucks[truck] = (trucks[truck] || 0) + 1;
  });

  return {
    generatedAt: Utilities.formatDate(
      pbl18Now(),
      PBL18_CONFIG.TIME_ZONE,
      "dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm:ss"
    ),

    today: today,

    totalActiveTickets: tickets.length,

    uniqueProducts: Object.keys(products).length,
    uniqueClients: Object.keys(clients).length,
    uniqueTrucks: Object.keys(trucks).length,

    openAlerts: alerts.length,

    activeObjective: objective
      ? {
          name: objective.name || objective.title || "",
          status: objective.status || "ACTIVE"
        }
      : null,

    system: {
      scaleConnection: "NOT_CONFIRMED",
      manualEntryDefault: false,
      dashboardMode: "LIVE"
    }
  };
}

/* 905 — OPERATIONAL OVERVIEW API */

function apiPbl18OperationalOverview() {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl18BuildOperationalOverview()
  };
}

/* 906 — VALIDATE DASHBOARD DATA */

function pbl18ValidateDashboardData() {
  const dashboard = pbl18BuildDashboard();
  const errors = [];
  const warnings = [];

  const indicators = dashboard.mainIndicators;

  if (indicators.targetQuantity < 0) {
    errors.push("TARGET QUANTITY cannot be negative.");
  }

  if (indicators.cumulTonnes < 0) {
    errors.push("CUMUL TONNES cannot be negative.");
  }

  if (indicators.numberOfVoyages < 0) {
    errors.push("NB VOYAGES cannot be negative.");
  }

  if (indicators.remainingTonnes < 0) {
    warnings.push(
      "Remaining quantity is negative; check overdelivery calculation."
    );
  }

  if (
    indicators.targetQuantity === 0 &&
    indicators.cumulTonnes > 0
  ) {
    warnings.push(
      "Deliveries exist but no active target quantity is configured."
    );
  }

  if (!dashboard.date || !dashboard.generatedAt) {
    errors.push("Dashboard date or timestamp is missing.");
  }

  return {
    valid: errors.length === 0,
    errors: errors,
    warnings: warnings,
    checkedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/* 907 — VALIDATE DASHBOARD API */

function apiPbl18ValidateDashboardData(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl18ValidateDashboardData()
  };
}

/* 908 — DASHBOARD HEALTH */

function pbl18GetDashboardHealth() {
  const validation = pbl18ValidateDashboardData();
  const tickets = pbl18GetActiveTickets();

  return {
    status: validation.valid ? "HEALTHY" : "WARNING",
    valid: validation.valid,
    activeTickets: tickets.length,
    validation: validation,
    storageAvailable:
      typeof PBL13_STORAGE !== "undefined",
    dashboardVersion: PBL18_CONFIG.VERSION,
    checkedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/* 909 — DASHBOARD HEALTH API */

function apiPbl18DashboardHealth() {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl18GetDashboardHealth()
  };
}

/* 910 — INITIALIZE DASHBOARD ENGINE */

function apiPbl18InitializeDashboard() {
  const settings = pbl18GetDashboardSettings();
  const dashboard = pbl18BuildDashboard();
  const health = pbl18GetDashboardHealth();

  return {
    success: true,
    application: "PONT BASCULE LIVE",
    module: "ADVANCED DASHBOARD ENGINE",
    version: PBL18_CONFIG.VERSION,
    initializedAt: new Date().toISOString(),

    mainIndicators: dashboard.mainIndicators,
    deliveryProgress: dashboard.deliveryProgress,

    settings: settings,
    health: health
  };
}

/***************************************************************
 * END OF PART 18
 ***************************************************************/

/***************************************************************
 * PONT BASCULE LIVE
 * WEIGHBRIDGE MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
 *
 * PART 19 — ADVANCED TICKET MANAGEMENT
 * Functions 911–970
 *
 * FEATURES:
 * - Advanced ticket validation
 * - Duplicate detection
 * - Ticket correction history
 * - Ticket status workflow
 * - Ticket locking
 * - Ticket audit history
 * - Search and filtering
 * - Safe ticket updates
 ***************************************************************/

const PBL19_CONFIG = {
  VERSION: "19.0.0",
  MAX_SEARCH_RESULTS: 500,
  MAX_CORRECTION_HISTORY: 1000,
  ALLOWED_STATUSES: [
    "VALID",
    "PENDING",
    "CORRECTED",
    "CANCELLED",
    "DELETED"
  ],
  AUDIT_TYPE: "PBL19_TICKET_AUDIT",
  CORRECTION_TYPE: "PBL19_TICKET_CORRECTION"
};

/* ============================================================
   911 — GET TICKETS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19GetTickets() {
  if (typeof pbl18GetTickets === "function") {
    return pbl18GetTickets();
  }

  if (typeof getAllTickets === "function") {
    const result = pbl18SafeCall(
      function () {
        return getAllTickets();
      },
      []
    );

    return Array.isArray(result) ? result : [];
  }

  return [];
}

/* ============================================================
   912 — GET TICKET BY ID
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19GetTicketById(ticketId) {
  const id = String(ticketId || "").trim();

  if (!id) {
    return null;
  }

  return pbl19GetTickets().find(function (ticket) {
    return String(ticket.id || "") === id;
  }) || null;
}

/* ============================================================
   913 — GET TICKET BY NUMBER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19GetTicketByNumber(ticketNumber) {
  const number = String(ticketNumber || "").trim();

  if (!number) {
    return null;
  }

  return pbl19GetTickets().find(function (ticket) {
    return String(ticket.ticketNumber || "").trim() === number;
  }) || null;
}

/* ============================================================
   914 — VALIDATE REQUIRED FIELDS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19ValidateRequiredFields(ticket) {
  const errors = [];

  const required = [
    "ticketNumber",
    "date",
    "product",
    "truckRegistration"
  ];

  required.forEach(function (field) {
    if (
      ticket[field] === undefined ||
      ticket[field] === null ||
      String(ticket[field]).trim() === ""
    ) {
      errors.push(field + " is required.");
    }
  });

  return {
    valid: errors.length === 0,
    errors: errors
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   915 — VALIDATE WEIGHT VALUES
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19ValidateWeights(ticket) {
  const gross = Number(ticket.grossKg);
  const tare = Number(ticket.tareKg);
  const net = Number(ticket.netKg);

  const errors = [];

  if (!isFinite(gross) || gross <= 0) {
    errors.push("Gross weight must be greater than zero.");
  }

  if (!isFinite(tare) || tare < 0) {
    errors.push("Tare weight cannot be negative.");
  }

  if (!isFinite(net) || net < 0) {
    errors.push("Net weight cannot be negative.");
  }

  if (
    isFinite(gross) &&
    isFinite(tare) &&
    isFinite(net) &&
    gross >= tare
  ) {
    const calculated = gross - tare;

    if (Math.abs(calculated - net) > 1) {
      errors.push(
        "Net weight does not match gross weight minus tare weight."
      );
    }
  }

  if (
    isFinite(gross) &&
    isFinite(tare) &&
    gross < tare
  ) {
    errors.push(
      "Gross weight cannot be lower than tare weight."
    );
  }

  return {
    valid: errors.length === 0,
    errors: errors
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   916 — CHECK DUPLICATE TICKET NUMBER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19CheckDuplicateNumber(ticketNumber, excludedId) {
  const number = String(ticketNumber || "").trim();

  const duplicate = pbl19GetTickets().find(
    function (ticket) {
      return String(ticket.ticketNumber || "").trim() === number &&
        String(ticket.id || "") !== String(excludedId || "");
    }
  );

  return {
    duplicate: !!duplicate,
    existingTicketId: duplicate ? duplicate.id : null,
    ticketNumber: number
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   917 — COMPLETE TICKET VALIDATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19ValidateTicket(ticket, excludedId) {
  const data = ticket || {};

  const required = pbl19ValidateRequiredFields(data);
  const weights = pbl19ValidateWeights(data);

  const duplicate = pbl19CheckDuplicateNumber(
    data.ticketNumber,
    excludedId
  );

  const errors = []
    .concat(required.errors)
    .concat(weights.errors);

  if (duplicate.duplicate) {
    errors.push(
      "Ticket number already exists: " + data.ticketNumber
    );
  }

  const date = pbl18ParseDate(data.date);

  if (!date) {
    errors.push("Ticket date is invalid.");
  }

  return {
    valid: errors.length === 0,
    errors: errors,
    duplicate: duplicate.duplicate,
    checkedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   918 — AUDIT STORAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19GetAuditCollection() {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13ReadArray !== "function"
  ) {
    return [];
  }

  return pbl13ReadArray(PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY).filter(
    function (record) {
      return record &&
        record.recordType === PBL19_CONFIG.AUDIT_TYPE;
    }
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   919 — SAVE AUDIT RECORD
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19WriteAudit(action, ticket, details, token) {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13ReadArray !== "function" ||
    typeof pbl13WriteArray !== "function"
  ) {
    throw new Error("Ticket audit storage is unavailable.");
  }

  const records = pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  );

  const audit = {
    id: "PBL19-AUDIT-" + Utilities.getUuid(),
    recordType: PBL19_CONFIG.AUDIT_TYPE,
    action: String(action || "UNKNOWN"),
    ticketId: ticket ? ticket.id || "" : "",
    ticketNumber: ticket ? ticket.ticketNumber || "" : "",
    details: details || {},
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    actor: "AUTHORIZED_USER"
  };

  records.push(audit);

  const retained = records.filter(function (record) {
    return !record ||
      record.recordType !== PBL19_CONFIG.AUDIT_TYPE;
  }).concat(
    records.filter(function (record) {
      return record &&
        record.recordType === PBL19_CONFIG.AUDIT_TYPE;
    }).slice(-PBL19_CONFIG.MAX_CORRECTION_HISTORY)
  );

  pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    retained
  );

  if (typeof writeAuditLog === "function") {
    pbl18SafeCall(function () {
      writeAuditLog(
        "TICKET_" + action,
        audit.details,
        token
      );
    }, null);
  }

  return audit;
}

/* ============================================================
   920 — GET TICKET AUDIT HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19GetTicketAudit(ticketId) {
  const id = String(ticketId || "");

  return pbl19GetAuditCollection().filter(
    function (record) {
      return String(record.ticketId || "") === id;
    }
  ).sort(function (a, b) {
    return String(b.createdAt || "").localeCompare(
      String(a.createdAt || "")
    );
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   921 — CORRECTION HISTORY STORAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19GetCorrectionHistory() {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13ReadArray !== "function"
  ) {
    return [];
  }

  return pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  ).filter(function (record) {
    return record &&
      record.recordType === PBL19_CONFIG.CORRECTION_TYPE;
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   922 — SAVE CORRECTION RECORD
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19SaveCorrection(ticketId, before, after, reason, token) {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13ReadArray !== "function" ||
    typeof pbl13WriteArray !== "function"
  ) {
    throw new Error("Correction history storage is unavailable.");
  }

  const records = pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  );

  const correction = {
    id: "PBL19-CORR-" + Utilities.getUuid(),
    recordType: PBL19_CONFIG.CORRECTION_TYPE,
    ticketId: String(ticketId),
    reason: String(reason || "").trim(),
    before: before,
    after: after,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  records.push(correction);

  const corrections = records.filter(function (record) {
    return record &&
      record.recordType === PBL19_CONFIG.CORRECTION_TYPE;
  });

  const retainedIds = corrections
    .slice(-PBL19_CONFIG.MAX_CORRECTION_HISTORY)
    .map(function (record) {
      return record.id;
    });

  const retained = records.filter(function (record) {
    return !record ||
      record.recordType !== PBL19_CONFIG.CORRECTION_TYPE ||
      retainedIds.indexOf(record.id) !== -1;
  });

  pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    retained
  );

  pbl19WriteAudit(
    "CORRECTION",
    after,
    {
      reason: correction.reason,
      correctionId: correction.id
    },
    token
  );

  return correction;
}

/* ============================================================
   923 — GET CORRECTION HISTORY FOR TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19GetCorrectionsByTicket(ticketId) {
  return pbl19GetCorrectionHistory().filter(
    function (record) {
      return String(record.ticketId) === String(ticketId);
    }
  ).sort(function (a, b) {
    return String(b.createdAt || "").localeCompare(
      String(a.createdAt || "")
    );
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   924 — CREATE TICKET SNAPSHOT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19TicketSnapshot(ticket) {
  if (!ticket) {
    return null;
  }

  return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ticket));
}

/* ============================================================
   925 — CHECK EDITABLE FIELDS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19SanitizeChanges(changes) {
  const allowed = [
    "date",
    "time",
    "product",
    "client",
    "truckRegistration",
    "driver",
    "grossKg",
    "tareKg",
    "netKg",
    "notes"
  ];

  const sanitized = {};

  allowed.forEach(function (field) {
    if (
      Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
        changes || {},
        field
      )
    ) {
      sanitized[field] = changes[field];
    }
  });

  return sanitized;
}

/* ============================================================
   926 — BUILD UPDATED TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19BuildUpdatedTicket(ticket, changes) {
  const updated = Object.assign(
    {},
    ticket,
    pbl19SanitizeChanges(changes)
  );

  updated.netKg = pbl18Round(
    Number(updated.grossKg) - Number(updated.tareKg),
    2
  );

  updated.netTonnes = pbl18KgToTonnes(
    updated.netKg
  );

  updated.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  updated.status = "CORRECTED";

  return updated;
}

/* ============================================================
   927 — SAVE TICKET COLLECTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19SaveTickets(tickets) {
  if (
    typeof PBL_KEYS === "undefined" ||
    !PBL_KEYS.TICKETS ||
    typeof saveCollection !== "function"
  ) {
    throw new Error("Ticket storage is unavailable.");
  }

  return saveCollection(
    PBL_KEYS.TICKETS,
    tickets
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   928 — CORRECT TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19CorrectTicket(ticketId, changes, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (!String(reason || "").trim()) {
    throw new Error(
      "A correction reason is mandatory."
    );
  }

  const tickets = pbl19GetTickets();

  const index = tickets.findIndex(function (ticket) {
    return String(ticket.id) === String(ticketId);
  });

  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  const original = tickets[index];

  if (
    pbl18NormalizeStatus(original) === "CANCELLED" ||
    pbl18NormalizeStatus(original) === "DELETED"
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Cancelled or deleted tickets cannot be corrected."
    );
  }

  const before = pbl19TicketSnapshot(original);

  const updated = pbl19BuildUpdatedTicket(
    original,
    changes
  );

  const validation = pbl19ValidateTicket(
    updated,
    ticketId
  );

  if (!validation.valid) {
    throw new Error(
      validation.errors.join("\n")
    );
  }

  pbl19SaveCorrection(
    ticketId,
    before,
    updated,
    reason,
    token
  );

  tickets[index] = updated;

  pbl19SaveTickets(tickets);

  pbl19WriteAudit(
    "TICKET_UPDATED",
    updated,
    {
      reason: reason,
      changedFields: Object.keys(
        pbl19SanitizeChanges(changes)
      )
    },
    token
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    ticket: updated
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   929 — CORRECT TICKET API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl19CorrectTicket(
  ticketId,
  changes,
  reason,
  token
) {
  return pbl19CorrectTicket(
    ticketId,
    changes,
    reason,
    token
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   930 — CANCEL TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19CancelTicket(ticketId, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (!String(reason || "").trim()) {
    throw new Error("Cancellation reason is required.");
  }

  const tickets = pbl19GetTickets();

  const index = tickets.findIndex(function (ticket) {
    return String(ticket.id) === String(ticketId);
  });

  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  const before = pbl19TicketSnapshot(tickets[index]);

  tickets[index].status = "CANCELLED";
  tickets[index].cancelledAt = new Date().toISOString();
  tickets[index].cancellationReason = String(reason).trim();

  pbl19SaveTickets(tickets);

  pbl19WriteAudit(
    "TICKET_CANCELLED",
    tickets[index],
    {
      reason: reason,
      previousStatus: before.status || "VALID"
    },
    token
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    ticket: tickets[index]
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   931 — CANCEL TICKET API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl19CancelTicket(ticketId, reason, token) {
  return pbl19CancelTicket(
    ticketId,
    reason,
    token
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   932 — RESTORE CANCELLED TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19RestoreTicket(ticketId, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (!String(reason || "").trim()) {
    throw new Error("Restoration reason is required.");
  }

  const tickets = pbl19GetTickets();

  const index = tickets.findIndex(function (ticket) {
    return String(ticket.id) === String(ticketId);
  });

  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  if (
    pbl18NormalizeStatus(tickets[index]) !== "CANCELLED"
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Only cancelled tickets can be restored."
    );
  }

  tickets[index].status = "VALID";
  tickets[index].restoredAt = new Date().toISOString();
  tickets[index].restorationReason = String(reason).trim();

  pbl19SaveTickets(tickets);

  pbl19WriteAudit(
    "TICKET_RESTORED",
    tickets[index],
    { reason: reason },
    token
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    ticket: tickets[index]
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   933 — RESTORE TICKET API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl19RestoreTicket(ticketId, reason, token) {
  return pbl19RestoreTicket(
    ticketId,
    reason,
    token
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   934 — SOFT DELETE TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19SoftDeleteTicket(ticketId, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (!String(reason || "").trim()) {
    throw new Error("Deletion reason is required.");
  }

  const tickets = pbl19GetTickets();

  const index = tickets.findIndex(function (ticket) {
    return String(ticket.id) === String(ticketId);
  });

  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  const before = pbl19TicketSnapshot(tickets[index]);

  tickets[index].status = "DELETED";
  tickets[index].deleted = true;
  tickets[index].deletedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  tickets[index].deletionReason = String(reason).trim();

  pbl19SaveTickets(tickets);

  pbl19WriteAudit(
    "TICKET_DELETED",
    tickets[index],
    {
      reason: reason,
      previousStatus: before.status || "VALID"
    },
    token
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    ticket: tickets[index]
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   935 — SOFT DELETE API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl19SoftDeleteTicket(ticketId, reason, token) {
  return pbl19SoftDeleteTicket(
    ticketId,
    reason,
    token
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   936 — GET TICKET DETAIL
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19GetTicketDetail(ticketId) {
  const ticket = pbl19GetTicketById(ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  return {
    ticket: ticket,
    auditHistory: pbl19GetTicketAudit(ticketId),
    corrections: pbl19GetCorrectionsByTicket(ticketId)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   937 — TICKET DETAIL API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl19TicketDetail(ticketId) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl19GetTicketDetail(ticketId)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   938 — SEARCH TICKETS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19SearchTickets(criteria) {
  const filters = criteria || {};

  const searchText = String(
    filters.search || ""
  ).trim().toLowerCase();

  const tickets = pbl19GetTickets().filter(
    function (ticket) {
      if (
        filters.includeCancelled !== true &&
        ["CANCELLED", "DELETED"].indexOf(
          pbl18NormalizeStatus(ticket)
        ) !== -1
      ) {
        return false;
      }

      if (filters.product) {
        if (
          String(ticket.product || "").toLowerCase() !==
          String(filters.product).toLowerCase()
        ) {
          return false;
        }
      }

      if (filters.client) {
        if (
          String(ticket.client || "").toLowerCase() !==
          String(filters.client).toLowerCase()
        ) {
          return false;
        }
      }

      if (filters.truckRegistration) {
        if (
          String(ticket.truckRegistration || "").toLowerCase() !==
          String(filters.truckRegistration).toLowerCase()
        ) {
          return false;
        }
      }

      if (filters.startDate || filters.endDate) {
        const date = pbl18ParseDate(ticket.date);

        if (!date) {
          return false;
        }

        if (filters.startDate) {
          const start = pbl18ParseDate(filters.startDate);

          if (start && date < start) {
            return false;
          }
        }

        if (filters.endDate) {
          const end = pbl18ParseDate(filters.endDate);

          if (end && date > end) {
            return false;
          }
        }
      }

      if (searchText) {
        const searchable = [
          ticket.ticketNumber,
          ticket.product,
          ticket.client,
          ticket.truckRegistration,
          ticket.driver,
          ticket.date
        ].join(" ").toLowerCase();

        if (searchable.indexOf(searchText) === -1) {
          return false;
        }
      }

      return true;
    }
  );

  return tickets.slice(
    0,
    Math.max(
      1,
      Math.min(
        PBL19_CONFIG.MAX_SEARCH_RESULTS,
        Number(filters.limit) || 100
      )
    )
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   939 — SEARCH API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl19SearchTickets(criteria) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl19SearchTickets(criteria)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   940 — GET TICKETS BY STATUS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19GetTicketsByStatus(status) {
  const selected = String(status || "").toUpperCase();

  return pbl19GetTickets().filter(function (ticket) {
    return pbl18NormalizeStatus(ticket) === selected;
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   941 — STATUS COUNTS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19GetStatusCounts() {
  const counts = {};

  PBL19_CONFIG.ALLOWED_STATUSES.forEach(function (status) {
    counts[status] = 0;
  });

  pbl19GetTickets().forEach(function (ticket) {
    const status = pbl18NormalizeStatus(ticket);

    counts[status] = (counts[status] || 0) + 1;
  });

  return counts;
}

/* ============================================================
   942 — STATUS COUNTS API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl19StatusCounts() {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl19GetStatusCounts()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   943 — DUPLICATE TICKET NUMBER REPORT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19FindDuplicateNumbers() {
  const groups = {};

  pbl19GetTickets().forEach(function (ticket) {
    const number = String(
      ticket.ticketNumber || ""
    ).trim();

    if (!number) {
      return;
    }

    if (!groups[number]) {
      groups[number] = [];
    }

    groups[number].push({
      id: ticket.id,
      ticketNumber: number,
      date: ticket.date,
      status: ticket.status
    });
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).filter(
    function (number) {
      return groups[number].length > 1;
    }
  ).map(function (number) {
    return {
      ticketNumber: number,
      occurrences: groups[number]
    };
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   944 — DUPLICATE REPORT API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl19DuplicateReport(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl19FindDuplicateNumbers()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   945 — VALIDATE ALL TICKETS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19ValidateAllTickets() {
  const tickets = pbl19GetTickets();

  const invalid = [];

  tickets.forEach(function (ticket) {
    if (
      ["CANCELLED", "DELETED"].indexOf(
        pbl18NormalizeStatus(ticket)
      ) !== -1
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const validation = pbl19ValidateTicket(
      ticket,
      ticket.id
    );

    if (!validation.valid) {
      invalid.push({
        ticketId: ticket.id,
        ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber,
        errors: validation.errors
      });
    }
  });

  return {
    checked: tickets.length,
    invalidCount: invalid.length,
    validCount: tickets.length - invalid.length,
    invalidTickets: invalid
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   946 — VALIDATE ALL TICKETS API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl19ValidateAllTickets(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl19ValidateAllTickets()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   947 — GET TICKET CORRECTION REPORT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19CorrectionReport(startDate, endDate) {
  return pbl19GetCorrectionHistory().filter(
    function (record) {
      const date = pbl18ParseDate(record.createdAt);

      if (!date) {
        return false;
      }

      const start = startDate
        ? pbl18ParseDate(startDate)
        : null;

      const end = endDate
        ? pbl18ParseDate(endDate)
        : null;

      if (start && date < start) {
        return false;
      }

      if (end && date > end) {
        return false;
      }

      return true;
    }
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   948 — CORRECTION REPORT API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl19CorrectionReport(startDate, endDate, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl19CorrectionReport(
      startDate,
      endDate
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   949 — GET TICKET AUDIT REPORT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19AuditReport(startDate, endDate) {
  return pbl19GetAuditCollection().filter(
    function (record) {
      const date = pbl18ParseDate(record.createdAt);

      if (!date) {
        return false;
      }

      const start = startDate
        ? pbl18ParseDate(startDate)
        : null;

      const end = endDate
        ? pbl18ParseDate(endDate)
        : null;

      return (!start || date >= start) &&
        (!end || date <= end);
    }
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   950 — AUDIT REPORT API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl19AuditReport(startDate, endDate, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl19AuditReport(
      startDate,
      endDate
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   951 — LOCK TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19LockTicket(ticketId, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const tickets = pbl19GetTickets();

  const index = tickets.findIndex(function (ticket) {
    return String(ticket.id) === String(ticketId);
  });

  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  tickets[index].locked = true;
  tickets[index].lockedAt = new Date().toISOString();

  pbl19SaveTickets(tickets);

  pbl19WriteAudit(
    "TICKET_LOCKED",
    tickets[index],
    {},
    token
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    ticket: tickets[index]
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   952 — LOCK TICKET API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl19LockTicket(ticketId, token) {
  return pbl19LockTicket(ticketId, token);
}

/* ============================================================
   953 — UNLOCK TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19UnlockTicket(ticketId, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (!String(reason || "").trim()) {
    throw new Error("Unlock reason is required.");
  }

  const tickets = pbl19GetTickets();

  const index = tickets.findIndex(function (ticket) {
    return String(ticket.id) === String(ticketId);
  });

  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  tickets[index].locked = false;
  tickets[index].unlockedAt = new Date().toISOString();

  pbl19SaveTickets(tickets);

  pbl19WriteAudit(
    "TICKET_UNLOCKED",
    tickets[index],
    { reason: reason },
    token
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    ticket: tickets[index]
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   954 — UNLOCK TICKET API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl19UnlockTicket(ticketId, reason, token) {
  return pbl19UnlockTicket(
    ticketId,
    reason,
    token
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   955 — CHECK TICKET LOCK
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19IsTicketLocked(ticketId) {
  const ticket = pbl19GetTicketById(ticketId);

  return !!(
    ticket &&
    ticket.locked === true
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   956 — GET TICKET WORKFLOW
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19GetTicketWorkflow(ticketId) {
  const ticket = pbl19GetTicketById(ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  const status = pbl18NormalizeStatus(ticket);

  return {
    ticketId: ticket.id,
    ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber,
    status: status,
    locked: ticket.locked === true,
    canCorrect: !ticket.locked &&
      status !== "CANCELLED" &&
      status !== "DELETED",
    canCancel: status === "VALID" ||
      status === "CORRECTED" ||
      status === "PENDING",
    canRestore: status === "CANCELLED",
    canDelete: status !== "DELETED"
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   957 — TICKET WORKFLOW API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl19TicketWorkflow(ticketId) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl19GetTicketWorkflow(ticketId)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   958 — GET TICKET WEIGHT SUMMARY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19GetWeightSummary(ticketId) {
  const ticket = pbl19GetTicketById(ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  const gross = Number(ticket.grossKg) || 0;
  const tare = Number(ticket.tareKg) || 0;
  const net = Number(ticket.netKg) || 0;

  return {
    grossKg: gross,
    tareKg: tare,
    netKg: net,
    netTonnes: pbl18KgToTonnes(net),
    calculatedNetKg: pbl18Round(gross - tare, 2),
    differenceKg: pbl18Round(
      net - (gross - tare),
      2
    ),
    valid: Math.abs(net - (gross - tare)) <= 1
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   959 — WEIGHT SUMMARY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl19WeightSummary(ticketId) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl19GetWeightSummary(ticketId)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   960 — MODULE HEALTH
   ============================================================ */

function pbl19GetModuleHealth() {
  return {
    module: "ADVANCED TICKET MANAGEMENT",
    version: PBL19_CONFIG.VERSION,
    tickets: pbl19GetTickets().length,
    activeTickets: pbl19GetTickets().filter(
      pbl18IsActiveTicket
    ).length,
    cancelledTickets: pbl19GetTicketsByStatus(
      "CANCELLED"
    ).length,
    deletedTickets: pbl19GetTicketsByStatus(
      "DELETED"
    ).length,
    duplicateNumbers: pbl19FindDuplicateNumbers().length,
    correctionRecords: pbl19GetCorrectionHistory().length,
    auditRecords: pbl19GetAuditCollection().length,
    checkedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/***************************************************************
 * PONT BASCULE LIVE
 * WEIGHBRIDGE MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
 *
 * PART 20 — ADVANCED REPORTING CENTER
 * Functions 971–1030
 *
 * REPORT MODULES:
 * - Daily reports
 * - Monthly reports
 * - Yearly reports
 * - Product analysis
 * - Client analysis
 * - Truck performance
 * - Driver performance
 * - Date range reports
 * - Delivery summaries
 * - Report archive
 * - CSV export preparation
 *
 * WEIGHTS:
 * Stored in KG
 * Displayed in TONNES
 ***************************************************************/

const PBL20_CONFIG = {
  VERSION: "20.0.0",
  TIME_ZONE: "Africa/Casablanca",
  MAX_REPORT_RECORDS: 1000,
  MAX_REPORT_RESULTS: 10000,
  REPORT_HISTORY_TYPE: "PBL20_REPORT_HISTORY",
  DEFAULT_LIMIT: 100
};

/* ============================================================
   971 — GET REPORT TICKETS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20GetTickets() {
  if (typeof pbl18GetActiveTickets === "function") {
    const result = pbl18SafeCall(
      function () {
        return pbl18GetActiveTickets();
      },
      []
    );

    if (Array.isArray(result)) return result;
  }

  if (typeof getAllTickets === "function") {
    const result = pbl18SafeCall(
      function () {
        return getAllTickets();
      },
      []
    );

    return Array.isArray(result)
      ? result.filter(pbl18IsActiveTicket)
      : [];
  }

  return [];
}

/* ============================================================
   972 — REPORT DATE VALIDATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20ValidateDates(startDate, endDate) {
  const start = pbl18ParseDate(startDate);
  const end = pbl18ParseDate(endDate);

  if (!start || !end) {
    throw new Error("Invalid report date range.");
  }

  if (start.getTime() > end.getTime()) {
    throw new Error(
      "Start date cannot be after end date."
    );
  }

  return {
    start: start,
    end: end,
    startKey: pbl18DateKey(start),
    endKey: pbl18DateKey(end)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   973 — FILTER REPORT TICKETS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20FilterTickets(startDate, endDate, filters) {
  const range = pbl20ValidateDates(
    startDate,
    endDate
  );

  const options = filters || {};

  return pbl20GetTickets().filter(function (ticket) {
    const dateKey = pbl18TicketDateKey(ticket);

    if (
      !dateKey ||
      dateKey < range.startKey ||
      dateKey > range.endKey
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      options.product &&
      String(ticket.product || "").toLowerCase() !==
      String(options.product).toLowerCase()
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      options.client &&
      String(ticket.client || "").toLowerCase() !==
      String(options.client).toLowerCase()
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      options.truck &&
      String(ticket.truckRegistration || "").toLowerCase() !==
      String(options.truck).toLowerCase()
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      options.driver &&
      String(ticket.driver || "").toLowerCase() !==
      String(options.driver).toLowerCase()
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    return true;
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   974 — BUILD REPORT TOTALS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20CalculateTotals(tickets) {
  const rows = tickets || [];

  const totalKg = rows.reduce(
    function (sum, ticket) {
      return sum + pbl18TicketKg(ticket);
    },
    0
  );

  const grossKg = rows.reduce(
    function (sum, ticket) {
      return sum + pbl18Number(ticket.grossKg, 0);
    },
    0
  );

  const tareKg = rows.reduce(
    function (sum, ticket) {
      return sum + pbl18Number(ticket.tareKg, 0);
    },
    0
  );

  return {
    voyages: rows.length,
    grossKg: pbl18Round(grossKg, 2),
    tareKg: pbl18Round(tareKg, 2),
    netKg: pbl18Round(totalKg, 2),
    netTonnes: pbl18KgToTonnes(totalKg),
    averageNetKg: rows.length
      ? pbl18Round(totalKg / rows.length, 2)
      : 0,
    averageNetTonnes: rows.length
      ? pbl18Round(
          pbl18KgToTonnes(totalKg) / rows.length,
          3
        )
      : 0
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   975 — GROUP REPORT DATA
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20GroupTickets(tickets, fieldName) {
  const groups = {};

  (tickets || []).forEach(function (ticket) {
    const name = String(
      ticket[fieldName] || "NON RENSEIGNÉ"
    ).trim();

    if (!groups[name]) {
      groups[name] = [];
    }

    groups[name].push(ticket);
  });

  return groups;
}

/* ============================================================
   976 — BUILD GROUP SUMMARY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20BuildGroupSummary(tickets, fieldName) {
  const groups = pbl20GroupTickets(
    tickets,
    fieldName
  );

  return Object.keys(groups).map(function (name) {
    const groupTickets = groups[name];
    const totals = pbl20CalculateTotals(groupTickets);

    return {
      name: name,
      voyages: totals.voyages,
      grossKg: totals.grossKg,
      tareKg: totals.tareKg,
      netKg: totals.netKg,
      netTonnes: totals.netTonnes,
      averageNetKg: totals.averageNetKg
    };
  }).sort(function (a, b) {
    return b.netKg - a.netKg;
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   977 — DAILY REPORT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20DailyReport(dateValue) {
  const date = pbl18ParseDate(
    dateValue || new Date()
  );

  if (!date) {
    throw new Error("Invalid daily report date.");
  }

  const dateKey = pbl18DateKey(date);

  const tickets = pbl20GetTickets().filter(
    function (ticket) {
      return pbl18TicketDateKey(ticket) === dateKey;
    }
  );

  return {
    reportType: "DAILY",
    date: dateKey,
    dateDisplay: pbl18FormatDate(date),
    totals: pbl20CalculateTotals(tickets),
    products: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "product"
    ),
    clients: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "client"
    ),
    trucks: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "truckRegistration"
    ),
    tickets: tickets.map(pbl20TicketReportRow),
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   978 — DAILY REPORT API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl20DailyReport(dateValue) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl20DailyReport(dateValue)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   979 — MONTHLY REPORT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20MonthlyReport(year, month) {
  const selectedYear = Number(year);
  const selectedMonth = Number(month);

  if (
    !Number.isInteger(selectedYear) ||
    !Number.isInteger(selectedMonth) ||
    selectedMonth < 1 ||
    selectedMonth > 12
  ) {
    throw new Error("Invalid monthly report period.");
  }

  const tickets = pbl20GetTickets().filter(
    function (ticket) {
      const date = pbl18ParseDate(ticket.date);

      return date &&
        date.getFullYear() === selectedYear &&
        date.getMonth() + 1 === selectedMonth;
    }
  );

  return {
    reportType: "MONTHLY",
    year: selectedYear,
    month: selectedMonth,
    totals: pbl20CalculateTotals(tickets),
    products: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "product"
    ),
    clients: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "client"
    ),
    trucks: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "truckRegistration"
    ),
    tickets: tickets.map(pbl20TicketReportRow),
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   980 — MONTHLY REPORT API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl20MonthlyReport(year, month) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl20MonthlyReport(year, month)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   981 — YEARLY REPORT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20YearlyReport(year) {
  const selectedYear = Number(year);

  if (!Number.isInteger(selectedYear)) {
    throw new Error("Invalid report year.");
  }

  const tickets = pbl20GetTickets().filter(
    function (ticket) {
      const date = pbl18ParseDate(ticket.date);

      return date &&
        date.getFullYear() === selectedYear;
    }
  );

  const monthly = [];

  for (let month = 1; month <= 12; month++) {
    const monthTickets = tickets.filter(
      function (ticket) {
        const date = pbl18ParseDate(ticket.date);

        return date &&
          date.getMonth() + 1 === month;
      }
    );

    const totals = pbl20CalculateTotals(
      monthTickets
    );

    monthly.push({
      month: month,
      voyages: totals.voyages,
      netKg: totals.netKg,
      netTonnes: totals.netTonnes
    });
  }

  return {
    reportType: "YEARLY",
    year: selectedYear,
    totals: pbl20CalculateTotals(tickets),
    monthly: monthly,
    products: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "product"
    ),
    clients: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "client"
    ),
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   982 — YEARLY REPORT API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl20YearlyReport(year) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl20YearlyReport(year)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   983 — TICKET REPORT ROW
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20TicketReportRow(ticket) {
  const netKg = pbl18TicketKg(ticket);

  return {
    id: ticket.id || "",
    ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber || "",
    date: ticket.date || "",
    time: ticket.time || "",
    product: ticket.product || "",
    client: ticket.client || "",
    truckRegistration: ticket.truckRegistration || "",
    driver: ticket.driver || "",
    grossKg: pbl18Number(ticket.grossKg, 0),
    tareKg: pbl18Number(ticket.tareKg, 0),
    netKg: netKg,
    netTonnes: pbl18KgToTonnes(netKg),
    status: ticket.status || "VALID"
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   984 — DATE RANGE REPORT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20DateRangeReport(startDate, endDate, filters) {
  const tickets = pbl20FilterTickets(
    startDate,
    endDate,
    filters
  );

  return {
    reportType: "DATE_RANGE",
    startDate: pbl18FormatDate(startDate),
    endDate: pbl18FormatDate(endDate),
    totals: pbl20CalculateTotals(tickets),
    products: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "product"
    ),
    clients: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "client"
    ),
    trucks: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "truckRegistration"
    ),
    tickets: tickets.map(pbl20TicketReportRow),
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   985 — DATE RANGE REPORT API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl20DateRangeReport(
  startDate,
  endDate,
  filters
) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl20DateRangeReport(
      startDate,
      endDate,
      filters
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   986 — PRODUCT REPORT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20ProductReport(productName, startDate, endDate) {
  const name = String(productName || "").trim();

  if (!name) {
    throw new Error("Product name is required.");
  }

  const tickets = pbl20FilterTickets(
    startDate,
    endDate,
    { product: name }
  );

  return {
    reportType: "PRODUCT",
    product: name,
    period: {
      startDate: pbl18FormatDate(startDate),
      endDate: pbl18FormatDate(endDate)
    },
    totals: pbl20CalculateTotals(tickets),
    clients: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "client"
    ),
    trucks: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "truckRegistration"
    ),
    tickets: tickets.map(pbl20TicketReportRow)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   990 — TRUCK REPORT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20TruckReport(truckRegistration, startDate, endDate) {
  const registration = String(
    truckRegistration || ""
  ).trim();

  if (!registration) {
    throw new Error("Truck registration is required.");
  }

  const tickets = pbl20FilterTickets(
    startDate,
    endDate,
    { truck: registration }
  );

  const totals = pbl20CalculateTotals(tickets);

  return {
    reportType: "TRUCK",
    truckRegistration: registration,
    totals: totals,
    averageVoyageKg: totals.averageNetKg,
    products: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "product"
    ),
    clients: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "client"
    ),
    tickets: tickets.map(pbl20TicketReportRow)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   994 — PRODUCT RANKING
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20ProductRanking(startDate, endDate) {
  const tickets = pbl20FilterTickets(
    startDate,
    endDate
  );

  return pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
    tickets,
    "product"
  ).map(function (item, index) {
    item.rank = index + 1;
    return item;
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   997 — REPORT ARCHIVE STORAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20GetReportArchive() {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13ReadArray !== "function"
  ) {
    return [];
  }

  return pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  ).filter(function (record) {
    return record &&
      record.recordType === PBL20_CONFIG.REPORT_HISTORY_TYPE;
  }).sort(function (a, b) {
    return String(b.createdAt || "").localeCompare(
      String(a.createdAt || "")
    );
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   998 — SAVE REPORT ARCHIVE ENTRY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20ArchiveReport(reportType, parameters, summary, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13ReadArray !== "function" ||
    typeof pbl13WriteArray !== "function"
  ) {
    throw new Error("Report archive storage is unavailable.");
  }

  const records = pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  );

  const entry = {
    id: "PBL20-REPORT-" + Utilities.getUuid(),
    recordType: PBL20_CONFIG.REPORT_HISTORY_TYPE,
    reportType: String(reportType || "CUSTOM"),
    parameters: parameters || {},
    summary: summary || {},
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  records.push(entry);

  const archive = records.filter(function (record) {
    return record &&
      record.recordType === PBL20_CONFIG.REPORT_HISTORY_TYPE;
  });

  const retainedIds = archive
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(b.createdAt || "").localeCompare(
        String(a.createdAt || "")
      );
    })
    .slice(0, PBL20_CONFIG.MAX_REPORT_RECORDS)
    .map(function (record) {
      return record.id;
    });

  const retained = records.filter(function (record) {
    return !record ||
      record.recordType !== PBL20_CONFIG.REPORT_HISTORY_TYPE ||
      retainedIds.indexOf(record.id) !== -1;
  });

  pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    retained
  );

  if (typeof writeAuditLog === "function") {
    pbl18SafeCall(function () {
      writeAuditLog(
        "REPORT_ARCHIVED",
        {
          reportType: entry.reportType,
          reportId: entry.id
        },
        token
      );
    }, null);
  }

  return entry;
}

/* ============================================================
   999 — ARCHIVE REPORT API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl20ArchiveReport(
  reportType,
  parameters,
  summary,
  token
) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl20ArchiveReport(
      reportType,
      parameters,
      summary,
      token
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1000 — GET REPORT ARCHIVE API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl20GetReportArchive(limit, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl20GetReportArchive().slice(
      0,
      Math.max(1, Number(limit) || 100)
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1001 — GET ARCHIVED REPORT BY ID
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20GetArchivedReport(reportId) {
  const id = String(reportId || "");

  return pbl20GetReportArchive().find(
    function (record) {
      return String(record.id) === id;
    }
  ) || null;
}

/* ============================================================
   1002 — ARCHIVED REPORT DETAIL API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl20ArchivedReportDetail(reportId, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const report = pbl20GetArchivedReport(reportId);

  if (!report) {
    throw new Error("Archived report not found.");
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    data: report
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1003 — BUILD CSV ROWS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20BuildCsvRows(report) {
  const columns = [
    "N° BON",
    "DATE",
    "HEURE",
    "PRODUIT",
    "CLIENT",
    "IMMATRICULATION",
    "CHAUFFEUR",
    "POIDS BRUT KG",
    "TARE KG",
    "NET KG",
    "NET TONNES",
    "STATUT"
  ];

  const rows = [columns];

  (report.tickets || []).forEach(function (ticket) {
    rows.push([
      ticket.ticketNumber,
      ticket.date,
      ticket.time,
      ticket.product,
      ticket.client,
      ticket.truckRegistration,
      ticket.driver,
      ticket.grossKg,
      ticket.tareKg,
      ticket.netKg,
      ticket.netTonnes,
      ticket.status
    ]);
  });

  return rows;
}

/* ============================================================
   1004 — ESCAPE CSV VALUE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20EscapeCsv(value) {
  let textValue = String(
    value === undefined || value === null
      ? ""
      : value
  );

  if (/^[=+\-@]/.test(textValue)) {
    textValue = "'" + textValue;
  }

  return '"' + textValue.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
}

/* ============================================================
   1005 — GENERATE CSV
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20GenerateCsv(report) {
  const rows = pbl20BuildCsvRows(report);

  return "\uFEFF" + rows.map(function (row) {
    return row.map(pbl20EscapeCsv).join(";");
  }).join("\r\n");
}

/* ============================================================
   1006 — CSV EXPORT API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl20ExportDailyCsv(dateValue, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const report = pbl20DailyReport(dateValue);

  return {
    success: true,
    filename: "PBL_DAILY_" +
      String(report.date).replace(/-/g, "") +
      ".csv",
    mimeType: "text/csv;charset=utf-8",
    content: pbl20GenerateCsv(report)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1009 — REPORT SUMMARY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20ReportSummary() {
  const tickets = pbl20GetTickets();
  const totals = pbl20CalculateTotals(tickets);

  return {
    totalTickets: tickets.length,
    totalVoyages: totals.voyages,
    totalNetKg: totals.netKg,
    totalNetTonnes: totals.netTonnes,
    totalProducts: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "product"
    ).length,
    totalClients: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "client"
    ).length,
    totalTrucks: pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
      tickets,
      "truckRegistration"
    ).length,
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

  };

/* ============================================================
   1011 — DAILY COMPARISON
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20CompareDaily(firstDate, secondDate) {
  const first = pbl20DailyReport(firstDate);
  const second = pbl20DailyReport(secondDate);

  return {
    firstDate: first.date,
    secondDate: second.date,

    firstVoyages: first.totals.voyages,
    secondVoyages: second.totals.voyages,

    voyageDifference:
      second.totals.voyages - first.totals.voyages,

    firstTonnes: first.totals.netTonnes,
    secondTonnes: second.totals.netTonnes,

    tonneDifference: pbl18Round(
      second.totals.netTonnes -
      first.totals.netTonnes,
      3
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1012 — DAILY COMPARISON API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl20CompareDaily(firstDate, secondDate) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl20CompareDaily(
      firstDate,
      secondDate
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1013 — MONTHLY COMPARISON
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20CompareMonthly(
  firstYear,
  firstMonth,
  secondYear,
  secondMonth
) {
  const first = pbl20MonthlyReport(
    firstYear,
    firstMonth
  );

  const second = pbl20MonthlyReport(
    secondYear,
    secondMonth
  );

  return {
    firstPeriod: {
      year: firstYear,
      month: firstMonth
    },
    secondPeriod: {
      year: secondYear,
      month: secondMonth
    },
    voyageDifference:
      second.totals.voyages - first.totals.voyages,
    tonneDifference: pbl18Round(
      second.totals.netTonnes -
      first.totals.netTonnes,
      3
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1014 — MONTHLY COMPARISON API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl20CompareMonthly(
  firstYear,
  firstMonth,
  secondYear,
  secondMonth
) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl20CompareMonthly(
      firstYear,
      firstMonth,
      secondYear,
      secondMonth
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1015 — REPORT FILTER OPTIONS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20GetFilterOptions() {
  const tickets = pbl20GetTickets();

  function uniqueValues(field) {
    const values = tickets.map(function (ticket) {
      return String(ticket[field] || "").trim();
    }).filter(Boolean);

    return Array.from(new Set(values)).sort();
  }

  return {
    products: uniqueValues("product"),
    clients: uniqueValues("client"),
    trucks: uniqueValues("truckRegistration"),
    drivers: uniqueValues("driver")
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1016 — FILTER OPTIONS API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl20FilterOptions() {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl20GetFilterOptions()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1017 — REPORT HEALTH
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20ReportHealth() {
  const tickets = pbl20GetTickets();

  const invalidDates = tickets.filter(
    function (ticket) {
      return !pbl18ParseDate(ticket.date);
    }
  );

  const invalidWeights = tickets.filter(
    function (ticket) {
      const net = pbl18TicketKg(ticket);
      return !isFinite(net) || net < 0;
    }
  );

  return {
    module: "ADVANCED REPORTING CENTER",
    version: PBL20_CONFIG.VERSION,
    totalTickets: tickets.length,
    invalidDates: invalidDates.length,
    invalidWeights: invalidWeights.length,
    archiveEntries: pbl20GetReportArchive().length,
    healthy:
      invalidDates.length === 0 &&
      invalidWeights.length === 0,
    checkedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1018 — REPORT HEALTH API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl20ReportHealth(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl20ReportHealth()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1023 — REPORT DATA VALIDATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20ValidateReport(report) {
  const errors = [];

  if (!report || typeof report !== "object") {
    errors.push("Report object is missing.");
  } else {
    if (!report.reportType) {
      errors.push("Report type is missing.");
    }

    if (!report.totals) {
      errors.push("Report totals are missing.");
    }

    if (
      report.totals &&
      report.totals.netKg < 0
    ) {
      errors.push("Report net weight is negative.");
    }
  }

  return {
    valid: errors.length === 0,
    errors: errors
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1024 — REPORT DATA VALIDATION API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl20ValidateReport(report, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl20ValidateReport(report)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1025 — TOP REPORT HIGHLIGHTS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20GetHighlights(startDate, endDate) {
  const tickets = pbl20FilterTickets(
    startDate,
    endDate
  );

  const totals = pbl20CalculateTotals(tickets);

  const products = pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
    tickets,
    "product"
  );

  const clients = pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
    tickets,
    "client"
  );

  const trucks = pbl20BuildGroupSummary(
    tickets,
    "truckRegistration"
  );

  return {
    totalVoyages: totals.voyages,
    totalTonnes: totals.netTonnes,
    averageVoyageKg: totals.averageNetKg,
    leadingProduct: products[0] || null,
    leadingClient: clients[0] || null,
    leadingTruck: trucks[0] || null,
    period: {
      startDate: pbl18FormatDate(startDate),
      endDate: pbl18FormatDate(endDate)
    }
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1026 — HIGHLIGHTS API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl20Highlights(startDate, endDate) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl20GetHighlights(
      startDate,
      endDate
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1027 — PRINT REPORT DATA
   ============================================================ */

function pbl20BuildPrintData(report) {
  const validation = pbl20ValidateReport(report);

  if (!validation.valid) {
    throw new Error(validation.errors.join("\n"));
  }

  return {
    title: "PONT BASCULE LIVE",
    subtitle: "RAPPORT DE PESAGE",
    reportType: report.reportType,
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    period: report.dateDisplay ||
      report.startDate + " — " + report.endDate ||
      "",
    totals: report.totals,
    tickets: report.tickets || [],
    products: report.products || [],
    clients: report.clients || [],
    trucks: report.trucks || [],
    note: "Poids exprimés en KG et TONNES."
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1028 — PRINT DATA API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl20PrintData(report, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl20BuildPrintData(report)
  };
}

    clientReports: true,
    truckReports: true,
    driverReports: true,
    csvPreparation: true,
    reportArchive:
      typeof PBL13_STORAGE !== "undefined" &&
      !!PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    pdfGeneration: false,
    xlsxGeneration: false,
    checkedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1031 — SAFE TEXT NORMALIZATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21NormalizeText(value) {
  return String(
    value === undefined || value === null
      ? ""
      : value
  )
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase()
    .normalize("NFD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "");
}

/* ============================================================
   1032 — GET SEARCH DATA
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21GetTickets() {
  if (typeof pbl20GetTickets === "function") {
    const tickets = pbl20GetTickets();
    return Array.isArray(tickets) ? tickets : [];
  }

  if (typeof getAllTickets === "function") {
    const tickets = getAllTickets();
    return Array.isArray(tickets)
      ? tickets.filter(pbl18IsActiveTicket)
      : [];
  }

  return [];
}

/* ============================================================
   1033 — MATCH TEXT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21MatchesText(value, searchTerm) {
  const valueText = pbl21NormalizeText(value);
  const termText = pbl21NormalizeText(searchTerm);

  if (!termText) return true;

  return valueText.indexOf(termText) !== -1;
}

/* ============================================================
   1034 — MATCH EXACT TEXT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21MatchesExact(value, expected) {
  if (
    expected === undefined ||
    expected === null ||
    expected === ""
  ) {
    return true;
  }

  return pbl21NormalizeText(value) ===
    pbl21NormalizeText(expected);
}

/* ============================================================
   1035 — SEARCH TICKET NUMBER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21SearchTicketNumber(term) {
  const query = pbl21NormalizeText(term);

  if (!query) return [];

  return pbl21GetTickets().filter(
    function (ticket) {
      return pbl21MatchesText(
        ticket.ticketNumber,
        query
      );
    }
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1036 — SEARCH PRODUCT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21SearchProduct(term) {
  const query = pbl21NormalizeText(term);

  return pbl21GetTickets().filter(
    function (ticket) {
      return pbl21MatchesText(
        ticket.product,
        query
      );
    }
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1037 — SEARCH CLIENT

function pbl21SearchClient(term) {
