  return pbl21GetTickets().filter(
    function (ticket) {
      return pbl21MatchesText(
        ticket.client,
        query
      );
    }
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1038 — SEARCH TRUCK
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21SearchTruck(term) {
  const query = pbl21NormalizeText(term);

  return pbl21GetTickets().filter(
    function (ticket) {
      return pbl21MatchesText(
        ticket.truckRegistration,
        query
      );
    }
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1039 — SEARCH DRIVER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21SearchDriver(term) {
  const query = pbl21NormalizeText(term);

  return pbl21GetTickets().filter(
    function (ticket) {
      return pbl21MatchesText(
        ticket.driver,
        query
      );
    }
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1040 — SEARCH ALL TICKET FIELDS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21GlobalSearch(term) {
  const query = pbl21NormalizeText(term);

  if (!query) return [];

  return pbl21GetTickets().filter(
    function (ticket) {
      const searchable = [
        ticket.ticketNumber,
        ticket.product,
        ticket.client,
        ticket.truckRegistration,
        ticket.driver,
        ticket.date,
        ticket.time,
        ticket.status
      ].join(" ");

      return pbl21MatchesText(
        searchable,
        query
      );
    }
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1041 — FILTER BY DATE RANGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21FilterByDate(tickets, startDate, endDate) {
  const start = startDate
    ? pbl18ParseDate(startDate)
    : null;

  const end = endDate
    ? pbl18ParseDate(endDate)
    : null;

  if (startDate && !start) {
    throw new Error("Invalid start date.");
  }

  if (endDate && !end) {
    throw new Error("Invalid end date.");
  }

  if (
    start &&
    end &&
    start.getTime() > end.getTime()
  ) {
    throw new Error("Invalid date range.");
  }

  return tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
    const date = pbl18ParseDate(ticket.date);

    if (!date) return false;

    if (
      start &&
      pbl18DateKey(date) < pbl18DateKey(start)
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      end &&
      pbl18DateKey(date) > pbl18DateKey(end)
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    return true;
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1042 — FILTER BY NET WEIGHT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21FilterByWeight(
  tickets,
  minimumKg,
  maximumKg
) {
  const min = minimumKg === "" ||
    minimumKg === null ||
    minimumKg === undefined
      ? null
      : Number(minimumKg);

  const max = maximumKg === "" ||
    maximumKg === null ||
    maximumKg === undefined
      ? null
      : Number(maximumKg);

  if (min !== null && (!isFinite(min) || min < 0)) {
    throw new Error("Invalid minimum weight.");
  }

  if (max !== null && (!isFinite(max) || max < 0)) {
    throw new Error("Invalid maximum weight.");
  }

  if (
    min !== null &&
    max !== null &&
    min > max
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Minimum weight cannot exceed maximum weight."
    );
  }

  return tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
    const weight = pbl18TicketKg(ticket);

    if (min !== null && weight < min) return false;
    if (max !== null && weight > max) return false;

    return true;
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1043 — FILTER BY TICKET STATUS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21FilterByStatus(tickets, status) {
  if (!status) return tickets;

  return tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
    return pbl21MatchesExact(
      ticket.status || "VALID",
      status
    );
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1044 — FILTER BY MULTIPLE FIELDS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21ApplyFilters(filters) {
  const options = filters || {};
  let tickets = pbl21GetTickets();

  if (options.search) {
    const term = pbl21NormalizeText(options.search);

    tickets = tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      return [
        ticket.ticketNumber,
        ticket.product,
        ticket.client,
        ticket.truckRegistration,
        ticket.driver,
        ticket.date,
        ticket.time,
        ticket.status
      ].some(function (value) {
        return pbl21MatchesText(value, term);
      });
    });
  }

  if (options.ticketNumber) {
    tickets = tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      return pbl21MatchesText(
        ticket.ticketNumber,
        options.ticketNumber
      );
    });
  }

  if (options.product) {
    tickets = tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      return pbl21MatchesText(
        ticket.product,
        options.product
      );
    });
  }

  if (options.client) {
    tickets = tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      return pbl21MatchesText(
        ticket.client,
        options.client
      );
    });
  }

  if (options.truck) {
    tickets = tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      return pbl21MatchesText(
        ticket.truckRegistration,
        options.truck
      );
    });
  }

  if (options.driver) {
    tickets = tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      return pbl21MatchesText(
        ticket.driver,
        options.driver
      );
    });
  }

  if (options.startDate || options.endDate) {
    tickets = pbl21FilterByDate(
      tickets,
      options.startDate,
      options.endDate
    );
  }

  if (
    options.minimumKg !== undefined ||
    options.maximumKg !== undefined
  ) {
    tickets = pbl21FilterByWeight(
      tickets,
      options.minimumKg,
      options.maximumKg
    );
  }

  if (options.status) {
    tickets = pbl21FilterByStatus(
      tickets,
      options.status
    );
  }

  return tickets;
}

/* ============================================================
   1045 — SORT TICKETS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21SortTickets(tickets, sortBy, direction) {
  const allowed = [
    "ticketNumber",
    "date",
    "time",
    "product",
    "client",
    "truckRegistration",
    "driver",
    "grossKg",
    "tareKg",
    "netKg",
    "status"
  ];

  const field = allowed.indexOf(sortBy) !== -1
    ? sortBy
    : "date";

  const order = String(direction || "DESC")
    .toUpperCase() === "ASC"
      ? 1
      : -1;

  return tickets.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
    let first = a[field];
    let second = b[field];

    if (
      field === "grossKg" ||
      field === "tareKg" ||
      field === "netKg"
    ) {
      first = field === "netKg"
        ? pbl18TicketKg(a)
        : pbl18Number(first, 0);

      second = field === "netKg"
        ? pbl18TicketKg(b)
        : pbl18Number(second, 0);

      return (first - second) * order;
    }

    if (field === "date") {
      first = pbl18TicketDateKey(a);
      second = pbl18TicketDateKey(b);
    }

    return String(first || "").localeCompare(
      String(second || ""),
      undefined,
      { numeric: true, sensitivity: "base" }
    ) * order;
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1046 — PAGINATE RESULTS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21Paginate(tickets, page, pageSize) {
  const requestedPage = Math.max(
    1,
    parseInt(page, 10) || PBL21_CONFIG.DEFAULT_PAGE
  );

  const requestedSize = Math.min(
    PBL21_CONFIG.MAX_PAGE_SIZE,
    Math.max(
      1,
      parseInt(pageSize, 10) ||
      PBL21_CONFIG.DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE
    )
  );

  const total = tickets.length;
  const totalPages = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil(total / requestedSize)
  );

  const currentPage = Math.min(
    requestedPage,
    totalPages
  );

  const startIndex =
    (currentPage - 1) * requestedSize;

  return {
    page: currentPage,
    pageSize: requestedSize,
    totalRecords: total,
    totalPages: totalPages,
    hasPrevious: currentPage > 1,
    hasNext: currentPage < totalPages,
    startRecord: total ? startIndex + 1 : 0,
    endRecord: Math.min(
      startIndex + requestedSize,
      total
    ),
    records: tickets.slice(
      startIndex,
      startIndex + requestedSize
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1047 — ADVANCED SEARCH ENGINE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21AdvancedSearch(parameters) {
  const options = parameters || {};

  let tickets = pbl21ApplyFilters(options);

  tickets = pbl21SortTickets(
    tickets,
    options.sortBy || "date",
    options.sortDirection || "DESC"
  );

  const pagination = pbl21Paginate(
    tickets,
    options.page,
    options.pageSize
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    filters: options,
    pagination: pagination,
    totals: typeof pbl20CalculateTotals === "function"
      ? pbl20CalculateTotals(tickets)
      : {
          voyages: tickets.length,
          netKg: 0,
          netTonnes: 0
        },
    records: pagination.records.map(
      function (ticket) {
        return typeof pbl20TicketReportRow === "function"
          ? pbl20TicketReportRow(ticket)
          : ticket;
      }
    ),
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1048 — ADVANCED SEARCH API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21AdvancedSearch(parameters) {
  return pbl21AdvancedSearch(parameters);
}

/* ============================================================
   1049 — QUICK SEARCH API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21QuickSearch(term, limit) {
  const query = pbl21NormalizeText(term);

  if (!query) {
    return {
      success: true,
      records: [],
      total: 0
    };
  }

  const results = pbl21GlobalSearch(query);

  const maximum = Math.min(
    200,
    Math.max(1, Number(limit) || 20)
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    total: results.length,
    records: results.slice(0, maximum).map(
      pbl20TicketReportRow
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1050 — EXACT TICKET LOOKUP
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21FindExactTicket(ticketNumber) {
  const number = pbl21NormalizeText(ticketNumber);

  if (!number) return null;

  return pbl21GetTickets().find(
    function (ticket) {
      return pbl21NormalizeText(
        ticket.ticketNumber
      ) === number;
    }
  ) || null;
}

/* ============================================================
   1051 — EXACT TICKET LOOKUP API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21FindExactTicket(ticketNumber) {
  const ticket = pbl21FindExactTicket(ticketNumber);

  return {
    success: true,
    found: !!ticket,
    data: ticket
      ? pbl20TicketReportRow(ticket)
      : null
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1052 — SEARCH SUGGESTIONS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21GetSuggestions(term) {
  const query = pbl21NormalizeText(term);

  if (!query) return [];

  const tickets = pbl21GetTickets();
  const fields = [
    "ticketNumber",
    "product",
    "client",
    "truckRegistration",
    "driver"
  ];

  const suggestions = {};

  tickets.forEach(function (ticket) {
    fields.forEach(function (field) {
      const value = String(ticket[field] || "").trim();

      if (
        value &&
        pbl21MatchesText(value, query)
      ) {
        suggestions[value] = true;
      }
    });
  });

  return Object.keys(suggestions)
    .sort()
    .slice(0, 30);
}

/* ============================================================
   1053 — SEARCH SUGGESTIONS API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21SearchSuggestions(term) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl21GetSuggestions(term)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1054 — STORAGE ACCESS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21ReadActivity() {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13ReadArray !== "function"
  ) {
    return [];
  }

  return pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1055 — SAVE SEARCH HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21SaveSearchHistory(
  parameters,
  resultCount,
  token
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  if (
    typeof pbl13WriteArray !== "function" ||
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  ) {
    throw new Error("Search history storage unavailable.");
  }

  const records = pbl21ReadActivity();

  const history = {
    id: "PBL21-HISTORY-" + Utilities.getUuid(),
    recordType: PBL21_CONFIG.SEARCH_HISTORY_TYPE,
    parameters: parameters || {},
    resultCount: Number(resultCount) || 0,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  records.push(history);

  const historyRecords = records.filter(
    function (record) {
      return record &&
        record.recordType === PBL21_CONFIG.SEARCH_HISTORY_TYPE;
    }
  );

  const keepIds = historyRecords
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(b.createdAt).localeCompare(
        String(a.createdAt)
      );
    })
    .slice(0, PBL21_CONFIG.MAX_SEARCH_HISTORY)
    .map(function (record) {
      return record.id;
    });

  const cleaned = records.filter(function (record) {
    return !record ||
      record.recordType !== PBL21_CONFIG.SEARCH_HISTORY_TYPE ||
      keepIds.indexOf(record.id) !== -1;
  });

  pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    cleaned
  );

  return history;
}

/* ============================================================
   1056 — SAVE SEARCH HISTORY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21SaveSearchHistory(
  parameters,
  resultCount,
  token
) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl21SaveSearchHistory(
      parameters,
      resultCount,
      token
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1057 — GET SEARCH HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21GetSearchHistory() {
  return pbl21ReadActivity()
    .filter(function (record) {
      return record &&
        record.recordType === PBL21_CONFIG.SEARCH_HISTORY_TYPE;
    })
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(b.createdAt || "").localeCompare(
        String(a.createdAt || "")
      );
    });
}

/* ============================================================
   1058 — SEARCH HISTORY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21GetSearchHistory(limit, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl21GetSearchHistory().slice(
      0,
      Math.max(1, Number(limit) || 50)
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1059 — DELETE SEARCH HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21ClearSearchHistory(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const records = pbl21ReadActivity().filter(
    function (record) {
      return !record ||
        record.recordType !== PBL21_CONFIG.SEARCH_HISTORY_TYPE;
    }
  );

  pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    records
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Search history cleared."
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1060 — CLEAR SEARCH HISTORY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21ClearSearchHistory(token) {
  return pbl21ClearSearchHistory(token);
}

/* ============================================================
   1061 — SAVE SEARCH DEFINITION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21SaveSearch(
  name,
  parameters,
  token
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const searchName = String(name || "").trim();

  if (!searchName) {
    throw new Error("Saved search name is required.");
  }

  if (
    typeof pbl13WriteArray !== "function" ||
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  ) {
    throw new Error("Saved search storage unavailable.");
  }

  const records = pbl21ReadActivity();

  const savedSearch = {
    id: "PBL21-SAVED-" + Utilities.getUuid(),
    recordType: PBL21_CONFIG.SAVED_SEARCH_TYPE,
    name: searchName,
    parameters: parameters || {},
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  records.push(savedSearch);

  const saved = records.filter(function (record) {
    return record &&
      record.recordType === PBL21_CONFIG.SAVED_SEARCH_TYPE;
  });

  const keepIds = saved
    .slice(-PBL21_CONFIG.MAX_SAVED_SEARCHES)
    .map(function (record) {
      return record.id;
    });

  const cleaned = records.filter(function (record) {
    return !record ||
      record.recordType !== PBL21_CONFIG.SAVED_SEARCH_TYPE ||
      keepIds.indexOf(record.id) !== -1;
  });

  pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    cleaned
  );

  return savedSearch;
}

/* ============================================================
   1062 — SAVE SEARCH API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21SaveSearch(
  name,
  parameters,
  token
) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl21SaveSearch(
      name,
      parameters,
      token
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1063 — GET SAVED SEARCHES
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21GetSavedSearches() {
  return pbl21ReadActivity()
    .filter(function (record) {
      return record &&
        record.recordType === PBL21_CONFIG.SAVED_SEARCH_TYPE;
    })
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(a.name || "").localeCompare(
        String(b.name || "")
      );
    });
}

/* ============================================================
   1064 — SAVED SEARCHES API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21GetSavedSearches(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl21GetSavedSearches()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1065 — GET SAVED SEARCH BY ID
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21GetSavedSearchById(searchId) {
  const id = String(searchId || "");

  return pbl21GetSavedSearches().find(
    function (record) {
      return String(record.id) === id;
    }
  ) || null;
}

/* ============================================================
   1066 — RUN SAVED SEARCH
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21RunSavedSearch(searchId, page) {
  const saved = pbl21GetSavedSearchById(searchId);

  if (!saved) {
    throw new Error("Saved search not found.");
  }

  const parameters = Object.assign(
    {},
    saved.parameters || {},
    { page: page || 1 }
  );

  return pbl21AdvancedSearch(parameters);
}

/* ============================================================
   1067 — RUN SAVED SEARCH API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21RunSavedSearch(searchId, page, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return pbl21RunSavedSearch(
    searchId,
    page
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1068 — DELETE SAVED SEARCH
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21DeleteSavedSearch(searchId, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const id = String(searchId || "");

  const records = pbl21ReadActivity();

  const exists = records.some(function (record) {
    return record &&
      record.recordType === PBL21_CONFIG.SAVED_SEARCH_TYPE &&
      String(record.id) === id;
  });

  if (!exists) {
    throw new Error("Saved search not found.");
  }

  const cleaned = records.filter(function (record) {
    return !record ||
      record.recordType !== PBL21_CONFIG.SAVED_SEARCH_TYPE ||
      String(record.id) !== id;
  });

  pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    cleaned
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Saved search deleted."
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1069 — DELETE SAVED SEARCH API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21DeleteSavedSearch(searchId, token) {
  return pbl21DeleteSavedSearch(
    searchId,
    token
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1070 — SEARCH STATISTICS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21GetSearchStatistics() {
  const tickets = pbl21GetTickets();

  return {
    totalTickets: tickets.length,
    totalProducts: new Set(
      tickets.map(function (ticket) {
        return ticket.product || "";
      }).filter(Boolean)
    ).size,
    totalClients: new Set(
      tickets.map(function (ticket) {
        return ticket.client || "";
      }).filter(Boolean)
    ).size,
    totalTrucks: new Set(
      tickets.map(function (ticket) {
        return ticket.truckRegistration || "";
      }).filter(Boolean)
    ).size,
    totalDrivers: new Set(
      tickets.map(function (ticket) {
        return ticket.driver || "";
      }).filter(Boolean)
    ).size,
    savedSearches: pbl21GetSavedSearches().length,
    historyEntries: pbl21GetSearchHistory().length
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1071 — SEARCH STATISTICS API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21SearchStatistics(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl21GetSearchStatistics()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1072 — SEARCH BY EXACT DATE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21SearchByExactDate(dateValue) {
  const date = pbl18ParseDate(dateValue);

  if (!date) {
    throw new Error("Invalid search date.");
  }

  const key = pbl18DateKey(date);

  return pbl21GetTickets().filter(
    function (ticket) {
      return pbl18TicketDateKey(ticket) === key;
    }
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1073 — SEARCH BY EXACT DATE API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21SearchByExactDate(dateValue) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl21SearchByExactDate(dateValue).map(
      pbl20TicketReportRow
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1074 — SEARCH BY NET WEIGHT RANGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21SearchByWeightRange(minimumKg, maximumKg) {
  return pbl21FilterByWeight(
    pbl21GetTickets(),
    minimumKg,
    maximumKg
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1075 — SEARCH BY WEIGHT RANGE API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21SearchByWeightRange(
  minimumKg,
  maximumKg
) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl21SearchByWeightRange(
      minimumKg,
      maximumKg
    ).map(pbl20TicketReportRow)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1076 — SEARCH DUPLICATE TICKET NUMBERS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21FindDuplicateTicketNumbers() {
  const map = {};

  pbl21GetTickets().forEach(function (ticket) {
    const number = String(
      ticket.ticketNumber || ""
    ).trim();

    if (!number) return;

    if (!map[number]) map[number] = [];

    map[number].push(ticket);
  });

  return Object.keys(map)
    .filter(function (number) {
      return map[number].length > 1;
    })
    .map(function (number) {
      return {
        ticketNumber: number,
        count: map[number].length,
        ticketIds: map[number].map(
          function (ticket) {
            return ticket.id || "";
          }
        )
      };
    });
}

/* ============================================================
   1077 — DUPLICATE SEARCH API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21FindDuplicateTicketNumbers(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl21FindDuplicateTicketNumbers()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1078 — SEARCH DATA QUALITY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21GetDataQuality() {
  const tickets = pbl21GetTickets();

  return {
    total: tickets.length,
    missingTicketNumber: tickets.filter(
      function (ticket) {
        return !String(ticket.ticketNumber || "").trim();
      }
    ).length,
    missingProduct: tickets.filter(
      function (ticket) {
        return !String(ticket.product || "").trim();
      }
    ).length,
    missingClient: tickets.filter(
      function (ticket) {
        return !String(ticket.client || "").trim();
      }
    ).length,
    missingTruck: tickets.filter(
      function (ticket) {
        return !String(ticket.truckRegistration || "").trim();
      }
    ).length,
    invalidNetWeight: tickets.filter(
      function (ticket) {
        return pbl18TicketKg(ticket) < 0;
      }
    ).length
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1082 — SEARCH RESULT SUMMARY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21BuildResultSummary(tickets) {
  const rows = tickets || [];

  const totalKg = rows.reduce(
    function (sum, ticket) {
      return sum + pbl18TicketKg(ticket);
    },
    0
  );

  return {
    records: rows.length,
    voyages: rows.length,
    totalNetKg: pbl18Round(totalKg, 2),
    totalNetTonnes: pbl18KgToTonnes(totalKg),
    averageNetKg: rows.length
      ? pbl18Round(totalKg / rows.length, 2)
      : 0
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1083 — SEARCH RESULT SUMMARY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21SearchResultSummary(parameters) {
  const tickets = pbl21ApplyFilters(parameters);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl21BuildResultSummary(tickets)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1084 — GET SEARCH FIELD COUNTS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21GetFieldCounts() {
  const tickets = pbl21GetTickets();

  function count(field) {
    const values = tickets.map(function (ticket) {
      return pbl21NormalizeText(ticket[field]);
    }).filter(Boolean);

    return new Set(values).size;
  }

  return {
    ticketNumbers: count("ticketNumber"),
    products: count("product"),
    clients: count("client"),
    trucks: count("truckRegistration"),
    drivers: count("driver")
  };
}

   1085 — FIELD COUNTS API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl21GetFieldCounts() {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl21GetFieldCounts()
  };
}

   1086 — SEARCH ENGINE HEALTH
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21HealthCheck() {
  const tickets = pbl21GetTickets();

  return {
    module: "PBL21_ADVANCED_SEARCH",
    version: PBL21_CONFIG.VERSION,
    ticketSourceAvailable: Array.isArray(tickets),
    ticketCount: tickets.length,
    activityStorageAvailable:
      typeof PBL13_STORAGE !== "undefined" &&
      !!PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY &&
      typeof pbl13ReadArray === "function",
    searchHistoryCount: pbl21GetSearchHistory().length,
    savedSearchCount: pbl21GetSavedSearches().length,
    checkedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1088 — CLEAR ALL SAVED SEARCHES
   ============================================================ */

function pbl21ClearSavedSearches(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const records = pbl21ReadActivity().filter(
    function (record) {
      return !record ||
        record.recordType !== PBL21_CONFIG.SAVED_SEARCH_TYPE;
    }
  );

  pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    records
  );

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "All saved searches have been cleared."
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1091 — SAFE STORAGE READER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22ReadRecords() {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13ReadArray !== "function"
  ) {
    return [];
  }

  const result = pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  );

  return Array.isArray(result) ? result : [];
}

/* ============================================================
   1092 — SAFE STORAGE WRITER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22WriteRecords(records) {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13WriteArray !== "function"
  ) {
    throw new Error("Interface storage unavailable.");
  }

  pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    records
  );

  return true;
}

/* ============================================================
   1094 — GET CURRENT ACCESS MODE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22GetAccessMode(token) {
  try {
    const status = typeof getAccessStatus === "function"
      ? getAccessStatus(token)
      : null;

    return {
      unlocked: !!(
        status &&
        (
          status.unlocked === true ||
          status.isUnlocked === true
        )
      ),
      mode: status && status.unlocked
        ? "EDIT"
        : "READ_ONLY"
    };
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      unlocked: false,
      mode: "READ_ONLY"
    };
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   1095 — CHECK MENU PERMISSION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22CanOpenMenu(menu, accessMode) {
  if (!menu || menu.visible === false) {
    return false;
  }

  if (menu.permission === "READ") {
    return true;
  }

  return !!(
    accessMode &&
    accessMode.unlocked === true
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1096 — GET PERMISSION-AWARE MENU
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22GetNavigationMenu(token) {
  const access = pbl22GetAccessMode(token);

  return pbl22GetMenuDefinitions()
    .filter(function (menu) {
      return pbl22CanOpenMenu(menu, access);
    })
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return a.order - b.order;
    })
    .map(function (menu) {
      return Object.assign({}, menu, {
        accessMode: access.mode
      });
    });
}

/* ============================================================
   1097 — NAVIGATION MENU API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl22GetNavigationMenu(token) {
  return {
    success: true,
    mode: pbl22GetAccessMode(token).mode,
    menu: pbl22GetNavigationMenu(token)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1098 — VALIDATE PAGE ID
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22ValidatePage(pageId) {
  const page = String(pageId || "").trim();

  const definitions = pbl22GetMenuDefinitions();

  const found = definitions.find(
    function (item) {
      return item.id === page;
    }
  );

  if (!found) {
    throw new Error("Unknown application page.");
  }

  return found;
}

/* ============================================================
   1099 — AUTHORIZE PAGE ACCESS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22AuthorizePage(pageId, token) {
  const page = pbl22ValidatePage(pageId);
  const access = pbl22GetAccessMode(token);

  if (!pbl22CanOpenMenu(page, access)) {
    throw new Error(
      "This page requires an unlocked session."
    );
  }

  return {
    allowed: true,
    page: page.id,
    mode: access.mode
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1100 — PAGE ROUTER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22GetPageData(pageId, token) {
  const authorization = pbl22AuthorizePage(
    pageId,
    token
  );

  const page = authorization.page;

  const routes = {
    dashboard: function () {
      return typeof pbl18GetLiveDashboard === "function"
        ? pbl18GetLiveDashboard()
        : getMainDashboardData();
    },

    weighing: function () {
      return typeof apiPbl16GetLiveWeightDisplay === "function"
        ? apiPbl16GetLiveWeightDisplay()
        : {
            connected: false,
            status: "NOT_IMPLEMENTED",
            weightKg: null
          };
    },

    tickets: function () {
      return typeof apiGetInitialData === "function"
        ? apiGetInitialData()
        : {};
    },

    objectives: function () {
      return typeof apiPbl17GetObjectiveDashboard === "function"
        ? apiPbl17GetObjectiveDashboard()
        : getMainDashboardData();
    },

    products: function () {
      return typeof apiGetProducts === "function"
        ? apiGetProducts()
        : [];
    },

    clients: function () {
      return typeof apiGetClients === "function"
        ? apiGetClients()
        : [];
    },

    trucks: function () {
      return typeof apiGetTrucks === "function"
        ? apiGetTrucks()
        : [];
    },

    drivers: function () {
      return typeof apiGetDrivers === "function"
        ? apiGetDrivers()
        : [];
    },

    reports: function () {
      return typeof apiPbl20GetReportCatalog === "function"
        ? apiPbl20GetReportCatalog()
        : [];
    },

    search: function () {
      return typeof apiPbl21InitializeSearchEngine === "function"
        ? apiPbl21InitializeSearchEngine()
        : {};
    },

    notifications: function () {
      return pbl22GetNotifications();
    },

    settings: function () {
      return typeof pbl12GetAllConfigurations === "function"
        ? pbl12GetAllConfigurations()
        : {};
    },

    maintenance: function () {
      return typeof pbl9GetDatabaseCheck === "function"
        ? pbl9GetDatabaseCheck()
        : {};
    },

    audit: function () {
      return typeof getAuditLogs === "function"
        ? getAuditLogs()
        : [];
    }
  };

  const handler = routes[page];

  if (!handler) {
    throw new Error("Page handler unavailable.");
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    page: page,
    accessMode: authorization.mode,
    data: handler(),
    loadedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1101 — PAGE DATA API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl22GetPageData(pageId, token) {
  return pbl22GetPageData(pageId, token);
}

/* ============================================================
   1102 — GET DEFAULT USER PREFERENCES
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22GetDefaultPreferences() {
  return {
    theme: PBL22_CONFIG.DEFAULT_THEME,
    language: PBL22_CONFIG.DEFAULT_LANGUAGE,
    compactMode: false,
    sidebarCollapsed: false,
    showClock: true,
    showNotifications: true,
    dashboardLayout: [
      "targetQuantity",
      "cumulTonnes",
      "numberOfVoyages",
      "remainingTonnes"
    ],
    defaultTicketSort: "date",
    defaultSortDirection: "DESC",
    defaultPageSize: 25,
    lastPage: PBL22_CONFIG.DEFAULT_PAGE
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1103 — GET USER PREFERENCES
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22GetPreferences() {
  const records = pbl22ReadRecords();

  const record = records.find(function (item) {
    return item &&
      item.recordType === PBL22_CONFIG.PREFERENCE_TYPE;
  });

  if (!record || !record.preferences) {
    return pbl22GetDefaultPreferences();
  }

  return Object.assign(
    {},
    pbl22GetDefaultPreferences(),
    record.preferences
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1104 — VALIDATE PREFERENCES
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22ValidatePreferences(input) {
  const defaults = pbl22GetDefaultPreferences();
  const value = input || {};

  const allowedThemes = [
    "dark",
    "light",
    "system"
  ];

  const allowedLanguages = [
    "fr",
    "en",
    "ar"
  ];

  const allowedPageSizes = [
    10,
    25,
    50,
    100,
    200
  ];

  const theme = allowedThemes.indexOf(value.theme) !== -1
    ? value.theme
    : defaults.theme;

  const language = allowedLanguages.indexOf(value.language) !== -1
    ? value.language
    : defaults.language;

  const pageSize = allowedPageSizes.indexOf(
    Number(value.defaultPageSize)
  ) !== -1
    ? Number(value.defaultPageSize)
    : defaults.defaultPageSize;

  return {
    theme: theme,
    language: language,
    compactMode: value.compactMode === true,
    sidebarCollapsed: value.sidebarCollapsed === true,
    showClock: value.showClock !== false,
    showNotifications: value.showNotifications !== false,
    dashboardLayout: defaults.dashboardLayout,
    defaultTicketSort: [
      "ticketNumber",
      "date",
      "product",
      "client",
      "netKg"
    ].indexOf(value.defaultTicketSort) !== -1
      ? value.defaultTicketSort
      : defaults.defaultTicketSort,
    defaultSortDirection:
      String(value.defaultSortDirection).toUpperCase() === "ASC"
        ? "ASC"
        : "DESC",
    defaultPageSize: pageSize,
    lastPage: defaults.lastPage
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1105 — SAVE USER PREFERENCES
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22SavePreferences(preferences, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const records = pbl22ReadRecords();

  const validated = pbl22ValidatePreferences(
    preferences
  );

  const existingIndex = records.findIndex(
    function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL22_CONFIG.PREFERENCE_TYPE;
    }
  );

  const record = {
    id: "PBL22-PREFERENCES",
    recordType: PBL22_CONFIG.PREFERENCE_TYPE,
    preferences: validated,
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  if (existingIndex >= 0) {
    records[existingIndex] = record;
  } else {
    records.push(record);
  }

  pbl22WriteRecords(records);

  if (typeof writeAuditLog === "function") {
    writeAuditLog(
      "INTERFACE_PREFERENCES_UPDATED",
      "Application interface preferences updated."
    );
  }

  return validated;
}

/* ============================================================
   1106 — SAVE PREFERENCES API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl22SavePreferences(preferences, token) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl22SavePreferences(
      preferences,
      token
    )
  };
}

function apiPbl22GetPreferences() {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl22GetPreferences()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1108 — SAVE INTERFACE STATE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22SaveInterfaceState(state, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const records = pbl22ReadRecords();

  const existingIndex = records.findIndex(
    function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL22_CONFIG.INTERFACE_STATE_TYPE;
    }
  );

  const record = {
    id: "PBL22-INTERFACE-STATE",
    recordType: PBL22_CONFIG.INTERFACE_STATE_TYPE,
    state: state || {},
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  if (existingIndex >= 0) {
    records[existingIndex] = record;
  } else {
    records.push(record);
  }

  pbl22WriteRecords(records);

  return record;
}

/* ============================================================
   1109 — GET INTERFACE STATE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22GetInterfaceState() {
  const record = pbl22ReadRecords().find(
    function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL22_CONFIG.INTERFACE_STATE_TYPE;
    }
  );

  return record && record.state
    ? record.state
    : {
        currentPage: PBL22_CONFIG.DEFAULT_PAGE,
        lastUpdated: null,
        recentPages: []
      };
}

/* ============================================================
   1110 — SAVE INTERFACE STATE API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl22SaveInterfaceState(state, token) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl22SaveInterfaceState(
      state,
      token
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1111 — RECORD RECENT PAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22RecordRecentPage(pageId, token) {
  const page = pbl22ValidatePage(pageId);

  const state = pbl22GetInterfaceState();

  const recent = Array.isArray(state.recentPages)
    ? state.recentPages
    : [];

  const updated = [
    {
      page: page.id,
      label: page.label,
      visitedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    }
  ].concat(
    recent.filter(function (item) {
      return item && item.page !== page.id;
    })
  ).slice(0, PBL22_CONFIG.MAX_RECENT_PAGES);

  state.currentPage = page.id;
  state.recentPages = updated;

  return pbl22SaveInterfaceState(
    state,
    token
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1112 — RECENT PAGES API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl22RecordRecentPage(pageId, token) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl22RecordRecentPage(
      pageId,
      token
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1113 — GET RECENT PAGES
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22GetRecentPages() {
  const state = pbl22GetInterfaceState();

  return Array.isArray(state.recentPages)
    ? state.recentPages
    : [];
}

/* ============================================================
   1114 — RECENT PAGES API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl22GetRecentPages() {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl22GetRecentPages()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1115 — CREATE NOTIFICATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22CreateNotification(
  title,
  message,
  category,
  priority,
  token
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const records = pbl22ReadRecords();

  const notification = {
    id: "PBL22-NOTIF-" + Utilities.getUuid(),
    recordType: PBL22_CONFIG.NOTIFICATION_TYPE,
    title: String(title || "Notification"),
    message: String(message || ""),
    category: String(category || "GENERAL"),
    priority: String(priority || "NORMAL"),
    read: false,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  records.push(notification);

  const notifications = records.filter(
    function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL22_CONFIG.NOTIFICATION_TYPE;
    }
  );

  const keepIds = notifications
    .slice(-PBL22_CONFIG.MAX_NOTIFICATIONS)
    .map(function (item) {
      return item.id;
    });

  const cleaned = records.filter(function (item) {
    return !item ||
      item.recordType !== PBL22_CONFIG.NOTIFICATION_TYPE ||
      keepIds.indexOf(item.id) !== -1;
  });

  pbl22WriteRecords(cleaned);

  return notification;
}

/* ============================================================
   1116 — CREATE NOTIFICATION API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl22CreateNotification(
  title,
  message,
  category,
  priority,
  token
) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl22CreateNotification(
      title,
      message,
      category,
      priority,
      token
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1117 — GET NOTIFICATIONS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22GetNotifications() {
  return pbl22ReadRecords()
    .filter(function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL22_CONFIG.NOTIFICATION_TYPE;
    })
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(b.createdAt || "").localeCompare(
        String(a.createdAt || "")
      );
    });
}

/* ============================================================
   1118 — GET NOTIFICATIONS API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl22GetNotifications(token) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl22GetNotifications(),
    unreadCount: pbl22GetUnreadCount()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1119 — UNREAD NOTIFICATION COUNT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22GetUnreadCount() {
  return pbl22GetNotifications().filter(
    function (item) {
      return item.read !== true;
    }
  ).length;
}

/* ============================================================
   1120 — UNREAD COUNT API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl22GetUnreadCount() {
  return {
    success: true,
    count: pbl22GetUnreadCount()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1121 — MARK NOTIFICATION READ
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22MarkNotificationRead(notificationId, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const records = pbl22ReadRecords();

  const record = records.find(function (item) {
    return item &&
      item.recordType === PBL22_CONFIG.NOTIFICATION_TYPE &&
      String(item.id) === String(notificationId);
  });

  if (!record) {
    throw new Error("Notification not found.");
  }

  record.read = true;
  record.readAt = new Date().toISOString();

  pbl22WriteRecords(records);

  return record;
}

/* ============================================================
   1122 — MARK READ API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl22MarkNotificationRead(
  notificationId,
  token
) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl22MarkNotificationRead(
      notificationId,
      token
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1123 — MARK ALL NOTIFICATIONS READ
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22MarkAllNotificationsRead(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const records = pbl22ReadRecords();
  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  records.forEach(function (item) {
    if (
      item &&
      item.recordType === PBL22_CONFIG.NOTIFICATION_TYPE
    ) {
      item.read = true;
      item.readAt = now;
    }
  });

  pbl22WriteRecords(records);

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "All notifications marked as read."
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1124 — MARK ALL READ API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl22MarkAllNotificationsRead(token) {
  return pbl22MarkAllNotificationsRead(token);
}

/* ============================================================
   1125 — DELETE NOTIFICATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22DeleteNotification(notificationId, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const id = String(notificationId || "");
  const records = pbl22ReadRecords();

  const exists = records.some(function (item) {
    return item &&
      item.recordType === PBL22_CONFIG.NOTIFICATION_TYPE &&
      String(item.id) === id;
  });

  if (!exists) {
    throw new Error("Notification not found.");
  }

  const cleaned = records.filter(function (item) {
    return !item ||
      item.recordType !== PBL22_CONFIG.NOTIFICATION_TYPE ||
      String(item.id) !== id;
  });

  pbl22WriteRecords(cleaned);

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Notification deleted."
  };
}

   1126 — DELETE NOTIFICATION API

function apiPbl22DeleteNotification(

  );

        item.read !== true;

/* ============================================================
   1129 — NOTIFICATION CENTER SUMMARY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22GetNotificationSummary() {
  const notifications = pbl22GetNotifications();

  return {
    total: notifications.length,
    unread: notifications.filter(
      function (item) {
        return item.read !== true;
      }
    ).length,
    read: notifications.filter(
      function (item) {
        return item.read === true;
      }
    ).length,
    urgent: notifications.filter(
      function (item) {
        return String(item.priority).toUpperCase() === "URGENT";
      }
    ).length,
    latest: notifications.length
      ? notifications[0]
      : null
  };
}

function apiPbl22GetNotificationSummary() {
  return {
    success: true,

  };
}

    recentPages: Array.isArray(input.recentPages)
      ? input.recentPages.slice(

  };

   1144 — GET MENU BY SECTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl22GetMenuBySection(token) {
  const menu = pbl22GetNavigationMenu(token);
  const sections = {};

  menu.forEach(function (item) {
    if (!sections[item.section]) {
      sections[item.section] = [];
    }

    sections[item.section].push(item);
  });

  return sections;
}

      );

/***************************************************************
 * END OF PART 22
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * This is the server-side interface backend.
 * The actual visual screens, buttons, navigation panels,
 * responsive layout and interactive controls belong in
 * Index.html.
 *
 * PART 23:
 * WEIGHING WORKFLOW CONTROL CENTER,
 * FIRST WEIGHING,
 * SECOND WEIGHING,
 * VEHICLE QUEUE,
 * TRANSACTION VALIDATION,
 * WEIGHING SESSION MONITORING.
 ***************************************************************/

/***************************************************************
 * PONT BASCULE LIVE
 * WEIGHBRIDGE MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
 *
 * PART 23 — WEIGHING WORKFLOW CONTROL CENTER
 * Functions 1151–1210
 *
 * WORKFLOW:
 * 1. Register vehicle arrival
 * 2. Select product and client
 * 3. Capture first weighing
 * 4. Vehicle leaves the scale
 * 5. Vehicle returns
 * 6. Capture second weighing
 * 7. Calculate gross, tare and net
 * 8. Validate transaction
 * 9. Generate weighing ticket
 * 10. Monitor active weighing sessions
 ***************************************************************/

const PBL23_CONFIG = {
  VERSION: "23.0.0",
  MAX_ACTIVE_SESSIONS: 100,
  MAX_QUEUE_SIZE: 200,
  SESSION_TIMEOUT_MINUTES: 240,
  MAX_WEIGHT_KG: 1000000,
  MIN_NET_WEIGHT_KG: 0,
  DEFAULT_TICKET_STATUS: "VALID",
  SESSION_TYPE: "PBL23_WEIGHING_WORKFLOW",
  QUEUE_TYPE: "PBL23_VEHICLE_QUEUE"
};

/* ============================================================
   1151 — SAFE WORKFLOW STORAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23ReadWorkflowRecords() {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13ReadArray !== "function"
  ) {
    return [];
  }

  const records = pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  );

  return Array.isArray(records) ? records : [];
}

/* ============================================================
   1152 — SAVE WORKFLOW STORAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23WriteWorkflowRecords(records) {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13WriteArray !== "function"
  ) {
    throw new Error("Workflow storage unavailable.");
  }

  pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    records
  );

  return true;
}

/* ============================================================
   1153 — GENERATE WORKFLOW ID
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23CreateId(prefix) {
  return String(prefix || "PBL23") +
    "-" +
    Utilities.getUuid();
}

/* ============================================================
   1154 — VALIDATE VEHICLE REGISTRATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23ValidateRegistration(value) {
  const registration = String(value || "")
    .trim()
    .toUpperCase();

  if (!registration) {
    throw new Error("Vehicle registration is required.");
  }

  if (registration.length > 30) {
    throw new Error("Vehicle registration is too long.");
  }

  return registration;
}

/* ============================================================
   1155 — VALIDATE WEIGHT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23ValidateWeight(value) {
  const weight = Number(value);

  if (
    !isFinite(weight) ||
    weight < 0 ||
    weight > PBL23_CONFIG.MAX_WEIGHT_KG
  ) {
    throw new Error("Invalid weight in KG.");
  }

  return Math.round(weight * 100) / 100;
}

/* ============================================================
   1156 — GET ACTIVE WORKFLOW SESSIONS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23GetActiveSessions() {
  return pbl23ReadWorkflowRecords()
    .filter(function (record) {
      return record &&
        record.recordType === PBL23_CONFIG.SESSION_TYPE &&
        record.status !== "COMPLETED" &&
        record.status !== "CANCELLED";
    })
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(a.createdAt || "").localeCompare(
        String(b.createdAt || "")
      );
    });
}

/* ============================================================
   1157 — GET WORKFLOW SESSION BY ID
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23GetSession(sessionId) {
  const id = String(sessionId || "");

  return pbl23GetActiveSessions().find(
    function (session) {
      return String(session.id) === id;
    }
  ) || null;
}

/* ============================================================
   1158 — SAVE OR UPDATE SESSION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23SaveSession(session) {
  const records = pbl23ReadWorkflowRecords();

  const index = records.findIndex(function (record) {
    return record &&
      record.recordType === PBL23_CONFIG.SESSION_TYPE &&
      String(record.id) === String(session.id);
  });

  session.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();

  if (index >= 0) {
    records[index] = session;
  } else {
    records.push(session);
  }

  pbl23WriteWorkflowRecords(records);

  return session;
}

/* ============================================================
   1159 — CHECK ACTIVE SESSION LIMIT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23CheckSessionLimit() {
  const active = pbl23GetActiveSessions();

  if (
    active.length >= PBL23_CONFIG.MAX_ACTIVE_SESSIONS
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Maximum active weighing sessions reached."
    );
  }

  return true;
}

/* ============================================================
   1160 — REGISTER VEHICLE ARRIVAL
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23RegisterArrival(data, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const input = data || {};

  pbl23CheckSessionLimit();

  const registration = pbl23ValidateRegistration(
    input.truckRegistration
  );

  const product = String(input.product || "").trim();
  const client = String(input.client || "").trim();

  if (!product) {
    throw new Error("Select a product.");
  }

  if (!client) {
    throw new Error("Select a client.");
  }

  const activeDuplicate = pbl23GetActiveSessions()
    .find(function (session) {
      return (
        session.truckRegistration === registration &&
        session.status !== "COMPLETED" &&
        session.status !== "CANCELLED"
      );
    });

  if (activeDuplicate) {
    throw new Error(
      "This vehicle already has an active weighing session."
    );
  }

  const session = {
    id: pbl23CreateId("PBL23-SESSION"),
    recordType: PBL23_CONFIG.SESSION_TYPE,
    truckRegistration: registration,
    product: product,
    client: client,
    driver: String(input.driver || "").trim(),
    firstWeightKg: null,
    secondWeightKg: null,
    grossKg: null,
    tareKg: null,
    netKg: null,
    firstWeighingAt: null,
    secondWeighingAt: null,
    status: "ARRIVED",
    stage: "WAITING_FIRST_WEIGHING",
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    createdBy: "PIN_AUTHORIZED_SESSION"
  };

  pbl23SaveSession(session);

  if (typeof writeAuditLog === "function") {
    writeAuditLog(
      "PBL23_VEHICLE_ARRIVAL",
      "Vehicle arrival registered: " + registration
    );
  }

  return session;
}

/* ============================================================
   1161 — REGISTER ARRIVAL API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl23RegisterArrival(data, token) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl23RegisterArrival(data, token)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1162 — VALIDATE FIRST WEIGHING STAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23ValidateFirstStage(session) {
  if (!session) {
    throw new Error("Weighing session not found.");
  }

  if (
    session.status !== "ARRIVED" &&
    session.status !== "WAITING_FIRST_WEIGHING"
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "First weighing is not available at this stage."
    );
  }

  if (session.firstWeightKg !== null) {
    throw new Error(
      "First weighing has already been registered."
    );
  }

  return true;
}

/* ============================================================
   1163 — REGISTER FIRST WEIGHING
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23RegisterFirstWeighing(
  sessionId,
  weightKg,
  token
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const session = pbl23GetSession(sessionId);

  pbl23ValidateFirstStage(session);

  const weight = pbl23ValidateWeight(weightKg);

  session.firstWeightKg = weight;
  session.firstWeighingAt = new Date().toISOString();
  session.status = "FIRST_WEIGHING_COMPLETED";
  session.stage = "WAITING_SECOND_WEIGHING";

  pbl23SaveSession(session);

  if (typeof writeAuditLog === "function") {
    writeAuditLog(
      "PBL23_FIRST_WEIGHING",
      session.truckRegistration +
        " — " +
        weight +
        " KG"
    );
  }

  return session;
}

/* ============================================================
   1164 — FIRST WEIGHING API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl23RegisterFirstWeighing(
  sessionId,
  weightKg,
  token
) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl23RegisterFirstWeighing(
      sessionId,
      weightKg,
      token
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1165 — VALIDATE SECOND WEIGHING STAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23ValidateSecondStage(session) {
  if (!session) {
    throw new Error("Weighing session not found.");
  }

  if (
    session.status !== "FIRST_WEIGHING_COMPLETED" &&
    session.status !== "WAITING_SECOND_WEIGHING"
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Second weighing is not available."
    );
  }

  if (session.firstWeightKg === null) {
    throw new Error(
      "First weighing is missing."
    );
  }

  if (session.secondWeightKg !== null) {
    throw new Error(
      "Second weighing already exists."
    );
  }

  return true;
}

/* ============================================================
   1166 — CALCULATE GROSS / TARE / NET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23CalculateWeights(firstWeight, secondWeight) {
  const first = pbl23ValidateWeight(firstWeight);
  const second = pbl23ValidateWeight(secondWeight);

  const gross = Math.max(first, second);
  const tare = Math.min(first, second);
  const net = Math.round((gross - tare) * 100) / 100;

  return {
    firstWeightKg: first,
    secondWeightKg: second,
    grossKg: gross,
    tareKg: tare,
    netKg: net,
    netTonnes: Math.round((net / 1000) * 1000) / 1000
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1167 — REGISTER SECOND WEIGHING
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23RegisterSecondWeighing(
  sessionId,
  weightKg,
  token
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const session = pbl23GetSession(sessionId);

  pbl23ValidateSecondStage(session);

  const weights = pbl23CalculateWeights(
    session.firstWeightKg,
    weightKg
  );

  session.secondWeightKg = weights.secondWeightKg;
  session.grossKg = weights.grossKg;
  session.tareKg = weights.tareKg;
  session.netKg = weights.netKg;
  session.netTonnes = weights.netTonnes;
  session.secondWeighingAt = new Date().toISOString();
  session.status = "SECOND_WEIGHING_COMPLETED";
  session.stage = "WAITING_VALIDATION";

  pbl23SaveSession(session);

  if (typeof writeAuditLog === "function") {
    writeAuditLog(
      "PBL23_SECOND_WEIGHING",
      session.truckRegistration +
        " — NET " +
        weights.netKg +
        " KG"
    );
  }

  return session;
}

/* ============================================================
   1168 — SECOND WEIGHING API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl23RegisterSecondWeighing(
  sessionId,
  weightKg,
  token
) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl23RegisterSecondWeighing(
      sessionId,
      weightKg,
      token
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1169 — VALIDATE COMPLETED WEIGHT DATA
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23ValidateCompletedWeights(session) {
  if (!session) {
    throw new Error("Session not found.");
  }

  const fields = [
    session.firstWeightKg,
    session.secondWeightKg,
    session.grossKg,
    session.tareKg,
    session.netKg
  ];

  if (fields.some(function (value) {
    return value === null ||
      value === undefined ||
      !isFinite(Number(value));
  })) {
    throw new Error("Incomplete weighing information.");
  }

  if (
    Number(session.grossKg) <
    Number(session.tareKg)
  ) {
    throw new Error("Gross weight cannot be below tare.");
  }

  const calculated =
    Number(session.grossKg) -
    Number(session.tareKg);

  if (Math.abs(calculated - Number(session.netKg)) > 0.01) {
    throw new Error("Net weight calculation mismatch.");
  }

  if (Number(session.netKg) < 0) {
    throw new Error("Negative net weight is not allowed.");
  }

  return true;
}

/* ============================================================
   1170 — CHECK DUPLICATE FINAL TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23CheckDuplicateTicket(session) {
  const tickets = typeof getAllTickets === "function"
    ? getAllTickets()
    : [];

  if (!Array.isArray(tickets)) return false;

  return tickets.some(function (ticket) {
    return (
      String(ticket.truckRegistration || "").toUpperCase() ===
        String(session.truckRegistration || "").toUpperCase() &&
      String(ticket.date || "") ===
        String(
          Utilities.formatDate(
            new Date(session.firstWeighingAt),
            PBL23_CONFIG.TIME_ZONE,
            "dd/MM/yyyy"
          )
        ) &&
      Number(ticket.netKg) === Number(session.netKg) &&
      String(ticket.product || "") ===
        String(session.product || "")
    );
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1171 — BUILD TICKET FROM WORKFLOW SESSION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23BuildTicketData(session) {
  pbl23ValidateCompletedWeights(session);

  const firstDate = new Date(session.firstWeighingAt);

  return {
    date: Utilities.formatDate(
      firstDate,
      PBL23_CONFIG.TIME_ZONE,
      "dd/MM/yyyy"
    ),
    time: Utilities.formatDate(
      firstDate,
      PBL23_CONFIG.TIME_ZONE,
      "HH:mm:ss"
    ),
    product: session.product,
    client: session.client,
    truckRegistration: session.truckRegistration,
    driver: session.driver || "",
    grossKg: session.grossKg,
    tareKg: session.tareKg,
    netKg: session.netKg,
    netTonnes: session.netTonnes,
    status: PBL23_CONFIG.DEFAULT_TICKET_STATUS,
    sourceSessionId: session.id
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1172 — FINALIZE WORKFLOW SESSION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23FinalizeSession(sessionId, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const session = pbl23GetSession(sessionId);

  if (!session) {
    throw new Error("Active session not found.");
  }

  if (session.status !== "SECOND_WEIGHING_COMPLETED") {
    throw new Error(
      "Session must complete both weighings before validation."
    );
  }

  pbl23ValidateCompletedWeights(session);

  if (pbl23CheckDuplicateTicket(session)) {
    throw new Error(
      "A similar ticket already exists. Check the ticket register."
    );
  }

  const ticketData = pbl23BuildTicketData(session);

  let createdTicket;

  if (typeof createTicket === "function") {
    createdTicket = createTicket(ticketData, token);
  } else if (typeof apiCreateTicket === "function") {
    createdTicket = apiCreateTicket(ticketData, token);
  } else {
    throw new Error(
      "Ticket creation function is unavailable."
    );
  }

  session.status = "COMPLETED";
  session.stage = "TICKET_CREATED";
  session.completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  session.ticketNumber =
    createdTicket &&
    createdTicket.ticketNumber
      ? createdTicket.ticketNumber
      : null;

  pbl23SaveSession(session);

  if (typeof writeAuditLog === "function") {
    writeAuditLog(
      "PBL23_SESSION_FINALIZED",
      session.truckRegistration +
        " — " +
        session.netKg +
        " KG"
    );
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    session: session,
    ticket: createdTicket
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1173 — FINALIZE SESSION API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl23FinalizeSession(sessionId, token) {
  return pbl23FinalizeSession(
    sessionId,
    token
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1174 — CANCEL WORKFLOW SESSION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23CancelSession(
  sessionId,
  reason,
  token
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const session = pbl23GetSession(sessionId);

  if (!session) {
    throw new Error("Session not found.");
  }

  const explanation = String(reason || "").trim();

  if (explanation.length < 3) {
    throw new Error(
      "A cancellation reason is required."
    );
  }

  session.status = "CANCELLED";
  session.stage = "CANCELLED";
  session.cancelReason = explanation;
  session.cancelledAt = new Date().toISOString();

  pbl23SaveSession(session);

  if (typeof writeAuditLog === "function") {
    writeAuditLog(
      "PBL23_SESSION_CANCELLED",
      session.truckRegistration +
        " — " +
        explanation
    );
  }

  return session;
}

/* ============================================================
   1175 — CANCEL SESSION API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl23CancelSession(
  sessionId,
  reason,
  token
) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl23CancelSession(
      sessionId,
      reason,
      token
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1176 — GET SESSION DETAILS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23GetSessionDetails(sessionId) {
  const session = pbl23GetSession(sessionId);

  if (!session) {
    throw new Error("Session not found.");
  }

  return {
    id: session.id,
    truckRegistration: session.truckRegistration,
    product: session.product,
    client: session.client,
    driver: session.driver,
    status: session.status,
    stage: session.stage,
    firstWeightKg: session.firstWeightKg,
    secondWeightKg: session.secondWeightKg,
    grossKg: session.grossKg,
    tareKg: session.tareKg,
    netKg: session.netKg,
    netTonnes: session.netTonnes,
    firstWeighingAt: session.firstWeighingAt,
    secondWeighingAt: session.secondWeighingAt,
    createdAt: session.createdAt,
    updatedAt: session.updatedAt
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1180 — GET WEIGHING WORKFLOW SUMMARY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23GetWorkflowSummary() {
  const sessions = pbl23GetActiveSessions();

  return {
    activeSessions: sessions.length,
    arrived: sessions.filter(function (item) {
      return item.status === "ARRIVED";
    }).length,
    waitingSecondWeighing: sessions.filter(function (item) {
      return item.status === "FIRST_WEIGHING_COMPLETED";
    }).length,
    awaitingValidation: sessions.filter(function (item) {
      return item.status === "SECOND_WEIGHING_COMPLETED";
    }).length,
    completed: pbl23ReadWorkflowRecords().filter(function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL23_CONFIG.SESSION_TYPE &&
        item.status === "COMPLETED";
    }).length,
    cancelled: pbl23ReadWorkflowRecords().filter(function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL23_CONFIG.SESSION_TYPE &&
        item.status === "CANCELLED";
    }).length
  };
}

   1181 — WORKFLOW SUMMARY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl23GetWorkflowSummary() {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl23GetWorkflowSummary()
  };
}

        sessionId: session.id,
        truckRegistration: session.truckRegistration,
        client: session.client,
        product: session.product,
        driver: session.driver,
        stage: session.stage,
        status: session.status,
        arrivedAt: session.createdAt,
        firstWeightKg: session.firstWeightKg,
        netKg: session.netKg
      };
    }
  );
