/* ============================================================
   1191 — SESSION AGE IN MINUTES
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23GetSessionAgeMinutes(session) {
  if (!session || !session.createdAt) return 0;

  const created = new Date(session.createdAt).getTime();

  if (!isFinite(created)) return 0;

  return Math.max(
    0,
    Math.floor((Date.now() - created) / 60000)
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1192 — FIND EXPIRED SESSIONS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23GetExpiredSessions() {
  return pbl23GetActiveSessions().filter(
    function (session) {
      return pbl23GetSessionAgeMinutes(session) >
        PBL23_CONFIG.SESSION_TIMEOUT_MINUTES;
    }
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1193 — EXPIRED SESSIONS API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl23GetExpiredSessions(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl23GetExpiredSessions()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1194 — VALIDATE SESSION BEFORE FIRST WEIGHING
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23PreWeighingValidation(sessionId) {
  const session = pbl23GetSession(sessionId);

  if (!session) {
    throw new Error("Session not found.");
  }

  const errors = [];

  if (!session.truckRegistration) {
    errors.push("Missing vehicle registration.");
  }

  if (!session.product) {
    errors.push("Missing product.");
  }

  if (!session.client) {
    errors.push("Missing client.");
  }

  return {
    valid: errors.length === 0,
    errors: errors,
    sessionId: session.id
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1195 — PRE-WEIGHING VALIDATION API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl23PreWeighingValidation(sessionId) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl23PreWeighingValidation(sessionId)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1196 — VALIDATE SESSION BEFORE FINALIZATION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23FinalValidation(sessionId) {
  const session = pbl23GetSession(sessionId);

  if (!session) {
    throw new Error("Session not found.");
  }

  const errors = [];

  try {
    pbl23ValidateCompletedWeights(session);
  } catch (error) {
    errors.push(error.message);
  }

  if (pbl23CheckDuplicateTicket(session)) {
    errors.push("Possible duplicate ticket detected.");
  }

  return {
    valid: errors.length === 0,
    errors: errors,
    weights: {
      grossKg: session.grossKg,
      tareKg: session.tareKg,
      netKg: session.netKg,
      netTonnes: session.netTonnes
    }
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1197 — FINAL VALIDATION API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl23FinalValidation(sessionId) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl23FinalValidation(sessionId)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1198 — SESSION STATUS LABEL
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23GetStatusLabel(status) {
  const labels = {
    ARRIVED: "ARRIVÉ",
    FIRST_WEIGHING_COMPLETED: "PREMIÈRE PESÉE TERMINÉE",
    WAITING_SECOND_WEIGHING: "EN ATTENTE DE DEUXIÈME PESÉE",
    SECOND_WEIGHING_COMPLETED: "DEUXIÈME PESÉE TERMINÉE",
    COMPLETED: "TERMINÉ",
    CANCELLED: "ANNULÉ"
  };

  return labels[status] || "STATUT INCONNU";
}

/* ============================================================
   1199 — ENRICH SESSION FOR DISPLAY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23PrepareSessionDisplay(session) {
  return Object.assign({}, session, {
    statusLabel: pbl23GetStatusLabel(session.status),
    ageMinutes: pbl23GetSessionAgeMinutes(session),
    firstWeightTonnes:
      session.firstWeightKg === null
        ? null
        : session.firstWeightKg / 1000,
    secondWeightTonnes:
      session.secondWeightKg === null
        ? null
        : session.secondWeightKg / 1000,
    netTonnes:
      session.netKg === null
        ? null
        : session.netKg / 1000
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1200 — SESSION DISPLAY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl23GetSessionDisplay(sessionId) {
  const session = pbl23GetSession(sessionId);

  if (!session) {
    throw new Error("Session not found.");
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl23PrepareSessionDisplay(session)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1201 — WORKFLOW OPERATIONAL STATISTICS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23GetOperationalStatistics() {
  const sessions = pbl23GetActiveSessions();

  const ages = sessions.map(
    pbl23GetSessionAgeMinutes
  );

  return {
    activeVehicles: sessions.length,
    averageWaitingMinutes: ages.length
      ? Math.round(
          ages.reduce(function (sum, value) {
            return sum + value;
          }, 0) / ages.length
        )
      : 0,
    oldestWaitingMinutes: ages.length
      ? Math.max.apply(null, ages)
      : 0,
    vehiclesWaitingFirst: pbl23GetWaitingFirstWeighing().length,
    vehiclesWaitingSecond: pbl23GetWaitingSecondWeighing().length,
    vehiclesAwaitingValidation: pbl23GetAwaitingValidation().length
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1202 — OPERATIONAL STATISTICS API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl23GetOperationalStatistics() {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl23GetOperationalStatistics()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1203 — SEARCH ACTIVE WORKFLOW
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23SearchActiveWorkflow(term) {
  const query = String(term || "").trim().toLowerCase();

  if (!query) return pbl23GetActiveSessions();

  return pbl23GetActiveSessions().filter(
    function (session) {
      return [
        session.truckRegistration,
        session.client,
        session.product,
        session.driver,
        session.id,
        session.status
      ].some(function (value) {
        return String(value || "")
          .toLowerCase()
          .indexOf(query) !== -1;
      });
    }
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   1204 — SEARCH WORKFLOW API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl23SearchActiveWorkflow(term) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl23SearchActiveWorkflow(term)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1205 — GET COMPLETED WORKFLOW HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl23GetCompletedHistory(limit) {
  const maximum = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(500, Number(limit) || 100)
  );

  return pbl23ReadWorkflowRecords()
    .filter(function (record) {
      return record &&
        record.recordType === PBL23_CONFIG.SESSION_TYPE &&
        (
          record.status === "COMPLETED" ||
          record.status === "CANCELLED"
        );
    })
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(b.updatedAt || "").localeCompare(
        String(a.updatedAt || "")
      );
    })
    .slice(0, maximum);
}

/* ============================================================
   1206 — COMPLETED HISTORY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl23GetCompletedHistory(limit, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl23GetCompletedHistory(limit)
  };
}

    storageAvailable:
      typeof PBL13_STORAGE !== "undefined" &&
      !!PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY &&
      typeof pbl13ReadArray === "function",
    activeSessions: pbl23GetActiveSessionCount(),
    expiredSessions: pbl23GetExpiredSessions().length,
    sessionLimit: PBL23_CONFIG.MAX_ACTIVE_SESSIONS,
    hardwareScaleIntegration:
      "REQUIRES VERIFIED HARDWARE ADAPTER",
    checkedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

function apiPbl23HealthCheck() {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl23HealthCheck()
  };
}

function apiPbl23InitializeWorkflow() {

 *    finalized tickets.

 *    against the existing Part 2 / Part 5 implementation.
 *
 * 3. The weighing workflow does not itself connect to a
 *    physical indicator. A verified hardware adapter is
 *    required for real scale readings.
 *
 * 4. Manual entry remains disabled by default.
 *
 * PART 24:
 * TICKET MANAGEMENT CONTROL CENTER,
 * TICKET NUMBERING,
 * TICKET VALIDATION,

 * TICKET REPRINTING,

 ***************************************************************/

/***************************************************************
 * PONT BASCULE LIVE
 * WEIGHBRIDGE MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
 *
 * PART 24 — TICKET MANAGEMENT CONTROL CENTER
 * Functions 1211–1270
 *
 * MODULES:
 * - Ticket numbering
 * - Ticket creation validation
 * - Ticket search
 * - Ticket correction
 * - Ticket cancellation
 * - Ticket restoration
 * - Ticket reprinting
 * - Ticket history
 * - Ticket audit
 * - Ticket integrity
 ***************************************************************/

const PBL24_CONFIG = {
  VERSION: "24.0.0",
  PREFIX: "PBL",
  NUMBER_LENGTH: 6,
  MAX_TICKET_HISTORY: 500,
  HISTORY_TYPE: "PBL24_TICKET_HISTORY",
  REPRINT_TYPE: "PBL24_REPRINT_HISTORY",
  CORRECTION_TYPE: "PBL24_CORRECTION_HISTORY",
  DEFAULT_STATUS: "VALID",
  MAX_CORRECTIONS: 20
};

/* ============================================================
   1211 — READ TICKET MANAGEMENT STORAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24ReadRecords() {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13ReadArray !== "function"
  ) {
    return [];
  }

  const records = pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  );

  return Array.isArray(records) ? records : [];
}

/* ============================================================
   1212 — WRITE TICKET MANAGEMENT STORAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24WriteRecords(records) {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13WriteArray !== "function"
  ) {
    throw new Error("Ticket history storage unavailable.");
  }

  pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    records
  );

  return true;
}

/* ============================================================
   1213 — GET ALL TICKETS SAFELY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24GetTickets() {
  if (typeof getAllTickets !== "function") {
    return [];
  }

  const tickets = getAllTickets();

  return Array.isArray(tickets) ? tickets : [];
}

/* ============================================================
   1214 — FIND TICKET BY ID
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24FindTicketById(ticketId) {
  const id = String(ticketId || "");

  return pbl24GetTickets().find(
    function (ticket) {
      return String(ticket.id || "") === id;
    }
  ) || null;
}

/* ============================================================
   1215 — FIND TICKET BY NUMBER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24FindTicketByNumber(ticketNumber) {
  const number = String(ticketNumber || "")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();

  if (!number) return null;

  return pbl24GetTickets().find(
    function (ticket) {
      return String(ticket.ticketNumber || "")
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase() === number;
    }
  ) || null;
}

/* ============================================================
   1216 — VALIDATE TICKET NUMBER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24ValidateTicketNumber(ticketNumber) {
  const number = String(ticketNumber || "").trim();

  if (!number) {
    throw new Error("Ticket number is required.");
  }

  if (number.length > 40) {
    throw new Error("Ticket number is too long.");
  }

  return number;
}

/* ============================================================
   1217 — CHECK TICKET NUMBER UNIQUENESS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24IsTicketNumberUnique(ticketNumber, excludedId) {
  const number = pbl24ValidateTicketNumber(ticketNumber);

  return !pbl24GetTickets().some(function (ticket) {
    return (
      String(ticket.ticketNumber || "").toLowerCase() ===
        number.toLowerCase() &&
      String(ticket.id || "") !== String(excludedId || "")
    );
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1218 — GENERATE TICKET NUMBER
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24GenerateTicketNumber() {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();

  lock.waitLock(10000);

  try {
    const tickets = pbl24GetTickets();

    let highest = 0;

    tickets.forEach(function (ticket) {
      const match = String(ticket.ticketNumber || "")
        .match(/(\d+)$/);

      if (match) {
        highest = Math.max(
          highest,
          Number(match[1])
        );
      }
    });

    const next = highest + 1;

    return PBL24_CONFIG.PREFIX +
      "-" +
      String(next).padStart(
        PBL24_CONFIG.NUMBER_LENGTH,
        "0"
      );
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   1219 — VALIDATE TICKET WEIGHTS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24ValidateWeights(ticket) {
  const gross = Number(ticket.grossKg);
  const tare = Number(ticket.tareKg);
  const net = Number(ticket.netKg);

  if (
    !isFinite(gross) ||
    !isFinite(tare) ||
    !isFinite(net)
  ) {
    throw new Error("Ticket weights must be numeric.");
  }

  if (gross < 0 || tare < 0 || net < 0) {
    throw new Error("Negative weights are not allowed.");
  }

  if (gross > 1000000 || tare > 1000000) {
    throw new Error("Weight exceeds maximum capacity.");
  }

  if (gross < tare) {
    throw new Error("Gross weight cannot be below tare.");
  }

  if (Math.abs(gross - tare - net) > 0.01) {
    throw new Error("Net weight does not match gross minus tare.");
  }

  return {
    grossKg: Math.round(gross * 100) / 100,
    tareKg: Math.round(tare * 100) / 100,
    netKg: Math.round(net * 100) / 100,
    netTonnes: Math.round((net / 1000) * 1000) / 1000
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1220 — VALIDATE TICKET DATA
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24ValidateTicketData(data) {
  const ticket = data || {};

  const errors = [];

  if (!String(ticket.product || "").trim()) {
    errors.push("Product is required.");
  }

  if (!String(ticket.client || "").trim()) {
    errors.push("Client is required.");
  }

  if (!String(ticket.truckRegistration || "").trim()) {
    errors.push("Vehicle registration is required.");
  }

  if (!ticket.date) {
    errors.push("Ticket date is required.");
  }

  let weights = null;

  try {
    weights = pbl24ValidateWeights(ticket);
  } catch (error) {
    errors.push(error.message);
  }

  return {
    valid: errors.length === 0,
    errors: errors,
    weights: weights
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1221 — TICKET CREATION PREVIEW
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24BuildCreationPreview(data) {
  const validation = pbl24ValidateTicketData(data);

  if (!validation.valid) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      errors: validation.errors
    };
  }

  return {
    valid: true,
    ticketNumber: pbl24GenerateTicketNumber(),
    product: data.product,
    client: data.client,
    truckRegistration: data.truckRegistration,
    driver: data.driver || "",
    date: data.date,
    time: data.time || "",
    grossKg: validation.weights.grossKg,
    tareKg: validation.weights.tareKg,
    netKg: validation.weights.netKg,
    netTonnes: validation.weights.netTonnes
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1222 — TICKET CREATION PREVIEW API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl24BuildCreationPreview(data) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl24BuildCreationPreview(data)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1223 — CREATE TICKET THROUGH EXISTING BACKEND
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24CreateTicket(data, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const validation = pbl24ValidateTicketData(data);

  if (!validation.valid) {
    throw new Error(validation.errors.join(" "));
  }

  if (typeof createTicket !== "function") {
    throw new Error(
      "Existing ticket creation function is unavailable."
    );
  }

  const payload = Object.assign({}, data, {
    grossKg: validation.weights.grossKg,
    tareKg: validation.weights.tareKg,
    netKg: validation.weights.netKg,
    netTonnes: validation.weights.netTonnes,
    status: PBL24_CONFIG.DEFAULT_STATUS
  });

  const result = createTicket(payload, token);

  pbl24RecordTicketHistory(
    "TICKET_CREATED",
    result && result.id ? result.id : "",
    result && result.ticketNumber
      ? result.ticketNumber
      : "",
    "Ticket created.",
    token
  );

  return result;
}

/* ============================================================
   1224 — CREATE TICKET API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl24CreateTicket(data, token) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl24CreateTicket(data, token)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1225 — RECORD TICKET HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24RecordTicketHistory(
  action,
  ticketId,
  ticketNumber,
  details,
  token
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const records = pbl24ReadRecords();

  const history = {
    id: "PBL24-HISTORY-" + Utilities.getUuid(),
    recordType: PBL24_CONFIG.HISTORY_TYPE,
    action: String(action || "UNKNOWN"),
    ticketId: String(ticketId || ""),
    ticketNumber: String(ticketNumber || ""),
    details: String(details || ""),
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  records.push(history);

  const historyRecords = records.filter(
    function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL24_CONFIG.HISTORY_TYPE;
    }
  );

  const keepIds = historyRecords
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(b.createdAt).localeCompare(
        String(a.createdAt)
      );
    })
    .slice(0, PBL24_CONFIG.MAX_TICKET_HISTORY)
    .map(function (item) {
      return item.id;
    });

  const cleaned = records.filter(function (item) {
    return !item ||
      item.recordType !== PBL24_CONFIG.HISTORY_TYPE ||
      keepIds.indexOf(item.id) !== -1;
  });

  pbl24WriteRecords(cleaned);

  return history;
}

/* ============================================================
   1226 — GET TICKET HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24GetTicketHistory(ticketId) {
  const id = String(ticketId || "");

  return pbl24ReadRecords()
    .filter(function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL24_CONFIG.HISTORY_TYPE &&
        String(item.ticketId) === id;
    })
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(b.createdAt).localeCompare(
        String(a.createdAt)
      );
    });
}

/* ============================================================
   1227 — TICKET HISTORY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl24GetTicketHistory(ticketId, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl24GetTicketHistory(ticketId)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1228 — RECORD TICKET CORRECTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24RecordCorrection(
  ticket,
  oldValues,
  newValues,
  reason,
  token
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const explanation = String(reason || "").trim();

  if (explanation.length < 5) {
    throw new Error(
      "Correction reason must contain at least 5 characters."
    );
  }

  const records = pbl24ReadRecords();

  const correction = {
    id: "PBL24-CORRECTION-" + Utilities.getUuid(),
    recordType: PBL24_CONFIG.CORRECTION_TYPE,
    ticketId: String(ticket.id || ""),
    ticketNumber: String(ticket.ticketNumber || ""),
    oldValues: oldValues || {},
    newValues: newValues || {},
    reason: explanation,
    correctedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  records.push(correction);

  pbl24WriteRecords(records);

  return correction;
}

/* ============================================================
   1229 — GET CORRECTION HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24GetCorrectionHistory(ticketId) {
  const id = String(ticketId || "");

  return pbl24ReadRecords()
    .filter(function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL24_CONFIG.CORRECTION_TYPE &&
        String(item.ticketId) === id;
    })
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(b.correctedAt).localeCompare(
        String(a.correctedAt)
      );
    });
}

/* ============================================================
   1230 — CORRECTION HISTORY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl24GetCorrectionHistory(ticketId, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl24GetCorrectionHistory(ticketId)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1231 — CORRECT TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24CorrectTicket(
  ticketId,
  changes,
  reason,
  token
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const ticket = pbl24FindTicketById(ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  if (
    ticket.status === "CANCELLED" ||
    ticket.status === "DELETED"
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Cancelled or deleted tickets cannot be corrected."
    );
  }

  const corrections = pbl24GetCorrectionHistory(ticketId);

  if (corrections.length >= PBL24_CONFIG.MAX_CORRECTIONS) {
    throw new Error("Maximum correction count reached.");
  }

  const allowedFields = [
    "product",
    "client",
    "truckRegistration",
    "driver",
    "grossKg",
    "tareKg",
    "netKg",
    "date",
    "time"
  ];

  const oldValues = {};
  const newValues = {};

  allowedFields.forEach(function (field) {
    oldValues[field] = ticket[field];
  });

  const updated = Object.assign({}, ticket);

  Object.keys(changes || {}).forEach(function (field) {
    if (allowedFields.indexOf(field) !== -1) {
      updated[field] = changes[field];
      newValues[field] = changes[field];
    }
  });

  const validation = pbl24ValidateTicketData(updated);

  if (!validation.valid) {
    throw new Error(validation.errors.join(" "));
  }

  if (
    newValues.grossKg !== undefined ||
    newValues.tareKg !== undefined ||
    newValues.netKg !== undefined
  ) {
    updated.grossKg = validation.weights.grossKg;
    updated.tareKg = validation.weights.tareKg;
    updated.netKg = validation.weights.netKg;
    updated.netTonnes = validation.weights.netTonnes;
    newValues.netTonnes = validation.weights.netTonnes;
  }

  if (typeof updateTicketRecord !== "function") {
    throw new Error(
      "Existing ticket update function is unavailable."
    );
  }

  pbl24RecordCorrection(
    ticket,
    oldValues,
    newValues,
    reason,
    token
  );

  const result = updateTicketRecord(
    ticketId,
    updated,
    token
  );

  pbl24RecordTicketHistory(
    "TICKET_CORRECTED",
    ticketId,
    ticket.ticketNumber,
    reason,
    token
  );

  return result;
}

/* ============================================================
   1232 — CORRECT TICKET API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl24CorrectTicket(
  ticketId,
  changes,
  reason,
  token
) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl24CorrectTicket(
      ticketId,
      changes,
      reason,
      token
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1233 — CANCEL TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24CancelTicket(ticketId, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const ticket = pbl24FindTicketById(ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  if (typeof cancelTicket !== "function") {
    throw new Error(
      "Existing ticket cancellation function unavailable."
    );
  }

  const explanation = String(reason || "").trim();

  if (explanation.length < 5) {
    throw new Error(
      "Cancellation reason is required."
    );
  }

  const result = cancelTicket(
    ticketId,
    explanation,
    token
  );

  pbl24RecordTicketHistory(
    "TICKET_CANCELLED",
    ticketId,
    ticket.ticketNumber,
    explanation,
    token
  );

  return result;
}

    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1235 — RESTORE TICKET
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24RestoreTicket(ticketId, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const ticket = pbl24FindTicketById(ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  if (typeof restoreCancelledTicket !== "function") {
    throw new Error(
      "Existing ticket restoration function unavailable."
    );
  }

  const explanation = String(reason || "").trim();

  if (explanation.length < 5) {
    throw new Error(
      "Restoration reason is required."
    );
  }

  const result = restoreCancelledTicket(
    ticketId,
    token
  );

  pbl24RecordTicketHistory(
    "TICKET_RESTORED",
    ticketId,
    ticket.ticketNumber,
    explanation,
    token
  );

  return result;
}

/* ============================================================
   1237 — BUILD REPRINT DATA
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24BuildReprintData(ticketId) {
  const ticket = pbl24FindTicketById(ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  if (
    ticket.status === "CANCELLED" ||
    ticket.status === "DELETED"
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Cancelled tickets cannot be reprinted as valid tickets."
    );
  }

  return {
    ticketId: ticket.id,
    ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber,
    date: ticket.date,
    time: ticket.time,
    product: ticket.product,
    client: ticket.client,
    truckRegistration: ticket.truckRegistration,
    driver: ticket.driver || "",
    grossKg: ticket.grossKg,
    tareKg: ticket.tareKg,
    netKg: ticket.netKg,
    netTonnes: ticket.netTonnes,
    status: ticket.status || "VALID",
    reprintWatermark: "DUPLICATA",
    preparedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1238 — BUILD REPRINT DATA API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl24BuildReprintData(ticketId) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl24BuildReprintData(ticketId)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1239 — RECORD REPRINT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24RecordReprint(ticketId, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const ticket = pbl24FindTicketById(ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  const records = pbl24ReadRecords();

  const history = {
    id: "PBL24-REPRINT-" + Utilities.getUuid(),
    recordType: PBL24_CONFIG.REPRINT_TYPE,
    ticketId: String(ticket.id),
    ticketNumber: String(ticket.ticketNumber),
    reason: String(reason || "Reprint requested."),
    reprintedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  records.push(history);

  pbl24WriteRecords(records);

  pbl24RecordTicketHistory(
    "TICKET_REPRINTED",
    ticketId,
    ticket.ticketNumber,
    history.reason,
    token
  );

  return history;
}

   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl24RecordReprint(ticketId, reason, token) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl24RecordReprint(
      ticketId,
      reason,
      token
    )
  };
}

  return pbl24ReadRecords()
    .filter(function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL24_CONFIG.REPRINT_TYPE &&

    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(b.reprintedAt).localeCompare(
        String(a.reprintedAt)
      );
    });
}

/* ============================================================
   1243 — GET TICKET MANAGEMENT SUMMARY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24GetTicketSummary() {
  const tickets = pbl24GetTickets();

  return {
    total: tickets.length,
    valid: tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      return ticket.status !== "CANCELLED" &&
        ticket.status !== "DELETED";
    }).length,
    cancelled: tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      return ticket.status === "CANCELLED";
    }).length,
    deleted: tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      return ticket.status === "DELETED";
    }).length,
    corrections: pbl24ReadRecords().filter(function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL24_CONFIG.CORRECTION_TYPE;
    }).length,
    reprints: pbl24ReadRecords().filter(function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL24_CONFIG.REPRINT_TYPE;
    }).length
  };
}

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl24GetTicketSummary()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1245 — GET TICKETS BY DATE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24GetTicketsByDate(dateValue) {
  const target = pbl18ParseDate(dateValue);

  if (!target) {
    throw new Error("Invalid date.");
  }

  const key = pbl18DateKey(target);

  return pbl24GetTickets().filter(function (ticket) {
    return pbl18TicketDateKey(ticket) === key;
  });
}

   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl24GetTicketsByDate(dateValue) {

  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1253 — VALIDATE TICKET INTEGRITY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24ValidateTicketIntegrity(ticket) {
  const errors = [];

  if (!ticket.id) {
    errors.push("Missing ticket ID.");
  }

  if (!ticket.ticketNumber) {
    errors.push("Missing ticket number.");
  }

  if (!ticket.product) {
    errors.push("Missing product.");
  }

  if (!ticket.client) {
    errors.push("Missing client.");
  }

  try {
    pbl24ValidateWeights(ticket);
  } catch (error) {
    errors.push(error.message);
  }

  return {
    valid: errors.length === 0,
    errors: errors
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1254 — VALIDATE ALL TICKETS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24ValidateAllTickets() {
  return pbl24GetTickets().map(function (ticket) {
    return {
      id: ticket.id || "",
      ticketNumber: ticket.ticketNumber || "",
      result: pbl24ValidateTicketIntegrity(ticket)
    };
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1255 — VALIDATE ALL TICKETS API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl24ValidateAllTickets(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const results = pbl24ValidateAllTickets();

  return {
    success: true,
    total: results.length,
    invalid: results.filter(function (item) {
      return !item.result.valid;
    }).length,
    data: results
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1256 — FIND DUPLICATE TICKET NUMBERS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24FindDuplicateNumbers() {
  const groups = {};

  pbl24GetTickets().forEach(function (ticket) {
    const number = String(ticket.ticketNumber || "")
      .trim();

    if (!number) return;

    if (!groups[number]) {
      groups[number] = [];
    }

    groups[number].push(ticket.id || "");
  });

  return Object.keys(groups)
    .filter(function (number) {
      return groups[number].length > 1;
    })
    .map(function (number) {
      return {
        ticketNumber: number,
        count: groups[number].length,
        ticketIds: groups[number]
      };
    });
}

/* ============================================================
   1257 — DUPLICATE NUMBER API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl24FindDuplicateNumbers(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl24FindDuplicateNumbers()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1258 — TICKET ACTION PERMISSIONS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24GetTicketPermissions(ticketId, token) {
  const ticket = pbl24FindTicketById(ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  const access = pbl22GetAccessMode(token);
  const valid = ticket.status !== "CANCELLED" &&
    ticket.status !== "DELETED";

  return {
    canView: true,
    canPrint: valid,
    canCorrect: access.unlocked && valid,
    canCancel: access.unlocked && valid,
    canRestore: access.unlocked &&
      (
        ticket.status === "CANCELLED" ||
        ticket.status === "DELETED"
      ),
    canReprint: access.unlocked && valid,
    accessMode: access.mode
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1259 — TICKET PERMISSIONS API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl24GetTicketPermissions(ticketId, token) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl24GetTicketPermissions(
      ticketId,
      token
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1260 — TICKET MANAGEMENT DASHBOARD
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24GetManagementDashboard() {
  const tickets = pbl24GetTickets();

  const today = Utilities.formatDate(
    new Date(),
    PBL17_CONFIG.TIME_ZONE,
    "dd/MM/yyyy"
  );

  const todayTickets = tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
    return String(ticket.date || "") === today;
  });

  const todayNetKg = todayTickets.reduce(
    function (sum, ticket) {
      return sum + Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
    },
    0
  );

  return {
    totalTickets: tickets.length,
    todayTickets: todayTickets.length,
    todayNetKg: Math.round(todayNetKg * 100) / 100,
    todayNetTonnes:
      Math.round((todayNetKg / 1000) * 1000) / 1000,
    ticketSummary: pbl24GetTicketSummary(),
    duplicateNumbers: pbl24FindDuplicateNumbers().length
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1261 — MANAGEMENT DASHBOARD API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl24GetManagementDashboard() {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl24GetManagementDashboard()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1262 — GET TICKET ACTION HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24GetCompleteTicketHistory(ticketId) {
  const id = String(ticketId || "");
  const records = pbl24ReadRecords();

  return records.filter(function (item) {
    return item &&
      String(item.ticketId || "") === id &&
      [
        PBL24_CONFIG.HISTORY_TYPE,
        PBL24_CONFIG.CORRECTION_TYPE,
        PBL24_CONFIG.REPRINT_TYPE
      ].indexOf(item.recordType) !== -1;
  }).sort(function (a, b) {
    const dateA = a.createdAt ||
      a.correctedAt ||
      a.reprintedAt ||
      "";

    const dateB = b.createdAt ||
      b.correctedAt ||
      b.reprintedAt ||
      "";

    return String(dateB).localeCompare(
      String(dateA)
    );
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1263 — COMPLETE TICKET HISTORY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl24GetCompleteTicketHistory(
  ticketId,
  token
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl24GetCompleteTicketHistory(ticketId)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1264 — PREPARE TICKET DETAIL VIEW
   ============================================================ */

function pbl24GetTicketDetail(ticketId, token) {
  const ticket = pbl24FindTicketById(ticketId);

  if (!ticket) {
    throw new Error("Ticket not found.");
  }

  return {
    ticket: ticket,
    permissions: pbl24GetTicketPermissions(
      ticketId,
      token
    ),
    history: pbl22GetAccessMode(token).unlocked
      ? pbl24GetCompleteTicketHistory(ticketId)
      : [],
    correctionCount:
      pbl24GetCorrectionHistory(ticketId).length,
    reprintCount:
      pbl24GetReprintHistory(ticketId).length
  };
}

 *   against the complete project before deployment.

 * PART 25:
 * PRODUCT MANAGEMENT ADVANCED CENTER,
 * PRODUCT CATALOG,

/***************************************************************
 * PONT BASCULE LIVE
 * WEIGHBRIDGE MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
 *
 * PART 25 — ADVANCED PRODUCT MANAGEMENT CENTER
 * Functions 1271–1330
 *
 * MODULES:
 * - Product catalog
 * - Product codes
 * - Product creation and modification
 * - Product archive and restoration
 * - Product pricing
 * - Product movement
 * - Product delivery statistics
 * - Product performance
 * - Product history
 * - Product integrity
 ***************************************************************/

const PBL25_CONFIG = {
  VERSION: "25.0.0",
  HISTORY_TYPE: "PBL25_PRODUCT_HISTORY",
  PRICE_HISTORY_TYPE: "PBL25_PRICE_HISTORY",
  MOVEMENT_TYPE: "PBL25_PRODUCT_MOVEMENT",
  MAX_HISTORY: 500,
  DEFAULT_UNIT: "TONNE",
  DEFAULT_STATUS: "ACTIVE",
  ALLOW_DUPLICATE_CODES: false
};

/* ============================================================
   1271 — READ PRODUCT MANAGEMENT STORAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25ReadRecords() {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13ReadArray !== "function"
  ) {
    return [];
  }

  const records = pbl13ReadArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY
  );

  return Array.isArray(records) ? records : [];
}

/* ============================================================
   1272 — WRITE PRODUCT MANAGEMENT STORAGE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25WriteRecords(records) {
  if (
    typeof PBL13_STORAGE === "undefined" ||
    !PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY ||
    typeof pbl13WriteArray !== "function"
  ) {
    throw new Error("Product history storage unavailable.");
  }

  pbl13WriteArray(
    PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    records
  );

  return true;
}

/* ============================================================
   1273 — GET PRODUCT COLLECTION
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25GetProducts() {
  if (typeof getAllProducts === "function") {
    const products = getAllProducts();

    return Array.isArray(products) ? products : [];
  }

  if (typeof pbl5GetCollection === "function") {
    return pbl5GetCollection(PBL_KEYS.PRODUCTS);
  }

  return [];
}

/* ============================================================
   1274 — FIND PRODUCT BY ID
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25FindProductById(productId) {
  const id = String(productId || "");

  return pbl25GetProducts().find(function (product) {
    return String(product.id || "") === id;
  }) || null;
}

/* ============================================================
   1275 — FIND PRODUCT BY NAME
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25FindProductByName(name) {
  const value = String(name || "")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();

  if (!value) return null;

  return pbl25GetProducts().find(function (product) {
    return String(product.name || "")
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase() === value;
  }) || null;
}

/* ============================================================
   1276 — FIND PRODUCT BY CODE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25FindProductByCode(code) {
  const value = String(code || "")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();

  if (!value) return null;

  return pbl25GetProducts().find(function (product) {
    return String(product.code || "")
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase() === value;
  }) || null;
}

/* ============================================================
   1277 — VALIDATE PRODUCT NAME
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25ValidateProductName(name) {
  const value = String(name || "").trim();

  if (!value) {
    throw new Error("Product name is required.");
  }

  if (value.length < 2) {
    throw new Error("Product name is too short.");
  }

  if (value.length > 100) {
    throw new Error("Product name is too long.");
  }

  return value;
}

/* ============================================================
   1278 — VALIDATE PRODUCT CODE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25ValidateProductCode(code) {
  const value = String(code || "").trim();

  if (!value) return "";

  if (value.length > 40) {
    throw new Error("Product code is too long.");
  }

  return value.toUpperCase();
}

/* ============================================================
   1279 — CHECK PRODUCT NAME UNIQUENESS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25IsNameUnique(name, excludedId) {
  const value = String(name || "")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();

  return !pbl25GetProducts().some(function (product) {
    return (
      String(product.name || "").trim().toLowerCase() === value &&
      String(product.id || "") !== String(excludedId || "")
    );
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1280 — CHECK PRODUCT CODE UNIQUENESS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25IsCodeUnique(code, excludedId) {
  const value = String(code || "")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();

  if (!value) return true;

  return !pbl25GetProducts().some(function (product) {
    return (
      String(product.code || "").trim().toLowerCase() === value &&
      String(product.id || "") !== String(excludedId || "")
    );
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1281 — VALIDATE PRODUCT DATA
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25ValidateProductData(data, excludedId) {
  const product = data || {};
  const errors = [];

  let name = "";
  let code = "";

  try {
    name = pbl25ValidateProductName(product.name);
  } catch (error) {
    errors.push(error.message);
  }

  try {
    code = pbl25ValidateProductCode(product.code);
  } catch (error) {
    errors.push(error.message);
  }

  if (!pbl25IsNameUnique(name, excludedId)) {
    errors.push("A product with this name already exists.");
  }

  if (
    !PBL25_CONFIG.ALLOW_DUPLICATE_CODES &&
    !pbl25IsCodeUnique(code, excludedId)
  ) {
    errors.push("This product code already exists.");
  }

  let price = Number(product.price || 0);

  if (!isFinite(price) || price < 0) {
    errors.push("Product price must be a non-negative number.");
  }

  return {
    valid: errors.length === 0,
    errors: errors,
    normalized: {
      name: name,
      code: code,
      price: price,
      unit: String(product.unit || PBL25_CONFIG.DEFAULT_UNIT),
      description: String(product.description || "").trim(),
      category: String(product.category || "").trim()
    }
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1282 — BUILD PRODUCT RECORD
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25BuildProductRecord(data) {
  const validation = pbl25ValidateProductData(data);

  if (!validation.valid) {
    throw new Error(validation.errors.join(" "));
  }

  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  return {
    id: "PBL25-PRODUCT-" + Utilities.getUuid(),
    name: validation.normalized.name,
    code: validation.normalized.code,
    price: validation.normalized.price,
    unit: validation.normalized.unit,
    description: validation.normalized.description,
    category: validation.normalized.category,
    status: PBL25_CONFIG.DEFAULT_STATUS,
    archived: false,
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1283 — RECORD PRODUCT HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25RecordHistory(
  action,
  product,
  details,
  token
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const records = pbl25ReadRecords();

  const history = {
    id: "PBL25-HISTORY-" + Utilities.getUuid(),
    recordType: PBL25_CONFIG.HISTORY_TYPE,
    action: String(action || "UNKNOWN"),
    productId: String(product && product.id || ""),
    productName: String(product && product.name || ""),
    details: String(details || ""),
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  records.push(history);

  const historyRecords = records.filter(function (item) {
    return item &&
      item.recordType === PBL25_CONFIG.HISTORY_TYPE;
  });

  const retained = historyRecords
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(b.createdAt).localeCompare(
        String(a.createdAt)
      );
    })
    .slice(0, PBL25_CONFIG.MAX_HISTORY)
    .map(function (item) {
      return item.id;
    });

  const cleaned = records.filter(function (item) {
    return !item ||
      item.recordType !== PBL25_CONFIG.HISTORY_TYPE ||
      retained.indexOf(item.id) !== -1;
  });

  pbl25WriteRecords(cleaned);

  return history;
}

/* ============================================================
   1284 — CREATE PRODUCT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25CreateProduct(data, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const product = pbl25BuildProductRecord(data);

  if (typeof createProduct !== "function") {
    throw new Error(
      "Existing product creation function unavailable."
    );
  }

  const result = createProduct(product, token);

  pbl25RecordHistory(
    "PRODUCT_CREATED",
    product,
    "New product created.",
    token
  );

  return result;
}

/* ============================================================
   1285 — CREATE PRODUCT API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25CreateProduct(data, token) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25CreateProduct(data, token)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1286 — UPDATE PRODUCT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25UpdateProduct(productId, changes, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const existing = pbl25FindProductById(productId);

  if (!existing) {
    throw new Error("Product not found.");
  }

  const updated = Object.assign({}, existing, changes || {});

  const validation = pbl25ValidateProductData(
    updated,
    productId
  );

  if (!validation.valid) {
    throw new Error(validation.errors.join(" "));
  }

  updated.name = validation.normalized.name;
  updated.code = validation.normalized.code;
  updated.price = validation.normalized.price;
  updated.unit = validation.normalized.unit;
  updated.description = validation.normalized.description;
  updated.category = validation.normalized.category;
  updated.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();

  if (typeof updateProduct !== "function") {
    throw new Error(
      "Existing product update function unavailable."
    );
  }

  const result = updateProduct(
    productId,
    updated,
    token
  );

  pbl25RecordHistory(
    "PRODUCT_UPDATED",
    updated,
    "Product information updated.",
    token
  );

  return result;
}

/* ============================================================
   1287 — UPDATE PRODUCT API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25UpdateProduct(
  productId,
  changes,
  token
) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25UpdateProduct(
      productId,
      changes,
      token
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1288 — ARCHIVE PRODUCT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25ArchiveProduct(productId, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const product = pbl25FindProductById(productId);

  if (!product) {
    throw new Error("Product not found.");
  }

  if (typeof archiveProduct !== "function") {
    throw new Error(
      "Existing product archive function unavailable."
    );
  }

  const explanation = String(reason || "").trim();

  if (explanation.length < 5) {
    throw new Error("Archive reason is required.");
  }

  const result = archiveProduct(productId, token);

  pbl25RecordHistory(
    "PRODUCT_ARCHIVED",
    product,
    explanation,
    token
  );

  return result;
}

    )
  };

   1290 — RESTORE PRODUCT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25RestoreProduct(productId, reason, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const product = pbl25FindProductById(productId);

  if (!product) {
    throw new Error("Product not found.");
  }

  if (typeof restoreProduct !== "function") {
    throw new Error(
      "Existing product restoration function unavailable."
    );
  }

  const explanation = String(reason || "").trim();

  if (explanation.length < 5) {
    throw new Error("Restoration reason is required.");
  }

  const result = restoreProduct(productId, token);

  pbl25RecordHistory(
    "PRODUCT_RESTORED",
    product,
    explanation,
    token
  );

  return result;
}

/* ============================================================
   1292 — SET PRODUCT PRICE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25SetProductPrice(
  productId,
  newPrice,
  reason,
  token
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const product = pbl25FindProductById(productId);

  if (!product) {
    throw new Error("Product not found.");
  }

  const price = Number(newPrice);

  if (!isFinite(price) || price < 0) {
    throw new Error("Invalid product price.");
  }

  const explanation = String(reason || "").trim();

  if (explanation.length < 5) {
    throw new Error("Price change reason is required.");
  }

  const records = pbl25ReadRecords();

  records.push({
    id: "PBL25-PRICE-" + Utilities.getUuid(),
    recordType: PBL25_CONFIG.PRICE_HISTORY_TYPE,
    productId: String(productId),
    productName: String(product.name),
    oldPrice: Number(product.price || 0),
    newPrice: price,
    reason: explanation,
    changedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  });

  pbl25WriteRecords(records);

  if (typeof updateProduct !== "function") {
    throw new Error(
      "Existing product update function unavailable."
    );
  }

  const updated = Object.assign({}, product, {
    price: price,
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  });

  const result = updateProduct(
    productId,
    updated,
    token
  );

  pbl25RecordHistory(
    "PRODUCT_PRICE_CHANGED",
    updated,
    explanation,
    token
  );

  return result;
}

/* ============================================================
   1293 — SET PRODUCT PRICE API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25SetProductPrice(
  productId,
  price,
  reason,
  token
) {
  return {

    data: pbl25SetProductPrice(
      productId,
      price,
      reason,
      token
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1294 — GET PRICE HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25GetPriceHistory(productId) {
  const id = String(productId || "");

  return pbl25ReadRecords()
    .filter(function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL25_CONFIG.PRICE_HISTORY_TYPE &&
        String(item.productId) === id;
    })
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(b.changedAt).localeCompare(
        String(a.changedAt)
      );
    });
}

   1295 — PRICE HISTORY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25GetPriceHistory(productId, token) {

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25GetPriceHistory(productId)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1296 — GET ACTIVE PRODUCTS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25GetActiveProducts() {
  return pbl25GetProducts().filter(function (product) {
    return product &&
      product.archived !== true &&
      product.status !== "ARCHIVED" &&
      product.active !== false;
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1297 — GET PRODUCT CATALOG
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25GetCatalog() {
  return pbl25GetActiveProducts()
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(a.name || "").localeCompare(
        String(b.name || "")
      );
    })
    .map(function (product) {
      return {
        id: product.id,
        name: product.name,
        code: product.code || "",
        category: product.category || "",
        price: Number(product.price || 0),
        unit: product.unit || PBL25_CONFIG.DEFAULT_UNIT,
        status: product.status || "ACTIVE"
      };
    });
}

/* ============================================================
   1298 — PRODUCT CATALOG API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25GetCatalog() {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25GetCatalog()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1299 — GET TICKETS FOR PRODUCT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25GetProductTickets(productName) {
  const value = String(productName || "")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();

  return pbl24GetTickets().filter(function (ticket) {
    return String(ticket.product || "")
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase() === value &&
      ticket.status !== "CANCELLED" &&
      ticket.status !== "DELETED";
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1300 — CALCULATE PRODUCT TOTALS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25CalculateProductTotals(productName) {
  const tickets = pbl25GetProductTickets(productName);

  const totals = tickets.reduce(function (result, ticket) {
    result.grossKg += Number(ticket.grossKg || 0);
    result.tareKg += Number(ticket.tareKg || 0);
    result.netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
    result.voyages += 1;

    return result;
  }, {
    grossKg: 0,
    tareKg: 0,
    netKg: 0,
    voyages: 0
  });

  return {
    grossKg: totals.grossKg,
    tareKg: totals.tareKg,
    netKg: totals.netKg,
    netTonnes: totals.netKg / 1000,
    voyages: totals.voyages,
    averageNetKg: totals.voyages
      ? totals.netKg / totals.voyages
      : 0
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1301 — PRODUCT PERFORMANCE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25GetProductPerformance(productName) {
  const product = pbl25FindProductByName(productName);

  if (!product) {
    throw new Error("Product not found.");
  }

  const totals = pbl25CalculateProductTotals(
    product.name
  );

  return {
    productId: product.id,
    productName: product.name,
    productCode: product.code || "",
    category: product.category || "",
    price: Number(product.price || 0),
    unit: product.unit || PBL25_CONFIG.DEFAULT_UNIT,
    ...totals
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1302 — PRODUCT PERFORMANCE API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25GetProductPerformance(productName) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25GetProductPerformance(productName)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1303 — PRODUCT RANKING
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25GetProductRanking() {
  return pbl25GetActiveProducts()
    .map(function (product) {
      const totals = pbl25CalculateProductTotals(
        product.name
      );

      return {
        productId: product.id,
        productName: product.name,
        voyages: totals.voyages,
        netKg: totals.netKg,
        netTonnes: totals.netTonnes
      };
    })
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return b.netKg - a.netKg;
    });
}

/* ============================================================
   1304 — PRODUCT RANKING API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25GetProductRanking() {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25GetProductRanking()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1305 — PRODUCT MOVEMENT RECORD
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25RecordMovement(
  productId,
  movementType,
  quantity,
  reason,
  token
) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const product = pbl25FindProductById(productId);

  if (!product) {
    throw new Error("Product not found.");
  }

  const amount = Number(quantity);

  if (!isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {
    throw new Error("Movement quantity must be positive.");
  }

  const type = String(movementType || "")
    .trim()
    .toUpperCase();

  if (
    ["IN", "OUT", "ADJUSTMENT"].indexOf(type) === -1
  ) {
    throw new Error("Invalid movement type.");
  }

  const explanation = String(reason || "").trim();

  if (explanation.length < 5) {
    throw new Error("Movement reason is required.");
  }

  const record = {
    id: "PBL25-MOVEMENT-" + Utilities.getUuid(),
    recordType: PBL25_CONFIG.MOVEMENT_TYPE,
    productId: String(productId),
    productName: String(product.name),
    movementType: type,
    quantity: amount,
    unit: product.unit || PBL25_CONFIG.DEFAULT_UNIT,
    reason: explanation,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  const records = pbl25ReadRecords();

  records.push(record);

  pbl25WriteRecords(records);

  return record;
}

/* ============================================================
   1306 — PRODUCT MOVEMENT API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25RecordMovement(
  productId,
  movementType,
  quantity,
  reason,
  token
) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25RecordMovement(
      productId,
      movementType,
      quantity,
      reason,
      token
    )
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1307 — GET PRODUCT MOVEMENTS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25GetProductMovements(productId) {
  const id = String(productId || "");

  return pbl25ReadRecords()
    .filter(function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL25_CONFIG.MOVEMENT_TYPE &&
        String(item.productId) === id;
    })
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(b.createdAt).localeCompare(
        String(a.createdAt)
      );
    });
}

/* ============================================================
   1308 — PRODUCT MOVEMENT HISTORY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25GetProductMovements(productId, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25GetProductMovements(productId)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1309 — CALCULATE MOVEMENT BALANCE
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25CalculateMovementBalance(productId) {
  const movements = pbl25GetProductMovements(productId);

  return movements.reduce(function (balance, movement) {
    const quantity = Number(movement.quantity || 0);

    if (movement.movementType === "IN") {
      return balance + quantity;
    }

    if (movement.movementType === "OUT") {
      return balance - quantity;
    }

    if (movement.movementType === "ADJUSTMENT") {
      return quantity;
    }

    return balance;
  }, 0);
}

/* ============================================================
   1310 — MOVEMENT BALANCE API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25GetMovementBalance(productId, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: {
      productId: productId,
      balance: pbl25CalculateMovementBalance(productId)
    }
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1311 — PRODUCT HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25GetProductHistory(productId) {
  const id = String(productId || "");

  return pbl25ReadRecords()
    .filter(function (item) {
      return item &&
        item.recordType === PBL25_CONFIG.HISTORY_TYPE &&
        String(item.productId) === id;
    })
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      return String(b.createdAt).localeCompare(
        String(a.createdAt)
      );
    });
}

/* ============================================================
   1312 — PRODUCT HISTORY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25GetProductHistory(productId, token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25GetProductHistory(productId)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1313 — PRODUCT SEARCH
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25SearchProducts(query) {
  const value = String(query || "")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();

  if (!value) return pbl25GetCatalog();

  return pbl25GetCatalog().filter(function (product) {
    return [
      product.name,
      product.code,
      product.category
    ].some(function (field) {
      return String(field || "")
        .toLowerCase()
        .indexOf(value) !== -1;
    });
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1314 — PRODUCT SEARCH API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25SearchProducts(query) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25SearchProducts(query)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1315 — PRODUCT USAGE BY CLIENT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25GetProductUsageByClient(productName) {
  const tickets = pbl25GetProductTickets(productName);
  const groups = {};

  tickets.forEach(function (ticket) {
    const client = String(ticket.client || "UNKNOWN");

    if (!groups[client]) {
      groups[client] = {
        client: client,
        voyages: 0,
        netKg: 0
      };
    }

    groups[client].voyages += 1;
    groups[client].netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).map(function (key) {
    const item = groups[key];

    return {
      client: item.client,
      voyages: item.voyages,
      netKg: item.netKg,
      netTonnes: item.netKg / 1000
    };
  }).sort(function (a, b) {
    return b.netKg - a.netKg;
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1316 — PRODUCT CLIENT USAGE API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25GetProductUsageByClient(productName) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25GetProductUsageByClient(productName)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1317 — PRODUCT USAGE BY TRUCK
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25GetProductUsageByTruck(productName) {
  const tickets = pbl25GetProductTickets(productName);
  const groups = {};

  tickets.forEach(function (ticket) {
    const registration = String(
      ticket.truckRegistration || "UNKNOWN"
    );

    if (!groups[registration]) {
      groups[registration] = {
        registration: registration,
        voyages: 0,
        netKg: 0
      };
    }

    groups[registration].voyages += 1;
    groups[registration].netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).map(function (key) {
    const item = groups[key];

    return {
      registration: item.registration,
      voyages: item.voyages,
      netKg: item.netKg,
      netTonnes: item.netKg / 1000
    };
  }).sort(function (a, b) {
    return b.netKg - a.netKg;
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1318 — PRODUCT TRUCK USAGE API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25GetProductUsageByTruck(productName) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25GetProductUsageByTruck(productName)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1319 — PRODUCT DAILY MOVEMENT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25GetDailyProductMovement(productName) {
  const tickets = pbl25GetProductTickets(productName);
  const groups = {};

  tickets.forEach(function (ticket) {
    const date = String(ticket.date || "UNKNOWN");

    if (!groups[date]) {
      groups[date] = {
        date: date,
        voyages: 0,
        netKg: 0
      };
    }

    groups[date].voyages += 1;
    groups[date].netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).sort().map(function (key) {
    const item = groups[key];

    return {
      date: item.date,
      voyages: item.voyages,
      netKg: item.netKg,
      netTonnes: item.netKg / 1000
    };
  });
}

   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25GetDailyProductMovement(productName) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25GetDailyProductMovement(productName)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1321 — PRODUCT MONTHLY MOVEMENT
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25GetMonthlyProductMovement(productName) {
  const tickets = pbl25GetProductTickets(productName);
  const groups = {};

  tickets.forEach(function (ticket) {
    const parsed = pbl18ParseDate(ticket.date);

    if (!parsed) return;

    const key = Utilities.formatDate(
      parsed,
      PBL17_CONFIG.TIME_ZONE,
      "yyyy-MM"
    );

    if (!groups[key]) {
      groups[key] = {
        month: key,
        voyages: 0,
        netKg: 0
      };
    }

    groups[key].voyages += 1;
    groups[key].netKg += Number(ticket.netKg || 0);
  });

  return Object.keys(groups).sort().map(function (key) {
    const item = groups[key];

    return {
      month: item.month,
      voyages: item.voyages,
      netKg: item.netKg,
      netTonnes: item.netKg / 1000
    };
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1322 — MONTHLY PRODUCT MOVEMENT API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25GetMonthlyProductMovement(productName) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25GetMonthlyProductMovement(productName)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1323 — PRODUCT CATALOG STATISTICS
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25GetCatalogStatistics() {
  const products = pbl25GetProducts();

  return {
    total: products.length,
    active: products.filter(function (product) {
      return product.archived !== true &&
        product.status !== "ARCHIVED";
    }).length,
    archived: products.filter(function (product) {
      return product.archived === true ||
        product.status === "ARCHIVED";
    }).length,
    withCode: products.filter(function (product) {
      return !!product.code;
    }).length,
    withPrice: products.filter(function (product) {
      return Number(product.price || 0) > 0;
    }).length
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1324 — CATALOG STATISTICS API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25GetCatalogStatistics() {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25GetCatalogStatistics()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1325 — PRODUCT DATA INTEGRITY
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25ValidateProductIntegrity() {
  return pbl25GetProducts().map(function (product) {
    const errors = [];

    if (!product.id) {
      errors.push("Missing product ID.");
    }

    if (!product.name) {
      errors.push("Missing product name.");
    }

    if (
      product.price !== undefined &&
      (
        !isFinite(Number(product.price)) ||
        Number(product.price) < 0
      )
    ) {
      errors.push("Invalid price.");
    }

    return {
      productId: product.id || "",
      productName: product.name || "",
      valid: errors.length === 0,
      errors: errors
    };
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   1326 — PRODUCT INTEGRITY API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25ValidateProductIntegrity(token) {
  requireEditAccess(token);

  const results = pbl25ValidateProductIntegrity();

  return {
    success: true,
    total: results.length,
    invalid: results.filter(function (item) {
      return !item.valid;
    }).length,
    data: results
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1327 — PRODUCT DETAIL
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25GetProductDetail(productId) {
  const product = pbl25FindProductById(productId);

  if (!product) {
    throw new Error("Product not found.");
  }

  return {
    product: product,
    performance: pbl25CalculateProductTotals(product.name),
    movementBalance:
      pbl25CalculateMovementBalance(productId),
    priceHistory:
      pbl25GetPriceHistory(productId),
    recentHistory:
      pbl25GetProductHistory(productId).slice(0, 20)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1328 — PRODUCT DETAIL API
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25GetProductDetail(productId, token) {
  return {
    success: true,
    data: pbl25GetProductDetail(productId)
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1329 — PRODUCT MANAGEMENT HEALTH
   ============================================================ */

function pbl25HealthCheck() {
  const products = pbl25GetProducts();

  return {
    module: "PBL25_PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT",
    version: PBL25_CONFIG.VERSION,
    productSourceAvailable:
      typeof getAllProducts === "function" ||
      typeof pbl5GetCollection === "function",
    totalProducts: products.length,
    activeProducts: pbl25GetActiveProducts().length,
    duplicateNames: products.length -
      new Set(products.map(function (product) {
        return String(product.name || "").toLowerCase();
      })).size,
    storageAvailable:
      typeof PBL13_STORAGE !== "undefined" &&
      !!PBL13_STORAGE.ACTIVITY,
    checkedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1330 — INITIALIZE PRODUCT MANAGEMENT
   ============================================================ */

function apiPbl25InitializeProductManagement() {
  return {
    success: true,
    application: "PONT BASCULE LIVE",
    module: "ADVANCED PRODUCT MANAGEMENT CENTER",
    version: PBL25_CONFIG.VERSION,
    configuration: {
      defaultUnit: PBL25_CONFIG.DEFAULT_UNIT,
      defaultStatus: PBL25_CONFIG.DEFAULT_STATUS,
      duplicateCodesAllowed:
        PBL25_CONFIG.ALLOW_DUPLICATE_CODES
    },
    catalogStatistics: pbl25GetCatalogStatistics(),
    health: pbl25HealthCheck(),
    initializedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}
